## Response:
7/15/2026, 3:55:04 AM

Saya akan susun sebagai peta onboarding praktis untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS berbasis Java 17+, dengan fokus CDI/Weld sebagai composition/runtime context layer, bukan sekadar teori DI.

Iya. Di bawah ini **Decomposition Map** untuk area **IoC, DI, AOP Proxies, Scopes, Context Propagation, Feature Flag, Config, Secret, External Config Source Loader dengan CDI/Weld** dalam konteks **Java 17+, Jersey/JAX-RS microservice**.

Catatan penting: untuk aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS**, jangan asumsikan semua DI otomatis sama seperti Spring. Jersey punya layer DI sendiri, yaitu HK2, sementara CDI/Weld adalah container CDI. Jersey memang punya dukungan integrasi CDI/Weld SE, tetapi boundary integrasinya harus jelas supaya resource, provider, filter, service, dan lifecycle bean tidak bentrok. Jersey mendokumentasikan custom injection/lifecycle management serta dukungan CDI/Weld SE secara terpisah. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/ioc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Introduction

Topik ini bisa dipahami sebagai **runtime composition layer** untuk microservice Java:

> “Bagaimana object dibuat, dipilih, dikonfigurasi, diproxy, diberi lifecycle, diberi context, dan diberi behavior lintas-cutting seperti logging, security, transaction, tenant, feature flag, tracing, dan config.”

Dalam aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS berbasis CDI/Weld, area ini biasanya menjadi fondasi untuk:

- REST resource creation dan injection
- service/repository wiring
- MyBatis mapper/session lifecycle
- tenant/user/security context
- request correlation dan tracing
- transaction boundary
- feature flag evaluation
- config loading dari env var, system properties, classpath, secrets, external source
- async/thread propagation
- Camunda workflow/process context bridging

CDI sendiri menyediakan model bean, injection, qualifier, scope, interceptor, decorator, event, producer, dan extension. CDI 4.1 adalah spesifikasi Jakarta CDI yang relevan untuk namespace modern `jakarta.*`; Weld adalah implementasi CDI/reference implementation yang banyak dipakai untuk Java SE dan container integration. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 3. Decomposition Table

| Area | Practical Skill | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Implementasi Praktis di Jersey + CDI/Weld | Failure Mode yang Sering Terjadi |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| **IoC Mental Model** | Core | Paham bahwa object lifecycle dikendalikan container, bukan manual `new` di mana-mana. | Pisahkan resource, service, repository, config provider, client factory sebagai CDI beans. | Object dibuat manual sehingga `@Inject`, interceptor, scope, config, dan proxy tidak aktif. |
| **CDI Bootstrapping** | Core | Cara Weld dimulai di Java SE, servlet container, embedded server, atau integration module. | Pastikan Weld aktif sebelum Jersey resource/provider butuh injection. | Resource JAX-RS hidup, tetapi dependency CDI `null` atau tidak ter-resolve. |
| **Jersey DI vs CDI/Weld** | Core | Pahami boundary HK2/Jersey injection dan CDI injection. | Gunakan satu strategi dominan: CDI untuk app/service layer, Jersey provider untuk HTTP layer, bridge dengan hati-hati. | Dua container membuat lifecycle berbeda untuk class yang sama. |
| **Bean Discovery** | Core | `beans.xml`, annotated discovery, package scanning, classpath visibility. | Pastikan bean berada di module/package yang ditemukan Weld. | Bean tidak ditemukan karena discovery mode, dependency Maven, atau module path. |
| **Injection Point** | Core | Constructor injection, field injection, method injection, optional injection. | Prefer constructor injection untuk service/repository; gunakan producer untuk resource eksternal. | Circular dependency, ambiguous dependency, unsatisfied dependency. |
| **Qualifier** | Core | Memilih implementasi bean berdasarkan annotation, bukan string/manual if. | Contoh: `@PrimaryDb`, `@ReadReplica`, `@ExternalPaymentClient`, `@InternalClient`. | Multiple implementation menyebabkan ambiguous injection. |
| **Producer Method/Field** | Core | Membuat bean dari factory, config, external client, mapper, connection provider. | `@Produces DataSource`, `@Produces SqlSessionFactory`, `@Produces Clock`, `@Produces ObjectMapper`. | Producer terlalu berat, tidak thread-safe, atau salah scope. |
| **Scopes** | Core | `@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, `@Dependent`, custom scope. | Service stateless biasanya `@ApplicationScoped`; request context seperti user/tenant biasanya `@RequestScoped`; object kecil/transient bisa `@Dependent`. Weld menjelaskan bahwa scope menentukan lifecycle instance dan client mana yang melihat instance tersebut. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest/en-US/html/scopescontexts.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | State request disimpan di singleton/application scope. |
| **Client Proxy** | Core | Normal scoped bean biasanya di-inject sebagai proxy, bukan direct instance. | Aman inject `@RequestScoped` ke `@ApplicationScoped` karena proxy resolve instance sesuai active context. CDI spec menjelaskan normal-scoped contextual reference sebagai client proxy; Weld docs juga menekankan scope/context lifecycle. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Baca/tulis field langsung pada proxied bean; final class/method/private constructor membuat bean sulit diproxy. |
| **AOP / Interceptor** | Core | Cross-cutting behavior via interceptor binding. | Buat `@Audited`, `@Measured`, `@Authorized`, `@TransactionalBoundary`, `@Idempotent`. Jakarta Interceptors mendefinisikan interposition pada business method dan lifecycle events; Weld docs membahas business method interception. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/interceptors/2.2/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Self-invocation tidak melewati proxy/interceptor; interceptor dipasang di method private/final; urutan interceptor tidak jelas. |
| **Decorator** | Intermediate | Menambahkan behavior pada interface/service tertentu. | Cocok untuk wrapping business service: audit enrichment, masking, fallback, validation. | Dipakai untuk semua hal seperti interceptor padahal targetnya spesifik. |
| **CDI Events** | Intermediate | In-process event decoupling. | Event internal: config refreshed, tenant changed, request completed, audit emitted. | Disalahgunakan untuk business-critical async event tanpa durability. |
| **Request Context** | Core | Membuat context object per HTTP request. | `ContainerRequestFilter` membaca headers lalu set `RequestContext`: correlation ID, user ID, tenant ID, locale, auth claims. | Context leak antar request karena ThreadLocal tidak di-clear. |
| **Security Context** | Core | Mapping auth token/claims ke principal/roles/permissions. | Bridge `jakarta.ws.rs.core.SecurityContext` ke CDI `CurrentUser`. | Role/tenant diambil dari header mentah tanpa validasi token. |
| **Tenant Context** | Core | Tenant resolution, validation, propagation, isolation. | Resolve dari JWT claim, header internal, subdomain, atau route; inject `TenantContext`. | Tenant spoofing, query tanpa tenant filter, cross-tenant cache leak. |
| **Transaction Context** | Core | Boundary transaksi DB/service. | Untuk MyBatis, kelola `SqlSession`/connection lifecycle per unit-of-work; interceptor bisa membuka/commit/rollback jika arsitektur mendukung. | Transaction tersebar di resource/service/repository; session leak; rollback tidak konsisten. |
| **Async Context Propagation** | Advanced | Propagate context ke executor/thread. | Snapshot context sebelum submit task; restore di worker; clear setelah selesai. | ThreadLocal context hilang di async, atau malah bocor ke task berikutnya. |
| **Multi-thread Safety** | Advanced | Thread-safety pada scoped bean dan singleton service. | `@ApplicationScoped` harus stateless atau immutable; state mutable request harus `@RequestScoped`. | Race condition karena mutable field di singleton service. |
| **HTTP Service-to-Service Context** | Intermediate | Propagate auth, correlation, tenant, trace, locale. | Client filter/interceptor menambahkan `Authorization`, `X-Correlation-Id`, `X-Tenant-Id`, `traceparent`. | Downstream tidak bisa audit/trace karena header tidak diteruskan. |
| **gRPC Context Propagation** | Intermediate | Metadata propagation. | Map request context ke gRPC metadata. | Metadata security-sensitive diteruskan ke service yang tidak berhak. |
| **Kafka Context Propagation** | Intermediate | Header propagation untuk async messaging. | Producer menulis correlation ID, causation ID, tenant ID, schema version, auth-subject jika aman. Consumer membuat context baru dari headers. | Consumer memproses message tanpa tenant/correlation. |
| **Camunda Process Context** | Advanced | Bridge antara HTTP/request context dan workflow/process variables. | Simpan stable business context sebagai process variables; jangan simpan transient request object. | Tenant/user/correlation hilang setelah async job/retry; process variable berisi secret. |
| **Feature Flag** | Intermediate | Runtime behavior switching tanpa redeploy. | Gunakan abstraction seperti `FeatureFlagService`; OpenFeature menyediakan spesifikasi/API vendor-agnostic untuk feature flagging. ([OpenFeature](https://openfeature.dev/docs/reference/intro?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Flag dievaluasi tanpa user/tenant context; flag permanen menjadi technical debt. |
| **Application Profile** | Core | Environment-specific behavior. | `dev`, `test`, `staging`, `prod`, `onprem`, `aws`, `azure`; config menentukan provider/client/endpoint. | Logic profile tersebar dalam `if` di banyak tempat. |
| **System Properties** | Core | JVM-level config via `-Dkey=value`. | Untuk override runtime kecil: feature off, endpoint, timeout, profile. MicroProfile Config default source mencakup system properties. ([GitHub](https://github.com/eclipse/microprofile-config/blob/master/spec/src/main/asciidoc/configsources.asciidoc?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Sulit diaudit jika tidak dicetak sanitized saat startup. |
| **Environment Variables** | Core | Container/K8s-friendly config. | Docker/Kubernetes inject env var; map ke config keys. MicroProfile Config menjelaskan mapping env var dan default source env variables. ([Eclipse Downloads](https://download.eclipse.org/microprofile/microprofile-config-3.1/microprofile-config-spec-3.1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Naming mismatch antara `MY_APP_TIMEOUT` dan `my.app.timeout`. |
| **Classpath Resources** | Core | Static config bundled dalam artifact. | `META-INF/microprofile-config.properties`, default config, templates, SQL files. MicroProfile Config default source mencakup `META-INF/microprofile-config.properties`. ([GitHub](https://github.com/eclipse/microprofile-config/blob/master/spec/src/main/asciidoc/configsources.asciidoc?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Secret ikut ter-bundle ke JAR/image. |
| **External Config Source Loader** | Advanced | Load config dari file, DB, Vault, AWS/Azure, config server. | Implement config source abstraction: priority, refresh, cache, validation, fallback. MicroProfile Config memakai konsep `ConfigSource` untuk menggabungkan config dari banyak sumber. ([MicroProfile](https://microprofile.io/specifications/microprofile-config-2/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Startup tergantung remote config tanpa fallback; config berubah tanpa audit. |
| **Secret Management** | Core | Pisahkan secret dari config biasa. | Secret via Kubernetes Secret, Vault, AWS Secrets Manager, Azure Key Vault; inject via provider, bukan hardcoded. | Secret masuk log, exception, process args, image layer, GitHub repo. |
| **Config Validation** | Core | Fail fast saat config invalid. | Validasi required keys, range timeout, URL, enum profile, secret presence saat startup. | App running dengan config default yang salah. |
| **Config Precedence** | Core | Menentukan urutan override. | Contoh umum: system properties > env vars > classpath config; MicroProfile Config punya default ordinals untuk sumber-sumber ini. ([GitHub](https://github.com/eclipse/microprofile-config/blob/master/spec/src/main/asciidoc/configsources.asciidoc?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Nilai prod kalah oleh default classpath tanpa sadar. |
| **Runtime Reload** | Advanced | Dynamic config/flag refresh. | Gunakan cache TTL, versioning, event notification, safe rollback. | Perubahan config tidak atomic; instance berbeda membaca nilai berbeda. |
| **Observability Context** | Intermediate | Correlation, trace, span, request ID, causation ID. | Buat `CorrelationContext`; propagate ke logs, metrics, HTTP/Kafka/gRPC. | Log tidak bisa direkonstruksi across microservices. |
| **Testing CDI/Weld** | Core | Test injection, scope, interceptor, config. | Weld JUnit/container test; mock producer; test request context activation. | Unit test lolos, runtime gagal karena CDI lifecycle tidak diuji. |
| **Operational Hardening** | Advanced | Safe startup/shutdown, readiness, config print, dependency health. | Saat startup: print active profile, config source sanitized, enabled flags, bean wiring summary. | Debug production sulit karena runtime composition tidak terlihat. |

---

# 4. Practical Grouping: Cara Memecah Skill Besar Ini

## A. Composition Skills

Fokusnya: **bagaimana object dibuat dan disusun**.

```text
Composition
├── Bean discovery
├── Injection
├── Qualifier
├── Producer
├── Alternative
├── Lifecycle callback
└── Extension
```

Skill praktis yang harus bisa dibuat:

- service injection
- repository injection
- mapper/client producer
- multiple implementation selection
- test double injection
- environment-specific bean selection

Contoh target latihan:

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface ReadReplica {}

@ApplicationScoped
public class DatabaseProducer {

    @Produces
    @ReadReplica
    public DataSource readReplicaDataSource(AppConfig config) {
        return buildDataSource(config.readReplicaUrl());
    }
}
```

---

## B. Scope & Lifecycle Skills

Fokusnya: **object ini hidup berapa lama dan aman dipakai oleh siapa**.

```text
Scope
├── @ApplicationScoped
├── @RequestScoped
├── @Dependent
├── custom scope
├── proxyability
├── lifecycle callback
└── destruction/cleanup
```

Rule praktis:

| Bean Type | Scope yang Umum | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Stateless service | `@ApplicationScoped` | Jangan simpan request state di field. |
| Request context | `@RequestScoped` | User, tenant, correlation ID. |
| Config provider | `@ApplicationScoped` | Immutable/cache-aware. |
| SQL session/unit of work | Request/transaction bound | Jangan global singleton. |
| Lightweight helper | `@Dependent` | Ikut lifecycle injection target. |
| External client | `@ApplicationScoped` | Pastikan thread-safe dan close saat shutdown. |

---

## C. AOP / Proxy Skills

Fokusnya: **behavior tambahan tanpa menyebar boilerplate**.

```text
AOP/CDI Interceptor
├── Logging
├── Metrics
├── Audit
├── Authorization
├── Transaction boundary
├── Retry/timeout
├── Idempotency
└── Feature guard
```

Contoh pattern:

```java
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Audited {
}
```

```java
@Audited
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {
    public Decision approveCase(String caseId) {
        return doApproval(caseId);
    }
}
```

Yang harus diingat: interceptor hanya aktif saat method dipanggil melalui CDI-managed reference/proxy. Pemanggilan method internal dari class yang sama sering tidak melewati interceptor.

---

## D. Context Propagation Skills

Fokusnya: **data runtime yang harus ikut mengalir dari entry point ke downstream**.

```text
Context Propagation
├── HTTP request
│   ├── correlation ID
│   ├── tenant ID
│   ├── user ID
│   ├── roles/claims
│   └── locale
│
├── DB transaction
│   ├── connection/session
│   ├── rollback marker
│   └── audit actor
│
├── Async/thread
│   ├── context snapshot
│   ├── restore
│   └── cleanup
│
├── Service-to-service
│   ├── HTTP headers
│   ├── gRPC metadata
│   └── Kafka headers
│
└── Workflow/process
    ├── process variables
    ├── business key
    ├── tenant
    ├── initiator
    └── correlation ID
```

Practical invariant:

> Context boleh dipropagate, tetapi jangan semua hal dipropagate.  
> Propagate hanya data yang memang stabil, aman, dan dibutuhkan downstream.

Misalnya:

| Context | Boleh Dipropagate? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| correlation ID | Ya | Untuk tracing/logging. |
| tenant ID | Ya, dengan validasi | Jangan percaya header eksternal mentah. |
| user ID | Kadang | Tergantung trust boundary. |
| raw JWT | Hati-hati | Jangan forward ke service yang tidak perlu. |
| DB connection | Tidak | Tidak boleh lewat thread/service boundary. |
| transaction object | Tidak | Local process only. |
| Camunda business key | Ya | Stabil untuk workflow correlation. |
| secret | Tidak | Jangan pernah menjadi context umum. |

---

## E. Config & Secret Skills

Fokusnya: **bagaimana aplikasi membaca nilai runtime dengan aman dan deterministik**.

```text
Configuration
├── Static config
│   ├── application.properties
│   ├── classpath resource
│   └── default value
│
├── Runtime override
│   ├── system properties
│   ├── environment variables
│   └── container args
│
├── Deployment config
│   ├── Docker env
│   ├── Kubernetes ConfigMap
│   ├── Kubernetes Secret
│   ├── Helm values
│   └── GitHub Actions variables
│
├── External config
│   ├── file mount
│   ├── database
│   ├── config server
│   ├── AWS Parameter Store/Secrets Manager
│   ├── Azure App Configuration/Key Vault
│   └── Vault
│
└── Runtime controls
    ├── validation
    ├── precedence
    ├── reload
    ├── cache
    ├── fallback
    └── audit
```

MicroProfile Config berguna sebagai mental model karena menggabungkan konfigurasi dari banyak `ConfigSource`; default source yang disebut spesifikasi mencakup system properties, environment variables, dan `META-INF/microprofile-config.properties`. ([MicroProfile](https://microprofile.io/specifications/microprofile-config-2/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 5. Roadmap Alur Belajar yang Direkomendasikan

## Phase 1 - Foundation CDI/Weld

Pelajari:

1. `@Inject`
2. constructor injection
3. qualifier
4. producer
5. bean discovery
6. `@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, `@Dependent`
7. client proxy

Target praktis:

- bisa membuat REST resource → service → repository dengan CDI
- bisa membuat producer untuk config/client/mapper
- bisa menjelaskan kenapa bean tertentu singleton-like, request-bound, atau dependent

---

## Phase 2 - Jersey + CDI Integration

Pelajari:

1. Jersey resource lifecycle
2. Jersey provider/filter lifecycle
3. Weld SE bootstrap
4. CDI injection ke resource/provider
5. perbedaan HK2 vs CDI
6. testing resource dengan injection aktif

Target praktis:

- `ContainerRequestFilter` bisa inject service/context
- `ExceptionMapper` bisa inject error mapper/config
- resource tidak manual `new Service()`

---

## Phase 3 - Scope, Proxy, and Thread Safety

Pelajari:

1. normal scope vs pseudo scope
2. proxyability rules
3. final class/method limitation
4. mutable state risk
5. request state vs application state
6. cleanup lifecycle

Target praktis:

- tidak ada request/user/tenant state di `@ApplicationScoped`
- bisa inject request context ke service singleton dengan aman
- paham kenapa ThreadLocal harus dibersihkan

---

## Phase 4 - AOP/Interceptor Practical Patterns

Pelajari:

1. interceptor binding
2. audit interceptor
3. metrics interceptor
4. authorization interceptor
5. transaction boundary interceptor
6. idempotency interceptor
7. ordering interceptor

Target praktis:

- buat `@Audited`
- buat `@Measured`
- buat `@RequiresPermission`
- buat `@TransactionalBoundary`
- tahu batasan self-invocation

---

## Phase 5 - Context Propagation

Pelajari:

1. request context
2. security context
3. tenant context
4. correlation/trace context
5. async/thread context
6. HTTP/gRPC/Kafka propagation
7. Camunda process variable bridging

Target praktis:

- setiap request punya correlation ID
- downstream HTTP/Kafka membawa correlation/tenant
- async executor tidak kehilangan context
- Camunda process punya business key, tenant, initiator, correlation ID

---

## Phase 6 - Config, Profile, Secret

Pelajari:

1. application properties
2. profile config
3. system properties
4. env vars
5. classpath resource
6. config precedence
7. secret provider
8. external config source
9. validation and fail-fast

Target praktis:

- app bisa jalan di local, Docker, Kubernetes, on-prem, AWS, Azure
- secret tidak masuk JAR/log/Git
- startup print sanitized config summary
- config invalid membuat app fail fast

---

## Phase 7 - Feature Flag and Runtime Control

Pelajari:

1. boolean flag
2. percentage rollout
3. tenant/user targeting
4. kill switch
5. experiment flag
6. operational flag
7. cleanup lifecycle

Target praktis:

- flag dievaluasi berdasarkan tenant/user/context
- critical integration punya kill switch
- flag punya owner dan expiry date
- tidak ada flag lama yang menjadi permanent branching

---

## Response:
7/15/2026, 3:56:52 AM

Saya rekomendasikan **14 seri** untuk topik ini supaya praktis, tidak terlalu padat, dan urut dari fondasi sampai production hardening.

| Seri | Topik |
|---:|---|
| 1 | Mental Model IoC/DI/CDI/Weld di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice |
| 2 | Bootstrapping Jersey + Weld: Resource, Service, Repository, Provider |

Urutan ini paling aman untuk onboarding karena mulai dari **composition model**, lalu **lifecycle/context**, lalu **cross-cutting behavior**, lalu **config/secret/feature flag**, terakhir **operational hardening**.

## Response:
7/15/2026, 3:59:00 AM

Saya mulai dari **Seri 1/14**: mental model praktis IoC/DI/CDI/Weld di Jersey/JAX-RS microservice. Fokusnya akan saya arahkan ke keputusan desain, implementasi minimum, dan failure mode yang sering muncul saat onboarding ke codebase nyata.

# Seri 1/14 - Mental Model IoC/DI/CDI/Weld di Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

Seri ini membangun fondasi: **bagaimana object dibuat, dihubungkan, diproxy, diberi lifecycle, dan dipakai secara aman** dalam aplikasi **Java 17+, Jersey/JAX-RS, CDI/Weld**.

Intinya:

> CDI/Weld bukan hanya “alat `@Inject`”.  
> CDI/Weld adalah **runtime composition model**: container menentukan object mana yang hidup, kapan dibuat, dependency mana yang dipakai, scope-nya apa, dan behavior tambahan apa yang aktif melalui interceptor/proxy.

CDI menyediakan contextual lifecycle, injection, interceptor, decorator, event, producer, qualifier, dan extension. Jakarta CDI 4.1 adalah spesifikasi modern berbasis `jakarta.*`, sedangkan Weld adalah implementasi CDI yang umum dipakai, termasuk untuk Java SE/server-side integration. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 1. Mental Model Praktis

Dalam microservice Jersey/JAX-RS, jangan bayangkan aplikasi sebagai kumpulan class yang saling `new`.

Bayangkan seperti ini:

```text
HTTP Request
   │
   ▼
Jersey Resource / Filter / Provider
   │
   ▼
CDI/Weld Bean Graph
   │
   ├── Service
   ├── Repository
   ├── MyBatis Session/Mapper Producer
   ├── Config Provider
   ├── Feature Flag Service
   ├── Tenant/User/Request Context
   └── External Client
```

CDI/Weld bertugas menjawab pertanyaan:

| Pertanyaan | Dijawab oleh |
|---|---|
| Object ini dibuat oleh siapa? | CDI container / Weld |
| Dependency disuntikkan dari mana? | `@Inject`, `@Produces`, qualifier |
| Object ini hidup berapa lama? | Scope |
| Object ini instance asli atau proxy? | CDI client proxy |
| Behavior seperti audit/metrics/transaction masuk di mana? | Interceptor |
| Config/client/mapper dibuat di mana? | Producer |
| Per request user/tenant/correlation ID disimpan di mana? | Request-scoped context bean |
| Apa yang tidak boleh dibuat manual? | Bean yang butuh injection/interceptor/scope |

---

## 2. Diagram Praktis

```text
Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice
│
├── HTTP Layer
│   ├── Resource: CaseResource
│   ├── Request Filter: Correlation/Tenant/Auth
│   ├── Response Filter
│   └── ExceptionMapper
│
├── CDI/Weld Composition Layer
│   ├── @Inject
│   ├── @Qualifier
│   ├── @Produces
│   ├── @ApplicationScoped
│   ├── @RequestScoped
│   ├── @Dependent
│   ├── Interceptor
│   └── Client Proxy
│
├── Application Layer
│   ├── CaseService
│   ├── ApprovalService
│   ├── AuditService
│   └── FeatureFlagService
│
├── Persistence Layer
│   ├── MyBatis Mapper
│   ├── SqlSession
│   ├── Transaction Boundary
│   └── PostgreSQL
│
├── Context Layer
│   ├── RequestContext
│   ├── CurrentUser
│   ├── TenantContext
│   ├── CorrelationContext
│   └── TransactionContext
│
└── External Runtime
    ├── application.properties
    ├── env vars
    ├── system properties
    ├── Kubernetes ConfigMap/Secret
    ├── AWS/Azure secret/config source
    └── Docker/Linux process environment
```

---

## 3. Core Principle: CDI/Weld adalah Composition Boundary

Dalam aplikasi enterprise, problem utamanya bukan cuma “bagaimana inject class A ke class B”.

Problem sebenarnya:

> Bagaimana memastikan semua object penting dibuat secara konsisten, testable, observable, aman terhadap scope, dan bisa diganti sesuai environment?

Contoh buruk:

```java
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseService caseService = new CaseService();

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getCase(@PathParam("id") String id) {
        return Response.ok(caseService.getCase(id)).build();
    }
}
```

Masalahnya:

| Masalah | Dampak |
|---|---|
| `CaseService` dibuat manual | Dependency di dalamnya tidak di-inject |
| Interceptor tidak aktif | Audit/metrics/security/transaction bisa tidak jalan |
| Config tidak masuk | Service memakai default/hardcoded value |
| Testing sulit | Tidak bisa swap implementation |
| Lifecycle tidak dikontrol | Resource leak lebih mudah terjadi |

Versi yang benar secara CDI:

```java
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseService caseService;

    @Inject
    public CaseResource(CaseService caseService) {
        this.caseService = caseService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getCase(@PathParam("id") String id) {
        return Response.ok(caseService.getCase(id)).build();
    }
}
```

Lalu service-nya:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        String tenantId = requestContext.tenantId();
        return caseRepository.findByTenantAndCaseId(tenantId, caseId);
    }
}
```

---

## 4. CDI vs Jersey DI: Hal yang Harus Dipahami Sejak Awal

Jersey punya mekanisme DI/lifecycle sendiri, historisnya berbasis HK2. CDI/Weld adalah container CDI. Karena itu, dalam aplikasi Jersey + Weld, perlu jelas class mana yang dikelola Jersey, mana yang dikelola CDI, dan bagaimana integrasinya. Jersey mendokumentasikan dukungan CDI/Weld SE untuk memakai fitur CDI dalam environment Jersey. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/cdi.support.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

| Komponen | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Business service | CDI bean |
| Repository / DAO | CDI bean |
| Config provider | CDI bean |
| External client factory | CDI producer |
| MyBatis factory/session producer | CDI producer |
| JAX-RS resource | Bisa CDI-managed, tergantung integrasi Jersey-Weld |
| JAX-RS filter/provider | Pastikan injection bekerja lewat integrasi yang benar |
| Low-level Jersey binding | Jangan dicampur tanpa alasan kuat |
| Object utility stateless kecil | Bisa plain class/static jika tidak butuh lifecycle/context/interceptor |

Jangan membuat class yang sama kadang dikelola Jersey/HK2, kadang CDI. Itu sumber bug lifecycle yang halus.

---

## 5. Bean Graph: Cara Membaca Aplikasi CDI

Saat onboarding ke codebase, baca aplikasi sebagai **bean graph**.

Contoh:

```text
CaseResource
   └── CaseService
          ├── CaseRepository
          │      └── SqlSessionProvider
          ├── FeatureFlagService
          │      └── ConfigProvider
          ├── AuditService
          │      └── CurrentRequestContext
          └── ExternalDecisionClient
                 └── HttpClientConfig
```

Pertanyaan praktis saat membaca code:

| Pertanyaan | Kenapa Penting |
|---|---|
| Ini dibuat manual atau oleh CDI? | Menentukan apakah injection/interceptor/scope aktif |
| Scope-nya apa? | Menentukan lifecycle dan thread-safety |
| Ada qualifier? | Menentukan implementation mana yang dipilih |
| Ada producer? | Menentukan factory logic |
| Ada interceptor? | Menentukan behavior tersembunyi |
| Ada request context? | Menentukan user/tenant/correlation tersedia |
| Bisa dites tanpa container? | Menentukan kualitas desain |

---

## 6. Skill 1 - Injection

Gunakan **constructor injection** untuk service utama.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ApprovalService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final AuditService auditService;

    @Inject
    public ApprovalService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            AuditService auditService
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.auditService = auditService;
    }
}
```

Kenapa constructor injection lebih aman:

| Alasan | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Dependency eksplisit | Mudah dibaca saat onboarding |
| Bisa dites tanpa CDI | Unit test lebih sederhana |
| Menghindari partially initialized object | Lebih aman |
| Cocok dengan `final` field | Object lebih immutable |

Field injection masih sering ditemukan:

```java
@Inject
private CaseRepository caseRepository;
```

Boleh, tapi untuk codebase baru biasanya constructor injection lebih baik.

---

## 7. Skill 2 - Scope

Scope menjawab: **instance ini hidup berapa lama?**

Weld menjelaskan bahwa scope mendefinisikan lifecycle dan visibility instance bean; CDI context model juga extensible dan menyediakan scope bawaan. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest/en-US/html_single/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Scope | Penggunaan Praktis |
|---|---|
| `@ApplicationScoped` | Service stateless, config provider, reusable client |
| `@RequestScoped` | User context, tenant context, correlation context |
| `@Dependent` | Object kecil yang ikut lifecycle injection target |
| Custom scope | Jarang; hanya jika benar-benar perlu runtime context khusus |

Contoh service stateless:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {
    // Jangan simpan state request di field mutable.
}
```

Contoh request context:

```java
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {

    private String correlationId;
    private String tenantId;
    private String userId;

    public String correlationId() {
        return correlationId;
    }

    public String tenantId() {
        return tenantId;
    }

    public String userId() {
        return userId;
    }

    public void initialize(String correlationId, String tenantId, String userId) {
        this.correlationId = correlationId;
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
        this.userId = userId;
    }
}
```

Rule penting:

```text
@ApplicationScoped  = boleh shared across request
@RequestScoped      = satu instance per request
@Dependent          = ikut lifecycle pemilik/injection target
```

Kesalahan umum:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private String currentTenantId; // SALAH

    public void setTenantId(String tenantId) {
        this.currentTenantId = tenantId;
    }
}
```

Kenapa salah?

Karena `@ApplicationScoped` dipakai bersama banyak request. Kalau menyimpan `currentTenantId` di field mutable, request A dan request B bisa saling bocor.

---

## 8. Skill 3 - Qualifier

Qualifier dipakai saat ada lebih dari satu implementation.

Contoh masalah:

```java
public interface PaymentClient {
    PaymentResult pay(PaymentRequest request);
}
```

Ada dua implementation:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MockPaymentClient implements PaymentClient {
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class RealPaymentClient implements PaymentClient {
}
```

Kalau inject begini:

```java
@Inject
PaymentClient paymentClient;
```

CDI bisa bingung karena ada lebih dari satu candidate.

Solusinya pakai qualifier:

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface RealPayment {
}
```

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface MockPayment {
}
```

```java
@RealPayment
@ApplicationScoped
public class RealPaymentClient implements PaymentClient {
}
```

```java
@MockPayment
@ApplicationScoped
public class MockPaymentClient implements PaymentClient {
}
```

Pemakaian:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentService {

    private final PaymentClient paymentClient;

    @Inject
    public PaymentService(@RealPayment PaymentClient paymentClient) {
        this.paymentClient = paymentClient;
    }
}
```

Practical use case qualifier:

| Use Case | Qualifier |
|---|---|
| Primary DB vs read replica | `@PrimaryDb`, `@ReadReplica` |
| Real client vs mock client | `@RealClient`, `@MockClient` |
| Internal vs external API client | `@InternalApi`, `@ExternalApi` |
| AWS vs Azure implementation | `@AwsProvider`, `@AzureProvider` |
| Sync vs async implementation | `@Sync`, `@Async` |

---

## 9. Skill 4 - Producer

Producer dipakai untuk object yang tidak bisa atau tidak seharusnya dibuat langsung oleh CDI.

Contoh:

- `DataSource`
- `SqlSessionFactory`
- `ObjectMapper`
- HTTP client
- gRPC channel
- Kafka producer
- Config object
- Clock
- Feature flag client

Contoh producer config:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AppConfigProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return AppConfig.load();
    }
}
```

Contoh producer `Clock` untuk testability:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class TimeProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public Clock clock() {
        return Clock.systemUTC();
    }
}
```

Service memakai `Clock`:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AuditService {

    private final Clock clock;

    @Inject
    public AuditService(Clock clock) {
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public AuditRecord createRecord(String action) {
        return new AuditRecord(action, Instant.now(clock));
    }
}
```

Keuntungan:

| Tanpa Producer | Dengan Producer |
|---|---|
| `Instant.now()` tersebar | Time source bisa diganti |
| Config parsing tersebar | Config centralized |
| Client dibuat manual | Client lifecycle controlled |
| Sulit testing | Mudah inject fake/mock |

---

## 10. Skill 5 - Interceptor / AOP

Interceptor dipakai untuk cross-cutting concern:

- audit
- metrics
- authorization
- transaction boundary
- retry
- idempotency
- logging
- feature guard

Jakarta Interceptors menyediakan model untuk menyisipkan behavior di sekitar method/lifecycle invocation; CDI Full juga mendukung integrasi interceptor binding. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.0/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh interceptor binding:

```java
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Audited {
}
```

Pemakaian:

```java
@Audited
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseApprovalService {

    public void approve(String caseId) {
        // business logic
    }
}
```

Interceptor:

```java
@Audited
@Interceptor
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION)
public class AuditInterceptor {

    @Inject
    CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        String userId = requestContext.userId();
        String method = ctx.getMethod().getName();

        try {
            Object result = ctx.proceed();
            // write success audit
            return result;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            // write failure audit
            throw ex;
        }
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Audited
    public void approve(String caseId) {
        validate(caseId);
        doApprove(caseId);
    }

    @Audited
    public void doApprove(String caseId) {
        // ...
    }
}
```

Kalau `approve()` memanggil `doApprove()` di class yang sama, pemanggilan internal seperti ini sering tidak melewati proxy/interceptor. Jadi jangan mengandalkan interceptor untuk self-invocation.

---

## 11. Skill 6 - Request Context

Dalam microservice, request biasanya membawa:

- correlation ID
- tenant ID
- user ID
- roles
- locale
- auth claims
- trace context

Pattern praktis:

```text
ContainerRequestFilter
   └── baca header/JWT
        └── validasi
             └── initialize CurrentRequestContext
                  └── service layer bisa inject context
```

Contoh filter:

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class RequestContextFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Inject
    CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String correlationId = getOrCreateCorrelationId(ctx);
        String tenantId = resolveTenant(ctx);
        String userId = resolveUser(ctx);

        requestContext.initialize(correlationId, tenantId, userId);
    }

    private String getOrCreateCorrelationId(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String value = ctx.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        return value != null && !value.isBlank()
                ? value
                : UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    }

    private String resolveTenant(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        return ctx.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");
    }

    private String resolveUser(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        return "resolved-user-id";
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

| Jangan | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Percaya langsung `X-Tenant-Id` dari public request | Bisa spoofing |
| Simpan tenant/user di singleton service | Bisa bocor antar request |
| Masukkan raw JWT ke semua downstream | Over-sharing credential |
| Lupa correlation ID | Observability rusak |
| Lupa clear ThreadLocal di async | Context leak |

---

## 12. Skill 7 - CDI untuk Config

Minimal mental model config:

```text
Config Value
│
├── default application.properties
├── profile-specific config
├── system properties
├── environment variables
├── mounted config file
├── Kubernetes ConfigMap/Secret
├── AWS/Azure/Vault external source
└── runtime override
```

CDI tidak otomatis menjadi config framework penuh. Biasanya kita membuat abstraction sendiri atau memakai MicroProfile Config sebagai model/implementasi. MicroProfile Config mendefinisikan konsep `ConfigSource` dan default source seperti system properties, environment variables, dan `META-INF/microprofile-config.properties`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh abstraction:

```java
public interface AppConfig {
    String profile();
    Duration requestTimeout();
    URI paymentBaseUrl();
    boolean featureEnabled(String key);
}
```

Producer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ConfigProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return new LayeredAppConfig(
                List.of(
                        new SystemPropertyConfigSource(),
                        new EnvironmentVariableConfigSource(),
                        new ClasspathConfigSource("application.properties")
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Service memakai config:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ExternalPaymentClient {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public ExternalPaymentClient(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    public PaymentResponse call(PaymentRequest request) {
        URI endpoint = config.paymentBaseUrl();
        Duration timeout = config.requestTimeout();

        // call external API
        return null;
    }
}
```

---

## 13. Practical Onboarding Checklist

Saat masuk ke codebase Jersey + CDI/Weld, cek ini dulu:

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Jersey major version | Pakai `javax.*` atau `jakarta.*`? |
| CDI/Weld version | Selaras dengan Jakarta/Jersey version? |
| Bean discovery | Ada `beans.xml`? discovery mode apa? |
| Resource lifecycle | JAX-RS resource dikelola Jersey saja atau CDI juga? |
| Injection style | Constructor, field, atau campuran? |
| Scope usage | Ada request state di singleton? |
| Producer | DataSource/client/config dibuat di mana? |
| Qualifier | Multiple implementation dibedakan dengan apa? |
| Interceptor | Audit/transaction/security tersembunyi di mana? |
| Context | User/tenant/correlation ID disimpan dan diteruskan bagaimana? |
| Config precedence | Env var mengalahkan properties atau sebaliknya? |
| Secret | Secret dari mana dan apakah pernah masuk log? |
| Testing | Ada test yang menjalankan CDI container? |
| Async | Context propagation aman atau ThreadLocal bocor? |

---

## 14. Anti-pattern yang Harus Cepat Dikenali

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Manual object creation | `new CaseService()` | Injection/interceptor/scope mati |
| Service singleton menyimpan request state | `private String tenantId;` | Cross-request leak |
| Semua class `@ApplicationScoped` | Context lifecycle salah | Race condition |
| Semua dependency field injection | Dependency tersembunyi | Sulit test/refactor |
| Producer melakukan kerja berat saat startup tanpa timeout | Remote config/client blocking | Startup lambat/gagal |
| Qualifier tidak dipakai padahal banyak implementation | Ambiguous dependency | Deployment gagal |
| Interceptor untuk logic bisnis utama | Behavior tersembunyi | Sulit debug |
| Raw header jadi tenant/user context | Security bypass | Tenant spoofing |
| Config default silent | App jalan dengan nilai salah | Production incident |
| Secret di classpath properties | Secret masuk JAR/image | Security incident |

---

## 15. Mini Implementation Target untuk Seri Ini

Setelah seri 1, target praktisnya adalah bisa membuat skeleton seperti ini:

```text
src/main/java
└── com.company.caseapp
    ├── api
    │   ├── CaseResource.java
    │   └── RequestContextFilter.java
    │
    ├── app
    │   ├── CaseService.java
    │   └── ApprovalService.java
    │
    ├── context
    │   ├── CurrentRequestContext.java
    │   └── TenantContext.java
    │
    ├── config
    │   ├── AppConfig.java
    │   └── ConfigProducer.java
    │
    ├── persistence
    │   ├── CaseRepository.java
    │   └── MyBatisProducer.java
    │
    ├── audit
    │   ├── Audited.java
    │   └── AuditInterceptor.java
    │
    └── external
        └── PaymentClient.java
```

Minimum dependency direction:

```text
api        → app
app        → persistence / external / audit abstraction / context
persistence → config
external   → config / context
context    → no heavy dependency
config     → no app dependency
```

Jangan sampai:

```text
persistence → api
config      → app
context     → persistence
```

Karena itu membuat dependency graph melingkar dan sulit dites.

---

# Ringkasan Seri 1

Yang perlu dibawa dari seri ini:

1. **CDI/Weld adalah runtime composition layer**, bukan hanya fitur `@Inject`.
2. **Jangan manual `new` object yang butuh injection, scope, config, atau interceptor.**
3. **Scope adalah keputusan arsitektur**, bukan sekadar annotation.
4. **`@ApplicationScoped` harus stateless atau immutable.**
5. **Request/user/tenant/correlation harus masuk ke request-scoped context.**
6. **Producer adalah tempat membuat object eksternal seperti config, client, mapper, dan factory.**
7. **Interceptor cocok untuk cross-cutting behavior, bukan business logic utama.**
8. **Jersey + CDI/Weld perlu boundary jelas karena Jersey punya lifecycle/DI sendiri.**
9. **Config/secret/feature flag sebaiknya masuk lewat abstraction dan producer.**
10. **Saat onboarding, baca aplikasi sebagai bean graph dan lifecycle graph.**

**Seri 1/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 2 - Bootstrapping Jersey + Weld: Resource, Service, Repository, Provider**, dengan fokus pada struktur Maven, dependency alignment `javax` vs `jakarta`, `beans.xml`, Weld bootstrap, Jersey `ResourceConfig`, dan contoh wiring end-to-end.

---

## Response:
7/15/2026, 4:00:24 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 2/14**. Di seri ini fokusnya bukan teori CDI, tetapi bagaimana membuat wiring end-to-end: Maven dependencies, `beans.xml`, Weld bootstrap, Jersey `ResourceConfig`, resource/filter/provider, service, repository, dan producer.

# Seri 2/14 - Bootstrapping Jersey + Weld: Resource, Service, Repository, Provider

Target seri ini: membuat **kerangka aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld** yang bisa dipakai sebagai baseline microservice.

Fokus praktisnya:

> Request masuk ke Jersey → resource/filter/provider aktif → dependency masuk dari CDI/Weld → service/repository/client/config bisa di-inject → request scope, interceptor, dan producer bisa bekerja.

Jersey mendukung penggunaan CDI bean sebagai root resource, provider, dan `Application` subclass jika CDI sudah diaktifkan; untuk container non-Jakarta EE seperti Tomcat/Grizzly, Jersey menyediakan modul integrasi CDI/Weld dan request-scope binding. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/user-guide.html))

---

## 1. Mental Model Bootstrapping

Ada dua runtime yang harus “nyala” dan sinkron:

```text
Runtime Boot
│
├── 1. CDI/Weld Container
│   ├── scan bean archive
│   ├── register beans
│   ├── activate scopes
│   ├── prepare interceptors
│   └── expose BeanManager
│
└── 2. Jersey Runtime
    ├── register ResourceConfig
    ├── scan/register @Path resources
    ├── register @Provider filters/mappers
    ├── map HTTP endpoint
    └── bind HTTP request lifecycle
```

Masalah utama bukan “cara menjalankan Jersey”, tapi **bagaimana Jersey request lifecycle tersambung ke CDI lifecycle**. Jersey docs menekankan bahwa integrasi CDI/Servlet bergantung pada CDI container yang sudah bootstrapped dan request scope binding yang benar; tanpa request scope binding, `@RequestScoped` bean tidak akan aman dipakai di resource/filter. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/cdi.support.html))

---

## 2. Pilihan Deployment Model

Untuk microservice Java modern, biasanya ada tiga model.

| Model | Cocok Untuk | Karakter | Catatan Praktis |
|---|---|---|---|
| **WAR di Servlet Container** | Tomcat, Jetty, app server internal | Container mengatur servlet lifecycle | Pastikan CDI/Weld servlet integration aktif. |
| **Embedded HTTP Server + Jersey + Weld SE** | Microservice standalone, Docker/Kubernetes | `main()` start Weld lalu start HTTP server | Cocok untuk service ringan tanpa full Jakarta EE server. |
| **Full Jakarta EE / Compatible Runtime** | GlassFish, Payara, WildFly, Open Liberty | JAX-RS + CDI biasanya sudah integrated | Lebih banyak fasilitas container, tapi runtime lebih besar. |

Untuk konteks onboarding ke **Java SE 17+, Docker, Kubernetes, on-prem/AWS/Azure**, model **embedded HTTP server + Jersey + Weld SE** sering paling mudah dipahami karena semua lifecycle terlihat eksplisit.

---

## 3. Namespace Compatibility: `javax.*` vs `jakarta.*`

Ini wajib dicek sebelum coding.

```text
Jersey 2.x  → umumnya javax.ws.rs.*
Jersey 3.x+ → jakarta.ws.rs.*
CDI 3.x/4.x → jakarta.enterprise.*
```

Rule praktis:

| Kondisi Codebase | Yang Harus Dilakukan |
|---|---|
| Banyak import `javax.ws.rs.*` | Kemungkinan Jersey 2.x / Java EE namespace |
| Banyak import `jakarta.ws.rs.*` | Kemungkinan Jersey 3.x+ / Jakarta namespace |
| Campuran `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` | Red flag besar |
| CDI package `jakarta.enterprise.*` tapi JAX-RS `javax.ws.rs.*` | Potensi dependency mismatch |
| Upgrade dari Jersey 2 ke 3 | Treat sebagai migration, bukan sekadar version bump |

Untuk aplikasi baru berbasis Java 17+, arah modern biasanya `jakarta.*`. Tapi untuk onboarding, **ikuti dulu namespace existing codebase**. Jangan campur.

---

## 4. Struktur Project Minimum

Baseline struktur yang saya rekomendasikan:

```text
case-service
├── pom.xml
├── src/main/java
│   └── com.company.caseapp
│       ├── Main.java
│       ├── api
│       │   ├── CaseApplication.java
│       │   ├── CaseResource.java
│       │   ├── RequestContextFilter.java
│       │   └── ApiExceptionMapper.java
│       ├── app
│       │   └── CaseService.java
│       ├── persistence
│       │   ├── CaseRepository.java
│       │   ├── MyBatisCaseRepository.java
│       │   └── MyBatisProducer.java
│       ├── config
│       │   ├── AppConfig.java
│       │   ├── AppConfigProducer.java
│       │   └── EnvironmentConfigSource.java
│       └── context
│           └── CurrentRequestContext.java
└── src/main/resources
    ├── META-INF
    │   └── beans.xml
    └── application.properties
```

Dependency direction:

```text
api          → app, context
app          → persistence, config, context
persistence  → config
config       → no app dependency
context      → no persistence dependency
```

Jangan sampai:

```text
persistence → api
config      → api
context     → app
```

Karena nanti lifecycle dan testing menjadi kusut.

## 5. Maven Baseline

Gunakan BOM untuk menjaga versi Jersey tetap selaras. Jangan mencampur manual version di setiap artifact kecuali ada alasan kuat. Jersey docs sendiri memperlihatkan modul server-side deployment seperti Grizzly dan Servlet container modules sebagai dependency yang harus disesuaikan dengan runtime deployment. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/user-guide.html))

Contoh `pom.xml` konseptual untuk **Jakarta namespace / Jersey 3.x style**:

```xml
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.company</groupId>
    <artifactId>case-service</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0</version>

    <properties>
        <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>

        <!-- Pin sesuai company baseline/security approval -->
        <jersey.version>3.1.11</jersey.version>
        <weld.version>5.1.7.Final</weld.version>
    </properties>

    <dependencyManagement>
        <dependencies>
            <dependency>
                <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey</groupId>
                <artifactId>jersey-bom</artifactId>
                <version>${jersey.version}</version>
                <type>pom</type>
                <scope>import</scope>
            </dependency>
        </dependencies>
    </dependencyManagement>

    <dependencies>
        <!-- Jersey server core -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.core</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-server</artifactId>
        </dependency>

        <!-- Embedded Grizzly runtime -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.containers</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-container-grizzly2-http</artifactId>
        </dependency>

        <!-- JSON support -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.media</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-media-json-jackson</artifactId>
        </dependency>

        <!-- Jersey CDI integration -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.ext.cdi</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-cdi1x</artifactId>
        </dependency>

        <!-- Jersey + Weld SE request-scope integration.
             Nama artifact historisnya tetap jersey-weld2-se,
             tetapi dokumentasi Jersey 3.1.x menyebut modul ini untuk Weld 4.x integration. -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.ext.cdi</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-weld2-se</artifactId>
        </dependency>

        <!-- Weld SE -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.jboss.weld.se</groupId>
            <artifactId>weld-se-core</artifactId>
            <version>${weld.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <!-- CDI API biasanya transitif, tapi explicit boleh untuk clarity -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>jakarta.enterprise</groupId>
            <artifactId>jakarta.enterprise.cdi-api</artifactId>
            <scope>provided</scope>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</project>
```

Catatan penting:

- Jangan copy version di atas secara membabi buta. Pakai versi yang sudah disetujui di perusahaan, lalu cek dependency tree.
- Untuk Jersey 3.1.x docs, modul integrasi CDI yang ditunjukkan adalah `org.glassfish.jersey.ext.cdi:jersey-cdi1x`, dan untuk Weld SE request-scope binding adalah `jersey-weld2-se`; dokumentasi Jersey 3.1.x menyebut modul ini dipakai untuk Weld 4.x implementation. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/cdi.support.html))
- Weld reference yang saya cek adalah Weld 5.1.7.Final; jadi kalau company memakai Weld 5/6/7, lakukan spike test karena kompatibilitas Jersey extension harus diverifikasi, bukan diasumsikan. Weld 5 docs menyatakan Weld SE menyediakan CDI bean manager di Java SE, managed beans, lifecycle callback, DI with qualifiers/alternatives, scopes, interceptors, decorators, events, dan extensions. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/5.1.7.Final/en-US/html/part5.html))

---

## 6. `beans.xml`

Minimal file:

```xml
<!-- src/main/resources/META-INF/beans.xml -->
<beans xmlns="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee"
       xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
       xsi:schemaLocation="
           https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee
           https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee/beans_4_0.xsd"
       bean-discovery-mode="annotated">
</beans>
```

Untuk Jakarta CDI 4.x, default bean discovery berubah menjadi `annotated`; artinya hanya class dengan bean-defining annotation seperti `@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, `@Dependent`, stereotype, atau scope lain yang dianggap bean. Kalau deployment bergantung pada discovery semua class, perlu `bean-discovery-mode="all"` atau tambahkan annotation eksplisit pada beans. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

Practical recommendation:

| Mode | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|
| `annotated` | Default sehat untuk microservice modern |
| `all` | Legacy migration, banyak class belum diberi bean-defining annotation |
| `none` | Jar yang tidak ingin menjadi bean archive |

Saya sarankan mulai dengan `annotated` agar bean graph eksplisit.

---

## 7. `ResourceConfig`: Jersey Application

Jersey `ResourceConfig` adalah implementasi Jersey dari JAX-RS `Application` yang memudahkan registration/scanning resource dan provider; docs menyebut `ResourceConfig` dapat di-extend dan dikonfigurasi di constructor, termasuk package scanning untuk root resource/provider. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/user-guide.html))

```java
package com.company.caseapp.api;

import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        packages(
                "com.company.caseapp.api",
                "com.company.caseapp.app",
                "com.company.caseapp.persistence",
                "com.company.caseapp.config",
                "com.company.caseapp.context"
        );

        register(RequestContextFilter.class);
        register(ApiExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

Practical note:

- `packages(...)` untuk JAX-RS discovery, bukan pengganti CDI bean discovery.
- CDI tetap bergantung pada bean archive, `beans.xml`, annotation, dan classpath/module visibility.
- Jangan register singleton instance manual kalau object tersebut butuh CDI proxy/interceptor/request context.

Contoh yang berisiko:

```java
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {
    public CaseApplication() {
        register(new CaseResource(new CaseService())); // SALAH
    }
}
```

Kenapa salah?

Karena object dibuat manual. CDI tidak mengelola dependency, scope, interceptor, producer, dan lifecycle-nya.

---

## 8. Main Bootstrap: Weld Dulu, Jersey Setelahnya

Untuk embedded mode, pattern-nya:

```text
main()
  ├── start Weld
  ├── create ResourceConfig
  ├── start Grizzly/Jersey HTTP server
  ├── wait/shutdown hook
  └── shutdown HTTP server + Weld
```

Jersey docs untuk Weld SE menunjukkan pola: `new Weld()`, `weld.initialize()`, lalu start Grizzly server, dan saat shutdown panggil server shutdown serta `weld.shutdown()`. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/cdi.support.html))

```java
package com.company.caseapp;

import com.company.caseapp.api.CaseApplication;
import org.glassfish.grizzly.http.server.HttpServer;
import org.glassfish.jersey.grizzly2.httpserver.GrizzlyHttpServerFactory;
import org.jboss.weld.environment.se.Weld;
import org.jboss.weld.environment.se.WeldContainer;

import java.net.URI;

public final class Main {

    private Main() {
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        URI baseUri = URI.create(
                System.getProperty("http.baseUri", "http://0.0.0.0:8080/")
        );

        Weld weld = new Weld();

        try (WeldContainer container = weld.initialize()) {
            CaseApplication application = new CaseApplication();

            HttpServer server = GrizzlyHttpServerFactory.createHttpServer(
                    baseUri,
                    application,
                    false
            );

            Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(() -> {
                server.shutdownNow();
                weld.shutdown();
            }));

            server.start();

            System.out.printf("Server started at %s%n", baseUri);

            Thread.currentThread().join();
        }
    }
}
```

Practical hardening:

| Concern | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Startup error | Fail fast, jangan fallback silent |
| Shutdown | Tutup server, DB pool, HTTP client, executor |
| Base URI | Ambil dari config/env/system property |
| Port binding | Di Docker/K8s biasanya `0.0.0.0`, bukan `localhost` |
| Readiness | Jangan mark ready sebelum DB/config/client minimum siap |
| Logging | Print active profile + sanitized config summary |

---

## 9. REST Resource

```java
package com.company.caseapp.api;

import com.company.caseapp.app.CaseService;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.PathParam;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/cases")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseService caseService;

    @Inject
    public CaseResource(CaseService caseService) {
        this.caseService = caseService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        CaseDto result = caseService.getCase(caseId);
        return Response.ok(result).build();
    }
}
```

CDI bean dapat digunakan sebagai Jersey root resource jika CDI sudah enabled; Jersey docs juga menyebut provider dan `Application` subclass dapat menjadi CDI beans, dengan catatan provider dan `Application` subclass harus singleton/application scoped. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/user-guide.html))

Untuk resource, saya biasanya tidak langsung memberi `@ApplicationScoped` kecuali memang resource stateless dan runtime integration sudah jelas. Banyak tim membiarkan resource lifecycle dikelola Jersey/CDI integration, sedangkan service layer diberi scope eksplisit.

---

## 10. Service Layer

```java
package com.company.caseapp.app;

import com.company.caseapp.context.CurrentRequestContext;
import com.company.caseapp.persistence.CaseRepository;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;

@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        String tenantId = requestContext.tenantId();
        return caseRepository.findByTenantAndCaseId(tenantId, caseId);
    }
}
```

Invariant:

```text
ApplicationScoped service boleh inject RequestScoped context,
selama context tersebut CDI normal-scoped proxy dan request context aktif.
```

Yang tidak boleh:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {
    private String currentTenantId; // jangan
}
```

---

## 11. Repository Interface + Implementation

```java
package com.company.caseapp.persistence;

import com.company.caseapp.app.CaseDto;

public interface CaseRepository {
    CaseDto findByTenantAndCaseId(String tenantId, String caseId);
}
```

```java
package com.company.caseapp.persistence;

import com.company.caseapp.app.CaseDto;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;

@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisCaseRepository(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    @Override
    public CaseDto findByTenantAndCaseId(String tenantId, String caseId) {
        try (var session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
            return mapper.findByTenantAndCaseId(tenantId, caseId);
        }
    }
}
```

Practical note untuk MyBatis:

- `SqlSessionFactory` biasanya application-scoped/thread-safe.
- `SqlSession` biasanya unit-of-work scoped, jangan disimpan sebagai singleton field.
- Seri transaksi MyBatis akan dibahas khusus di **Seri 8**.

---

## 12. Producer untuk MyBatis / External Object

Object eksternal seperti `SqlSessionFactory`, `DataSource`, HTTP client, Kafka producer, gRPC channel, `ObjectMapper`, dan config object sebaiknya dibuat lewat CDI producer.

```java
package com.company.caseapp.persistence;

import com.company.caseapp.config.AppConfig;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.enterprise.inject.Produces;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;

@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisProducer(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory() {
        // build MyBatis configuration from config
        // return new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(reader);
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Implement MyBatis factory creation");
    }
}
```

Pattern producer:

```text
Producer class
  ├── inject config
  ├── validate config
  ├── create external object
  ├── expose as CDI bean
  └── close/destroy on shutdown if needed
```

Kalau object perlu cleanup, gunakan disposer method:

```java
@Produces
@ApplicationScoped
public SomeClient someClient(AppConfig config) {
    return new SomeClient(config.endpoint());
}

public void closeSomeClient(@Disposes SomeClient client) {
    client.close();
}
```

---

## 13. Config Producer

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import java.net.URI;
import java.time.Duration;

public interface AppConfig {
    String profile();
    URI databaseUrl();
    Duration requestTimeout();
}
```

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.enterprise.inject.Produces;

@ApplicationScoped
public class AppConfigProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public AppConfig appConfig() {
        return new LayeredAppConfig();
    }
}
```

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import java.net.URI;
import java.time.Duration;

public class LayeredAppConfig implements AppConfig {

    @Override
    public String profile() {
        return get("APP_PROFILE", "local");
    }

    @Override
    public URI databaseUrl() {
        return URI.create(required("DATABASE_URL"));
    }

    @Override
    public Duration requestTimeout() {
        return Duration.ofMillis(
                Long.parseLong(get("REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS", "3000"))
        );
    }

    private String required(String key) {
        String value = System.getenv(key);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing required config: " + key);
        }
        return value;
    }

    private String get(String key, String fallback) {
        String value = System.getenv(key);
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? fallback : value;
    }
}
```

Practical invariant:

```text
Config harus fail fast untuk required value.
Config default hanya boleh untuk value yang aman.
Secret tidak boleh dicetak mentah di log.
```

---

## 14. Request Context

```java
package com.company.caseapp.context;

import jakarta.enterprise.context.RequestScoped;

@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {

    private String correlationId;
    private String tenantId;
    private String userId;

    public void initialize(String correlationId, String tenantId, String userId) {
        this.correlationId = correlationId;
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
        this.userId = userId;
    }

    public String correlationId() {
        return correlationId;
    }

    public String tenantId() {
        return tenantId;
    }

    public String userId() {
        return userId;
    }
}
```

Request context ini harus diisi di JAX-RS filter.

```java
package com.company.caseapp.api;

import com.company.caseapp.context.CurrentRequestContext;
import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.util.UUID;

@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class RequestContextFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public RequestContextFilter(CurrentRequestContext requestContext) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String correlationId = getOrCreateCorrelationId(ctx);
        String tenantId = resolveTenant(ctx);
        String userId = resolveUser(ctx);

        requestContext.initialize(correlationId, tenantId, userId);
    }

    private String getOrCreateCorrelationId(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String header = ctx.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        return header == null || header.isBlank()
                ? UUID.randomUUID().toString()
                : header;
    }

    private String resolveTenant(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String tenantId = ctx.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");

        if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()) {
            throw new UnauthorizedException("Missing tenant");
        }

        return tenantId;
    }

    private String resolveUser(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        // Production: resolve from validated JWT/session/internal auth context
        return "demo-user";
    }
}
```

Di production, jangan percaya `X-Tenant-Id` dari public client begitu saja. Untuk public-facing endpoint, tenant harus divalidasi dari JWT claim, mTLS identity, API key, atau trusted gateway.

---

## 15. Exception Mapper

```java
package com.company.caseapp.api;

import com.company.caseapp.context.CurrentRequestContext;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class ApiExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public ApiExceptionMapper(CurrentRequestContext requestContext) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "Unexpected error",
                requestContext.correlationId()
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .entity(body)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Provider seperti `ExceptionMapper` sering jadi tempat bug injection. Kalau `@Inject` tidak aktif di provider, biasanya masalahnya ada di CDI/Jersey integration, registration mode, atau provider dibuat sebagai singleton manual.

---

## 16. Startup Validation Bean

Tambahkan bean yang validasi wiring dan config saat startup.

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import jakarta.annotation.PostConstruct;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;

@ApplicationScoped
public class StartupValidator {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public StartupValidator(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @PostConstruct
    public void validate() {
        // Trigger required config validation
        config.profile();
        config.databaseUrl();
        config.requestTimeout();

        System.out.println("Startup validation completed. profile=" + config.profile());
    }
}
```

Di CDI, `@PostConstruct` lifecycle callback tersedia pada managed beans; Weld SE docs juga menyebut managed beans dengan `@PostConstruct` dan `@PreDestroy` sebagai bagian dari fitur Java SE environment. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/5.1.7.Final/en-US/html/part5.html))

---

## 17. Health Endpoint Minimum

```java
package com.company.caseapp.api;

import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/health")
public class HealthResource {

    @GET
    public Response health() {
        return Response.ok("{\"status\":\"UP\"}").build();
    }
}
```

Praktisnya pisahkan:

| Endpoint | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| `/health/live` | Process masih hidup |
| `/health/ready` | Siap menerima traffic |
| `/health/startup` | Startup dependency selesai |

Untuk Kubernetes, readiness tidak boleh `UP` kalau DB/config critical belum siap.

---

## 18. Boot Smoke Test

Setelah bootstrap, lakukan smoke test:

```bash
curl -i http://localhost:8080/health

curl -i \
  -H 'X-Correlation-Id: test-corr-001' \
  -H 'X-Tenant-Id: tenant-a' \
  http://localhost:8080/cases/CASE-001
```

Expected behavior:

```text
/health
  → 200 OK

/cases/{id}
  → resource created
  → CaseService injected
  → CaseRepository injected
  → CurrentRequestContext injected
  → correlation/tenant available
```

Kalau gagal, cek urutan ini:

| Symptom | Kemungkinan Penyebab |
|---|---|
| `UnsatisfiedResolutionException` | Bean belum annotated, package tidak ter-scan, `beans.xml` salah |
| `AmbiguousResolutionException` | Ada lebih dari satu implementation tanpa qualifier |
| `request context not active` | Request scope binding Jersey-Weld belum aktif |
| `NullPointerException` pada dependency | Object dibuat manual, bukan CDI-managed |
| Interceptor tidak jalan | Method tidak dipanggil via CDI proxy, binding belum aktif |
| Provider tidak inject | Provider tidak CDI-managed atau salah registration |
| ClassNotFound `javax`/`jakarta` | Namespace dependency campur |

---

## 19. Practical Checklist Bootstrapping

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Java | `maven.compiler.release=17` atau sesuai baseline |
| Namespace | Jangan campur `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` |
| Jersey | Pakai BOM, jangan version mismatch antar artifact |
| Weld | Pastikan versi compatible dengan Jersey CDI integration |
| CDI | Ada `META-INF/beans.xml` |
| Discovery | Pakai `bean-discovery-mode="annotated"` untuk eksplisit |
| ResourceConfig | Register package/resource/provider secara jelas |
| Main | Start Weld sebelum HTTP server |
| Shutdown | Shutdown HTTP server dan Weld |
| Resource | Jangan manual `new` service |
| Service | `@ApplicationScoped`, constructor injection |
| Context | `@RequestScoped`, diisi oleh filter |
| Repository | Interface + implementation, MyBatis session jangan singleton |
| Config | Producer + fail fast |
| Provider | Pastikan `ExceptionMapper`/filter bisa inject CDI bean |
| Test | Smoke test endpoint + injection + request context |

---

## 20. Baseline End-to-End Flow

```text
HTTP GET /cases/CASE-001
│
├── Grizzly/Jersey receives request
│
├── RequestContextFilter
│   ├── reads X-Correlation-Id
│   ├── resolves tenant
│   ├── resolves user
│   └── initializes CurrentRequestContext
│
├── CaseResource
│   └── injected CaseService
│
├── CaseService
│   ├── injected CaseRepository
│   └── injected CurrentRequestContext proxy
│
├── MyBatisCaseRepository
│   └── opens SqlSession from injected SqlSessionFactory
│
├── PostgreSQL query
│
└── Response returned with correlation-aware error handling/logging
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 2

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. Bootstrapping Jersey + Weld berarti **menyalakan dua lifecycle yang harus sinkron**.
2. Untuk embedded runtime, start **Weld dulu**, baru start **Jersey HTTP server**.
3. `ResourceConfig` mengatur JAX-RS resource/provider registration; CDI bean discovery tetap urusan CDI/Weld.
4. `beans.xml` dengan `bean-discovery-mode="annotated"` membuat bean graph lebih eksplisit.
5. Jangan mencampur `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*`.
6. Jangan register object CDI sebagai manual singleton instance di Jersey.
7. Resource, filter, dan mapper harus terbukti bisa menerima `@Inject`.
8. `@RequestScoped` context baru aman kalau request scope binding aktif.
9. Producer adalah tempat membuat config, client, factory, dan object eksternal.
10. Smoke test pertama harus membuktikan resource → service → repository → context injection berjalan.

**Seri 2/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 3 - Bean Discovery, Injection, Qualifier, Producer, Alternative**, dengan fokus pada cara memilih implementation, membuat factory object eksternal, menghindari ambiguous/unsatisfied dependency, dan membuat wiring yang testable.

---
