- [Jersey 2.47 User Guide](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/user-guide.html)
- [Weld Reference Guide](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/5.1.7.Final/en-US/html/part5.html)
- [Jakarta Contexts and Dependency Injection](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 4:02:09 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 3/14**. Fokusnya: bagaimana CDI/Weld menemukan bean, memilih dependency yang tepat, membuat object eksternal lewat producer, dan mengganti implementation tanpa membuat wiring rapuh.

# Seri 3/14 - Bean Discovery, Injection, Qualifier, Producer, Alternative

Seri ini fokus pada **wiring praktis CDI/Weld**:

> Bagaimana CDI/Weld menemukan bean, memilih implementation yang benar, membuat object eksternal, mengganti dependency per environment/test/profile, dan menghindari error seperti `UnsatisfiedResolutionException` atau `AmbiguousResolutionException`.

Dalam CDI modern, bean bukan sekadar class Java biasa. Bean punya **type**, **qualifier**, **scope**, **lifecycle**, dan bisa dibuat dari class langsung maupun dari producer method/field. CDI 4.1 adalah spesifikasi Jakarta CDI saat ini, dan Weld adalah implementasi CDI/reference implementation yang menyediakan perilaku seperti injection, qualifier, alternatives, producer, scopes, interceptors, decorators, events, dan extension. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 1. Mental Model Utama

Saat kamu menulis:

```java
@Inject
CaseRepository caseRepository;
```

CDI/Weld tidak sekadar mencari class bernama `CaseRepository`.

CDI melakukan resolution kira-kira seperti ini:

```text
Injection Point
│
├── Required Type
│   └── CaseRepository
│
├── Required Qualifier
│   └── @Default, kecuali ada qualifier eksplisit
│
├── Candidate Beans
│   ├── MyBatisCaseRepository
│   ├── InMemoryCaseRepository
│   └── ExternalCaseRepository
│
├── Resolution
│   ├── 0 candidate  → Unsatisfied dependency
│   ├── 1 candidate  → OK
│   └── >1 candidate → Ambiguous dependency
│
└── Inject contextual reference/proxy
```

Jadi dependency injection di CDI selalu melibatkan minimal:

| Komponen | Makna Praktis |
|---|---|
| **Bean type** | Interface/class yang diminta injection point |
| **Qualifier** | Penanda untuk memilih implementation |
| **Scope** | Lifecycle bean |
| **Producer** | Factory untuk object yang tidak dibuat langsung oleh CDI |
| **Alternative** | Implementation pengganti yang bisa diaktifkan |
| **Bean discovery** | Cara CDI menemukan class yang menjadi bean |

---

## 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
CDI/Weld Wiring Layer
│
├── 1. Bean Discovery
│   ├── beans.xml
│   ├── bean-discovery-mode
│   ├── bean-defining annotation
│   ├── package/classpath visibility
│   └── module/JAR boundaries
│
├── 2. Injection
│   ├── constructor injection
│   ├── field injection
│   ├── method injection
│   ├── optional/lazy injection
│   └── circular dependency detection
│
├── 3. Qualifier
│   ├── @Default
│   ├── @Any
│   ├── custom qualifier
│   ├── qualifier member
│   └── implementation selection
│
├── 4. Producer
│   ├── config producer
│   ├── DataSource producer
│   ├── SqlSessionFactory producer
│   ├── HTTP/gRPC/Kafka client producer
│   ├── ObjectMapper producer
│   └── disposer/cleanup
│
├── 5. Alternative
│   ├── test implementation
│   ├── profile-specific implementation
│   ├── on-prem/cloud implementation
│   ├── mock/stub/fake
│   └── selected alternative
│
├── 6. Dynamic Lookup
│   ├── Instance<T>
│   ├── programmatic selection
│   ├── optional dependency
│   └── plugin-like strategy selection
│
└── 7. Failure Modes
    ├── unsatisfied dependency
    ├── ambiguous dependency
    ├── unproxyable bean
    ├── wrong scope
    ├── manual object creation
    └── producer lifecycle leak
```

---

## 3. Bean Discovery

Bean discovery menjawab:

> Class mana yang dianggap CDI bean?

Di CDI 4.x, discovery yang sehat untuk microservice modern biasanya **annotated discovery**, artinya class perlu punya bean-defining annotation seperti scope annotation (`@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, `@Dependent`, dan sejenisnya) agar ditemukan sebagai bean. Spesifikasi CDI 4.1 mendefinisikan model bean archive dan discovery, sementara dokumentasi Weld menekankan bahwa producer, disposer, dan observer method juga bergantung pada class producer-nya ditemukan sebagai bean. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh `beans.xml`:

```xml
<!-- src/main/resources/META-INF/beans.xml -->
<beans xmlns="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee"
       xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
       xsi:schemaLocation="
           https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee
           https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee/beans_4_0.xsd"
       bean-discovery-mode="annotated">
</beans>
```

### Praktisnya

Class seperti ini akan ditemukan:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {
}
```

Class seperti ini kemungkinan tidak ditemukan pada mode `annotated`:

```java
public class CaseService {
}
```

Kecuali class itu diproduksi oleh producer atau discovery mode diubah.

### Bean Discovery Checklist

| Checklist | Kenapa Penting |
|---|---|
| Ada `META-INF/beans.xml` | Menandai bean archive secara eksplisit |
| Discovery mode jelas | Menghindari “kenapa bean tidak ditemukan?” |
| Class punya scope annotation | Membuat bean graph eksplisit |
| Producer class juga annotated | Producer method tidak berguna kalau class producer tidak ditemukan |
| Package ada di classpath runtime | Maven dependency belum tentu masuk runtime |
| Tidak campur `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` | Namespace mismatch bisa membuat annotation tidak dikenali |

---

## 4. Injection Style

Ada tiga gaya umum:

```text
Injection
├── Constructor injection
├── Field injection
└── Method injection
```

### 4.1 Constructor Injection - Rekomendasi Utama

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final AuditService auditService;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            AuditService auditService
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.auditService = auditService;
    }
}
```

Kelebihan praktis:

| Benefit | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Dependency eksplisit | Mudah dibaca saat onboarding |
| Bisa pakai `final` field | Object lebih aman |
| Bisa unit test tanpa CDI | Test lebih cepat |
| Constructor menjadi contract | Dependency tidak tersembunyi |
| Mudah refactor | IDE/static analysis lebih membantu |

### 4.2 Field Injection - Boleh, Tapi Hati-hati

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Inject
    CaseRepository caseRepository;
}
```

Masalah field injection:

| Masalah | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Dependency tersembunyi | Sulit lihat kebutuhan class |
| Sulit test manual | Harus reflection/setter/container |
| Object bisa terlihat valid padahal belum injected | Potensi NPE |
| Tidak cocok dengan immutable design | Field tidak `final` |

Field injection masih oke untuk:

- interceptor
- framework/provider class tertentu
- legacy code
- prototype cepat

Tapi untuk service/repository utama, prefer constructor injection.

### 4.3 Method Injection

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private CaseRepository caseRepository;

    @Inject
    void configure(CaseRepository caseRepository) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
    }
}
```

Jarang diperlukan. Biasanya hanya berguna untuk konfigurasi lanjutan atau integrasi framework tertentu.

---

## 5. Basic Wiring Pattern

Misalnya kita punya use case:

```text
CaseResource
  → CaseService
      → CaseRepository
          → SqlSessionFactory
```

Resource:

```java
@Path("/cases")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseService caseService;

    @Inject
    public CaseResource(CaseService caseService) {
        this.caseService = caseService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return Response.ok(caseService.getCase(caseId)).build();
    }
}
```

Service:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(CaseRepository caseRepository) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
    }

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        return caseRepository.findById(caseId);
    }
}
```

Repository interface:

```java
public interface CaseRepository {
    CaseDto findById(String caseId);
}
```

Implementation:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisCaseRepository(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    @Override
    public CaseDto findById(String caseId) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
            return mapper.findById(caseId);
        }
    }
}
```

Producer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisProducer(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory() {
        // Build from config
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Build SqlSessionFactory here");
    }
}
```

---

## 6. Qualifier

Qualifier menjawab:

> Dari beberapa implementation, mana yang harus dipakai?

Tanpa qualifier, CDI memakai `@Default`.

Contoh problem:

```java
public interface CaseRepository {
    CaseDto findById(String caseId);
}
```

Ada dua implementation:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class InMemoryCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

Injection ini menjadi ambiguous:

```java
@Inject
CaseRepository caseRepository;
```

Karena CDI melihat dua bean dengan type `CaseRepository`.

### 6.1 Custom Qualifier

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface PrimaryRepository {
}
```

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface TestRepository {
}
```

Implementation:

```java
@PrimaryRepository
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

```java
@TestRepository
@ApplicationScoped
public class InMemoryCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

Injection:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(@PrimaryRepository CaseRepository caseRepository) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
    }
}
```

### 6.2 Qualifier Naming Pattern

Gunakan qualifier untuk **semantic role**, bukan sekadar nama class.

| Kurang Bagus | Lebih Bagus |
|---|---|
| `@MyBatisImpl` | `@PrimaryRepository` |
| `@PostgresImpl` | `@WriteStore` |
| `@RedisImpl` | `@CacheStore` |
| `@AwsImpl` | `@CloudSecretStore` |
| `@MockImpl` | `@TestDouble` |

Kenapa?

Karena implementation bisa berubah, tetapi role biasanya lebih stabil.

---

## 7. Qualifier untuk Database dan External Client

### 7.1 Primary DB vs Read Replica

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface PrimaryDb {
}
```

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface ReadReplica {
}
```

Producer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class DataSourceProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public DataSourceProducer(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Produces
    @PrimaryDb
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource primaryDataSource() {
        return createDataSource(config.primaryDbUrl());
    }

    @Produces
    @ReadReplica
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource readReplicaDataSource() {
        return createDataSource(config.readReplicaDbUrl());
    }

    private DataSource createDataSource(String jdbcUrl) {
        // HikariCP or company-approved DataSource implementation
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Create DataSource");
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseReadRepository {

    private final DataSource readDataSource;

    @Inject
    public CaseReadRepository(@ReadReplica DataSource readDataSource) {
        this.readDataSource = readDataSource;
    }
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseWriteRepository {

    private final DataSource primaryDataSource;

    @Inject
    public CaseWriteRepository(@PrimaryDb DataSource primaryDataSource) {
        this.primaryDataSource = primaryDataSource;
    }
}
```

### 7.2 Internal vs External HTTP Client

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface InternalApiClient {
}
```

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface ExternalApiClient {
}
```

Producer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class HttpClientProducer {

    @Produces
    @InternalApiClient
    @ApplicationScoped
    public HttpClient internalHttpClient() {
        return HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
                .build();
    }

    @Produces
    @ExternalApiClient
    @ApplicationScoped
    public HttpClient externalHttpClient() {
        return HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                .build();
    }
}
```

---

## 8. Producer

Producer dipakai ketika object:

1. berasal dari library eksternal,
2. butuh factory/config kompleks,
3. perlu qualifier,
4. perlu lifecycle/cleanup,
5. tidak bisa diberi annotation CDI langsung,
6. perlu dibuat dari environment/profile/secret.

Contoh object yang cocok dibuat lewat producer:

| Object | Kenapa Producer |
|---|---|
| `DataSource` | Butuh JDBC URL, credential, pool config |
| `SqlSessionFactory` | Dibuat dari MyBatis config |
| `HttpClient` | Timeout, proxy, TLS, executor |
| gRPC `ManagedChannel` | Host, port, TLS, interceptors |
| Kafka producer/consumer | Config map, serializers, security |
| `ObjectMapper` | Module, naming strategy, date/time config |
| `Clock` | Testability |
| `AppConfig` | Layered config source |
| Feature flag client | SDK/config/secret/context |

CDI/Weld mendukung producer method dan producer field; Weld documentation juga menjelaskan bahwa qualifier bisa dipakai pada producer method maupun argumen producer/disposer/observer. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest/en-US/html_single/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 8.1 Producer untuk `ObjectMapper`

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class JsonProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public ObjectMapper objectMapper() {
        return JsonMapper.builder()
                .findAndAddModules()
                .disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AuditPayloadSerializer {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    @Inject
    public AuditPayloadSerializer(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }
}
```

### 8.2 Producer untuk `Clock`

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class TimeProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public Clock clock() {
        return Clock.systemUTC();
    }
}
```

Business service:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseTimelineService {

    private final Clock clock;

    @Inject
    public CaseTimelineService(Clock clock) {
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public Instant now() {
        return Instant.now(clock);
    }
}
```

Benefit: test bisa inject fixed clock.

---

## 9. Producer + Disposer

Kalau object perlu ditutup, tambahkan disposer.

Contoh:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ExternalClientProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public ExternalClient externalClient(AppConfig config) {
        return new ExternalClient(config.externalBaseUrl());
    }

    public void closeExternalClient(@Disposes ExternalClient client) {
        client.close();
    }
}
```

Pattern ini penting untuk:

| Resource | Perlu Cleanup? |
|---|---:|
| DB pool | Ya |
| HTTP client custom executor | Kadang |
| gRPC channel | Ya |
| Kafka producer | Ya |
| Kafka consumer | Ya |
| Scheduler/executor | Ya |
| File watcher/config watcher | Ya |

Failure mode yang sering terjadi:

```text
Producer membuat resource mahal,
tetapi tidak ada disposer.
Akibatnya graceful shutdown bocor:
DB connection pool, Kafka producer, gRPC channel, executor thread.
```

---

## 10. Alternative

Alternative menjawab:

> Bagaimana mengganti implementation tanpa mengubah semua injection point?

Use case:

| Use Case | Alternative |
|---|---|
| Unit/integration test | Fake repository |
| Local development | Stub external client |
| On-prem deployment | File-based secret provider |
| AWS deployment | AWS secret provider |
| Azure deployment | Azure secret provider |
| Migration | New implementation behind selected alternative |

Contoh:

```java
public interface SecretProvider {
    String getSecret(String key);
}
```

Default implementation:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class EnvSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    @Override
    public String getSecret(String key) {
        return System.getenv(key);
    }
}
```

Alternative implementation:

```java
@Alternative
@ApplicationScoped
public class FileSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    @Override
    public String getSecret(String key) {
        // Read from mounted file
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Read secret from file");
    }
}
```

Untuk mengaktifkan alternative, bisa lewat `beans.xml` atau mekanisme selected alternatives yang disediakan CDI/Weld. CDI mendefinisikan alternatives sebagai cara mengganti bean implementation, dan Weld menyediakan dukungan practical untuk mengaktifkan/menyeleksi alternatives dalam deployment. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh via `beans.xml`:

```xml
<beans xmlns="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee"
       bean-discovery-mode="annotated">
    <alternatives>
        <class>com.company.caseapp.secret.FileSecretProvider</class>
    </alternatives>
</beans>
```

### Alternative vs Qualifier

| Pilihan | Gunakan Saat |
|---|---|
| **Qualifier** | Beberapa implementation valid hidup bersamaan |
| **Alternative** | Satu implementation menggantikan implementation lain |
| **Producer** | Object dibuat dari factory/config/runtime |
| **Instance<T>** | Perlu programmatic/dynamic selection |

Contoh:

| Problem | Solusi |
|---|---|
| Primary DB dan read replica dipakai bersamaan | Qualifier |
| Local stub menggantikan real external client | Alternative |
| `DataSource` dibuat dari config | Producer |
| Pilih handler berdasarkan request type | `Instance<T>` atau registry pattern |

---

## 11. Profile-Specific Implementation

Misalnya deployment kamu bisa:

```text
local
dev
staging
prod
onprem
aws
azure
```

Jangan menyebar logic seperti ini di semua service:

```java
if (profile.equals("aws")) {
    ...
} else if (profile.equals("azure")) {
    ...
}
```

Lebih baik isolate di composition/config layer.

### Option A - Producer Memilih Implementation

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretProviderProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public SecretProviderProducer(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public SecretProvider secretProvider() {
        return switch (config.runtimePlatform()) {
            case "aws" -> new AwsSecretProvider(config);
            case "azure" -> new AzureSecretProvider(config);
            case "onprem" -> new FileSecretProvider(config);
            default -> new EnvironmentSecretProvider();
        };
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

- simple
- mudah dibaca
- cocok untuk sedikit variasi

Kekurangan:

- object dibuat manual, sehingga implementation yang dibuat `new` tidak akan otomatis mendapat CDI injection/interceptor/scope
- untuk implementation kompleks, lebih baik producer inject semua dependency eksplisit atau gunakan CDI-managed selection

### Option B - Qualifier + Alternative

```java
@Alternative
@ApplicationScoped
public class AwsSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {
}
```

```java
@Alternative
@ApplicationScoped
public class AzureSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {
}
```

Aktifkan satu alternative berdasarkan build/deployment profile.

Kelebihan:

- implementation tetap CDI-managed
- bisa punya dependency sendiri
- lebih aman untuk object kompleks

Kekurangan:

- activation harus rapi
- perlu governance config/deployment

---

## 12. Dynamic Lookup dengan `Instance<T>`

Kadang dependency tidak ingin dipilih statis saat startup, tetapi runtime.

Contoh: pilih handler berdasarkan channel.

```java
public interface NotificationHandler {
    String channel();
    void send(Notification notification);
}
```

Implementations:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class EmailNotificationHandler implements NotificationHandler {

    @Override
    public String channel() {
        return "email";
    }

    @Override
    public void send(Notification notification) {
        // send email
    }
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SmsNotificationHandler implements NotificationHandler {

    @Override
    public String channel() {
        return "sms";
    }

    @Override
    public void send(Notification notification) {
        // send sms
    }
}
```

Registry:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class NotificationHandlerRegistry {

    private final Map<String, NotificationHandler> handlers;

    @Inject
    public NotificationHandlerRegistry(Instance<NotificationHandler> handlerInstances) {
        Map<String, NotificationHandler> map = new HashMap<>();

        for (NotificationHandler handler : handlerInstances) {
            map.put(handler.channel(), handler);
        }

        this.handlers = Map.copyOf(map);
    }

    public NotificationHandler get(String channel) {
        NotificationHandler handler = handlers.get(channel);

        if (handler == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported channel: " + channel);
        }

        return handler;
    }
}
```

Gunakan `Instance<T>` untuk:

| Use Case | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Plugin-like handlers | Ya |
| Optional dependency | Ya |
| Semua dependency biasa | Tidak |
| Menghindari qualifier karena malas | Tidak |
| Runtime strategy registry | Ya |

---

## 13. Common CDI Resolution Errors

### 13.1 Unsatisfied Dependency

Contoh error konseptual:

```text
Unsatisfied dependency for type CaseRepository
```

Penyebab umum:

| Penyebab | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Implementation belum ada | Interface dibuat, class belum |
| Implementation tidak annotated | Tidak ada `@ApplicationScoped` |
| Bean discovery gagal | `beans.xml` salah/tidak ada |
| Package/JAR tidak masuk runtime | Dependency scope salah |
| Qualifier injection tidak cocok | Inject `@PrimaryDb`, producer tidak punya `@PrimaryDb` |
| Namespace mismatch | Pakai `javax.inject.Inject` vs stack `jakarta.*` secara campur |

Fix:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

Atau kalau pakai qualifier:

```java
@PrimaryRepository
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

Dan injection harus match:

```java
@Inject
public CaseService(@PrimaryRepository CaseRepository caseRepository) {
    this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
}
```

---

### 13.2 Ambiguous Dependency

Contoh:

```text
Ambiguous dependencies for type CaseRepository
```

Penyebab:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class InMemoryCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

Injection:

```java
@Inject
CaseRepository caseRepository;
```

Fix dengan qualifier:

```java
@Inject
public CaseService(@PrimaryRepository CaseRepository caseRepository) {
    this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
}
```

Atau jadikan salah satu alternative yang tidak aktif secara default.

---

### 13.3 Unproxyable Bean

CDI normal-scoped bean sering di-inject sebagai proxy. Kalau class tidak bisa diproxy, deployment bisa gagal.

Contoh berisiko:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public final class CaseService {
}
```

Atau:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private CaseService() {
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

| Hindari pada normal-scoped bean | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| `final class` | Sulit diproxy |
| `final method` untuk method business penting | Interceptor/proxy bisa bermasalah |
| private no-arg constructor saja | Container sulit membuat instance |
| primitive/final external resource tanpa producer | Lifecycle tidak jelas |

Untuk service CDI, biasanya aman:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Inject
    public CaseService(CaseRepository caseRepository) {
    }
}
```

---

## 14. Practical Pattern: Clean Dependency Graph

Target graph:

```text
CaseResource
  └── CaseService
      ├── CaseRepository
      │   └── SqlSessionFactory
      │       └── DataSource
      │           └── AppConfig / SecretProvider
      ├── FeatureFlagService
      │   └── FeatureFlagClient
      └── AuditService
          └── CurrentRequestContext
```

Code package:

```text
com.company.caseapp
├── api
│   └── resource/filter/mapper
├── app
│   └── service/use case
├── domain
│   └── model/policy/value object
├── persistence
│   └── repository/mybatis/mapper
├── external
│   └── clients
├── config
│   └── config source/producer
├── secret
│   └── secret provider
├── context
│   └── request/user/tenant/correlation context
└── wiring
    └── producers/qualifiers/alternatives
```

Dependency direction:

```text
api        → app
app        → domain, persistence abstraction, external abstraction, context
persistence → config, secret
external   → config, secret, context
config     → no app dependency
context    → no persistence dependency
domain     → no CDI dependency if possible
```

Practical recommendation:

- Domain model/value object sebaiknya tidak bergantung pada CDI.
- Service/application layer boleh CDI-managed.
- Infrastructure layer memakai producer/qualifier.
- Context layer harus kecil dan tidak menarik dependency berat.
- Config/secret tidak boleh tergantung API/resource.

---

## 15. Practical Example: External Client with Qualifier + Producer

Problem:

- Ada internal API client dan external API client.
- Timeout berbeda.
- Header propagation berbeda.
- Keduanya memakai Java `HttpClient`.

Qualifier:

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface InternalClient {
}
```

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface ExternalClient {
}
```

Producer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class HttpClientProducer {

    @Produces
    @InternalClient
    @ApplicationScoped
    public HttpClient internalClient() {
        return HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(1))
                .build();
    }

    @Produces
    @ExternalClient
    @ApplicationScoped
    public HttpClient externalClient() {
        return HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                .build();
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class DecisionEngineClient {

    private final HttpClient httpClient;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public DecisionEngineClient(
            @InternalClient HttpClient httpClient,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    public DecisionResponse evaluate(DecisionRequest request) {
        String correlationId = requestContext.correlationId();

        // Build HTTP request with correlation header
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Call decision engine");
    }
}
```

---

## 16. Practical Example: MyBatis Mapper Producer Pattern

Ada dua pendekatan.

### Approach A - Repository membuka session sendiri

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisCaseRepository(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    @Override
    public CaseDto findById(String caseId) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
            return session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class).findById(caseId);
        }
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

- simple
- lifecycle jelas
- cocok untuk query sederhana

Kekurangan:

- transaction boundary lintas repository lebih sulit
- session lifecycle tersebar

### Approach B - Request/transaction-bound session provider

```java
@RequestScoped
public class SqlSessionProvider {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;
    private SqlSession session;

    @Inject
    public SqlSessionProvider(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    public SqlSession currentSession() {
        if (session == null) {
            session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);
        }

        return session;
    }

    @PreDestroy
    public void close() {
        if (session != null) {
            session.close();
        }
    }
}
```

Repository:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final SqlSessionProvider sessionProvider;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisCaseRepository(SqlSessionProvider sessionProvider) {
        this.sessionProvider = sessionProvider;
    }

    @Override
    public CaseDto findById(String caseId) {
        return sessionProvider.currentSession()
                .getMapper(CaseMapper.class)
                .findById(caseId);
    }
}
```

Catatan: transaksi MyBatis/PostgreSQL akan dibahas lebih dalam di **Seri 8**.

---

## 17. Alternative untuk Test

Misalnya production pakai MyBatis:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {
}
```

Test pakai fake:

```java
@Alternative
@ApplicationScoped
public class FakeCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final Map<String, CaseDto> cases = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @Override
    public CaseDto findById(String caseId) {
        return cases.get(caseId);
    }

    public void put(CaseDto dto) {
        cases.put(dto.id(), dto);
    }
}
```

Aktifkan di test `beans.xml`:

```xml
<beans xmlns="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee"
       bean-discovery-mode="annotated">
    <alternatives>
        <class>com.company.caseapp.persistence.FakeCaseRepository</class>
    </alternatives>
</beans>
```

Kapan bagus?

| Test Type | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Unit test pure Java | Biasanya tidak perlu CDI |
| CDI integration test | Ya |
| Repository integration test | Lebih baik pakai Testcontainers/PostgreSQL |
| External client test | Fake/stub alternative cocok |
| End-to-end test | Gunakan real wiring sebanyak mungkin |

---

## 18. Anti-pattern yang Harus Dikenali

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Manual `new` untuk dependency CDI | `new MyBatisCaseRepository()` | Injection/interceptor/scope mati |
| Semua implementation `@ApplicationScoped` tanpa qualifier | 3 bean implement interface sama | Ambiguous dependency |
| Producer class tidak annotated | Producer tidak ditemukan | Unsatisfied dependency |
| Business logic di producer | Producer terlalu pintar | Sulit test/debug |
| Profile logic tersebar | `if (profile)` di banyak service | Coupling tinggi |
| Alternative untuk runtime choice per request | Salah konsep | Gunakan strategy/registry |
| Qualifier terlalu teknis | `@MyBatisImpl` | Migration sulit |
| `Instance<T>` dipakai di mana-mana | Service locator disguised as DI | Dependency tidak eksplisit |
| Config/secret dibaca langsung di service | `System.getenv()` tersebar | Sulit test/audit |
| Singleton mutable | `@ApplicationScoped` dengan request state | Race/leak |

---

## 19. Debugging Flow

Saat deployment gagal karena CDI, ikuti urutan ini.

```text
CDI failure?
│
├── 1. Baca error type
│   ├── Unsatisfied?
│   ├── Ambiguous?
│   ├── Unproxyable?
│   └── Scope/context not active?
│
├── 2. Cek injection point
│   ├── required type
│   ├── qualifier
│   └── package/import namespace
│
├── 3. Cek candidate bean
│   ├── class exists?
│   ├── annotated?
│   ├── correct qualifier?
│   ├── in runtime classpath?
│   └── not excluded?
│
├── 4. Cek producer
│   ├── producer class discovered?
│   ├── producer return type match?
│   ├── qualifier match?
│   └── scope sane?
│
└── 5. Cek lifecycle
    ├── object manually created?
    ├── request context active?
    ├── proxyable?
    └── disposer/cleanup needed?
```

---

## 20. Practical Checklist untuk Code Review

Gunakan checklist ini saat review PR.

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Bean discovery | Apakah class punya bean-defining annotation? |
| Injection | Apakah constructor injection dipakai untuk service utama? |
| Interface | Apakah dependency memakai abstraction bila perlu? |
| Qualifier | Apakah multiple implementation dibedakan jelas? |
| Producer | Apakah object eksternal dibuat terpusat? |
| Disposer | Apakah resource yang perlu close sudah punya cleanup? |
| Scope | Apakah scope sesuai lifecycle? |
| Config | Apakah config dibaca via abstraction, bukan tersebar? |
| Secret | Apakah secret tidak hardcoded/tidak dilog? |
| Testability | Apakah dependency bisa diganti di test? |
| Alternative | Apakah alternative dipakai untuk replacement, bukan runtime strategy? |
| Dynamic lookup | Apakah `Instance<T>` tidak disalahgunakan sebagai service locator? |

---

# Ringkasan Seri 3

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. CDI memilih dependency berdasarkan **type + qualifier**, bukan nama variable.
2. `@Default` berlaku kalau tidak ada qualifier eksplisit.
3. Kalau tidak ada candidate → **unsatisfied dependency**.
4. Kalau lebih dari satu candidate → **ambiguous dependency**.
5. Gunakan **constructor injection** untuk service utama.
6. Gunakan **qualifier** saat beberapa implementation hidup bersamaan.
7. Gunakan **producer** untuk object eksternal seperti `DataSource`, `SqlSessionFactory`, HTTP client, Kafka producer, gRPC channel, `ObjectMapper`, `Clock`, dan config.
8. Gunakan **disposer** untuk object yang harus ditutup saat shutdown.
9. Gunakan **alternative** untuk mengganti implementation per deployment/test.
10. Gunakan `Instance<T>` hanya untuk dynamic/plugin-like selection, bukan sebagai pengganti desain dependency yang jelas.
11. Producer class harus ikut ditemukan CDI; kalau tidak, producer method tidak berguna.
12. Hindari profile/config logic tersebar di business service.

**Seri 3/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 4 - Scope & Lifecycle: `@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, `@Dependent`, Proxy**, dengan fokus pada lifecycle bean, client proxy, request state, thread-safety, dan failure mode seperti context leak atau unproxyable bean.

---

**Sources:**

- [Jakarta Contexts and Dependency Injection](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Weld 7.0.0.Beta1 - CDI Reference Implementation](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest/en-US/html_single/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 4:03:45 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 4/14**. Fokusnya: scope, lifecycle, proxy, request state, thread-safety, dan failure mode yang biasanya paling sering menyebabkan bug halus di production.

# Seri 4/14 - Scope & Lifecycle: `@ApplicationScoped`, `@RequestScoped`, `@Dependent`, Proxy

Target seri ini: kamu bisa membaca dan mendesain lifecycle object di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld** tanpa membuat bug halus seperti **request state bocor antar user**, **context tidak aktif**, **unproxyable bean**, atau **resource tidak pernah ditutup**.

Intinya:

> Scope bukan sekadar annotation. Scope adalah keputusan lifecycle, thread-safety, memory ownership, dan visibility state.

CDI mendefinisikan context/scope sebagai mekanisme untuk menghubungkan client dengan instance bean yang benar sesuai lifecycle aktif. Weld sebagai CDI reference implementation menjelaskan konsep scopes, contexts, normal scopes, dependent pseudo-scope, dan client proxy sebagai bagian inti dari runtime CDI. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 1. Mental Model

Dalam CDI, object tidak hanya “dibuat lalu dipakai”. Object punya lifecycle.

```text
CDI Bean
│
├── Type
│   └── interface/class yang bisa di-inject
│
├── Qualifier
│   └── penanda dependency mana yang dipilih
│
├── Scope
│   └── berapa lama instance hidup
│
├── Context
│   └── tempat instance scope itu disimpan/di-resolve
│
├── Proxy
│   └── object perantara yang memilih instance aktual
│
└── Lifecycle callback
    ├── @PostConstruct
    └── @PreDestroy
```

Perbedaan penting:

```text
Scope  = aturan lifecycle
Context = runtime storage/resolution untuk scope tersebut
Proxy  = object perantara yang membuat injection tetap aman lintas scope
```

Contoh praktis:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {
}
```

Artinya:

```text
CaseService
  → hidup selama aplikasi berjalan
  → shared antar request
  → harus stateless atau thread-safe
```

Contoh lain:

```java
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {
}
```

Artinya:

```text
CurrentRequestContext
  → hidup selama satu HTTP request
  → berbeda untuk request A dan request B
  → cocok untuk user, tenant, correlation ID
```

---

## 2. Diagram Scope dalam Microservice

```text
Application Runtime
│
├── @ApplicationScoped
│   ├── CaseService
│   ├── AuditService
│   ├── FeatureFlagService
│   ├── AppConfig
│   ├── SqlSessionFactory
│   ├── HTTP Client
│   └── Kafka/gRPC Client
│
├── @RequestScoped
│   ├── CurrentRequestContext
│   ├── CurrentUser
│   ├── TenantContext
│   ├── CorrelationContext
│   └── RequestValidationState
│
├── @Dependent
│   ├── Small helper
│   ├── DTO mapper helper
│   ├── Producer-created transient object
│   └── Object owned by injection target
│
├── Transaction / Unit-of-Work Bound
│   ├── SqlSession
│   ├── DB transaction state
│   └── rollback marker
│
└── External Lifecycle
    ├── ExecutorService
    ├── DB pool
    ├── gRPC channel
    ├── Kafka producer
    └── Config/secret watcher
```

---

## 3. Scope Table

| Scope | Lifecycle | Cocok Untuk | Jangan Dipakai Untuk | Risiko Utama |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `@ApplicationScoped` | Selama aplikasi hidup | Stateless service, config, shared client, factory, cache thread-safe | User/tenant/request state | Race condition dan data leak |
| `@RequestScoped` | Satu HTTP request | Current user, tenant, correlation ID, locale, request metadata | Heavy resource, long-running async state | Context tidak aktif di thread lain |
| `@Dependent` | Ikut lifecycle injection target | Helper kecil, object transient, producer default kecil | Resource mahal, object yang perlu sharing | Multiple instance tidak terkontrol |
| Custom scope | Ditentukan sendiri | Workflow context, job context, test scope | Kalau hanya ingin “beda lifecycle sedikit” | Kompleks dan sulit debug |
| Manual lifecycle | Diatur sendiri | External runtime resource tertentu | Business service CDI | Injection/interceptor/scope mati |

---

# 4. `@ApplicationScoped`

`@ApplicationScoped` adalah scope untuk object yang hidup sepanjang aplikasi. Di CDI, ini termasuk normal scope, sehingga injection biasanya melalui **client proxy**, bukan instance langsung. Konsep normal-scoped bean dan proxy penting karena CDI harus bisa resolve instance sesuai context aktif. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Cocok untuk

```text
@ApplicationScoped
├── Stateless service
├── Config provider
├── Feature flag service
├── Audit service
├── Repository implementation
├── DataSource / pool wrapper
├── SqlSessionFactory
├── HTTP client
├── gRPC channel factory
├── Kafka producer wrapper
└── Immutable cache/config snapshot
```

Contoh benar:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        String tenantId = requestContext.tenantId();
        return caseRepository.findByTenantAndCaseId(tenantId, caseId);
    }
}
```

Ini aman karena `CaseService` stateless. `CurrentRequestContext` memang `@RequestScoped`, tetapi yang di-inject ke `CaseService` adalah contextual reference/proxy yang akan resolve instance sesuai request aktif.

## Contoh salah

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private String currentTenantId;
    private String currentUserId;

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId, String tenantId, String userId) {
        this.currentTenantId = tenantId;
        this.currentUserId = userId;

        return doGet(caseId);
    }

    private CaseDto doGet(String caseId) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

Ini salah karena `CaseService` dipakai bersama oleh banyak request.

Kemungkinan bug:

```text
Request A tenant = tenant-a
Request B tenant = tenant-b

Karena currentTenantId disimpan di field singleton-like service,
request A bisa membaca tenant-b.
```

## Rule praktis

```text
@ApplicationScoped bean:
  boleh punya dependency
  boleh punya immutable config
  boleh punya thread-safe cache
  boleh punya shared client
  tidak boleh punya mutable request/user/tenant state
```

---

# 5. `@RequestScoped`

`@RequestScoped` cocok untuk data yang hanya valid selama satu HTTP request. Dalam aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS, request context harus benar-benar aktif saat resource/filter/service mengakses bean request-scoped; kalau integrasi Jersey-CDI/Weld tidak benar, error seperti “request context not active” bisa muncul.

## Cocok untuk

```text
@RequestScoped
├── CurrentRequestContext
├── CurrentUser
├── TenantContext
├── CorrelationContext
├── LocaleContext
├── RequestAuthorizationContext
└── Per-request validation state
```

Contoh:

```java
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {

    private String correlationId;
    private String tenantId;
    private String userId;

    public void initialize(String correlationId, String tenantId, String userId) {
        this.correlationId = correlationId;
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
        this.userId = userId;
    }

    public String correlationId() {
        return requireInitialized(correlationId, "correlationId");
    }

    public String tenantId() {
        return requireInitialized(tenantId, "tenantId");
    }

    public String userId() {
        return requireInitialized(userId, "userId");
    }

    private String requireInitialized(String value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Request context not initialized: " + name);
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

Filter pengisi context:

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class RequestContextFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public RequestContextFilter(CurrentRequestContext requestContext) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String correlationId = getOrCreateCorrelationId(ctx);
        String tenantId = resolveTenant(ctx);
        String userId = resolveUser(ctx);

        requestContext.initialize(correlationId, tenantId, userId);
    }

    private String getOrCreateCorrelationId(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String value = ctx.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        return value == null || value.isBlank()
                ? UUID.randomUUID().toString()
                : value;
    }

    private String resolveTenant(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String tenantId = ctx.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");
        if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()) {
            throw new NotAuthorizedException("Missing tenant");
        }
        return tenantId;
    }

    private String resolveUser(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        return "resolved-user";
    }
}
```

## Praktis: satu request, satu context

```text
HTTP Request A
  └── CurrentRequestContext instance A
      ├── tenant-a
      ├── user-1
      └── correlation-aaa

HTTP Request B
  └── CurrentRequestContext instance B
      ├── tenant-b
      ├── user-2
      └── correlation-bbb
```

## Jangan pakai `@RequestScoped` untuk

| Jangan | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| DB pool | Terlalu mahal dibuat per request |
| `SqlSessionFactory` | Harus reusable |
| HTTP client shared | Client biasanya thread-safe dan mahal dibuat |
| Kafka producer | Mahal dan perlu lifecycle jelas |
| Config provider | Biasanya application-level |
| Long-running async job state | Request context selesai saat HTTP request selesai |

---

# 6. `@Dependent`

`@Dependent` adalah pseudo-scope. Instance dependent biasanya mengikuti lifecycle bean yang meng-inject-nya. Weld documentation menjelaskan dependent pseudo-scope sebagai scope default untuk banyak object ketika tidak ada scope eksplisit, dengan lifecycle yang berbeda dari normal scopes. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest-6.0/en-US/html_single/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Cocok untuk

```text
@Dependent
├── Helper kecil
├── Stateless mapper helper
├── Lightweight formatter
├── Object yang dibuat producer dan dimiliki injection target
└── Class yang memang tidak perlu shared context
```

Contoh:

```java
@Dependent
public class CaseNumberFormatter {

    public String format(String rawCaseNumber) {
        return rawCaseNumber.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }
}
```

Dipakai oleh service:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseNumberFormatter formatter;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(CaseNumberFormatter formatter) {
        this.formatter = formatter;
    }
}
```

Dalam contoh ini, `CaseNumberFormatter` ikut lifecycle `CaseService`.

## Hati-hati dengan `@Dependent`

Contoh berbahaya:

```java
@Dependent
public class ExpensiveClient {

    public ExpensiveClient() {
        // buka koneksi, start thread, load config besar
    }
}
```

Masalah:

```text
Setiap injection target bisa punya instance sendiri.
Kalau object mahal, jumlah instance sulit dikontrol.
Kalau perlu close, cleanup bisa terlewat.
```

Rule praktis:

```text
@Dependent:
  bagus untuk helper kecil
  buruk untuk resource mahal
  buruk untuk state yang perlu lifecycle eksplisit
```

---

# 7. Client Proxy

Ini bagian yang sangat penting.

Saat kamu inject normal-scoped bean seperti `@ApplicationScoped` atau `@RequestScoped`, yang sering diterima bukan instance asli, tetapi **client proxy**. Proxy ini akan meneruskan pemanggilan method ke contextual instance yang benar. CDI specification mendefinisikan proxy untuk normal scopes, sedangkan Weld documentation menjelaskan bahwa normal-scoped bean memakai client proxy agar context dapat memilih instance aktual. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Contoh

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(CurrentRequestContext requestContext) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    public String currentTenant() {
        return requestContext.tenantId();
    }
}
```

Padahal `CaseService` hidup sepanjang aplikasi, sedangkan `CurrentRequestContext` hidup per request.

Ini bisa bekerja karena:

```text
CaseService
  └── injected CurrentRequestContext proxy
         ├── Request A → resolve instance A
         ├── Request B → resolve instance B
         └── no request → context not active error
```

## Practical implication

Jangan lakukan ini:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;
    private String cachedTenant;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(CurrentRequestContext requestContext) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
        this.cachedTenant = requestContext.tenantId(); // SALAH
    }
}
```

Kenapa salah?

Pada constructor aplikasi, belum tentu request context aktif. Selain itu, tenant tidak boleh di-cache di application-scoped object.

Yang benar:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(CurrentRequestContext requestContext) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    public void execute(String caseId) {
        String tenantId = requestContext.tenantId(); // resolve saat method dipanggil dalam request
    }
}
```

---

# 8. Proxyability Rules

Normal-scoped bean harus bisa diproxy. CDI specification mendefinisikan batasan untuk unproxyable bean types, dan Weld akan gagal deployment jika bean normal-scoped tidak bisa diproxy. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Hindari ini pada normal-scoped bean

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public final class CaseService {
}
```

Masalah: `final class` sulit/tidak bisa diproxy.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private CaseService() {
    }
}
```

Masalah: constructor tidak bisa dipakai container/proxy dengan benar.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    public final void approve(String caseId) {
    }
}
```

Masalah: method final bisa bermasalah untuk proxy/interceptor.

## Pattern aman

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository repository;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(CaseRepository repository) {
        this.repository = repository;
    }

    public ApprovalResult approve(String caseId) {
        return repository.approve(caseId);
    }
}
```

Kalau menemukan error seperti:

```text
UnproxyableResolutionException
```

Cek:

| Penyebab | Fix |
|---|---|
| Class `final` | Hapus `final` dari bean normal-scoped |
| Method intercepted `final` | Jangan final untuk method yang perlu interceptor |
| Constructor tidak sesuai | Sediakan constructor yang bisa dipakai CDI |
| Class library eksternal tidak proxyable | Produksi sebagai interface atau pakai producer dengan scope sesuai |
| Primitive/array/final type sebagai normal-scoped producer | Ubah scope atau expose via wrapper/interface |

---

# 9. Lifecycle Callback: `@PostConstruct` dan `@PreDestroy`

Lifecycle callback berguna untuk validasi startup dan cleanup. Weld SE documentation menyebut managed beans dengan lifecycle callback seperti `@PostConstruct` dan `@PreDestroy` sebagai fitur yang tersedia di environment Java SE. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest-6.0/en-US/html_single/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## `@PostConstruct`

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class StartupValidator {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public StartupValidator(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @PostConstruct
    void validate() {
        config.requireValid();
        System.out.println("Startup validation completed");
    }
}
```

Gunakan untuk:

| Use Case | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Validasi config wajib | Ya |
| Membuat koneksi remote blocking lama | Hati-hati |
| Warmup cache kecil | Ya |
| Migrasi database besar | Tidak |
| Start background thread tanpa shutdown | Tidak |

## `@PreDestroy`

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ManagedExecutorHolder {

    private ExecutorService executor;

    @PostConstruct
    void start() {
        executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(8);
    }

    public ExecutorService executor() {
        return executor;
    }

    @PreDestroy
    void stop() {
        executor.shutdown();
    }
}
```

Gunakan untuk:

| Resource | Perlu `@PreDestroy` / disposer? |
|---|---:|
| ExecutorService | Ya |
| gRPC channel | Ya |
| Kafka producer | Ya |
| DB pool | Ya |
| Config watcher | Ya |
| Plain stateless service | Tidak perlu |

---

# 10. Scope Decision Matrix

Gunakan matrix ini saat menentukan scope.

| Object | Recommended Scope | Alasan |
|---|---|---|
| `CaseService` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Stateless business service |
| `ApprovalService` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Stateless business service |
| `AuditService` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Shared service, context via request proxy |
| `CaseRepository` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Stateless repository |
| `SqlSessionFactory` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Factory shared |
| `SqlSession` | Request/transaction-bound, bukan application singleton | Unit-of-work |
| `DataSource` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Pool shared |
| `ObjectMapper` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Reusable setelah configured |
| `HttpClient` | `@ApplicationScoped` | Shared client |
| `CurrentRequestContext` | `@RequestScoped` | Per HTTP request |
| `TenantContext` | `@RequestScoped` | Per HTTP request |
| `CurrentUser` | `@RequestScoped` | Per HTTP request |
| DTO | Plain object | Jangan CDI kalau hanya data |
| Entity/model | Plain object | Jangan campur persistence/domain dengan CDI |
| Mapper helper kecil | `@Dependent` atau static/plain | Tergantung kebutuhan injection |
| Config provider | `@ApplicationScoped` | Shared + immutable/cache-aware |
| Secret provider | `@ApplicationScoped` | Shared abstraction, jangan expose raw secret sembarang |
| Feature flag service | `@ApplicationScoped` | Shared evaluator |
| Feature flag evaluation context | `@RequestScoped` | User/tenant/request-aware |
| Camunda delegate | Tergantung integration | Hati-hati dengan process/job lifecycle |

---

# 11. Jersey/JAX-RS Resource Scope

JAX-RS resource lifecycle bisa berbeda tergantung runtime/integrasi. Dalam aplikasi Jersey + CDI/Weld, yang penting adalah jangan mengasumsikan resource lifecycle sama dengan service lifecycle.

Pattern aman:

```java
@Path("/cases")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseService caseService;

    @Inject
    public CaseResource(CaseService caseService) {
        this.caseService = caseService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    public Response get(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return Response.ok(caseService.getCase(caseId)).build();
    }
}
```

Business state tetap di service/context, bukan resource.

Jangan:

```java
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    private String currentTenantId; // jangan

    @GET
    public Response get(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String tenantId) {
        this.currentTenantId = tenantId;
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

Karena lifecycle resource bisa tidak sesuai asumsi, dan menyimpan request state di field rawan bocor.

Rule:

```text
Resource:
  boleh menerima request
  boleh memanggil service
  boleh mapping request/response
  jangan menyimpan mutable request state di field
```

---

# 12. Scope + MyBatis

Untuk MyBatis, bedakan object yang reusable dan object yang unit-of-work.

```text
@ApplicationScoped
  └── SqlSessionFactory

Request/Transaction-bound
  └── SqlSession

@ApplicationScoped
  └── Repository implementation
       └── mengambil SqlSession dari provider/unit-of-work
```

## Pattern sederhana

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisCaseRepository(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    public CaseDto findById(String caseId) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
            return session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class).findById(caseId);
        }
    }
}
```

Cocok untuk read/query sederhana.

## Pattern request-bound session provider

```java
@RequestScoped
public class SqlSessionProvider {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;
    private SqlSession sqlSession;

    @Inject
    public SqlSessionProvider(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    public SqlSession currentSession() {
        if (sqlSession == null) {
            sqlSession = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);
        }
        return sqlSession;
    }

    public void commit() {
        if (sqlSession != null) {
            sqlSession.commit();
        }
    }

    public void rollback() {
        if (sqlSession != null) {
            sqlSession.rollback();
        }
    }

    @PreDestroy
    public void close() {
        if (sqlSession != null) {
            sqlSession.close();
        }
    }
}
```

Cocok untuk transaction boundary lintas repository.

Tapi hati-hati:

```text
@RequestScoped SqlSessionProvider
  hanya aman selama request context aktif.
  tidak otomatis aman untuk async thread, scheduled job, atau Camunda job executor.
```

Untuk async/Camunda, biasanya perlu unit-of-work context sendiri, bukan HTTP request scope.

---

# 13. Scope + Async / Multithread

Ini sumber bug besar.

`@RequestScoped` context biasanya aktif di thread request. Saat kamu pindah ke `ExecutorService`, thread baru tidak otomatis punya request context.

Contoh berbahaya:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncApprovalService {

    private final ExecutorService executor;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public AsyncApprovalService(
            ExecutorService executor,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        this.executor = executor;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    public void approveAsync(String caseId) {
        executor.submit(() -> {
            String tenantId = requestContext.tenantId(); // bisa gagal
            // process
        });
    }
}
```

Masalah:

```text
Thread worker tidak otomatis punya active request context.
Kalau request selesai, request-scoped data juga selesai.
```

Pattern lebih aman:

```java
public record RequestContextSnapshot(
        String correlationId,
        String tenantId,
        String userId
) {
}
```

```java
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {

    private String correlationId;
    private String tenantId;
    private String userId;

    public RequestContextSnapshot snapshot() {
        return new RequestContextSnapshot(correlationId, tenantId, userId);
    }
}
```

Async service:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncApprovalService {

    private final ExecutorService executor;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;
    private final ApprovalWorker worker;

    @Inject
    public AsyncApprovalService(
            ExecutorService executor,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext,
            ApprovalWorker worker
    ) {
        this.executor = executor;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
        this.worker = worker;
    }

    public void approveAsync(String caseId) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = requestContext.snapshot();

        executor.submit(() -> {
            worker.process(caseId, snapshot);
        });
    }
}
```

Worker menerima explicit snapshot:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ApprovalWorker {

    public void process(String caseId, RequestContextSnapshot snapshot) {
        String tenantId = snapshot.tenantId();
        String correlationId = snapshot.correlationId();

        // process async job with explicit context
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Jangan membawa CDI request-scoped bean ke thread lain.
Bawa immutable context snapshot.
```

---

# 14. Scope + ThreadLocal

Kadang tim memakai `ThreadLocal` untuk context. Ini bisa valid, tapi harus disiplin.

Contoh wrapper:

```java
public final class RequestContextHolder {

    private static final ThreadLocal<RequestContextSnapshot> CURRENT = new ThreadLocal<>();

    private RequestContextHolder() {
    }

    public static void set(RequestContextSnapshot context) {
        CURRENT.set(context);
    }

    public static RequestContextSnapshot get() {
        RequestContextSnapshot context = CURRENT.get();
        if (context == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("No request context bound to current thread");
        }
        return context;
    }

    public static void clear() {
        CURRENT.remove();
    }
}
```

Filter:

```java
@Provider
public class ThreadLocalContextFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        RequestContextHolder.set(resolveSnapshot(requestContext));
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext, ContainerResponseContext responseContext) {
        RequestContextHolder.clear();
    }

    private RequestContextSnapshot resolveSnapshot(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        return new RequestContextSnapshot(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                requestContext.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id"),
                "resolved-user"
        );
    }
}
```

Masalah terbesar `ThreadLocal`:

```text
Kalau tidak clear:
  request berikutnya di thread yang sama bisa melihat context lama.
```

Di server thread pool, ini bisa fatal.

Rule:

| Rule | Wajib? |
|---|---:|
| Set di awal request | Ya |
| Clear di finally/response filter | Ya |
| Snapshot untuk async | Ya |
| Jangan simpan secret/raw token | Ya |
| Jangan pakai untuk mengganti dependency injection | Ya |

---

# 15. Scope + Feature Flag

Feature flag service biasanya application-scoped.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureFlagService {

    private final FeatureFlagClient client;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public FeatureFlagService(
            FeatureFlagClient client,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        this.client = client;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    public boolean isEnabled(String flagKey) {
        return client.evaluate(
                flagKey,
                Map.of(
                        "tenantId", requestContext.tenantId(),
                        "userId", requestContext.userId()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Flag client:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureFlagClient {
}
```

Evaluation context:

```java
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
FeatureFlagService = @ApplicationScoped
Flag evaluation context = @RequestScoped / explicit snapshot
```

Jangan:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureFlagService {

    private String currentTenant; // salah
}
```

---

# 16. Scope + Config

Config biasanya `@ApplicationScoped`, tetapi harus diputuskan apakah immutable atau reloadable.

## Immutable config

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AppConfig {

    private final String profile;
    private final Duration requestTimeout;

    @Inject
    public AppConfig(ConfigSource source) {
        this.profile = source.required("APP_PROFILE");
        this.requestTimeout = Duration.ofMillis(
                Long.parseLong(source.get("REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS", "3000"))
        );
    }

    public String profile() {
        return profile;
    }

    public Duration requestTimeout() {
        return requestTimeout;
    }
}
```

Cocok untuk:

| Config | Immutable Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| DB URL | Ya |
| HTTP port | Ya |
| Active profile | Ya |
| Required secret location | Ya |
| Static timeout | Ya |

## Reloadable config

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ReloadableConfig {

    private final AtomicReference<ConfigSnapshot> current =
            new AtomicReference<>();

    @PostConstruct
    void load() {
        current.set(loadSnapshot());
    }

    public ConfigSnapshot current() {
        return current.get();
    }

    public void reload() {
        current.set(loadSnapshot());
    }

    private ConfigSnapshot loadSnapshot() {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Load config snapshot");
    }
}
```

Cocok untuk:

| Config | Reloadable Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Feature flag fallback | Ya |
| Kill switch | Ya |
| Dynamic threshold | Ya |
| DB URL | Biasanya tidak |
| Secret rotation | Bisa, tapi hati-hati |
| Thread pool size | Sulit, perlu lifecycle khusus |

---

# 17. Scope + Secret

Secret provider biasanya application-scoped.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretProvider {

    public String getSecret(String key) {
        // load from env/file/vault/cloud
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException();
    }
}
```

Jangan simpan secret di request context kecuali benar-benar perlu.

Jangan log secret:

```java
System.out.println("db.password=" + password); // salah
```

Boleh log sanitized summary:

```java
System.out.println("db.password=<redacted>");
```

Rule:

```text
Secret provider = shared service
Secret value = short-lived where possible
Secret logging = never raw
Secret propagation = minimal
```

---

# 18. Scope + Camunda Workflow

Camunda process/job lifecycle tidak sama dengan HTTP request lifecycle.

Jangan membuat Camunda delegate bergantung pada HTTP request context kecuali delegate memang dipanggil dalam request yang sama. Untuk async job executor, request context biasanya tidak aktif.

Contoh berbahaya:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ApprovalDelegate {

    @Inject
    CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    public void execute() {
        String userId = requestContext.userId(); // bisa gagal di job executor
    }
}
```

Pattern lebih aman:

```text
HTTP Request
  ├── resolve user/tenant/correlation
  ├── start Camunda process
  └── store stable values as process variables
       ├── initiatorUserId
       ├── tenantId
       ├── correlationId
       └── businessKey
```

Delegate:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ApprovalDelegate {

    public void execute(DelegateExecution execution) {
        String tenantId = (String) execution.getVariable("tenantId");
        String initiatorUserId = (String) execution.getVariable("initiatorUserId");
        String correlationId = (String) execution.getVariable("correlationId");

        // process workflow step
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
HTTP request context tidak sama dengan workflow/process context.
Untuk workflow, simpan stable context sebagai process variable.
Jangan simpan raw token/secret sebagai process variable.
```

---

# 19. Practical Failure Modes

## 19.1 Request state bocor antar user

Penyebab:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class UserAwareService {
    private String userId;
}
```

Fix:

```java
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentUser {
    private String userId;
}
```

Lalu inject `CurrentUser` ke service.

---

## 19.2 Context not active

Penyebab:

```text
@RequestScoped bean diakses:
  - saat startup
  - di background thread
  - di scheduler
  - di Camunda async job
  - setelah request selesai
```

Fix:

```text
Gunakan explicit context snapshot,
atau buat scope/context khusus untuk job,
atau jangan bergantung pada HTTP request-scoped bean.
```

---

## 19.3 Unproxyable bean

Penyebab:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public final class PaymentService {
}
```

Fix:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentService {
}
```

Atau expose interface.

---

## 19.4 Resource leak

Penyebab:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class KafkaProducerProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public KafkaProducer<String, String> producer() {
        return new KafkaProducer<>(props());
    }
}
```

Tidak ada disposer.

Fix:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class KafkaProducerProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public KafkaProducer<String, String> producer() {
        return new KafkaProducer<>(props());
    }

    public void close(@Disposes KafkaProducer<String, String> producer) {
        producer.close();
    }
}
```

---

## 19.5 Wrong scope for repository/session

Salah:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class BadRepository {

    private final SqlSession session;

    @Inject
    public BadRepository(SqlSession session) {
        this.session = session;
    }
}
```

Fix:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class GoodRepository {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @Inject
    public GoodRepository(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    public CaseDto find(String id) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
            return session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class).find(id);
        }
    }
}
```

Atau gunakan request/transaction-bound `SqlSessionProvider`.

---

# 20. Practical Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| `@ApplicationScoped` | Apakah class benar-benar stateless/thread-safe? |
| Request state | Apakah user/tenant/correlation disimpan di `@RequestScoped`, bukan singleton? |
| Mutable field | Apakah ada field mutable di service singleton? |
| Proxyability | Apakah bean normal-scoped tidak `final` dan bisa diproxy? |
| Constructor | Apakah injection jelas dan tidak memanggil request context di constructor? |
| `@Dependent` | Apakah object dependent ringan dan tidak resource-heavy? |
| Resource cleanup | Apakah client/pool/executor punya disposer/`@PreDestroy`? |
| Async | Apakah request context tidak dipakai langsung di worker thread? |
| ThreadLocal | Apakah selalu di-clear? |
| MyBatis | Apakah `SqlSession` tidak disimpan sebagai singleton? |
| Config | Apakah config immutable/reloadable diputuskan jelas? |
| Camunda | Apakah workflow tidak bergantung pada HTTP request context? |

---

# 21. Roadmap Praktis Mini untuk Menguasai Scope

Urutan latihan yang paling efektif:

```text
1. Buat @ApplicationScoped service stateless
2. Buat @RequestScoped CurrentRequestContext
3. Isi context dari ContainerRequestFilter
4. Inject request context ke application service
5. Buktikan dua request berbeda tidak saling bocor
6. Tambahkan @PostConstruct startup validator
7. Tambahkan @PreDestroy/disposer untuk resource
8. Buat contoh async dengan context snapshot
9. Buat test untuk unproxyable bean/failure mode
10. Refactor mutable singleton menjadi request-scoped context
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 4

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. **Scope adalah keputusan lifecycle dan thread-safety.**
2. `@ApplicationScoped` cocok untuk service stateless, config, shared client, factory, dan repository stateless.
3. `@RequestScoped` cocok untuk user, tenant, correlation ID, locale, dan request metadata.
4. `@Dependent` cocok untuk helper kecil, bukan resource mahal.
5. Normal-scoped bean biasanya di-inject lewat **client proxy**.
6. Application-scoped service boleh inject request-scoped context karena yang masuk adalah proxy, tetapi hanya aman saat request context aktif.
7. Jangan akses request-scoped bean di constructor startup, background thread, scheduled job, atau Camunda async job tanpa explicit context strategy.
8. Jangan simpan mutable request state di `@ApplicationScoped`.
9. `SqlSessionFactory` bisa shared; `SqlSession` harus unit-of-work/request/transaction-bound.
10. Resource seperti DB pool, executor, Kafka producer, dan gRPC channel butuh cleanup.
11. Untuk async, bawa **immutable context snapshot**, bukan CDI request-scoped bean.
12. Untuk workflow, simpan stable context sebagai process variables, bukan mengandalkan HTTP request context.

**Seri 4/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 5 - Jersey Request Pipeline + CDI Context: Filter, ExceptionMapper, SecurityContext**, dengan fokus pada lifecycle request JAX-RS, initialization context, auth/tenant/correlation handling, response/error mapping, dan integrasi aman dengan CDI request context.

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 5/14**. Fokusnya: request pipeline Jersey/JAX-RS, tempat terbaik menginisialisasi context CDI, cara menangani auth/tenant/correlation, dan error response yang konsisten.

# Seri 5/14 - Jersey Request Pipeline + CDI Context: Filter, ExceptionMapper, SecurityContext

Target seri ini: memahami **alur request Jersey/JAX-RS** dan tahu di mana meletakkan logic seperti:

- correlation ID
- tenant resolution
- authentication/authz preparation
- request context initialization
- response headers
- error mapping
- audit metadata
- security context bridging ke CDI

Intinya:

> JAX-RS resource sebaiknya tidak menjadi tempat semua concern request-level.  
> Gunakan filter, context bean, exception mapper, dan service layer secara terpisah supaya request lifecycle jelas dan testable.

Jakarta RESTful Web Services/JAX-RS menyediakan resource, provider, filter, interceptor, dan exception mapping untuk membangun REST API berbasis HTTP; spesifikasi Jakarta REST 4.0 juga menekankan arah integrasi yang lebih dekat dengan Jakarta CDI. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html))

---

# 1. Mental Model Request Pipeline

Untuk endpoint sederhana:

```http
GET /cases/CASE-001
X-Correlation-Id: corr-001
X-Tenant-Id: tenant-a
Authorization: Bearer <token>
```

pipeline idealnya seperti ini:

```text
HTTP Request
│
├── 1. Server receives request
│
├── 2. Pre-matching request filter
│   ├── raw request normalization
│   ├── method/path/header rewrite if needed
│   └── very early rejection
│
├── 3. Resource matching
│   └── Jersey chooses resource method
│
├── 4. Post-matching request filters
│   ├── correlation ID
│   ├── tenant resolution
│   ├── auth token validation
│   ├── user/security context
│   ├── CDI request context initialization
│   └── authorization guard
│
├── 5. ReaderInterceptor / MessageBodyReader
│   └── request body deserialization
│
├── 6. Resource method
│   └── CaseResource.getCase()
│
├── 7. Service layer
│   ├── CaseService
│   ├── Repository
│   ├── External client
│   └── Feature flag / audit / transaction
│
├── 8. ExceptionMapper if exception thrown
│   └── map exception → HTTP response
│
├── 9. Response filter
│   ├── correlation header
│   ├── security headers
│   ├── cache headers
│   └── response audit metadata
│
└── 10. WriterInterceptor / MessageBodyWriter
    └── response serialization
```

Jersey membedakan **filters** dan **interceptors**: filter cocok untuk memodifikasi request/response metadata seperti headers, URI, method, dan response parameters, sedangkan interceptor terutama untuk entity input/output stream. Jersey juga mendokumentasikan urutan umum: pre-matching request filters → resource matching → post-matching request filters → reader/writer interceptors → response filters. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/filters-and-interceptors.html))

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Jersey Request Pipeline + CDI Context
│
├── Request Entry
│   ├── HTTP method
│   ├── URI/path/query
│   ├── headers
│   ├── cookies
│   └── body
│
├── Request Filters
│   ├── CorrelationIdFilter
│   ├── TenantResolutionFilter
│   ├── AuthenticationFilter
│   ├── AuthorizationFilter
│   ├── RequestContextInitializationFilter
│   └── IdempotencyKeyFilter
│
├── CDI Context Beans
│   ├── CurrentRequestContext
│   ├── CurrentUser
│   ├── TenantContext
│   ├── CorrelationContext
│   ├── RequestSecurityContext
│   └── RequestAuditContext
│
├── Resource Layer
│   ├── @Path resource
│   ├── request DTO mapping
│   ├── response DTO mapping
│   └── service invocation
│
├── Service Layer
│   ├── business use case
│   ├── permission check
│   ├── transaction boundary
│   ├── repository call
│   └── downstream propagation
│
├── Exception Mapping
│   ├── validation errors
│   ├── auth errors
│   ├── not found
│   ├── conflict
│   ├── domain errors
│   └── unexpected errors
│
└── Response Filters
    ├── correlation header
    ├── audit result
    ├── security headers
    ├── cache control
    └── response time metric
```
