# 3. Practical Table

| Area | Komponen | Fungsi Praktis | Jangan Dipakai Untuk | Failure Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Request normalization | `@PreMatching ContainerRequestFilter` | Normalize path/header/method sebelum resource matching | Business auth kompleks | Mengubah request terlalu agresif sehingga routing sulit debug |
| Correlation | `ContainerRequestFilter` | Ambil/buat correlation ID | Generate ulang di setiap layer | Trace antar service putus |
| Tenant resolution | `ContainerRequestFilter` + validator | Tentukan tenant dari JWT/header/gateway context | Percaya header publik mentah | Tenant spoofing |
| Authentication | `ContainerRequestFilter` | Validate token/session/API key | Business authorization detail | Token valid tapi akses resource tetap salah |
| Authorization | Filter/interceptor/service policy | Guard role/permission/scope | Semua rule ditaruh di resource | Authorization tersebar |
| CDI request context | `@RequestScoped` beans | Menyimpan user/tenant/correlation per request | Menyimpan DB connection global | Context leak atau context not active |
| Resource | `@Path` class | HTTP mapping, DTO, status code | Business process utama | Resource menjadi god class |
| Exception mapping | `ExceptionMapper<T>` | Map exception ke response konsisten | Swallow semua error tanpa log | 500 tidak terlacak/correlation hilang |
| Response filter | `ContainerResponseFilter` | Tambahkan response headers, timing, correlation ID | Mengubah business result | Header tidak konsisten |
| SecurityContext bridge | `SecurityContext`/custom context | Expose principal/roles ke resource/service | Sumber authorization tunggal tanpa validasi | Principal/role tidak sinkron |

---

# 4. Filter Types: Pre-Matching vs Post-Matching

## 4.1 Pre-Matching Filter

Pre-matching filter berjalan **sebelum** resource method dipilih.

Cocok untuk:

- normalize request path
- override method dari header internal, jika memang ada legacy requirement
- very early rejection
- request ID/correlation ID awal
- log raw inbound metadata

Contoh:

```java
@Provider
@PreMatching
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public class CorrelationPreMatchingFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String correlationId = request.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
            request.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Correlation-Id", correlationId);
        }

        request.setProperty("correlationId", correlationId);
    }
}
```

Jangan taruh tenant/security final decision di sini kecuali memang tidak butuh matched resource metadata. Authorization sering butuh tahu resource/method/annotation yang akan dipanggil.

---

## 4.2 Post-Matching Filter

Post-matching filter berjalan setelah resource method cocok.

Cocok untuk:

- authentication
- authorization berbasis annotation/resource
- tenant validation
- request context initialization
- idempotency key validation
- feature guard berbasis endpoint

Contoh:

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final TokenVerifier tokenVerifier;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Inject
    public AuthenticationFilter(
            TokenVerifier tokenVerifier,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext
    ) {
        this.tokenVerifier = tokenVerifier;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String authorization = request.getHeaderString("Authorization");

        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal = tokenVerifier.verify(authorization);

        String correlationId = getCorrelationId(request);
        String tenantId = resolveTenant(request, principal);

        requestContext.initialize(
                correlationId,
                tenantId,
                principal.userId(),
                principal.roles()
        );
    }

    private String getCorrelationId(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        Object value = request.getProperty("correlationId");

        if (value instanceof String correlationId && !correlationId.isBlank()) {
            return correlationId;
        }

        String header = request.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        return header == null || header.isBlank()
                ? UUID.randomUUID().toString()
                : header;
    }

    private String resolveTenant(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal
    ) {
        String tenantFromHeader = request.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");

        if (tenantFromHeader == null || tenantFromHeader.isBlank()) {
            return principal.defaultTenantId();
        }

        if (!principal.allowedTenantIds().contains(tenantFromHeader)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Tenant is not allowed");
        }

        return tenantFromHeader;
    }
}
```

---

# 5. Filter Ordering dengan `@Priority`

Filter ordering penting. Jangan mengandalkan urutan registration yang tidak eksplisit.

```text
Priorities.AUTHENTICATION  = auth/token validation
Priorities.AUTHORIZATION   = permission/role checks
Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR = header decoration
```

Jersey mendokumentasikan bahwa provider untuk request processing seperti `ContainerRequestFilter` dan entity interceptor diurutkan berdasarkan `@Priority` secara ascending. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/filters-and-interceptors.html))

Practical ordering:

```text
Request Inbound
│
├── 1. Correlation filter
├── 2. Authentication filter
├── 3. Tenant validation filter
├── 4. Authorization filter
├── 5. Idempotency filter
└── 6. Resource method
```

Contoh:

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION - 100)
public class CorrelationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {
}
```

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {
}
```

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public class AuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {
}
```

---

# 6. CDI Request Context Bean

Context bean adalah tempat menyimpan metadata request yang sudah divalidasi.

```java
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {

    private String correlationId;
    private String tenantId;
    private String userId;
    private Set<String> roles = Set.of();

    public void initialize(
            String correlationId,
            String tenantId,
            String userId,
            Set<String> roles
    ) {
        this.correlationId = require(correlationId, "correlationId");
        this.tenantId = require(tenantId, "tenantId");
        this.userId = require(userId, "userId");
        this.roles = Set.copyOf(roles);
    }

    public String correlationId() {
        return require(correlationId, "correlationId");
    }

    public String tenantId() {
        return require(tenantId, "tenantId");
    }

    public String userId() {
        return require(userId, "userId");
    }

    public boolean hasRole(String role) {
        return roles.contains(role);
    }

    public Set<String> roles() {
        return roles;
    }

    public RequestContextSnapshot snapshot() {
        return new RequestContextSnapshot(
                correlationId(),
                tenantId(),
                userId(),
                roles()
        );
    }

    private static String require(String value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Request context missing: " + name);
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

Snapshot untuk async/service-to-service:

```java
public record RequestContextSnapshot(
        String correlationId,
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles
) {
    public RequestContextSnapshot {
        roles = Set.copyOf(roles);
    }
}
```

Invariant:

```text
CurrentRequestContext hanya berisi data yang sudah divalidasi.
Jangan menyimpan raw Authorization header, raw JWT, password, secret, atau object HTTP berat.
```

---

# 7. Correlation ID Pattern

Correlation ID wajib untuk production observability.

## Request Filter

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION - 100)
public class CorrelationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";
    public static final String PROPERTY = "correlationId";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String correlationId = request.getHeaderString(HEADER);

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        correlationId = sanitize(correlationId);

        request.setProperty(PROPERTY, correlationId);
        request.getHeaders().putSingle(HEADER, correlationId);
    }

    private String sanitize(String value) {
        if (value.length() > 128) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Correlation ID is too long");
        }

        if (!value.matches("[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+")) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Invalid correlation ID");
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

## Response Filter

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public class CorrelationResponseFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            ContainerResponseContext response
    ) {
        Object correlationId = request.getProperty(CorrelationFilter.PROPERTY);

        if (correlationId instanceof String value && !value.isBlank()) {
            response.getHeaders().putSingle(CorrelationFilter.HEADER, value);
        }
    }
}
```

Kenapa correlation ID perlu disanitasi?

Karena header masuk bisa dipakai untuk log injection atau header abuse kalau diterima mentah-mentah.

---

# 8. Tenant Resolution Pattern

Tenant resolution adalah area high-risk di sistem regulatory/case management karena menyangkut data isolation.

```text
Tenant Source Priority
│
├── 1. Verified JWT claim / gateway identity
├── 2. mTLS/client certificate mapping
├── 3. trusted internal header from gateway
├── 4. route/subdomain mapping
└── 5. raw public header
    └── only allowed after validation against authenticated principal
```

Contoh resolver:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class TenantResolver {

    public String resolve(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal
    ) {
        String requestedTenant = request.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");

        if (requestedTenant == null || requestedTenant.isBlank()) {
            return principal.defaultTenantId();
        }

        if (!principal.allowedTenantIds().contains(requestedTenant)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("User is not allowed for tenant");
        }

        return requestedTenant;
    }
}
```

Filter:

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION - 100)
public class TenantFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final TenantResolver tenantResolver;
    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;
    private final RequestPrincipalHolder principalHolder;

    @Inject
    public TenantFilter(
            TenantResolver tenantResolver,
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext,
            RequestPrincipalHolder principalHolder
    ) {
        this.tenantResolver = tenantResolver;
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
        this.principalHolder = principalHolder;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal = principalHolder.current();

        String tenantId = tenantResolver.resolve(request, principal);

        requestContext.initialize(
                currentCorrelationId(request),
                tenantId,
                principal.userId(),
                principal.roles()
        );
    }

    private String currentCorrelationId(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        Object value = request.getProperty(CorrelationFilter.PROPERTY);
        return value instanceof String text ? text : UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    }
}
```

Lebih rapi lagi: initialization context dilakukan satu kali setelah auth + tenant selesai. Hindari memanggil `initialize()` berkali-kali dari banyak filter kecuali memang idempotent.

---

# 9. Authentication vs Authorization

Jangan dicampur.

```text
Authentication:
  "Siapa kamu?"
  Output: principal/user/claims/session identity

Authorization:
  "Apakah kamu boleh melakukan aksi ini terhadap resource ini?"
  Output: allow/deny
```

## Authentication output

```java
public record AuthenticatedPrincipal(
        String userId,
        String subject,
        String defaultTenantId,
        Set<String> allowedTenantIds,
        Set<String> roles,
        Set<String> scopes
) {
    public AuthenticatedPrincipal {
        allowedTenantIds = Set.copyOf(allowedTenantIds);
        roles = Set.copyOf(roles);
        scopes = Set.copyOf(scopes);
    }
}
```

## Token verifier

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class TokenVerifier {

    public AuthenticatedPrincipal verify(String authorizationHeader) {
        if (authorizationHeader == null || authorizationHeader.isBlank()) {
            throw new NotAuthorizedException("Missing Authorization header");
        }

        if (!authorizationHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            throw new NotAuthorizedException("Unsupported authorization scheme");
        }

        String token = authorizationHeader.substring("Bearer ".length());

        // Production:
        // - validate signature
        // - validate issuer
        // - validate audience
        // - validate expiry
        // - validate tenant claim
        // - map roles/scopes
        return parseAndValidate(token);
    }

    private AuthenticatedPrincipal parseAndValidate(String token) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Implement JWT/session validation");
    }
}
```

---

# 10. SecurityContext Bridge

JAX-RS punya `SecurityContext` untuk akses principal/role/request security. API `SecurityContext` menyediakan `getUserPrincipal()` dan `isUserInRole(...)`; method tersebut bisa mengembalikan `null`/`false` ketika user tidak authenticated, dan ia berada dalam scope request. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.0/apidocs/jakarta/ws/rs/core/securitycontext?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Dalam Jersey/JAX-RS, kamu bisa membuat custom `SecurityContext` setelah token tervalidasi:

```java
public class AppSecurityContext implements SecurityContext {

    private final AuthenticatedPrincipal principal;
    private final SecurityContext original;

    public AppSecurityContext(
            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal,
            SecurityContext original
    ) {
        this.principal = principal;
        this.original = original;
    }

    @Override
    public Principal getUserPrincipal() {
        return () -> principal.userId();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isUserInRole(String role) {
        return principal.roles().contains(role);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSecure() {
        return original != null && original.isSecure();
    }

    @Override
    public String getAuthenticationScheme() {
        return "Bearer";
    }
}
```

Authentication filter:

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final TokenVerifier tokenVerifier;
    private final RequestPrincipalHolder principalHolder;

    @Inject
    public AuthenticationFilter(
            TokenVerifier tokenVerifier,
            RequestPrincipalHolder principalHolder
    ) {
        this.tokenVerifier = tokenVerifier;
        this.principalHolder = principalHolder;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
                tokenVerifier.verify(request.getHeaderString("Authorization"));

        principalHolder.set(principal);

        SecurityContext original = request.getSecurityContext();
        request.setSecurityContext(new AppSecurityContext(principal, original));
    }
}
```

Request-scoped principal holder:

```java
@RequestScoped
public class RequestPrincipalHolder {

    private AuthenticatedPrincipal principal;

    public void set(AuthenticatedPrincipal principal) {
        this.principal = Objects.requireNonNull(principal);
    }

    public AuthenticatedPrincipal current() {
        if (principal == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Principal not initialized");
        }
        return principal;
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
SecurityContext bagus untuk JAX-RS/resource compatibility.
CDI CurrentRequestContext/CurrentUser lebih bagus untuk service layer.
Jangan membuat service layer bergantung langsung pada ContainerRequestContext.
```

---

# 11. Authorization Pattern

Ada beberapa level authorization.

| Level | Lokasi | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Transport/API access | Gateway/filter | Token valid, audience benar |
| Endpoint-level | JAX-RS filter/name binding | Role `CASE_READER` boleh akses GET `/cases/{id}` |
| Resource-level | Service/policy | User boleh akses case milik tenant tertentu |
| State/action-level | Domain policy | Case dengan status `CLOSED` tidak boleh di-approve |
| Workflow-level | Camunda/task policy | User boleh claim/complete task tertentu |

Jangan mengandalkan filter saja untuk semua authorization. Filter bagus untuk coarse-grained access, tetapi resource-level dan state-level authorization sebaiknya di service/domain policy.

## Name Binding untuk Authorization

Jersey/JAX-RS mendukung name binding agar filter hanya berjalan pada resource/method tertentu; Jersey docs menjelaskan name binding sebagai cara membatasi filter/interceptor hanya untuk resource method tertentu, berbeda dari global filter. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/filters-and-interceptors.html))

Annotation:

```java
@NameBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface RequiresRole {
    String value();
}
```

Catatan: annotation dengan member seperti `String value()` tidak otomatis mudah dibaca oleh filter tanpa reflection ke matched resource method. Dalam Jersey, kamu bisa inject `ResourceInfo`.

```java
@Provider
@RequiresRole("")
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public class RoleAuthorizationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    @Inject
    CurrentRequestContext requestContext;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        RequiresRole annotation = findAnnotation();

        if (annotation == null) {
            return;
        }

        String requiredRole = annotation.value();

        if (!requestContext.hasRole(requiredRole)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Missing role: " + requiredRole);
        }
    }

    private RequiresRole findAnnotation() {
        Method method = resourceInfo.getResourceMethod();

        if (method != null && method.isAnnotationPresent(RequiresRole.class)) {
            return method.getAnnotation(RequiresRole.class);
        }

        Class<?> resourceClass = resourceInfo.getResourceClass();

        if (resourceClass != null && resourceClass.isAnnotationPresent(RequiresRole.class)) {
            return resourceClass.getAnnotation(RequiresRole.class);
        }

        return null;
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@Path("/cases")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseService caseService;

    @Inject
    public CaseResource(CaseService caseService) {
        this.caseService = caseService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    @RequiresRole("CASE_READER")
    public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return Response.ok(caseService.getCase(caseId)).build();
    }
}
```

Tetap lakukan resource-level authorization di service:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository repository;
    private final CaseAccessPolicy accessPolicy;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(
            CaseRepository repository,
            CaseAccessPolicy accessPolicy,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.repository = repository;
        this.accessPolicy = accessPolicy;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        CaseRecord record = repository.findByTenantAndId(context.tenantId(), caseId);

        accessPolicy.assertCanRead(context.userId(), record);

        return CaseDto.from(record);
    }
}
```

---

# 12. ExceptionMapper

`ExceptionMapper<E extends Throwable>` adalah provider yang memetakan Java exception menjadi JAX-RS `Response`; provider harus didaftarkan programmatically atau diberi `@Provider` agar ditemukan saat provider scanning. API Jakarta REST 4.0 juga menyebut bahwa `toResponse` mengembalikan `Response`, `null` menghasilkan 204, dan runtime exception dari mapper menghasilkan 500. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/ext/exceptionmapper))

## Error Response Contract

Buat kontrak error response yang stabil:

```java
public record ErrorResponse(
        String code,
        String message,
        String correlationId,
        Map<String, Object> details
) {
    public static ErrorResponse of(
            String code,
            String message,
            String correlationId
    ) {
        return new ErrorResponse(code, message, correlationId, Map.of());
    }
}
```

## Domain Exceptions

```java
public class CaseNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String caseId;

    public CaseNotFoundException(String caseId) {
        super("Case not found");
        this.caseId = caseId;
    }

    public String caseId() {
        return caseId;
    }
}
```

```java
public class BusinessConflictException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String code;

    public BusinessConflictException(String code, String message) {
        super(message);
        this.code = code;
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }
}
```

## Specific Mapper

```java
@Provider
public class CaseNotFoundExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<CaseNotFoundException> {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseNotFoundExceptionMapper(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(CaseNotFoundException exception) {
        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
                "CASE_NOT_FOUND",
                "Case not found",
                context.correlationId()
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.NOT_FOUND)
                .entity(body)
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .build();
    }
}
```

## Generic Mapper

```java
@Provider
public class GenericExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final ErrorLogger errorLogger;

    @Inject
    public GenericExceptionMapper(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            ErrorLogger errorLogger
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.errorLogger = errorLogger;
    }

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        String correlationId = safeCorrelationId();

        errorLogger.logUnexpected(exception, correlationId);

        ErrorResponse body = ErrorResponse.of(
                "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "Unexpected error",
                correlationId
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .entity(body)
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .build();
    }

    private String safeCorrelationId() {
        try {
            return context.correlationId();
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
            return "unavailable";
        }
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

```text
Generic ExceptionMapper jangan membocorkan exception message internal ke client.
Log detail internal di server, expose code/message aman ke client.
```

---

# 13. Error Mapping Matrix

| Exception | HTTP Status | Error Code | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `BadRequestException` | 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Request syntax/header invalid |
| Validation exception | 400/422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | Pilih 400 atau 422 sesuai API convention company |
| `NotAuthorizedException` | 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Token missing/invalid |
| `ForbiddenException` | 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Authenticated tapi tidak berhak |
| `NotFoundException` / domain not found | 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Jangan leak existence jika security-sensitive |
| `BusinessConflictException` | 409 | domain-specific | State conflict, duplicate command |
| Optimistic lock exception | 409 | `VERSION_CONFLICT` | Retry/refresh needed |
| Rate limit exception | 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Include retry-after jika ada |
| Timeout downstream | 504/503 | `DOWNSTREAM_TIMEOUT` | Tergantung gateway/service role |
| Unexpected exception | 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Log full with correlation ID |

---

# 14. Response Filter

Response filter cocok untuk metadata response.

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR)
public class StandardResponseHeaderFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            ContainerResponseContext response
    ) {
        addCorrelationId(request, response);
        addSecurityHeaders(response);
        addCachePolicy(response);
    }

    private void addCorrelationId(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            ContainerResponseContext response
    ) {
        Object correlationId = request.getProperty(CorrelationFilter.PROPERTY);

        if (correlationId instanceof String value && !value.isBlank()) {
            response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Correlation-Id", value);
        }
    }

    private void addSecurityHeaders(ContainerResponseContext response) {
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
    }

    private void addCachePolicy(ContainerResponseContext response) {
        if (!response.getHeaders().containsKey("Cache-Control")) {
            response.getHeaders().putSingle("Cache-Control", "no-store");
        }
    }
}
```

Jangan ubah business result di response filter. Kalau response body perlu business transformation, lakukan di resource/service/mapper yang eksplisit.

---

# 15. Request Timing / Metrics Filter

Pattern sederhana:

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.HEADER_DECORATOR - 10)
public class RequestTimingFilter
        implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    private static final String START_NANOS = "request.startNanos";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        request.setProperty(START_NANOS, System.nanoTime());
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            ContainerResponseContext response
    ) {
        Object value = request.getProperty(START_NANOS);

        if (value instanceof Long startNanos) {
            long elapsedMillis =
                    TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime() - startNanos);

            response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Response-Time-Ms", elapsedMillis);
        }
    }
}
```

Production note:

- header timing boleh untuk internal API, tetapi untuk public API bisa menjadi information leak.
- metrics sebaiknya dikirim ke metrics backend, bukan hanya header.

---

# 16. ContainerRequestContext vs CDI Context

Jangan membuat service layer bergantung pada JAX-RS object.

Kurang bagus:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Context
    ContainerRequestContext requestContext;

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        String tenantId = requestContext.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");
        return null;
    }
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        String tenantId = context.tenantId();
        return null;
    }
}
```

Kenapa?

| Service pakai `ContainerRequestContext` | Service pakai `CurrentRequestContext` |
|---|---|
| Terikat JAX-RS | Framework-agnostic |
| Sulit test | Mudah test |
| Header mentah tersebar | Data sudah tervalidasi |
| Sulit dipakai dari Kafka/Camunda/job | Bisa diganti snapshot/context lain |
| Security logic rawan duplikasi | Security normalization terpusat |

---

# 17. Request Context Initialization Pattern

Pattern yang saya rekomendasikan:

```text
CorrelationFilter
  └── create/validate correlation ID
      ↓
AuthenticationFilter
  └── validate token → RequestPrincipalHolder
      ↓
TenantContextFilter
  └── resolve tenant → CurrentRequestContext.initialize(...)
      ↓
AuthorizationFilter
  └── check endpoint-level permission
      ↓
Resource
  └── call service
```

Alternatif yang lebih sederhana: satu `RequestBootstrapFilter`.

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class RequestBootstrapFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final TokenVerifier tokenVerifier;
    private final TenantResolver tenantResolver;
    private final CurrentRequestContext currentRequestContext;

    @Inject
    public RequestBootstrapFilter(
            TokenVerifier tokenVerifier,
            TenantResolver tenantResolver,
            CurrentRequestContext currentRequestContext
    ) {
        this.tokenVerifier = tokenVerifier;
        this.tenantResolver = tenantResolver;
        this.currentRequestContext = currentRequestContext;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String correlationId = resolveCorrelationId(request);
        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
                tokenVerifier.verify(request.getHeaderString("Authorization"));
        String tenantId = tenantResolver.resolve(request, principal);

        currentRequestContext.initialize(
                correlationId,
                tenantId,
                principal.userId(),
                principal.roles()
        );
    }

    private String resolveCorrelationId(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String value = request.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");
        return value == null || value.isBlank()
                ? UUID.randomUUID().toString()
                : value;
    }
}
```

Trade-off:

| Model | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| Banyak filter kecil | Modular, urutan jelas, mudah disable | Ordering lebih kompleks |
| Satu bootstrap filter | Sederhana, mudah trace | Bisa menjadi terlalu besar |
| Hybrid | Balance | Perlu convention kuat |

Untuk onboarding cepat, satu bootstrap filter sering lebih mudah. Untuk codebase besar, pecah menjadi filter modular.

---

# 18. Public Endpoint / Anonymous Endpoint

Tidak semua endpoint perlu auth.

Contoh:

- `/health/live`
- `/health/ready`
- `/metrics` internal
- `/openapi`
- callback endpoint tertentu
- public webhook dengan signature validation

Buat annotation:

```java
@NameBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface PublicEndpoint {
}
```

Authentication filter:

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    private final TokenVerifier tokenVerifier;
    private final RequestPrincipalHolder principalHolder;

    @Inject
    public AuthenticationFilter(
            TokenVerifier tokenVerifier,
            RequestPrincipalHolder principalHolder
    ) {
        this.tokenVerifier = tokenVerifier;
        this.principalHolder = principalHolder;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        if (isPublicEndpoint()) {
            principalHolder.set(AuthenticatedPrincipal.anonymous());
            return;
        }

        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
                tokenVerifier.verify(request.getHeaderString("Authorization"));

        principalHolder.set(principal);
    }

    private boolean isPublicEndpoint() {
        Method method = resourceInfo.getResourceMethod();
        Class<?> resourceClass = resourceInfo.getResourceClass();

        return method != null && method.isAnnotationPresent(PublicEndpoint.class)
                || resourceClass != null && resourceClass.isAnnotationPresent(PublicEndpoint.class);
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@Path("/health")
@PublicEndpoint
public class HealthResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/live")
    public Response live() {
        return Response.ok(Map.of("status", "UP")).build();
    }
}
```

---

# 19. Idempotency Key Filter

Untuk command endpoint seperti payment, approval, submit, create case, idempotency penting.

```java
@NameBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface RequiresIdempotencyKey {
}
```

Filter:

```java
@Provider
@RequiresIdempotencyKey
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION + 100)
public class IdempotencyKeyFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final IdempotencyService idempotencyService;

    @Inject
    public IdempotencyKeyFilter(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            IdempotencyService idempotencyService
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.idempotencyService = idempotencyService;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String key = request.getHeaderString("Idempotency-Key");

        if (key == null || key.isBlank()) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Missing Idempotency-Key");
        }

        idempotencyService.assertUsable(context.tenantId(), context.userId(), key);
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@POST
@Path("/{caseId}/approve")
@RequiresRole("CASE_APPROVER")
@RequiresIdempotencyKey
public Response approve(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
    return Response.ok(caseService.approve(caseId)).build();
}
```

---

# 20. Resource Layer: Keep It Thin

Resource layer sebaiknya:

- menerima HTTP request
- mapping path/query/body
- memanggil service
- memilih HTTP status code
- tidak menyimpan state
- tidak memuat business workflow kompleks

Contoh:

```java
@Path("/cases")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseService caseService;

    @Inject
    public CaseResource(CaseService caseService) {
        this.caseService = caseService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    @RequiresRole("CASE_READER")
    public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        CaseDto result = caseService.getCase(caseId);
        return Response.ok(result).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{caseId}/approve")
    @RequiresRole("CASE_APPROVER")
    @RequiresIdempotencyKey
    public Response approveCase(
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
            ApproveCaseRequest request
    ) {
        ApprovalResult result = caseService.approve(caseId, request.comment());
        return Response.ok(result).build();
    }
}
```

Jangan:

```java
@POST
@Path("/{caseId}/approve")
public Response approveCase(...) {
    // validate token
    // resolve tenant
    // open DB transaction
    // query DB
    // check workflow state
    // call external system
    // write audit
    // map response
}
```

Itu resource menjadi god method.

---

# 21. Practical Failure Modes

| Failure Mode | Penyebab | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `@Inject` di filter/provider null | Provider tidak CDI-managed atau integrasi Jersey/Weld salah | Pastikan CDI integration dan registration benar |
| `CurrentRequestContext` belum initialized | Filter belum jalan atau priority salah | Bootstrap context lebih awal |
| `request context not active` | Akses `@RequestScoped` di thread/job/startup | Gunakan snapshot atau job context |
| Tenant spoofing | Percaya `X-Tenant-Id` mentah | Validate terhadap principal/gateway trust |
| Correlation hilang di error response | Exception terjadi sebelum context init | Simpan correlation di request property dan safe fallback |
| Generic mapper leak detail | Return `exception.getMessage()` ke client | Gunakan safe message + log internal |
| AuthZ terlalu kasar | Hanya role-level di filter | Tambah resource/domain policy di service |
| Public endpoint ikut auth | Filter tidak cek annotation/path | Gunakan `@PublicEndpoint` / whitelist eksplisit |
| Header response tidak konsisten | Ditambahkan manual di setiap resource | Gunakan response filter |
| Filter order kacau | Tidak pakai `@Priority` | Definisikan priority convention |

# 23. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Filter order | Apakah `@Priority` jelas? |
| Correlation | Apakah correlation ID dibuat, divalidasi, dan dikembalikan ke response? |
| Tenant | Apakah tenant divalidasi terhadap principal/trusted source? |
| Auth | Apakah token verifier validate issuer/audience/expiry/signature? |
| SecurityContext | Apakah principal/role diset jika resource butuh compatibility JAX-RS? |
| CDI context | Apakah `CurrentRequestContext` hanya berisi data tervalidasi? |
| Resource | Apakah resource thin dan stateless? |
| ExceptionMapper | Apakah error response konsisten dan tidak leak detail internal? |
| Response header | Apakah header umum ditambahkan via response filter? |
| Public endpoint | Apakah whitelist eksplisit? |
| Idempotency | Apakah command endpoint punya key bila operasi tidak aman diulang? |
| Async | Apakah tidak membawa request-scoped bean ke thread lain? |

# 24. Latihan Praktis Seri 5

Implementasi minimum yang sebaiknya kamu buat:

```text
1. CorrelationFilter
2. RequestBootstrapFilter
3. CurrentRequestContext
4. RequestPrincipalHolder
5. TokenVerifier dummy
6. TenantResolver
7. AppSecurityContext
8. @RequiresRole
9. RoleAuthorizationFilter
10. ErrorResponse
11. CaseNotFoundExceptionMapper
12. GenericExceptionMapper
13. StandardResponseHeaderFilter
14. HealthResource dengan @PublicEndpoint
15. CaseResource dengan @RequiresRole
```

Smoke test:

```bash
curl -i http://localhost:8080/health/live
```

Expected:

```text
200 OK
X-Correlation-Id: <generated>
```

```bash
curl -i \
  -H 'Authorization: Bearer demo-token' \
  -H 'X-Tenant-Id: tenant-a' \
  -H 'X-Correlation-Id: corr-001' \
  http://localhost:8080/cases/CASE-001
```

Expected:

```text
200 OK or domain response
X-Correlation-Id: corr-001
```

Unauthorized:

```bash
curl -i http://localhost:8080/cases/CASE-001
```

Expected:

```text
401 Unauthorized
{
  "code": "UNAUTHENTICATED",
  "message": "...",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Forbidden tenant:

```bash
curl -i \
  -H 'Authorization: Bearer demo-token' \
  -H 'X-Tenant-Id: tenant-not-allowed' \
  http://localhost:8080/cases/CASE-001
```

Expected:

```text
403 Forbidden
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 5

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. Request pipeline Jersey/JAX-RS punya urutan jelas: request filter → resource matching → request filter post-match → resource/service → exception mapper/response filter.
2. Filter cocok untuk metadata request/response seperti header, auth bootstrap, correlation, tenant, idempotency, dan coarse authorization.
3. Interceptor JAX-RS lebih cocok untuk entity stream, bukan metadata header.
4. `CurrentRequestContext` harus diisi dari filter dan berisi data yang sudah divalidasi.
5. Service layer sebaiknya bergantung pada CDI context abstraction, bukan `ContainerRequestContext`.
6. `SecurityContext` berguna sebagai bridge JAX-RS principal/role, tetapi service layer lebih bersih memakai CDI context.
7. Tenant tidak boleh dipercaya dari raw public header tanpa validasi terhadap principal/trusted gateway.
8. `ExceptionMapper` menjaga response error konsisten dan menyertakan correlation ID.
9. Generic exception mapper harus log detail internal, tetapi response client harus aman.
10. Response filter cocok untuk correlation response header, security headers, cache headers, dan timing metadata.
11. Authorization perlu berlapis: endpoint-level di filter, resource/state/action-level di service/domain policy.
12. Untuk command endpoint, idempotency key sebaiknya menjadi bagian request pipeline.

**Seri 5/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 6 - AOP/Interceptor Practical: Audit, Metrics, Authorization, Transaction Boundary**, dengan fokus pada CDI interceptor binding, `@AroundInvoke`, ordering, self-invocation limitation, audit trail, metrics timing, permission guard, dan boundary transaksi MyBatis/PostgreSQL.

- [Chapter 10. Filters and Interceptors](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/filters-and-interceptors.html)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 4:08:32 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 6/14**. Fokusnya: membuat CDI interceptor yang benar-benar berguna di microservice—audit, metrics, permission guard, feature guard, idempotency, dan transaction boundary—beserta batasan proxy/self-invocation.

# Seri 6/14 - AOP/Interceptor Practical: Audit, Metrics, Authorization, Transaction Boundary

Target seri ini: kamu bisa memakai **CDI Interceptor/AOP** secara praktis untuk cross-cutting concern di microservice **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld + MyBatis/PostgreSQL**.

Fokusnya bukan sekadar “cara bikin annotation”, tetapi:

- kapan memakai **CDI interceptor**
- kapan tetap memakai **JAX-RS filter**
- bagaimana membuat `@Audited`, `@Measured`, `@RequiresPermission`, `@FeatureGate`, `@TransactionalBoundary`, `@Idempotent`
- bagaimana ordering interceptor
- apa batasan proxy/self-invocation
- bagaimana mencegah transaction/audit/security behavior menjadi “magic” yang sulit debug

Jakarta Interceptors mendefinisikan mekanisme untuk menyisipkan logic pada business method invocation dan lifecycle event managed classes; CDI menambahkan cara mengasosiasikan interceptor ke bean lewat **typesafe interceptor binding**. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/interceptors/2.2/jakarta-interceptors-spec-2.2))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Interceptor adalah cara membuat behavior lintas-cutting tanpa menaruh boilerplate di setiap service method.

```text id="98v1ig"
Client / Resource
   │
   ▼
CDI Proxy
   │
   ├── MetricsInterceptor
   ├── AuthorizationInterceptor
   ├── TransactionInterceptor
   ├── AuditInterceptor
   └── Target Method
          └── CaseService.approveCase(...)
```

Dengan kata lain:

```text id="g2984e"
Tanpa interceptor:
  CaseService.approve()
    ├── start timer
    ├── check permission
    ├── begin transaction
    ├── write audit start
    ├── business logic
    ├── commit
    ├── write audit success
    └── record metric

Dengan interceptor:
  @Measured
  @RequiresPermission("CASE_APPROVE")
  @TransactionalBoundary
  @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
  public ApprovalResult approve(...)
```

Tapi ada batas penting:

> Interceptor hanya aktif kalau method dipanggil melalui CDI-managed reference/proxy. Kalau object dibuat manual dengan `new`, atau method dipanggil internal dari class yang sama, interceptor bisa tidak jalan.

---

# 2. CDI Interceptor vs JAX-RS Filter vs JAX-RS Entity Interceptor

Jangan semua hal dimasukkan ke CDI interceptor. Pilih extension point sesuai layer.

| Concern | Tempat yang Lebih Tepat | Alasan |
|---|---|---|
| Correlation ID dari HTTP header | JAX-RS `ContainerRequestFilter` | Concern HTTP metadata |
| Auth token parsing | JAX-RS request filter | Butuh header/request object |
| Endpoint-level auth | JAX-RS filter atau CDI interceptor | Tergantung apakah rule di HTTP layer atau service layer |
| Business permission | CDI interceptor/service policy | Dekat dengan use case |
| Transaction boundary | CDI interceptor/service layer | Business method boundary |
| Audit business action | CDI interceptor | Cross-cutting use case |
| Metrics method duration | CDI interceptor | Ukur business method |
| Request/response header | JAX-RS response filter | Concern HTTP response |
| Body stream compression/logging | JAX-RS `ReaderInterceptor`/`WriterInterceptor` | Entity stream concern |
| Retry downstream call | CDI interceptor atau client wrapper | Tergantung apakah per method atau per client |

JAX-RS filters dan entity interceptors adalah provider yang berjalan pada extension point request/response/entity stream; filters tidak membungkus method invocation, sedangkan entity interceptors berhubungan dengan read/write entity stream. CDI interceptor berbeda karena ia membungkus business method invocation pada managed bean. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html))

---

# 3. Diagram Decomposition

```text id="vovltc"
CDI/AOP Interceptor Practical Map
│
├── 1. Binding Model
│   ├── @InterceptorBinding
│   ├── binding members
│   ├── @Nonbinding
│   ├── class-level binding
│   └── method-level binding
│
├── 2. Interceptor Implementation
│   ├── @Interceptor
│   ├── @AroundInvoke
│   ├── InvocationContext
│   ├── ctx.proceed()
│   ├── parameters/result
│   └── exception handling
│
├── 3. Enablement & Ordering
│   ├── @Priority
│   ├── beans.xml
│   ├── smaller number first
│   └── undefined order if same priority
│
├── 4. Practical Cross-Cutting
│   ├── @Measured
│   ├── @Audited
│   ├── @RequiresPermission
│   ├── @FeatureGate
│   ├── @TransactionalBoundary
│   ├── @Idempotent
│   └── @Retryable
│
├── 5. Context Dependencies
│   ├── CurrentRequestContext
│   ├── CurrentUser
│   ├── TenantContext
│   ├── CorrelationContext
│   ├── SqlSessionProvider
│   └── AuditContext
│
├── 6. Failure Modes
│   ├── self-invocation
│   ├── manual new
│   ├── final class/method
│   ├── missing @Priority
│   ├── wrong ordering
│   ├── swallowing exception
│   └── transaction/audit mismatch
│
└── 7. Governance
    ├── annotation naming
    ├── documented ordering
    ├── safe logging
    ├── test coverage
    └── operational visibility
```

---

# 4. Basic CDI Interceptor Anatomy

Ada tiga bagian:

```text id="l8lfc9"
1. Binding annotation
2. Interceptor class
3. Target method/class
```

Jakarta Interceptors memakai `@AroundInvoke` untuk method yang menyisipkan behavior di sekitar business method. `InvocationContext.proceed()` meneruskan invocation chain ke interceptor berikutnya atau target method. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/interceptors/2.2/jakarta-interceptors-spec-2.2))

## 4.1 Binding Annotation

```java id="k9z2k7"
import jakarta.interceptor.InterceptorBinding;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

import static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.METHOD;
import static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.TYPE;
import static java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME;

@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Measured {
}
```

## 4.2 Interceptor Class

```java id="alpu43"
import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.interceptor.AroundInvoke;
import jakarta.interceptor.Interceptor;
import jakarta.interceptor.InvocationContext;

@Measured
@Interceptor
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION)
public class MetricsInterceptor {

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        long started = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            return ctx.proceed();
        } finally {
            long elapsedNanos = System.nanoTime() - started;
            String method = ctx.getMethod().toGenericString();

            // Production: record to metrics backend, not System.out
            System.out.printf("method=%s elapsedMs=%d%n",
                    method,
                    elapsedNanos / 1_000_000
            );
        }
    }
}
```

## 4.3 Target Method

```java id="778cwp"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Measured
    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

---

# 5. Enablement dan Ordering

CDI interceptor binding biasanya diaktifkan dengan `@Priority` atau melalui `beans.xml`. Dengan `@Priority`, nilai lebih kecil dipanggil lebih awal; jika dua interceptor punya nilai priority yang sama, urutan relatifnya tidak terdefinisi. Jakarta Interceptors juga mendefinisikan range seperti `PLATFORM_BEFORE`, `LIBRARY_BEFORE`, `APPLICATION`, `LIBRARY_AFTER`, dan `PLATFORM_AFTER`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/interceptors/2.2/jakarta-interceptors-spec-2.2))

Practical convention:

```java id="ycd1oy"
public final class AppInterceptorPriority {

    private AppInterceptorPriority() {
    }

    public static final int METRICS = Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION;
    public static final int FEATURE_GUARD = Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 100;
    public static final int AUTHORIZATION = Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 200;
    public static final int IDEMPOTENCY = Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 300;
    public static final int TRANSACTION = Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 400;
    public static final int AUDIT = Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 500;
}
```

Kenapa ordering penting?

```text id="tp2ojp"
Recommended chain:
  Metrics
    └── Feature guard
        └── Authorization
            └── Idempotency
                └── Transaction
                    └── Audit
                        └── Business method
```

Interpretasi:

| Interceptor | Posisi | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Metrics | Paling luar | Ukur total durasi termasuk guard/transaction/audit |
| Feature guard | Sebelum mahal | Kalau feature off, jangan buka transaksi |
| Authorization | Sebelum transaksi | Kalau tidak berhak, jangan buka transaksi |
| Idempotency | Sebelum transaksi / bisa dengan transaksi | Cegah duplicate command |
| Transaction | Dekat business method | Menjamin repository writes dalam unit-of-work |
| Audit | Bisa di luar/dalam transaksi tergantung kebutuhan | Audit success/failure harus jelas semantiknya |

Tidak ada satu urutan universal. Yang penting: **urutan harus eksplisit dan dites**.

---

# 6. Binding Annotation dengan Member dan `@Nonbinding`

Kalau annotation punya member, nilai member default-nya ikut menentukan interceptor resolution. Kalau satu interceptor ingin menangani semua nilai annotation member, gunakan `@Nonbinding`. CDI mendefinisikan bahwa member dengan `@Nonbinding` diabaikan saat interceptor resolution. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

Contoh:

```java id="3iihza"
import jakarta.enterprise.util.Nonbinding;
import jakarta.interceptor.InterceptorBinding;

import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

import static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.METHOD;
import static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.TYPE;
import static java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME;

@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Audited {

    @Nonbinding
    String action();

    @Nonbinding
    boolean includeResult() default false;
}
```

Kenapa `@Nonbinding` penting?

Kalau tidak:

```java id="h95hsx"
@Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
```

dan

```java id="pcfy6e"
@Audited(action = "CASE_REJECT")
```

bisa dianggap binding berbeda, sehingga interceptor yang tidak memilih nilai spesifik tidak cocok.

---

# 7. Practical Pattern: `@Measured`

## Binding

```java id="r5tw7o"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Measured {

    @Nonbinding
    String value() default "";
}
```

## Metrics Recorder

```java id="2fpig7"
@ApplicationScoped
public class MetricsRecorder {

    public void recordDuration(
            String metricName,
            long elapsedNanos,
            boolean success
    ) {
        // Production:
        // - Micrometer/OpenTelemetry/company metrics abstraction
        // - tags: class, method, outcome, exception type, tenant tier if allowed
    }
}
```

## Interceptor

```java id="ez5pgd"
@Measured
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.METRICS)
public class MetricsInterceptor {

    private final MetricsRecorder recorder;

    @Inject
    public MetricsInterceptor(MetricsRecorder recorder) {
        this.recorder = recorder;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        long started = System.nanoTime();
        boolean success = false;

        try {
            Object result = ctx.proceed();
            success = true;
            return result;
        } finally {
            long elapsedNanos = System.nanoTime() - started;
            Measured measured = resolveMeasured(ctx);

            String metricName = measured.value().isBlank()
                    ? defaultMetricName(ctx)
                    : measured.value();

            recorder.recordDuration(metricName, elapsedNanos, success);
        }
    }

    private Measured resolveMeasured(InvocationContext ctx) {
        Measured methodAnnotation = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(Measured.class);

        if (methodAnnotation != null) {
            return methodAnnotation;
        }

        return ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(Measured.class);
    }

    private String defaultMetricName(InvocationContext ctx) {
        return ctx.getTarget().getClass().getSimpleName()
                + "."
                + ctx.getMethod().getName();
    }
}
```

## Usage

```java id="gctw7d"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Measured("case.approve.duration")
    public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

| Jangan | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Tag metrics dengan raw user ID | High cardinality + privacy risk |
| Tag metrics dengan case ID | High cardinality |
| Log parameter mentah | Bisa berisi PII/secret |
| Menelan exception | Metrics harus record failure, lalu rethrow |

---

# 8. Practical Pattern: `@Audited`

Audit di regulatory/case management biasanya tidak sama dengan log. Audit harus menjawab:

```text id="dt273i"
who did what,
to which business object,
under which tenant,
when,
from which request/correlation,
with what outcome.
```

## Binding

```java id="0401t4"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Audited {

    @Nonbinding
    String action();

    @Nonbinding
    boolean includeResult() default false;
}
```

## Audit Event

```java id="jx2c1o"
public record AuditEvent(
        String action,
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        String correlationId,
        String targetType,
        String targetId,
        String outcome,
        String errorCode,
        Instant occurredAt
) {
}
```

## Audit Sink

```java id="dbzju6"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AuditSink {

    public void write(AuditEvent event) {
        // Production:
        // - write to audit table/outbox/event stream
        // - must be append-only or tamper-evident depending requirement
        // - avoid raw PII/secret
    }
}
```

## Target Resolver

Jangan hardcode bahwa parameter pertama pasti case ID untuk semua method. Tapi untuk baseline praktis, bisa mulai dengan convention.

```java id="cuw5ei"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AuditTargetResolver {

    public AuditTarget resolve(InvocationContext ctx) {
        Object[] params = ctx.getParameters();

        if (params.length > 0 && params[0] instanceof String id) {
            return new AuditTarget("UNKNOWN", id);
        }

        return new AuditTarget("UNKNOWN", "unknown");
    }
}
```

```java id="5k3g5h"
public record AuditTarget(String type, String id) {
}
```

## Interceptor

```java id="0sjepe"
@Audited(action = "")
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.AUDIT)
public class AuditInterceptor {

    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;
    private final AuditSink auditSink;
    private final AuditTargetResolver targetResolver;
    private final Clock clock;

    @Inject
    public AuditInterceptor(
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext,
            AuditSink auditSink,
            AuditTargetResolver targetResolver,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
        this.auditSink = auditSink;
        this.targetResolver = targetResolver;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        Audited audited = resolveAudited(ctx);
        AuditTarget target = targetResolver.resolve(ctx);

        try {
            Object result = ctx.proceed();

            auditSink.write(new AuditEvent(
                    audited.action(),
                    requestContext.tenantId(),
                    requestContext.userId(),
                    requestContext.correlationId(),
                    target.type(),
                    target.id(),
                    "SUCCESS",
                    null,
                    Instant.now(clock)
            ));

            return result;
        } catch (BusinessException ex) {
            auditSink.write(new AuditEvent(
                    audited.action(),
                    requestContext.tenantId(),
                    requestContext.userId(),
                    requestContext.correlationId(),
                    target.type(),
                    target.id(),
                    "BUSINESS_FAILURE",
                    ex.code(),
                    Instant.now(clock)
            ));

            throw ex;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            auditSink.write(new AuditEvent(
                    audited.action(),
                    requestContext.tenantId(),
                    requestContext.userId(),
                    requestContext.correlationId(),
                    target.type(),
                    target.id(),
                    "SYSTEM_FAILURE",
                    ex.getClass().getSimpleName(),
                    Instant.now(clock)
            ));

            throw ex;
        }
    }

    private Audited resolveAudited(InvocationContext ctx) {
        Audited method = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(Audited.class);
        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        Audited type = ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(Audited.class);
        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("@Audited not found");
    }
}
```

## Usage

```java id="7dsz9x"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
    public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

## Audit Transaction Semantics

Audit bisa ditempatkan:

| Model | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| Audit dalam DB transaction yang sama | Konsisten dengan business write | Kalau rollback, audit failure/success ikut hilang |
| Audit di luar transaction | Failure tetap tercatat | Bisa ada audit success padahal commit gagal jika ordering salah |
| Audit via outbox dalam transaction | Konsisten dan durable | Perlu outbox processor |
| Audit sink terpisah append-only | Bagus untuk compliance | Perlu desain retry/idempotency |

Untuk regulatory system, pola yang sering kuat adalah:

```text id="sg85oj"
Business transaction
  ├── update business tables
  └── insert audit/outbox event
Commit
  └── async publisher writes to immutable audit/event sink
```

---

# 9. Practical Pattern: `@RequiresPermission`

Endpoint-level role sudah bisa dilakukan di JAX-RS filter. Tapi business permission lebih baik dekat use case.

## Binding

```java id="y35kao"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface RequiresPermission {

    @Nonbinding
    String value();
}
```

## Permission Service

```java id="wa1v0q"
@ApplicationScoped
public class PermissionService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public PermissionService(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    public void assertAllowed(String permission, InvocationContext invocation) {
        if (!hasPermission(permission)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Missing permission: " + permission);
        }
    }

    private boolean hasPermission(String permission) {
        // Production:
        // - RBAC/ABAC/policy engine
        // - tenant-aware
        // - resource-aware when possible
        return context.roles().contains(permission);
    }
}
```

## Interceptor

```java id="f1ji8g"
@RequiresPermission("")
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.AUTHORIZATION)
public class PermissionInterceptor {

    private final PermissionService permissionService;

    @Inject
    public PermissionInterceptor(PermissionService permissionService) {
        this.permissionService = permissionService;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        RequiresPermission annotation = resolve(ctx);
        permissionService.assertAllowed(annotation.value(), ctx);
        return ctx.proceed();
    }

    private RequiresPermission resolve(InvocationContext ctx) {
        RequiresPermission method = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(RequiresPermission.class);
        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        RequiresPermission type =
                ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(RequiresPermission.class);
        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("@RequiresPermission not found");
    }
}
```

## Usage

```java id="f5f69v"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @RequiresPermission("CASE_APPROVE")
    public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

```text id="r8xa4u"
Permission interceptor cocok untuk coarse business permission.
Resource-specific checks tetap harus di service/policy setelah data business dibaca.
```

Contoh resource-specific policy:

```java id="1rvylw"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseAccessPolicy {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseAccessPolicy(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    public void assertCanApprove(CaseRecord record) {
        if (!record.tenantId().equals(context.tenantId())) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Cross-tenant access denied");
        }

        if (record.status() == CaseStatus.CLOSED) {
            throw new BusinessConflictException(
                    "CASE_CLOSED",
                    "Closed case cannot be approved"
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Practical Pattern: `@FeatureGate`

Feature flag sebaiknya bisa memakai request context: tenant, user, environment, role, region.

## Binding

```java id="yl5jhm"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface FeatureGate {

    @Nonbinding
    String value();

    @Nonbinding
    boolean failClosed() default true;
}
```

## Feature Flag Service

```java id="w82wzt"
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureFlagService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public FeatureFlagService(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    public boolean isEnabled(String flagKey) {
        FeatureEvaluationContext evalContext = new FeatureEvaluationContext(
                context.tenantId(),
                context.userId(),
                context.roles()
        );

        // Production:
        // - OpenFeature/vendor SDK/company abstraction
        // - local fallback
        // - timeout/cache
        return evaluate(flagKey, evalContext);
    }

    private boolean evaluate(String flagKey, FeatureEvaluationContext context) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Evaluate feature flag");
    }
}
```

```java id="xt0b8x"
public record FeatureEvaluationContext(
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles
) {
    public FeatureEvaluationContext {
        roles = Set.copyOf(roles);
    }
}
```

## Interceptor

```java id="t7s0jl"
@FeatureGate("")
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.FEATURE_GUARD)
public class FeatureGateInterceptor {

    private final FeatureFlagService featureFlagService;

    @Inject
    public FeatureGateInterceptor(FeatureFlagService featureFlagService) {
        this.featureFlagService = featureFlagService;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        FeatureGate gate = resolve(ctx);

        boolean enabled;
        try {
            enabled = featureFlagService.isEnabled(gate.value());
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            if (gate.failClosed()) {
                throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Feature flag unavailable");
            }
            enabled = true;
        }

        if (!enabled) {
            throw new NotFoundException("Feature not available");
        }

        return ctx.proceed();
    }

    private FeatureGate resolve(InvocationContext ctx) {
        FeatureGate method = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(FeatureGate.class);
        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        FeatureGate type = ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(FeatureGate.class);
        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("@FeatureGate not found");
    }
}
```

## Usage

```java id="oq17kz"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseExportService {

    @FeatureGate("case.bulk-export")
    public ExportJob submitBulkExport(ExportRequest request) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

| Scenario | Fail Closed? |
|---|---:|
| Security-sensitive feature | Ya |
| Money movement/payment | Ya |
| Read-only cosmetic feature | Bisa tidak |
| Emergency kill switch | Ya |
| Migration fallback | Tergantung risk |

---

# 11. Practical Pattern: `@TransactionalBoundary` untuk MyBatis/PostgreSQL

Di stack non-Spring/non-JTA, transaction boundary sering perlu dibuat eksplisit. Dengan MyBatis, `SqlSession` dan commit/rollback harus jelas.

## Binding

```java id="l1uzh8"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface TransactionalBoundary {

    @Nonbinding
    boolean readOnly() default false;
}
```

## Transaction Context

```java id="j9m063"
@RequestScoped
public class TransactionContext {

    private boolean active;
    private boolean rollbackOnly;

    public boolean isActive() {
        return active;
    }

    public void begin() {
        if (active) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Transaction already active");
        }
        this.active = true;
    }

    public void markRollbackOnly() {
        this.rollbackOnly = true;
    }

    public boolean rollbackOnly() {
        return rollbackOnly;
    }

    public void end() {
        this.active = false;
        this.rollbackOnly = false;
    }
}
```

## SqlSessionProvider

```java id="8n04pk"
@RequestScoped
public class SqlSessionProvider {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;
    private SqlSession session;

    @Inject
    public SqlSessionProvider(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    public SqlSession currentSession() {
        if (session == null) {
            session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);
        }
        return session;
    }

    public void commit() {
        if (session != null) {
            session.commit();
        }
    }

    public void rollback() {
        if (session != null) {
            session.rollback();
        }
    }

    @PreDestroy
    public void close() {
        if (session != null) {
            session.close();
        }
    }
}
```

## Interceptor

```java id="876b6e"
@TransactionalBoundary
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.TRANSACTION)
public class TransactionInterceptor {

    private final TransactionContext transactionContext;
    private final SqlSessionProvider sqlSessionProvider;

    @Inject
    public TransactionInterceptor(
            TransactionContext transactionContext,
            SqlSessionProvider sqlSessionProvider
    ) {
        this.transactionContext = transactionContext;
        this.sqlSessionProvider = sqlSessionProvider;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        if (transactionContext.isActive()) {
            return ctx.proceed(); // join existing transaction
        }

        transactionContext.begin();

        try {
            Object result = ctx.proceed();

            if (transactionContext.rollbackOnly()) {
                sqlSessionProvider.rollback();
            } else {
                sqlSessionProvider.commit();
            }

            return result;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            transactionContext.markRollbackOnly();
            sqlSessionProvider.rollback();
            throw ex;
        } finally {
            transactionContext.end();
        }
    }
}
```

## Usage

```java id="xmb3qt"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseCommandService {

    @TransactionalBoundary
    @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
    public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

## Repository

```java id="2oou7s"
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final SqlSessionProvider sessionProvider;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisCaseRepository(SqlSessionProvider sessionProvider) {
        this.sessionProvider = sessionProvider;
    }

    @Override
    public CaseRecord findByIdForUpdate(String caseId) {
        return sessionProvider.currentSession()
                .getMapper(CaseMapper.class)
                .findByIdForUpdate(caseId);
    }

    @Override
    public void update(CaseRecord record) {
        sessionProvider.currentSession()
                .getMapper(CaseMapper.class)
                .update(record);
    }
}
```

## Transaction Boundary Rules

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Transaction boundary di service/use-case method | Jangan di setiap repository method |
| Repository join session aktif | Repository tidak commit sendiri |
| Nested call harus join existing transaction | Jangan buka session/transaction baru diam-diam |
| Exception harus rollback | Jangan swallow exception |
| Read-only tetap perlu boundary kalau butuh consistent read | Tapi bisa optimize di DB/session config |
| Async/Camunda job perlu context sendiri | Jangan bergantung pada HTTP request scope |

Catatan penting: `@TransactionalBoundary` di atas adalah pola lokal untuk MyBatis/manual transaction. Ini bukan pengganti JTA penuh. Kalau environment memakai Jakarta Transactions/JTA, modelnya berbeda dan harus mengikuti transaction manager container.

---

# 12. Practical Pattern: `@Idempotent`

Idempotency penting untuk command endpoint yang bisa diulang karena retry client, timeout, network failure, atau double-submit.

## Binding

```java id="563ux6"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Idempotent {

    @Nonbinding
    String operation();

    @Nonbinding
    int ttlSeconds() default 86_400;
}
```

## Idempotency Service

```java id="stiqrk"
@ApplicationScoped
public class IdempotencyService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final IdempotencyRepository repository;

    @Inject
    public IdempotencyService(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            IdempotencyRepository repository
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.repository = repository;
    }

    public IdempotencyDecision begin(String operation, String key, String requestHash) {
        return repository.tryBegin(
                context.tenantId(),
                operation,
                key,
                requestHash
        );
    }

    public void complete(String operation, String key, Object result) {
        repository.complete(
                context.tenantId(),
                operation,
                key,
                result
        );
    }

    public void fail(String operation, String key, Exception ex) {
        repository.fail(
                context.tenantId(),
                operation,
                key,
                ex.getClass().getSimpleName()
        );
    }
}
```

```java id="u9gyvq"
public sealed interface IdempotencyDecision {

    record Proceed() implements IdempotencyDecision {
    }

    record ReturnCached(Object result) implements IdempotencyDecision {
    }

    record Conflict(String reason) implements IdempotencyDecision {
    }
}
```

## Request Key Provider

Karena CDI interceptor berada di service layer, ia tidak otomatis tahu HTTP headers. Maka idempotency key sebaiknya sudah dipindahkan dari JAX-RS filter ke request context.

```java id="iyiyu8"
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {

    private String idempotencyKey;

    public void setIdempotencyKey(String idempotencyKey) {
        this.idempotencyKey = idempotencyKey;
    }

    public Optional<String> idempotencyKey() {
        return Optional.ofNullable(idempotencyKey);
    }
}
```

## Interceptor

```java id="g963ws"
@Idempotent(operation = "")
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.IDEMPOTENCY)
public class IdempotencyInterceptor {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final IdempotencyService idempotencyService;
    private final RequestHasher requestHasher;

    @Inject
    public IdempotencyInterceptor(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            IdempotencyService idempotencyService,
            RequestHasher requestHasher
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.idempotencyService = idempotencyService;
        this.requestHasher = requestHasher;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        Idempotent annotation = resolve(ctx);

        String key = context.idempotencyKey()
                .orElseThrow(() -> new BadRequestException("Missing Idempotency-Key"));

        String requestHash = requestHasher.hash(ctx.getParameters());

        IdempotencyDecision decision =
                idempotencyService.begin(annotation.operation(), key, requestHash);

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.ReturnCached cached) {
            return cached.result();
        }

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.Conflict conflict) {
            throw new ConflictException(conflict.reason());
        }

        try {
            Object result = ctx.proceed();
            idempotencyService.complete(annotation.operation(), key, result);
            return result;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            idempotencyService.fail(annotation.operation(), key, ex);
            throw ex;
        }
    }

    private Idempotent resolve(InvocationContext ctx) {
        Idempotent method = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(Idempotent.class);
        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        Idempotent type = ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(Idempotent.class);
        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("@Idempotent not found");
    }
}
```

## Usage

```java id="5e9cbp"
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentCommandService {

    @Idempotent(operation = "PAYMENT_CAPTURE")
    @TransactionalBoundary
    @Audited(action = "PAYMENT_CAPTURE")
    public PaymentResult capture(String paymentId, CaptureCommand command) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

Practical DB constraint:

```sql id="b2cpfy"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_idempotency_key
ON idempotency_record (tenant_id, operation, idempotency_key);
```

Failure mode:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Tidak hash request body | Same key bisa dipakai untuk request berbeda |
| Cache result sebelum commit | Return success padahal business rollback |
| Tidak tenant-scoped | Cross-tenant key collision |
| Tidak ada unique constraint | Race condition |
| Tidak ada expiry/cleanup | Table tumbuh tanpa batas |

---

# 13. Practical Pattern: `@Retryable`

Untuk retry, hati-hati. Retry tidak boleh sembarang dipasang pada operation non-idempotent.

## Binding

```java id="x8clrw"
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Retryable {

    @Nonbinding
    int maxAttempts() default 3;

    @Nonbinding
    long delayMillis() default 100;

    @Nonbinding
    Class<? extends Throwable>[] on() default { TransientException.class };
}
```

## Interceptor

```java id="r4fazn"
@Retryable
@Interceptor
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 700)
public class RetryInterceptor {

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        Retryable retryable = resolve(ctx);
        int attempt = 0;

        while (true) {
            attempt++;

            try {
                return ctx.proceed();
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                if (attempt >= retryable.maxAttempts() || !isRetryable(ex, retryable.on())) {
                    throw ex;
                }

                sleep(retryable.delayMillis());
            }
        }
    }

    private boolean isRetryable(Throwable ex, Class<? extends Throwable>[] retryableTypes) {
        for (Class<? extends Throwable> type : retryableTypes) {
            if (type.isInstance(ex)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private void sleep(long delayMillis) {
        try {
            Thread.sleep(delayMillis);
        } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new RuntimeException("Retry interrupted", interrupted);
        }
    }

    private Retryable resolve(InvocationContext ctx) {
        Retryable method = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(Retryable.class);
        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        Retryable type = ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(Retryable.class);
        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("@Retryable not found");
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

```text id="rp0vls"
Retry + transaction + idempotency ordering harus jelas.
Jangan retry seluruh transaction kalau efek eksternal sudah terjadi tapi state lokal rollback.
```

Lebih aman untuk:

| Operation | Retry Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| GET/read downstream | Ya |
| Idempotent PUT/upsert | Bisa |
| Payment capture | Hanya dengan idempotency key |
| Email/SMS send | Hati-hati, bisa duplicate |
| DB deadlock/serialization failure | Bisa, di boundary yang benar |
| Kafka produce | Bisa dengan producer config/idempotency |

---

# 14. Self-Invocation Limitation

Ini salah satu bug paling sering.

```java id="b1zo89"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    public void approveFromApi(String caseId) {
        approveCase(caseId); // self-invocation
    }

    @TransactionalBoundary
    @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
    public void approveCase(String caseId) {
```
