```java id="b1zo89"
        // business logic
    }
}
```

Masalah:

```text id="kr8yz7"
approveFromApi() memanggil approveCase() dari object yang sama.
Call ini tidak melewati CDI proxy.
Akibatnya interceptor pada approveCase() bisa tidak jalan.
```

Solusi desain:

## Option A - Pecah ke bean lain

```java id="imqi1y"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseApiService {

    private final CaseApprovalService approvalService;

    @Inject
    public CaseApiService(CaseApprovalService approvalService) {
        this.approvalService = approvalService;
    }

    public void approveFromApi(String caseId) {
        approvalService.approveCase(caseId);
    }
}
```

```java id="wmt7er"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseApprovalService {

    @TransactionalBoundary
    @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
    public void approveCase(String caseId) {
        // business logic
    }
}
```

## Option B - Put interceptor on outer public use-case method

```java id="e8azhp"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @TransactionalBoundary
    @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
    public void approveFromApi(String caseId) {
        validate(caseId);
        doApprove(caseId);
    }

    private void doApprove(String caseId) {
        // internal helper, no interceptor expected
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="2yqxuf"
Pasang interceptor di boundary method yang dipanggil dari luar bean.
Jangan bergantung pada interceptor di private/internal/self-invoked method.
```

---

# 15. Proxyability, Final Class, Final Method

CDI interceptor binding pada managed bean mengharuskan bean bisa diproxy. CDI menyebut managed bean dengan class-level/method-level interceptor binding harus merupakan proxyable bean type. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

Hindari:

```java id="osictd"
@ApplicationScoped
public final class CaseService {

    @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
    public final void approve(String caseId) {
    }
}
```

Lebih aman:

```java id="95bv4x"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
    public void approve(String caseId) {
    }
}
```

Rule praktis:

| Hindari pada intercepted bean | Alasan |
|---|---|
| `final class` | Proxy/interception bermasalah |
| `final method` | Method interception bisa tidak jalan |
| private method sebagai target interceptor | Bukan business method boundary yang dipanggil client |
| manual `new` | Bukan CDI-managed |
| static method | Tidak melalui CDI proxy |

---

# 16. Parameter dan Result Handling

`InvocationContext` memungkinkan interceptor membaca/mengubah parameter dan result. Tapi jangan sembarang modifikasi.

```java id="zralyy"
@AroundInvoke
public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
    Object[] parameters = ctx.getParameters();

    // Boleh validasi metadata, tapi hati-hati mengubah parameter.
    return ctx.proceed();
}
```

Rule:

| Action | Aman? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Baca method annotation | Ya | Umum |
| Baca parameter ID | Ya, jika tidak sensitif |
| Hash parameter untuk idempotency | Ya | Jangan simpan raw payload sensitif |
| Masking log parameter | Ya | Harus whitelist/masking |
| Mengubah parameter | Hati-hati | Bisa membuat debugging sulit |
| Mengubah return value | Hati-hati | Jangan menyembunyikan business behavior |
| Menelan exception | Hampir selalu tidak | Harus rethrow kecuali memang fallback eksplisit |

---

# 17. Exception Handling dalam Interceptor

Pattern umum:

```java id="92jynk"
try {
    Object result = ctx.proceed();
    // success side effect
    return result;
} catch (ExpectedBusinessException ex) {
    // failure side effect with domain code
    throw ex;
} catch (Exception ex) {
    // failure side effect with technical code
    throw ex;
} finally {
    // cleanup/metrics
}
```

Jangan:

```java id="6zayjd"
catch (Exception ex) {
    return null; // salah
}
```

Karena:

| Dampak | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Transaction bisa commit padahal business gagal | Exception hilang |
| API return 200/null | Salah kontrak |
| Audit success/failure salah | Outcome tidak akurat |
| Observability rusak | Error tidak terlihat |
| Retry/circuit breaker tidak aktif | Exception tidak keluar |

---

# 18. Combined Usage Example

```java id="l8768l"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseCommandService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CaseAccessPolicy accessPolicy;

    @Inject
    public CaseCommandService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            CaseAccessPolicy accessPolicy
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.accessPolicy = accessPolicy;
    }

    @Measured("case.approve.duration")
    @FeatureGate("case.approval")
    @RequiresPermission("CASE_APPROVE")
    @Idempotent(operation = "CASE_APPROVE")
    @TransactionalBoundary
    @Audited(action = "CASE_APPROVE")
    public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
        CaseRecord record = caseRepository.findByIdForUpdate(caseId);

        accessPolicy.assertCanApprove(record);

        record.approve(command.comment());

        caseRepository.update(record);

        return ApprovalResult.from(record);
    }
}
```

Expected chain:

```text id="pf3fis"
Metrics
  └── FeatureGate
      └── Permission
          └── Idempotency
              └── Transaction
                  └── Audit
                      └── approveCase()
```

Tapi audit inside/outside transaction perlu diputuskan. Kalau audit harus committed bersama business state, lebih baik audit write dilakukan di repository/outbox dalam transaction, bukan remote audit call di interceptor.

---

# 19. Testing Strategy

## 19.1 Unit Test Business Service Tanpa Interceptor

```java id="9ji8vi"
@Test
void approveCase_shouldUpdateRecord() {
    CaseRepository repository = new FakeCaseRepository();
    CaseAccessPolicy policy = new AllowAllCaseAccessPolicy();

    CaseCommandService service = new CaseCommandService(repository, policy);

    ApprovalResult result = service.approveCase("CASE-001", new ApproveCommand("ok"));

    assertEquals("APPROVED", result.status());
}
```

Ini menguji business logic, bukan interceptor.

## 19.2 CDI Integration Test untuk Interceptor

Yang perlu dibuktikan:

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| `@Measured` terpanggil saat method dipanggil dari CDI bean | Membuktikan proxy aktif |
| `@Audited` menulis success/failure | Membuktikan outcome benar |
| `@RequiresPermission` reject user tanpa permission | Security guard aktif |
| `@TransactionalBoundary` commit saat success | Transaction aktif |
| `@TransactionalBoundary` rollback saat exception | Failure path benar |
| self-invocation tidak diandalkan | Desain tidak keliru |
| priority ordering benar | Chain sesuai convention |

Pseudo test:

```java id="7s6zre"
@Test
void auditedMethod_shouldWriteAuditEvent() {
    CaseCommandService service = container.select(CaseCommandService.class).get();

    service.approveCase("CASE-001", new ApproveCommand("ok"));

    assertThat(auditSink.events())
            .anyMatch(event -> event.action().equals("CASE_APPROVE")
                    && event.outcome().equals("SUCCESS"));
}
```

---

# 20. Observability untuk Interceptor

Saat production incident, interceptor yang “tersembunyi” bisa membuat debugging sulit. Tambahkan observability ringan.

Startup log sanitized:

```text id="spk2r8"
Enabled CDI interceptors:
  - MetricsInterceptor priority=2000
  - FeatureGateInterceptor priority=2100
  - PermissionInterceptor priority=2200
  - IdempotencyInterceptor priority=2300
  - TransactionInterceptor priority=2400
  - AuditInterceptor priority=2500
```

Metric/log yang berguna:

| Signal | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Method duration | `case.approve.duration` |
| Transaction rollback count | `transaction.rollback.count` |
| Permission deny count | `authorization.denied.count` |
| Feature disabled count | `feature_gate.disabled.count` |
| Idempotency replay count | `idempotency.replay.count` |
| Audit write failure count | `audit.write.failure.count` |
| Interceptor exception count | `interceptor.failure.count` |

Jangan log:

- raw token
- password/secret
- full request body sensitif
- full PII tanpa masking
- tenant data lintas boundary

---

# 21. Anti-pattern

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Semua logic bisnis masuk interceptor | `@ApproveCase` melakukan business update | Behavior tersembunyi |
| Interceptor dipakai untuk HTTP concern | Baca raw `ContainerRequestContext` di service interceptor | Layer bocor |
| Self-invocation | Method annotated dipanggil dari class yang sama | Interceptor tidak jalan |
| Manual object creation | `new CaseCommandService()` | Interceptor tidak aktif |
| Same priority semua interceptor | Urutan undefined | Bug intermittent |
| Audit remote call sebelum commit | Audit success padahal DB rollback | Compliance mismatch |
| Transaction interceptor swallow exception | Commit salah | Data corruption |
| Permission hanya di annotation | Tidak cek resource-specific policy | Access control lemah |
| Retry non-idempotent operation | Duplicate side effect | Payment/email duplicate |
| Logging parameter mentah | PII/secret leak | Security incident |
| Annotation member tanpa `@Nonbinding` | Interceptor tidak match | Silent missing behavior |

---

# 22. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan Review |
|---|---|
| Binding | Apakah annotation pakai `@InterceptorBinding` dan target `TYPE/METHOD`? |
| Members | Apakah annotation member yang hanya metadata diberi `@Nonbinding`? |
| Enablement | Apakah interceptor punya `@Priority` atau diaktifkan via `beans.xml`? |
| Ordering | Apakah priority tidak sama sembarangan? |
| Proxy | Apakah target bean CDI-managed dan proxyable? |
| Self-invocation | Apakah annotated method dipanggil dari luar bean? |
| Exception | Apakah exception di-rethrow? |
| Transaction | Apakah commit/rollback jelas? |
| Audit | Apakah audit outcome konsisten dengan transaction semantics? |
| Security | Apakah annotation permission bukan satu-satunya resource-level check? |
| Metrics | Apakah tag tidak high-cardinality/PII? |
| Idempotency | Apakah ada DB unique constraint? |
| Retry | Apakah hanya untuk operation aman/idempotent? |
| Testing | Apakah ada CDI integration test, bukan hanya unit test? |

---

# 23. Roadmap Latihan Praktis Seri 6

Urutan latihan paling efektif:

```text id="4yha4z"
1. Buat @Measured + MetricsInterceptor
2. Tambahkan priority convention
3. Buat @Audited + AuditInterceptor
4. Inject CurrentRequestContext ke interceptor
5. Test audit success/failure
6. Buat @RequiresPermission + PermissionInterceptor
7. Tambahkan service-level CaseAccessPolicy
8. Buat @TransactionalBoundary + SqlSessionProvider
9. Test commit/rollback
10. Buat @FeatureGate
11. Buat @Idempotent untuk command endpoint
12. Buat test self-invocation failure agar tim paham batasannya
13. Tambahkan startup log daftar interceptor + priority
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 6

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. CDI interceptor cocok untuk **business method cross-cutting concern**, bukan semua request/HTTP concern.
2. JAX-RS filter cocok untuk header, auth bootstrap, correlation, tenant resolution, response header, dan endpoint-level request pipeline.
3. CDI interceptor butuh `@InterceptorBinding`, `@Interceptor`, `@AroundInvoke`, dan enablement seperti `@Priority`.
4. `InvocationContext.proceed()` wajib dipanggil kecuali interceptor memang sengaja menghentikan invocation.
5. Interceptor ordering harus eksplisit; priority lebih kecil dipanggil lebih awal.
6. Annotation member yang hanya metadata seperti `action`, `permission`, `flagKey`, `operation` biasanya perlu `@Nonbinding`.
7. `@Audited`, `@Measured`, `@RequiresPermission`, `@FeatureGate`, `@TransactionalBoundary`, dan `@Idempotent` adalah pattern praktis yang relevan untuk microservice regulatory/case management.
8. Jangan mengandalkan interceptor pada self-invocation, private method, static method, final method, object manual `new`, atau bean tidak proxyable.
9. Transaction boundary harus jelas: repository join transaction, service method menjadi unit-of-work, exception menyebabkan rollback.
10. Audit semantics harus diputuskan: dalam transaction, outbox, atau external audit sink.
11. Retry hanya aman untuk operation idempotent atau yang punya idempotency key.
12. Interceptor harus dites dengan CDI integration test, bukan hanya unit test biasa.

**Seri 6/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 7 - Context Propagation: Correlation ID, Request Context, User, Security, Tenant**, dengan fokus pada bagaimana context dibuat dari HTTP request, disimpan di CDI request scope, dipropagate ke service/downstream, dan tidak bocor ke async/thread/workflow.

---

**Sources:**

- [Jakarta Interceptors](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/interceptors/2.2/jakarta-interceptors-spec-2.2)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 4:10:51 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 7/14**. Fokusnya: context propagation end-to-end—dari HTTP request ke CDI context, lalu ke service, log, DB/audit, HTTP/gRPC/Kafka downstream, async thread, dan Camunda workflow tanpa context leak.

# Seri 7/14 - Context Propagation: Correlation ID, Request Context, User, Security, Tenant

Target seri ini: kamu bisa mendesain **context propagation** yang aman dan konsisten di aplikasi **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Camunda + HTTP/gRPC/Kafka**.

Intinya:

> Context propagation adalah mekanisme membawa metadata runtime yang relevan dari satu boundary ke boundary lain: HTTP request → CDI context → service → repository/audit/log → downstream HTTP/gRPC/Kafka → async thread → workflow/process.

Yang perlu dijaga:

1. **context harus cukup lengkap untuk tracing/audit/security**
2. **tidak boleh membawa data sensitif sembarangan**
3. **tidak boleh bocor antar request/thread/tenant**
4. **tidak boleh bergantung pada HTTP request context di async/job/workflow**
5. **harus punya contract header/metadata yang konsisten antar service**

CDI `@RequestScoped` memang dirancang untuk bean yang hidup dalam request context; Jakarta CDI mendefinisikan CDI sebagai mekanisme untuk memperoleh object agar lebih reusable, testable, dan maintainable, sementara `@RequestScoped` adalah normal scope untuk request context. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Context propagation bukan berarti “semua header diteruskan ke semua service”.

Model yang benar:

```text
Inbound Boundary
  └── normalize + validate raw metadata
        └── build trusted application context
              └── use inside service
                    └── selectively propagate safe context outward
```

Jadi ada perbedaan besar antara:

```text
Raw Context
  = header/token/body/request metadata mentah dari luar

Trusted Context
  = data yang sudah divalidasi dan dinormalisasi

Propagation Context
  = subset trusted context yang aman diteruskan ke downstream
```

Contoh:

| Data | Raw Source | Trusted Context? | Boleh Dipropagate? | Catatan |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Header | Ya setelah validasi format | Ya | Untuk trace/log/audit |
| `traceparent` | Header | Ya jika format valid | Ya | W3C Trace Context mendefinisikan `traceparent` sebagai header interoperable antar tracing system. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| `tenantId` | JWT/header/gateway | Ya setelah validasi principal/tenant | Ya, terbatas | Jangan percaya header publik mentah |
| `userId` | Token/session | Ya setelah auth | Kadang | Tergantung trust boundary |
| raw JWT | Authorization header | Tidak sebagai context umum | Biasanya tidak | Jangan forward token ke service yang tidak perlu |
| role/permission | Token/policy | Ya setelah mapping | Kadang | Biasanya service internal cukup perlu user/tenant/scope |
| DB transaction | Runtime lokal | Ya lokal | Tidak | Tidak boleh melintasi service/thread boundary |
| `SqlSession` | MyBatis | Ya lokal | Tidak | Unit-of-work lokal |
| secret | Vault/env/K8s | Tidak untuk request context | Tidak | Jangan taruh di context/log/process variable |
| business key | Domain input | Ya setelah validasi | Ya | Cocok untuk workflow correlation |

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Context Propagation Map
│
├── 1. Inbound HTTP Context
│   ├── correlation ID
│   ├── traceparent/tracestate
│   ├── tenant ID
│   ├── user/principal
│   ├── roles/scopes
│   ├── locale
│   ├── idempotency key
│   └── client/app identity
│
├── 2. CDI Request Context
│   ├── CurrentRequestContext
│   ├── CurrentUser
│   ├── TenantContext
│   ├── CorrelationContext
│   ├── SecurityContext bridge
│   └── RequestContextSnapshot
│
├── 3. Service Context Usage
│   ├── authorization policy
│   ├── tenant-aware query
│   ├── audit event
│   ├── metrics/logging tags
│   ├── feature flag evaluation
│   └── idempotency scope
│
├── 4. Downstream Propagation
│   ├── HTTP headers
│   ├── gRPC metadata
│   ├── Kafka headers
│   ├── Camunda variables
│   └── audit/outbox event metadata
│
├── 5. Async / Thread Context
│   ├── immutable snapshot
│   ├── executor wrapper
│   ├── ThreadLocal/MDC setup
│   ├── cleanup/finally
│   └── no raw request-scoped bean
│
├── 6. Workflow / Process Context
│   ├── business key
│   ├── process variables
│   ├── tenant
│   ├── initiator
│   ├── correlation ID
│   └── message correlation key
│
└── 7. Failure Modes
    ├── tenant spoofing
    ├── context leak
    ├── lost correlation
    ├── wrong async context
    ├── raw token propagation
    ├── transaction boundary confusion
    └── workflow context mismatch
```

---

# 3. Context Types

| Context Type | Scope | Source | Owner | Propagation Target |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Application context | App lifetime | Config/profile/runtime | Config layer | Usually not per request |
| HTTP request context | Per request | Headers/path/JWT | JAX-RS filter | CDI request context |
| Security context | Per request | Token/session/mTLS | Auth filter | Service policy, JAX-RS `SecurityContext` |
| Tenant context | Per request/job/process | JWT/gateway/domain | Tenant resolver | DB query, audit, downstream |
| Correlation context | Per request/message/job | Header/generated | Correlation filter | Log, response, downstream |
| Trace context | Per distributed trace | `traceparent`/tracing SDK | tracing layer | HTTP/gRPC/Kafka |
| Transaction context | Per local unit-of-work | Transaction interceptor | persistence layer | Local only |
| Async context | Per task | Snapshot | executor wrapper | Worker thread |
| Workflow context | Per process instance | process variables/business key | workflow engine/app | Camunda delegates/messages |
| Feature evaluation context | Per evaluation | tenant/user/env | feature flag service | Flag provider |

---

# 4. Canonical Request Context Model

Buat satu model context yang jelas. Jangan biarkan service membaca header mentah.

```java
public record RequestContextSnapshot(
        String correlationId,
        String traceParent,
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles,
        Locale locale,
        Optional<String> idempotencyKey
) {
    public RequestContextSnapshot {
        roles = Set.copyOf(roles);
        idempotencyKey = idempotencyKey == null ? Optional.empty() : idempotencyKey;
    }
}
```

CDI request-scoped bean:

```java
import jakarta.enterprise.context.RequestScoped;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;

@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {

    private String correlationId;
    private String traceParent;
    private String tenantId;
    private String userId;
    private Set<String> roles = Set.of();
    private Locale locale = Locale.ENGLISH;
    private String idempotencyKey;

    public void initialize(
            String correlationId,
            String traceParent,
            String tenantId,
            String userId,
            Set<String> roles,
            Locale locale
    ) {
        this.correlationId = require(correlationId, "correlationId");
        this.traceParent = traceParent;
        this.tenantId = require(tenantId, "tenantId");
        this.userId = require(userId, "userId");
        this.roles = Set.copyOf(roles);
        this.locale = locale == null ? Locale.ENGLISH : locale;
    }

    public String correlationId() {
        return require(correlationId, "correlationId");
    }

    public Optional<String> traceParent() {
        return Optional.ofNullable(traceParent).filter(value -> !value.isBlank());
    }

    public String tenantId() {
        return require(tenantId, "tenantId");
    }

    public String userId() {
        return require(userId, "userId");
    }

    public Set<String> roles() {
        return roles;
    }

    public Locale locale() {
        return locale;
    }

    public Optional<String> idempotencyKey() {
        return Optional.ofNullable(idempotencyKey).filter(value -> !value.isBlank());
    }

    public void setIdempotencyKey(String idempotencyKey) {
        this.idempotencyKey = idempotencyKey;
    }

    public boolean hasRole(String role) {
        return roles.contains(role);
    }

    public RequestContextSnapshot snapshot() {
        return new RequestContextSnapshot(
                correlationId(),
                traceParent().orElse(null),
                tenantId(),
                userId(),
                roles(),
                locale(),
                idempotencyKey()
        );
    }

    private static String require(String value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Request context missing: " + name);
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

Practical invariant:

```text
CurrentRequestContext berisi trusted context.
Tidak boleh berisi raw Authorization header, raw JWT, secret, password, DB connection, SqlSession, atau ContainerRequestContext.
```

---

# 5. Inbound HTTP Context Bootstrap

Context harus dibangun di request pipeline.

```text
HTTP Request
  ├── CorrelationFilter
  ├── AuthenticationFilter
  ├── TenantResolver
  ├── LocaleResolver
  ├── IdempotencyKeyFilter
  └── CurrentRequestContext.initialize(...)
```

JAX-RS `ContainerRequestFilter` adalah extension point untuk memproses inbound request; Jakarta RESTful Web Services mendefinisikan API RESTful service, provider, filters, interceptors, dan exception mapping. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh filter gabungan:

```java
import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.ws.rs.BadRequestException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.UUID;

@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class RequestContextBootstrapFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final String CORRELATION_HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";
    private static final String TRACEPARENT_HEADER = "traceparent";
    private static final String IDEMPOTENCY_HEADER = "Idempotency-Key";

    private final TokenVerifier tokenVerifier;
    private final TenantResolver tenantResolver;
    private final CurrentRequestContext currentRequestContext;

    @Inject
    public RequestContextBootstrapFilter(
            TokenVerifier tokenVerifier,
            TenantResolver tenantResolver,
            CurrentRequestContext currentRequestContext
    ) {
        this.tokenVerifier = tokenVerifier;
        this.tenantResolver = tenantResolver;
        this.currentRequestContext = currentRequestContext;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String correlationId = resolveCorrelationId(request);
        String traceParent = resolveTraceParent(request);

        AuthenticatedPrincipal principal =
                tokenVerifier.verify(request.getHeaderString("Authorization"));

        String tenantId = tenantResolver.resolve(request, principal);
        Locale locale = resolveLocale(request);

        currentRequestContext.initialize(
                correlationId,
                traceParent,
                tenantId,
                principal.userId(),
                principal.roles(),
                locale
        );

        String idempotencyKey = request.getHeaderString(IDEMPOTENCY_HEADER);
        if (idempotencyKey != null && !idempotencyKey.isBlank()) {
            currentRequestContext.setIdempotencyKey(validateIdempotencyKey(idempotencyKey));
        }

        request.setProperty("correlationId", correlationId);
        request.setProperty("tenantId", tenantId);
        request.setProperty("userId", principal.userId());
    }

    private String resolveCorrelationId(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String value = request.getHeaderString(CORRELATION_HEADER);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        if (value.length() > 128 || !value.matches("[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+")) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Invalid correlation ID");
        }

        return value;
    }

    private String resolveTraceParent(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String value = request.getHeaderString(TRACEPARENT_HEADER);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }

        // Minimal validation. Production should delegate to tracing library if available.
        if (value.length() > 256) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Invalid traceparent");
        }

        return value;
    }

    private Locale resolveLocale(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        String acceptLanguage = request.getHeaderString("Accept-Language");

        if (acceptLanguage == null || acceptLanguage.isBlank()) {
            return Locale.ENGLISH;
        }

        return Locale.LanguageRange.parse(acceptLanguage)
                .stream()
                .findFirst()
                .map(range -> Locale.forLanguageTag(range.getRange()))
                .orElse(Locale.ENGLISH);
    }

    private String validateIdempotencyKey(String value) {
        if (value.length() > 128 || !value.matches("[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+")) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Invalid Idempotency-Key");
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

---

# 6. Correlation ID vs Trace ID

Jangan mencampur semuanya menjadi satu konsep.

| Concept | Purpose | Source | Common Carrier |
|---|---|---|---|
| Correlation ID | Business/application-level request tracking | Generated/app header | `X-Correlation-Id` |
| Trace ID | Distributed tracing | Tracing system | `traceparent` |
| Span ID | Segment of trace | Tracing system | `traceparent` |
| Causation ID | Event/message cause chain | App/event system | Kafka/event header |
| Business key | Domain/process correlation | Domain | Camunda business key/process variable |

W3C Trace Context mendefinisikan `traceparent` dan `tracestate` untuk propagasi tracing lintas vendor; `traceparent` membawa informasi trace yang dipahami oleh tracing system berbeda. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

```text
Gunakan traceparent untuk distributed tracing.
Gunakan X-Correlation-Id untuk aplikasi/log/audit/business debugging.
Gunakan businessKey untuk workflow/process correlation.
```

Response filter:

```java
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class ContextResponseFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            ContainerResponseContext response
    ) {
        Object correlationId = request.getProperty("correlationId");

        if (correlationId instanceof String value && !value.isBlank()) {
            response.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Correlation-Id", value);
        }

        // Usually tracing libraries handle trace response behavior.
        // Do not invent traceparent response semantics unless company standard requires it.
    }
}
```

---

# 7. Tenant Context: Security-Critical

Tenant context harus dianggap security boundary.

```text
Bad:
  tenantId = request.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id")

Better:
  tenantId = validate(requestedTenant, authenticatedPrincipal.allowedTenants)
```

Tenant resolver:

```java
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ForbiddenException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;

@ApplicationScoped
public class TenantResolver {

    public String resolve(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            AuthenticatedPrincipal principal
    ) {
        String requestedTenant = request.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");

        if (requestedTenant == null || requestedTenant.isBlank()) {
            return principal.defaultTenantId();
        }

        if (!principal.allowedTenantIds().contains(requestedTenant)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Tenant is not allowed for current principal");
        }

        return requestedTenant;
    }
}
```

Database usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final CaseRepository repository;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            CaseRepository repository
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.repository = repository;
    }

    public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
        return repository.findByTenantAndCaseId(
                context.tenantId(),
                caseId
        );
    }
}
```

Repository harus tenant-aware:

```java
public interface CaseRepository {
    CaseDto findByTenantAndCaseId(String tenantId, String caseId);
}
```

Anti-pattern:

```java
public CaseDto findByCaseId(String caseId) {
    // SALAH untuk multi-tenant case management
}
```

Untuk regulatory/case platform, query tanpa tenant boundary adalah red flag besar.

---

# 8. User/Security Context

JAX-RS punya `SecurityContext` untuk principal dan role di request. Jakarta REST API mendefinisikan `SecurityContext` sebagai akses ke informasi security request, termasuk `getUserPrincipal()` dan `isUserInRole(...)`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Namun service layer sebaiknya tidak bergantung langsung pada `ContainerRequestContext` atau raw JAX-RS object.

Pattern:

```text
JAX-RS SecurityContext
  = compatibility untuk resource/filter/JAX-RS ecosystem

CDI CurrentRequestContext / CurrentUser
  = service-layer context abstraction
```

Principal:

```java
public record AuthenticatedPrincipal(
        String userId,
        String subject,
        String defaultTenantId,
        Set<String> allowedTenantIds,
        Set<String> roles,
        Set<String> scopes
) {
    public AuthenticatedPrincipal {
        allowedTenantIds = Set.copyOf(allowedTenantIds);
        roles = Set.copyOf(roles);
        scopes = Set.copyOf(scopes);
    }
}
```

Service-level policy:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseAccessPolicy {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseAccessPolicy(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    public void assertCanRead(CaseRecord record) {
        if (!record.tenantId().equals(context.tenantId())) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Cross-tenant access denied");
        }

        if (!context.hasRole("CASE_READER")) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Missing role: CASE_READER");
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Logging Context / MDC Pattern

Banyak Java logging stack mendukung MDC/thread-local diagnostic context. Tujuannya: setiap log line otomatis membawa metadata seperti correlation ID, tenant ID, user ID.

Pattern:

```text
Request start:
  MDC.put(correlationId, tenantId, userId)
Request end:
  MDC.clear()
Async:
  capture snapshot → restore MDC in worker → clear finally
```

Filter:

```java
@Provider
public class LoggingContextFilter
        implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public LoggingContextFilter(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        // This filter must run after CurrentRequestContext has been initialized.
        MdcContext.put(context.snapshot());
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            ContainerResponseContext response
    ) {
        MdcContext.clear();
    }
}
```

Helper:

```java
public final class MdcContext {

    private MdcContext() {
    }

    public static void put(RequestContextSnapshot snapshot) {
        MDC.put("correlationId", snapshot.correlationId());
        MDC.put("tenantId", snapshot.tenantId());
        MDC.put("userId", snapshot.userId());
        snapshot.traceParent().ifPresent(value -> MDC.put("traceparent", value));
    }

    public static void clear() {
        MDC.clear();
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
MDC/ThreadLocal harus selalu clear.
Di thread pool, lupa clear berarti log request berikutnya bisa memakai context request lama.
```

---

# 10. HTTP Downstream Propagation

Saat service A memanggil service B via HTTP, propagate subset context yang aman.

Headers yang umum:

```text
X-Correlation-Id
traceparent
tracestate
X-Tenant-Id        internal/trusted only
X-User-Id          internal/trusted only, if needed
Idempotency-Key    only for command propagation when semantically correct
```

HTTP client wrapper:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ContextPropagatingHttpClient {

    private final HttpClient httpClient;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public ContextPropagatingHttpClient(
            HttpClient httpClient,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public HttpResponse<String> send(HttpRequest.Builder builder)
            throws IOException, InterruptedException {

        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        builder.header("X-Correlation-Id", snapshot.correlationId());
        builder.header("X-Tenant-Id", snapshot.tenantId());

        snapshot.traceParent()
                .ifPresent(value -> builder.header("traceparent", value));

        HttpRequest request = builder.build();
        return httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
    }
}
```

Security warning:

```text
Jangan forward Authorization header mentah secara default.
Gunakan token exchange, service credential, mTLS, atau internal auth mechanism sesuai standar perusahaan.
```

---

# 11. gRPC Metadata Propagation

gRPC metadata adalah key-value data yang dikirim bersama request/response RPC dan dapat dipakai untuk auth credentials, tracing information, atau custom headers. ([gRPC](https://grpc.io/docs/guides/metadata/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Conceptual mapping:

| App Context | gRPC Metadata Key |
|---|---|
| correlation ID | `x-correlation-id` |
| traceparent | `traceparent` |
| tenant ID | `x-tenant-id` |
| user ID | `x-user-id` internal only |
| service identity | auth metadata/mTLS/service credential |

Client interceptor pattern:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class GrpcContextClientInterceptor implements ClientInterceptor {

    private static final Metadata.Key<String> CORRELATION_ID =
            Metadata.Key.of("x-correlation-id", Metadata.ASCII_STRING_MARSHALLER);

    private static final Metadata.Key<String> TENANT_ID =
            Metadata.Key.of("x-tenant-id", Metadata.ASCII_STRING_MARSHALLER);

    private static final Metadata.Key<String> TRACEPARENT =
            Metadata.Key.of("traceparent", Metadata.ASCII_STRING_MARSHALLER);

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public GrpcContextClientInterceptor(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    @Override
    public <ReqT, RespT> ClientCall<ReqT, RespT> interceptCall(
            MethodDescriptor<ReqT, RespT> method,
            CallOptions callOptions,
            Channel next
    ) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        return new ForwardingClientCall.SimpleForwardingClientCall<>(
                next.newCall(method, callOptions)
        ) {
            @Override
            public void start(Listener<RespT> responseListener, Metadata headers) {
                headers.put(CORRELATION_ID, snapshot.correlationId());
                headers.put(TENANT_ID, snapshot.tenantId());
                snapshot.traceParent().ifPresent(value -> headers.put(TRACEPARENT, value));
                super.start(responseListener, headers);
            }
        };
    }
}
```

Server-side interceptor should do the inverse: read metadata → validate → build trusted context.

---

# 12. Kafka Header Propagation

Kafka records support headers; Apache Kafka’s `Headers` API exposes methods like `add`, `lastHeader`, `headers`, `remove`, and `toArray`. ([Apache Kafka](https://kafka.apache.org/0110/javadoc/org/apache/kafka/common/header/Headers.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Producer mapping:

| App Context | Kafka Header |
|---|---|
| correlation ID | `x-correlation-id` |
| traceparent | `traceparent` |
| tenant ID | `x-tenant-id` |
| causation ID | `x-causation-id` |
| message ID | `x-message-id` |
| schema version | `x-schema-version` |
| producer service | `x-producer-service` |

Producer helper:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class KafkaContextHeaders {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public KafkaContextHeaders(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    public void applyTo(ProducerRecord<String, byte[]> record) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        add(record, "x-correlation-id", snapshot.correlationId());
        add(record, "x-tenant-id", snapshot.tenantId());
        add(record, "x-user-id", snapshot.userId());

        snapshot.traceParent()
                .ifPresent(value -> add(record, "traceparent", value));
    }

    private void add(ProducerRecord<String, byte[]> record, String key, String value) {
        if (value != null && !value.isBlank()) {
            record.headers().add(key, value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
    }
}
```

Consumer side:

```java
public record MessageContextSnapshot(
        String correlationId,
        String tenantId,
        String traceParent,
        String messageId,
        String causationId
) {
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class KafkaMessageContextExtractor {

    public MessageContextSnapshot extract(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record) {
        return new MessageContextSnapshot(
                header(record, "x-correlation-id").orElse(UUID.randomUUID().toString()),
                header(record, "x-tenant-id").orElseThrow(() ->
                        new IllegalStateException("Missing tenant header")),
                header(record, "traceparent").orElse(null),
                header(record, "x-message-id").orElse(null),
                header(record, "x-causation-id").orElse(null)
        );
    }

    private Optional<String> header(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record, String key) {
        Header header = record.headers().lastHeader(key);

        if (header == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        return Optional.of(new String(header.value(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }
}
```

Kafka-specific rule:

```text
Kafka consumer tidak berada dalam HTTP request context.
Jangan inject CurrentRequestContext kecuali kamu membuat message/job scope sendiri.
Gunakan MessageContextSnapshot eksplisit.
```

---

# 13. Async / Multi-thread Context Propagation

Masalah klasik:

```java
executor.submit(() -> {
    context.tenantId(); // bisa gagal, atau lebih buruk: membaca context salah
});
```

Karena `@RequestScoped` context dan MDC/ThreadLocal biasanya terkait thread/request. MicroProfile Context Propagation menyediakan model seperti `ManagedExecutor` dan `ThreadContext` agar thread context propagation bisa dilakukan secara type-safe dan mengurangi boilerplate; spesifikasinya juga membedakan executor yang propagate context dan yang tidak untuk alasan performa. ([MicroProfile](https://microprofile.io/specifications/microprofile-context-propagation/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Kalau belum memakai MicroProfile Context Propagation, buat wrapper eksplisit.

## Context Snapshot

```java
public record RequestContextSnapshot(
        String correlationId,
        String traceParent,
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles,
        Locale locale,
        Optional<String> idempotencyKey
) {
}
```

## Context-aware Runnable

```java
public final class ContextAwareRunnable implements Runnable {

    private final RequestContextSnapshot snapshot;
    private final Runnable delegate;

    public ContextAwareRunnable(
            RequestContextSnapshot snapshot,
            Runnable delegate
    ) {
        this.snapshot = snapshot;
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        try {
            MdcContext.put(snapshot);
            delegate.run();
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

## Executor Wrapper

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ContextAwareExecutor {

    private final ExecutorService executorService;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public ContextAwareExecutor(
            ExecutorService executorService,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.executorService = executorService;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public Future<?> submit(Runnable task) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();
        return executorService.submit(new ContextAwareRunnable(snapshot, task));
    }
}
```

But note:

```text
Ini hanya propagate log/context snapshot.
Ini tidak mengaktifkan CDI @RequestScoped di worker thread.
Jangan akses CurrentRequestContext langsung dari worker.
```

Worker should receive explicit snapshot:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncCaseWorker {

    public void process(String caseId, RequestContextSnapshot snapshot) {
        // Use snapshot explicitly.
        String tenantId = snapshot.tenantId();
        String correlationId = snapshot.correlationId();

        // Do work with tenant/correlation.
    }
}
```

---

# 14. Transaction Context Is Local Only

Transaction context tidak boleh dipropagate ke HTTP/gRPC/Kafka.

```text
Local transaction context:
  - SqlSession
  - JDBC Connection
  - rollbackOnly flag
  - transaction depth
  - lock state

Never propagate:
  - DB connection
  - SqlSession
  - transaction object
  - transaction ThreadLocal
```

Yang boleh dipropagate:

```text
correlation ID
tenant ID
business key
causation ID
idempotency key if command semantics require it
```

Untuk MyBatis:

```java
@RequestScoped
public class SqlSessionProvider {
    // local unit-of-work only
}
```

Untuk event/outbox:

```java
public record OutboxEvent(
        String eventId,
        String aggregateId,
        String tenantId,
        String correlationId,
        String causationId,
        String eventType,
        String payloadJson
) {
}
```

Business transaction writes outbox with context:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
    CaseRecord record = repository.findByIdForUpdate(caseId);
    record.approve(command.comment());
    repository.update(record);

    RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

    outboxRepository.insert(new OutboxEvent(
            UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
            caseId,
            snapshot.tenantId(),
            snapshot.correlationId(),
            command.commandId(),
            "CASE_APPROVED",
            payloadJson(record)
    ));

    return ApprovalResult.from(record);
}
```

---

# 15. Camunda Workflow Context

Workflow/process context tidak sama dengan HTTP request context.

Camunda 7 business key adalah domain-specific identifier untuk process instance; Camunda menjelaskan business key sebagai identifier domain yang menghubungkan process instance ke entity bisnis seperti order/case ID. ([Camunda](https://camunda.com/blog/2018/10/business-key/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Saat start process:

```text
HTTP Request
  ├── tenantId
  ├── userId
  ├── correlationId
  ├── caseId
  └── commandId
        ↓
Start Camunda process
  ├── businessKey = caseId or caseId:workflowType
  └── process variables:
      ├── tenantId
      ├── initiatorUserId
      ├── correlationId
      ├── commandId
      └── caseId
```

Example:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseWorkflowService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final RuntimeService runtimeService;

    @Inject
    public CaseWorkflowService(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            RuntimeService runtimeService
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.runtimeService = runtimeService;
    }

    public String startApprovalWorkflow(String caseId) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        Map<String, Object> variables = Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "tenantId", snapshot.tenantId(),
                "initiatorUserId", snapshot.userId(),
                "correlationId", snapshot.correlationId()
        );

        ProcessInstance instance = runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                "case_approval",
                caseId,
                variables
        );

        return instance.getProcessInstanceId();
    }
}
```

Camunda delegate/job:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class NotifyApprovalDelegate implements JavaDelegate {

    @Override
    public void execute(DelegateExecution execution) {
        String caseId = (String) execution.getVariable("caseId");
        String tenantId = (String) execution.getVariable("tenantId");
        String correlationId = (String) execution.getVariable("correlationId");

        // Use workflow context, not HTTP request context.
    }
}
```

For Camunda 8/Zeebe, message correlation targets a running workflow asynchronously; Camunda 8 docs describe message correlation as targeting a running workflow with a state update from an external system. ([Camunda Docs](https://unsupported.docs.camunda.io/8.1/docs/guides/message-correlation/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

```text
HTTP request context dies after response.
Workflow context must live as process variables/business key.
Never store raw JWT, password, secret, or large request payload as process variable.
```

---

# 16. Context Propagation Contract

A good microservice platform should define standard context keys.

## HTTP Headers

| Header | Required? | Direction | Notes |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Yes | inbound/outbound | Generate if missing |
| `traceparent` | Recommended | inbound/outbound | Use tracing library if available |
| `tracestate` | Optional | inbound/outbound | Preserve per tracing policy |
| `X-Tenant-Id` | Internal/trusted | inbound/outbound | Validate before trust |
| `X-User-Id` | Internal only | outbound | Avoid for public APIs |
| `Idempotency-Key` | Command only | inbound | Tenant + operation scoped |
| `Accept-Language` | Optional | inbound | For locale |
| `Authorization` | Yes for protected endpoint | inbound | Do not forward blindly |

## Kafka Headers

| Header | Required? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| `x-message-id` | Yes | Unique event/message ID |
| `x-correlation-id` | Yes | Trace chain |
| `x-causation-id` | Recommended | Previous command/event ID |
| `x-tenant-id` | Yes for multi-tenant | Required for safe processing |
| `traceparent` | Recommended | Distributed tracing |
| `x-schema-version` | Yes | Consumer compatibility |
| `x-producer-service` | Recommended | Debuggability |

## Camunda Variables

| Variable | Required? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| `businessKey` | Strongly recommended | Domain correlation |
| `caseId` / aggregate ID | Yes | Domain entity |
| `tenantId` | Yes for multi-tenant | Isolation/audit |
| `initiatorUserId` | Recommended | Audit |
| `correlationId` | Recommended | Trace/log |
| `commandId` | Recommended | Idempotency/causation |
| `locale` | Optional | User/task communication |

---

# 17. Context Propagation to Audit

Audit event should include trusted context, not raw request.

```java
public record AuditEvent(
        String auditId,
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        String correlationId,
        String action,
        String targetType,
        String targetId,
        String outcome,
        Instant occurredAt
) {
}
```

Audit writer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AuditService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final AuditRepository repository;

    @Inject
    public AuditService(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            Clock clock,
            AuditRepository repository
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.repository = repository;
    }

    public void success(String action, String targetType, String targetId) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        repository.insert(new AuditEvent(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                snapshot.tenantId(),
                snapshot.userId(),
                snapshot.correlationId(),
                action,
                targetType,
                targetId,
                "SUCCESS",
                Instant.now(clock)
        ));
    }
}
```

Audit invariant:

```text
Audit context harus berasal dari trusted context.
Audit tidak boleh mengambil tenant/user dari parameter bebas tanpa validasi.
```

---

# 18. Context Propagation to Feature Flags

Feature flag evaluation needs context.

```java
public record FeatureContext(
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles,
        Locale locale
) {
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureFlagService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final FeatureFlagClient client;

    @Inject
    public FeatureFlagService(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            FeatureFlagClient client
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.client = client;
    }

    public boolean enabled(String flagKey) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        return client.evaluate(
                flagKey,
                new FeatureContext(
                        snapshot.tenantId(),
                        snapshot.userId(),
                        snapshot.roles(),
                        snapshot.locale()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Feature flag warning:

```text
Kalau flag dievaluasi tanpa tenant/user context, rollout bisa salah sasaran.
Kalau flag key tersebar tanpa owner/expiry, flag menjadi technical debt.
```

---

# 19. Context Propagation to Error Response

Error response harus memuat correlation ID.

```java
public record ErrorResponse(
        String code,
        String message,
        String correlationId
) {
}
```

Mapper:

```java
@Provider
public class GenericExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final ErrorLogger logger;

    @Inject
    public GenericExceptionMapper(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            ErrorLogger logger
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.logger = logger;
    }

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        String correlationId = safeCorrelationId();

        logger.logUnexpected(exception, correlationId);

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                        "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                        "Unexpected error",
                        correlationId
                ))
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .build();
    }

    private String safeCorrelationId() {
        try {
            return context.correlationId();
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
            return "unavailable";
        }
    }
}
```

Why safe fallback?

```text
Exception bisa terjadi sebelum CurrentRequestContext initialized.
Generic mapper tidak boleh ikut gagal karena context belum tersedia.
```

---

# 20. Anti-pattern yang Harus Dihindari

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Propagate semua inbound header | Copy all headers to downstream | Credential/PII leak |
| Percaya tenant header publik | `tenantId = X-Tenant-Id` | Tenant spoofing |
| Simpan request context di singleton field | `@ApplicationScoped private String userId` | Cross-request leak |
| Akses `@RequestScoped` di async worker | `context.tenantId()` inside executor | Context not active / wrong context |
| Forward raw JWT ke semua service | `Authorization` blindly copied | Overprivileged downstream |
| Simpan secret di process variable | `password` in Camunda variables | Security incident |
| Simpan DB transaction di context propagation | pass `SqlSession` | Broken transaction model |
| Kafka consumer pakai HTTP context | inject `CurrentRequestContext` | Context mismatch |
| Correlation ID tidak disanitasi | raw header masuk log | Log/header injection |
| Generate correlation baru di tiap layer | trace chain putus | Debugging sulit |
| Audit pakai raw user input tenant | `command.tenantId()` | Audit bisa dipalsukan |
| MDC tidak di-clear | thread pool reuse | Log salah tenant/user |

---

# 21. Failure Mode Simulation

## Case 1 - Tenant leak karena singleton mutable

Salah:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class TenantAwareService {

    private String currentTenantId;

    public void setTenant(String tenantId) {
        this.currentTenantId = tenantId;
    }

    public CaseDto get(String caseId) {
        return repository.findByTenantAndCaseId(currentTenantId, caseId);
    }
}
```

Fix:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class TenantAwareService {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public TenantAwareService(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    public CaseDto get(String caseId) {
        return repository.findByTenantAndCaseId(context.tenantId(), caseId);
    }
}
```

## Case 2 - Async loses request context

Salah:

```java
executor.submit(() -> {
    auditService.write(context.userId()); // bad
});
```

Fix:

```java
RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

executor.submit(() -> {
    auditWorker.write(snapshot);
});
```

## Case 3 - Workflow depends on HTTP request

Salah:

```java
public void execute(DelegateExecution execution) {
    String user = currentRequestContext.userId();
}
```

Fix:

```java
public void execute(DelegateExecution execution) {
    String user = (String) execution.getVariable("initiatorUserId");
}
```

---

# 22. Practical Implementation Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Correlation | Generate if missing, validate format, include in response |
| Trace | Preserve `traceparent`/`tracestate` via tracing library where possible |
| Tenant | Resolve from trusted source, validate against principal |
| User | Normalize from token/session to `AuthenticatedPrincipal` |
| CDI context | Store trusted data in `@RequestScoped CurrentRequestContext` |
| Service usage | Service reads context abstraction, not raw HTTP object |
| DB | Every tenant-aware query includes tenant boundary |
| Audit | Audit event includes tenant/user/correlation |
| HTTP downstream | Propagate only safe headers |
| gRPC | Use metadata/interceptor |
| Kafka | Use headers with message/correlation/tenant metadata |
| Async | Use immutable snapshot, not request-scoped bean |
| MDC | Put at boundary, clear in finally/response filter |
| Camunda | Store stable context as business key/process variables |
| Error response | Always include correlation ID |
| Test | Simulate two tenants, async, missing context, downstream propagation |

---

# 24. Testing Strategy

## Test 1 - Two requests do not leak tenant

```text
Request A tenant=tenant-a
Request B tenant=tenant-b
Assert:
  service A query uses tenant-a
  service B query uses tenant-b
```

## Test 2 - Missing tenant rejected

```text
Input:
  authenticated user without default tenant
  no X-Tenant-Id
Expected:
  400/403 depending policy
```

## Test 3 - Forbidden tenant rejected

```text
Input:
  user allowedTenants=[tenant-a]
  X-Tenant-Id=tenant-b
Expected:
  403
```

## Test 4 - HTTP downstream gets safe context

```text
Service A calls Service B
Assert headers:
  X-Correlation-Id present
  X-Tenant-Id present
  traceparent preserved if inbound
  Authorization not blindly forwarded
```

## Test 5 - Async uses snapshot

```text
Submit async task inside request
After request finishes:
  worker still has explicit snapshot
  worker does not access CurrentRequestContext
  MDC cleared after worker
```

## Test 6 - Kafka event has headers

```text
Produced event includes:
  x-message-id
  x-correlation-id
  x-tenant-id
  x-schema-version
```

## Test 7 - Camunda process variables

```text
Start workflow
Assert process variables:
  caseId
  tenantId
  initiatorUserId
  correlationId
Business key:
  caseId or domain-specific key
```

# 25. Roadmap Latihan Praktis Seri 7

Urutan latihan:

```text
1. Buat CurrentRequestContext + RequestContextSnapshot
2. Buat RequestContextBootstrapFilter
3. Buat TenantResolver dengan validasi principal
4. Buat ContextResponseFilter untuk X-Correlation-Id
5. Tambahkan MDC/logging context + cleanup
6. Refactor service agar tidak baca raw headers
7. Tambahkan tenant boundary ke repository method
8. Buat HTTP client wrapper untuk propagate safe headers
9. Buat Kafka header helper
10. Buat async executor wrapper berbasis snapshot
11. Buat workflow starter yang menyimpan businessKey + variables
12. Tambahkan test tenant leak dan async context
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 7

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. Context propagation bukan copy semua header; yang dipropagate adalah **subset trusted context**.
2. Pisahkan **raw inbound context**, **trusted request context**, dan **propagation context**.
3. `CurrentRequestContext` harus `@RequestScoped` dan hanya berisi data yang sudah divalidasi.
4. Tenant context adalah security boundary; jangan percaya raw `X-Tenant-Id`.
5. Correlation ID dipakai untuk log/audit/debug; trace context seperti `traceparent` dipakai untuk distributed tracing.
6. Service layer sebaiknya membaca context abstraction, bukan `ContainerRequestContext`.
7. HTTP/gRPC/Kafka punya carrier berbeda: HTTP headers, gRPC metadata, Kafka headers.
8. Async/thread tidak boleh membawa CDI request-scoped bean; gunakan immutable snapshot.
9. Transaction context lokal tidak boleh dipropagate ke service/message/thread lain.
10. Workflow/Camunda context harus disimpan sebagai business key dan process variables, bukan bergantung pada HTTP request context.
11. Audit, feature flag, idempotency, DB query, dan error response harus memakai trusted context yang sama.
12. Selalu test skenario tenant leak, context loss, async propagation, dan downstream propagation.

**Seri 7/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 8 - Transaction Context + MyBatis/PostgreSQL Unit of Work**, dengan fokus pada `SqlSessionFactory`, `SqlSession`, transaction boundary interceptor, commit/rollback, tenant-aware query, optimistic locking, deadlock/retry, dan outbox pattern.
