## Response:
7/15/2026, 4:12:48 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 8/14**. Fokusnya: transaction context, MyBatis/PostgreSQL unit-of-work, commit/rollback yang eksplisit, tenant-aware query, optimistic locking, deadlock handling, dan outbox pattern.

# Seri 8/14 - Transaction Context + MyBatis/PostgreSQL Unit of Work

Target seri ini: kamu bisa mendesain **transaction boundary** yang jelas untuk aplikasi **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, tanpa bergantung pada Spring/JTA, dan tanpa membuat bug seperti commit parsial, session leak, lost update, deadlock tidak di-retry, atau tenant boundary terlewat.

Intinya:

> Dalam stack Jersey + CDI/Weld + MyBatis, transaction boundary harus kamu desain secara eksplisit. Jangan berharap magic seperti Spring `@Transactional` otomatis ada kecuali memang stack perusahaan memasangnya.

MyBatis menjadikan `SqlSession` sebagai interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mendapatkan mapper, dan mengelola transaksi; `SqlSessionFactory` digunakan untuk membuat `SqlSession`. Ini cocok dengan model **factory shared, session per unit-of-work**. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text
HTTP Request / Camunda Job / Kafka Message
│
├── Application Use Case Boundary
│   └── @TransactionalBoundary
│
├── Transaction Context
│   ├── active / rollbackOnly
│   ├── readOnly
│   ├── isolation
│   └── retryable?
│
├── MyBatis SqlSessionProvider
│   ├── open SqlSession once per transaction
│   ├── provide mappers to repositories
│   ├── commit on success
│   ├── rollback on failure
│   └── close always
│
├── Repository Layer
│   ├── tenant-aware query
│   ├── optimistic locking
│   ├── SELECT FOR UPDATE where needed
│   └── no commit/rollback here
│
└── PostgreSQL
    ├── transaction isolation
    ├── row locks
    ├── unique constraints
    ├── deadlock detection
    └── serialization failure handling
```

Core invariant:

```text
Service/use-case method owns transaction boundary.
Repository participates in transaction.
Repository must not secretly commit/rollback.
SqlSession must not be stored as singleton.
Transaction context must not cross HTTP/gRPC/Kafka/Camunda boundaries.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Transaction Context + MyBatis/PostgreSQL
│
├── 1. Entry Boundary
│   ├── HTTP resource
│   ├── Camunda delegate/job
│   ├── Kafka consumer
│   └── scheduled/background task
│
├── 2. Use Case Transaction Boundary
│   ├── @TransactionalBoundary
│   ├── begin
│   ├── commit
│   ├── rollback
│   ├── rollbackOnly
│   └── retry classification
│
├── 3. MyBatis Runtime
│   ├── SqlSessionFactory
│   ├── SqlSession
│   ├── Mapper
│   ├── ExecutorType
│   └── transaction manager config
│
├── 4. Repository Pattern
│   ├── mapper interface
│   ├── XML/annotation SQL
│   ├── tenant-aware queries
│   ├── optimistic locking
│   ├── row locking
│   └── no transaction ownership
│
├── 5. PostgreSQL Concurrency
│   ├── READ COMMITTED
│   ├── REPEATABLE READ
│   ├── SERIALIZABLE
│   ├── SELECT FOR UPDATE
│   ├── unique constraints
│   ├── deadlock 40P01
│   └── serialization failure 40001
│
├── 6. Reliability Patterns
│   ├── retry whole transaction
│   ├── idempotency key
│   ├── outbox pattern
│   ├── lock ordering
│   ├── short transaction
│   └── timeout
│
└── 7. Failure Modes
    ├── session leak
    ├── commit inside repository
    ├── transaction split
    ├── lost update
    ├── tenant leak
    ├── deadlock loop
    ├── retry non-idempotent operation
    └── outbox/audit mismatch
```

---

# 3. Practical Table

| Area | Praktik | Yang Harus Dilakukan | Jangan Dilakukan | Failure Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SqlSessionFactory` | Shared factory | `@ApplicationScoped` producer | Buat ulang per request | Startup/latency overhead |
| `SqlSession` | Unit-of-work/session | Dibuka per transaction/request/job | Simpan di singleton field | Race/session leak |
| Repository | Data access only | Ambil mapper dari current session | Commit/rollback sendiri | Transaction split |
| Service | Use-case owner | Pasang `@TransactionalBoundary` | Buka session manual di tiap method | Commit parsial |
| Transaction context | Request/job scoped | Track active/rollbackOnly/readOnly | Propagate ke downstream | Broken transaction model |
| Tenant query | Mandatory | Semua query by tenant boundary | Query by ID global | Cross-tenant data leak |
| Optimistic lock | Version column | `WHERE id=? AND version=?` | Blind update | Lost update |
| Pessimistic lock | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` | Untuk command update high-conflict | Lock terlalu luas/lama | Deadlock/latency |
| Retry | Whole transaction | Retry SQLSTATE `40001`, kadang `40P01` | Retry satu SQL statement saja | Partial state/replay bug |
| Outbox | Durable integration | Insert event dalam transaksi yang sama | Publish Kafka sebelum commit | Ghost event |

---

# 4. MyBatis Primitive: `SqlSessionFactory`, `SqlSession`, Mapper

MyBatis official Java API menjelaskan `SqlSession` sebagai interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mendapatkan mapper, dan mengelola transaksi. `SqlSessionFactory` membuat instance `SqlSession`; API-nya menyediakan variasi `openSession()`, termasuk `openSession(boolean autoCommit)`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical mapping:

```text
SqlSessionFactory
  = expensive/shared factory
  = application scoped

SqlSession
  = transaction/unit-of-work object
  = short-lived
  = commit/rollback/close required

Mapper
  = proxy generated by MyBatis from SqlSession
  = should be used within active SqlSession
```

Recommended scope:

| Object | Scope |
|---|---|
| `DataSource` | `@ApplicationScoped` |
| `SqlSessionFactory` | `@ApplicationScoped` |
| `SqlSession` | transaction-bound/request-bound/job-bound |
| MyBatis mapper | obtained from current `SqlSession` |
| Repository implementation | `@ApplicationScoped` |
| Transaction context | request/job/message scope, not singleton |

---

# 5. Baseline CDI/Weld Components

## 5.1 `@TransactionalBoundary`

```java
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD })
public @interface TransactionalBoundary {

    @Nonbinding
    boolean readOnly() default false;

    @Nonbinding
    IsolationLevel isolation() default IsolationLevel.DEFAULT;

    @Nonbinding
    boolean retryable() default false;

    @Nonbinding
    int maxRetries() default 0;
}
```

```java
public enum IsolationLevel {
    DEFAULT,
    READ_COMMITTED,
    REPEATABLE_READ,
    SERIALIZABLE
}
```

## 5.2 Transaction Context

```java
@RequestScoped
public class TransactionContext {

    private int depth;
    private boolean rollbackOnly;
    private boolean readOnly;
    private IsolationLevel isolationLevel = IsolationLevel.DEFAULT;

    public boolean isActive() {
        return depth > 0;
    }

    public boolean isOwner() {
        return depth == 1;
    }

    public void begin(boolean readOnly, IsolationLevel isolationLevel) {
        depth++;

        if (depth == 1) {
            this.readOnly = readOnly;
            this.isolationLevel = isolationLevel;
            this.rollbackOnly = false;
        }
    }

    public void markRollbackOnly() {
        this.rollbackOnly = true;
    }

    public boolean rollbackOnly() {
        return rollbackOnly;
    }

    public boolean readOnly() {
        return readOnly;
    }

    public IsolationLevel isolationLevel() {
        return isolationLevel;
    }

    public void end() {
        if (depth <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("No active transaction");
        }

        depth--;

        if (depth == 0) {
            rollbackOnly = false;
            readOnly = false;
            isolationLevel = IsolationLevel.DEFAULT;
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text
depth dipakai supaya nested service call bisa join transaction yang sama.
Hanya boundary paling luar yang commit/rollback.
Nested method tidak boleh commit sendiri.
```

---

# 6. `SqlSessionProvider`

Provider ini bertanggung jawab membuka satu `SqlSession` untuk unit-of-work aktif.

```java
@RequestScoped
public class SqlSessionProvider {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;
    private final TransactionContext transactionContext;

    private SqlSession sqlSession;

    @Inject
    public SqlSessionProvider(
            SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory,
            TransactionContext transactionContext
    ) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
        this.transactionContext = transactionContext;
    }

    public SqlSession currentSession() {
        if (sqlSession == null) {
            sqlSession = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);
            applyTransactionSettings(sqlSession);
        }

        return sqlSession;
    }

    public void commit() {
        if (sqlSession != null) {
            sqlSession.commit();
        }
    }

    public void rollback() {
        if (sqlSession != null) {
            sqlSession.rollback();
        }
    }

    @PreDestroy
    public void close() {
        if (sqlSession != null) {
            sqlSession.close();
        }
    }

    private void applyTransactionSettings(SqlSession session) {
        // Optional:
        // - SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY
        // - SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL ...
        // But these must be executed before queries in the transaction.
        if (transactionContext.readOnly()) {
            session.getConnection(); // connection access can be used for JDBC-level config if needed
        }
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

```text
Jangan akses Connection/SET TRANSACTION secara sembarang setelah query pertama.
Di PostgreSQL, transaction-level settings seperti isolation/read-only harus dilakukan di awal transaction.
```

---

# 7. Transaction Interceptor

## 7.1 Basic Version

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.TRANSACTION)
public class TransactionInterceptor {

    private final TransactionContext transactionContext;
    private final SqlSessionProvider sqlSessionProvider;

    @Inject
    public TransactionInterceptor(
            TransactionContext transactionContext,
            SqlSessionProvider sqlSessionProvider
    ) {
        this.transactionContext = transactionContext;
        this.sqlSessionProvider = sqlSessionProvider;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        TransactionalBoundary annotation = resolveAnnotation(ctx);

        boolean alreadyActive = transactionContext.isActive();

        transactionContext.begin(annotation.readOnly(), annotation.isolation());

        try {
            Object result = ctx.proceed();

            if (!alreadyActive) {
                if (transactionContext.rollbackOnly()) {
                    sqlSessionProvider.rollback();
                } else {
                    sqlSessionProvider.commit();
                }
            }

            return result;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            transactionContext.markRollbackOnly();

            if (!alreadyActive) {
                sqlSessionProvider.rollback();
            }

            throw ex;
        } finally {
            transactionContext.end();
        }
    }

    private TransactionalBoundary resolveAnnotation(InvocationContext ctx) {
        TransactionalBoundary method =
                ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(TransactionalBoundary.class);

        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        TransactionalBoundary type =
                ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(TransactionalBoundary.class);

        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("@TransactionalBoundary not found");
    }
}
```

## 7.2 Important Invariant

```text
Only outermost transaction boundary commits or rolls back.
Inner transactional method joins existing unit-of-work.
If any inner method fails, mark rollbackOnly.
```

Contoh:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseCommandService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final AuditRepository auditRepository;

    @Inject
    public CaseCommandService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            AuditRepository auditRepository
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.auditRepository = auditRepository;
    }

    @TransactionalBoundary
    public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
        CaseRecord record = caseRepository.findByIdForUpdate(caseId);

        record.approve(command.comment());

        caseRepository.update(record);

        auditRepository.insertAudit("CASE_APPROVE", caseId);

        return ApprovalResult.from(record);
    }
}
```

Repository tidak melakukan commit:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final SqlSessionProvider sessionProvider;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisCaseRepository(SqlSessionProvider sessionProvider) {
        this.sessionProvider = sessionProvider;
    }

    @Override
    public CaseRecord findByIdForUpdate(String caseId) {
        return mapper().findByIdForUpdate(caseId);
    }

    @Override
    public void update(CaseRecord record) {
        int updated = mapper().update(record);

        if (updated != 1) {
            throw new OptimisticLockException("Case version conflict");
        }
    }

    private CaseMapper mapper() {
        return sessionProvider.currentSession().getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
    }
}
```

---

# 8. Mapper Design

## 8.1 Mapper Interface

```java
public interface CaseMapper {

    CaseRecord findByTenantAndId(
            @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
            @Param("caseId") String caseId
    );

    CaseRecord findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(
            @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
            @Param("caseId") String caseId
    );

    int updateWithVersion(CaseRecord record);

    int insertOutbox(OutboxRecord outbox);
}
```

## 8.2 Mapper XML

```xml
<mapper namespace="com.company.caseapp.persistence.CaseMapper">

    <select id="findByTenantAndId" resultType="com.company.caseapp.domain.CaseRecord">
        SELECT
            id,
            tenant_id,
            status,
            version,
            updated_at
        FROM cases
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND id = #{caseId}
    </select>

    <select id="findByTenantAndIdForUpdate" resultType="com.company.caseapp.domain.CaseRecord">
        SELECT
            id,
            tenant_id,
            status,
            version,
            updated_at
        FROM cases
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND id = #{caseId}
        FOR UPDATE
    </select>

    <update id="updateWithVersion">
        UPDATE cases
        SET
            status = #{status},
            version = version + 1,
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND id = #{id}
          AND version = #{version}
    </update>

</mapper>
```

PostgreSQL `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` mengunci row yang dipilih seolah-olah akan di-update, sehingga transaksi lain yang ingin update/delete/lock row yang sama akan tertahan atau berkonflik sesuai mode lock. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Tenant-Aware Query Invariant

Dalam sistem multi-tenant, query by ID saja hampir selalu salah.

Salah:

```sql
SELECT * FROM cases WHERE id = #{caseId}
```

Benar:

```sql
SELECT * FROM cases
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND id = #{caseId}
```

Untuk update:

```sql
UPDATE cases
SET status = #{status}
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND id = #{caseId}
```

Untuk delete:

```sql
DELETE FROM cases
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND id = #{caseId}
```

Invariant:

```text
Every business row query must include tenant boundary,
unless table is truly global/reference data and explicitly documented.
```

Recommended DB constraints:

```sql
ALTER TABLE cases
ADD CONSTRAINT pk_cases PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, id);
```

Atau jika `id` global UUID:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_cases_tenant_id_id
ON cases (tenant_id, id);
```

---

# 10. Optimistic Locking

Optimistic locking cocok ketika conflict relatif jarang dan kamu ingin mencegah lost update tanpa lock panjang.

Schema:

```sql
ALTER TABLE cases
ADD COLUMN version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
```

Update:

```sql
UPDATE cases
SET
    status = #{status},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND id = #{id}
  AND version = #{version}
```

Repository:

```java
@Override
public void update(CaseRecord record) {
    int updated = mapper().updateWithVersion(record);

    if (updated != 1) {
        throw new OptimisticLockException(
                "Case was modified by another transaction"
        );
    }
}
```

Service:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
    CaseRecord record = repository.findByTenantAndId(
            context.tenantId(),
            caseId
    );

    accessPolicy.assertCanApprove(record);

    record.approve(command.comment());

    repository.update(record);

    return ApprovalResult.from(record);
}
```

Kapan cocok:

| Scenario | Optimistic Lock Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| User edit form | Ya |
| Case status update moderate conflict | Ya |
| Approval command dengan double-submit risk | Ya, plus idempotency |
| High-frequency counter | Tidak ideal |
| Payment capture | Perlu idempotency + stricter design |

---

# 11. Pessimistic Locking dengan `FOR UPDATE`

Pessimistic locking cocok ketika kamu harus membaca state lalu mengubahnya, dan conflict cukup mungkin.

```sql
SELECT
    id,
    tenant_id,
    status,
    version
FROM cases
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND id = #{caseId}
FOR UPDATE
```

Service:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
    CaseRecord record = repository.findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(
            context.tenantId(),
            caseId
    );

    accessPolicy.assertCanApprove(record);

    record.approve(command.comment());

    repository.updateWithoutVersion(record);

    return ApprovalResult.from(record);
}
```

Kapan pakai:

| Scenario | `FOR UPDATE` Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| State transition command | Ya |
| Claim task/case | Ya |
| Payment state machine | Ya |
| Read-only detail page | Tidak |
| Bulk report | Tidak |
| Long-running workflow task | Tidak tahan lock lama |

Rule:

```text
Lock late, release early.
Jangan panggil external HTTP/gRPC/Kafka saat DB row lock masih ditahan.
```

Bad:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public void approveAndNotify(String caseId) {
    CaseRecord record = repository.findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(...);

    externalNotificationClient.send(...); // buruk jika lambat

    repository.update(record);
}
```

Better:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public void approve(String caseId) {
    CaseRecord record = repository.findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(...);
    record.approve();

    repository.update(record);
    outboxRepository.insert(caseApprovedEvent(record));
}

// Outbox publisher sends notification after commit.
```

---

# 12. PostgreSQL Isolation Level

PostgreSQL mendukung beberapa isolation level; dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan bahwa `Serializable` adalah level paling ketat dan aplikasi yang menggunakan level ini harus siap retry transaksi karena serialization failure. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical default:

| Isolation | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|
| `READ COMMITTED` | Default untuk banyak request CRUD |
| `REPEATABLE READ` | Perlu snapshot konsisten dalam transaksi |
| `SERIALIZABLE` | Perlu correctness kuat terhadap anomaly, siap retry |
| Explicit row lock | Untuk command state transition |
| Unique constraint + upsert | Untuk idempotency/dedup |

Guideline:

```text
Jangan naikkan isolation level sebagai solusi pertama.
Mulai dari:
  1. tenant-aware constraints
  2. optimistic locking
  3. row lock untuk state transition
  4. idempotency
  5. retry whole transaction
  6. baru evaluasi isolation level lebih tinggi
```

---

# 13. Retry: Serialization Failure dan Deadlock

PostgreSQL documentation menyebut SQLSTATE `40001` untuk `serialization_failure`; dokumentasi yang sama menyatakan deadlock failure dengan SQLSTATE `40P01` kadang juga layak di-retry. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/mvcc-serialization-failure-handling.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Important:

```text
Retry harus mengulang seluruh transaction/use-case,
bukan hanya satu SQL statement di tengah transaction.
```

## 13.1 Exception Classifier

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PostgreSqlRetryClassifier {

    public boolean isRetryable(Throwable throwable) {
        SQLException sql = findSqlException(throwable);

        if (sql == null) {
            return false;
        }

        String sqlState = sql.getSQLState();

        return "40001".equals(sqlState)  // serialization_failure
                || "40P01".equals(sqlState); // deadlock_detected
    }

    private SQLException findSqlException(Throwable throwable) {
        Throwable current = throwable;

        while (current != null) {
            if (current instanceof SQLException sql) {
                return sql;
            }
            current = current.getCause();
        }

        return null;
    }
}
```

## 13.2 Retry Executor

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class TransactionRetryExecutor {

    private final PostgreSqlRetryClassifier retryClassifier;

    @Inject
    public TransactionRetryExecutor(PostgreSqlRetryClassifier retryClassifier) {
        this.retryClassifier = retryClassifier;
    }

    public <T> T execute(
            int maxRetries,
            SupplierWithException<T> supplier
    ) throws Exception {
        int attempt = 0;

        while (true) {
            attempt++;

            try {
                return supplier.get();
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                if (attempt > maxRetries || !retryClassifier.isRetryable(ex)) {
                    throw ex;
                }

                sleep(backoffMillis(attempt));
            }
        }
    }

    private long backoffMillis(int attempt) {
        return Math.min(1_000L, 50L * attempt * attempt);
    }

    private void sleep(long millis) throws Exception {
        try {
            Thread.sleep(millis);
        } catch (InterruptedException ex) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw ex;
        }
    }
}
```

```java
@FunctionalInterface
public interface SupplierWithException<T> {
    T get() throws Exception;
}
```

## 13.3 Transaction Interceptor with Retry

Retry inside the same interceptor is tricky because `ctx.proceed()` may not be safely repeatable if parameters include consumed streams or side effects happen before DB commit. Prefer retry only for clearly idempotent command boundaries.

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.TRANSACTION)
public class TransactionInterceptor {

    private final TransactionExecutor transactionExecutor;

    @Inject
    public TransactionInterceptor(TransactionExecutor transactionExecutor) {
        this.transactionExecutor = transactionExecutor;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        TransactionalBoundary annotation = resolveAnnotation(ctx);

        if (!annotation.retryable() || annotation.maxRetries() <= 0) {
            return transactionExecutor.executeOnce(ctx, annotation);
        }

        return transactionExecutor.executeWithRetry(
                ctx,
                annotation,
                annotation.maxRetries()
        );
    }

    private TransactionalBoundary resolveAnnotation(InvocationContext ctx) {
        TransactionalBoundary method =
                ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(TransactionalBoundary.class);

        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        TransactionalBoundary type =
                ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(TransactionalBoundary.class);

        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("@TransactionalBoundary not found");
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

| Jangan Retry Jika | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Method mengirim email langsung | Bisa duplicate |
| Method call payment gateway langsung | Bisa double charge |
| Method publish Kafka sebelum commit | Bisa ghost/duplicate event |
| Method membaca stream request body | Stream bisa sudah consumed |
| Method tidak punya idempotency key | Replay tidak aman |

Retry aman jika:

```text
- seluruh side effect ada di DB transaction,
- external side effect lewat outbox,
- command punya idempotency key,
- method boundary benar-benar repeatable.
```

---

# 14. Deadlock Prevention

Deadlock biasanya bukan hanya “perlu retry”. Retry membantu recovery, tapi root cause harus dikurangi.

Common prevention:

```text
1. Lock rows in consistent order.
2. Keep transaction short.
3. Avoid external calls inside transaction.
4. Use narrow WHERE clause + indexes.
5. Avoid updating rows in arbitrary list order.
6. Use idempotency for command retry.
7. Prefer optimistic locking for low-conflict updates.
```

Example bad:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public void transfer(String fromCaseId, String toCaseId) {
    CaseRecord from = repository.findByIdForUpdate(fromCaseId);
    CaseRecord to = repository.findByIdForUpdate(toCaseId);
}
```

If another transaction locks in reverse order, deadlock risk rises.

Better:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public void transfer(String caseA, String caseB) {
    List<String> ordered = Stream.of(caseA, caseB)
            .sorted()
            .toList();

    CaseRecord first = repository.findByIdForUpdate(ordered.get(0));
    CaseRecord second = repository.findByIdForUpdate(ordered.get(1));
}
```

---

# 15. Idempotency Table Pattern

Untuk command yang bisa diulang, simpan idempotency key.

PostgreSQL `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT` dapat digunakan untuk menentukan alternatif saat insert bertabrakan dengan unique constraint/exclusion constraint, misalnya `DO NOTHING` atau `DO UPDATE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE idempotency_record (
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    operation TEXT NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    request_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    response_json JSONB,
    error_code TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, operation, idempotency_key)
);
```

Try begin:

```sql
INSERT INTO idempotency_record (
    tenant_id,
    operation,
    idempotency_key,
    request_hash,
    status
)
VALUES (
    #{tenantId},
    #{operation},
    #{idempotencyKey},
    #{requestHash},
    'IN_PROGRESS'
)
ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, operation, idempotency_key)
DO NOTHING
```

Decision:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public IdempotencyDecision begin(
        String tenantId,
        String operation,
        String key,
        String requestHash
) {
    int inserted = mapper.insertBegin(tenantId, operation, key, requestHash);

    if (inserted == 1) {
        return new IdempotencyDecision.Proceed();
    }

    IdempotencyRecord existing = mapper.find(tenantId, operation, key);

    if (!existing.requestHash().equals(requestHash)) {
        return new IdempotencyDecision.Conflict("Same key used with different request");
    }

    if ("COMPLETED".equals(existing.status())) {
        return new IdempotencyDecision.ReturnCached(existing.responseJson());
    }

    return new IdempotencyDecision.Conflict("Request already in progress");
}
```

Invariant:

```text
Idempotency scope = tenant_id + operation + idempotency_key.
Never use idempotency_key globally across tenants.
Always compare request_hash.
```

---

# 16. Outbox Pattern

Outbox pattern mencegah event terkirim padahal transaksi DB gagal.

Bad:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId) {
    CaseRecord record = repository.findByIdForUpdate(caseId);
    record.approve();
    repository.update(record);

    kafkaProducer.send(caseApprovedEvent(record)); // buruk: external side effect before commit

    return ApprovalResult.from(record);
}
```

Better:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId) {
    CaseRecord record = repository.findByIdForUpdate(caseId);

    record.approve();

    repository.update(record);

    outboxRepository.insert(new OutboxRecord(
            UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
            context.tenantId(),
            context.correlationId(),
            "CASE_APPROVED",
            caseId,
            toJson(record),
            Instant.now(clock)
    ));

    return ApprovalResult.from(record);
}
```

Outbox table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE outbox_event (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NEW',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_new
ON outbox_event (status, created_at)
WHERE status = 'NEW';
```

Publisher:

```text
Poll NEW outbox rows
  → publish to Kafka
  → mark PUBLISHED
  → retry on failure
```

Important:

```text
Outbox insert is inside business transaction.
Kafka publish is outside business transaction.
This prevents ghost event when DB rollback happens.
```

---

# 17. Read-Only Transaction

Read-only transaction bisa dipakai untuk intent dan optimization. Tetapi jangan mengandalkan read-only sebagai security boundary.

```java
@TransactionalBoundary(readOnly = true)
public CaseDto getCase(String caseId) {
    return repository.findByTenantAndId(context.tenantId(), caseId);
}
```

Possible provider setting:

```java
private void applyTransactionSettings(SqlSession session) {
    if (transactionContext.readOnly()) {
        session.getConnection().setReadOnly(true);
    }
}
```

Caution:

```text
Connection readOnly support depends on driver/database behavior.
It is useful as hint/guard, not as substitute for code review.
```

---

# 18. Transaction Boundary Placement

Good:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseCommandService {

    @TransactionalBoundary
    public ApprovalResult approveCase(...) {
        // full use-case
    }
}
```

Bad:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseRepository {

    @TransactionalBoundary
    public void updateCase(...) {
        // too low-level
    }
}
```

Why repository-level transaction is bad:

```text
Use case can involve:
  - read case
  - validate state
  - update case
  - insert audit
  - insert outbox
  - insert idempotency result

If each repository method owns its transaction, use-case atomicity is broken.
```

Boundary placement rule:

| Layer | Owns Transaction? |
|---|---:|
| JAX-RS Resource | Usually no |
| Application service/use-case | Yes |
| Domain object | No |
| Repository | No, joins transaction |
| Mapper | No |
| Outbox repository | No, joins transaction |
| External HTTP client | No DB transaction |

---

# 19. Camunda/Kafka/Async Entry Points

HTTP request scope is not the only entry point.

```text
HTTP request:
  CurrentRequestContext = @RequestScoped

Kafka consumer:
  MessageContextSnapshot = explicit message context

Camunda job:
  WorkflowContextSnapshot = process variables

Scheduled job:
  JobContextSnapshot = job metadata
```

Do not assume `@RequestScoped` is active outside HTTP. For Kafka/Camunda, use an explicit unit-of-work wrapper:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseMessageHandler {

    private final TransactionalWorkRunner workRunner;
    private final CaseRepository repository;

    @Inject
    public CaseMessageHandler(
            TransactionalWorkRunner workRunner,
            CaseRepository repository
    ) {
        this.workRunner = workRunner;
        this.repository = repository;
    }

    public void handle(CaseApprovedMessage message, MessageContextSnapshot context) {
        workRunner.run(() -> {
            CaseRecord record = repository.findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(
                    context.tenantId(),
                    message.caseId()
            );

            // process message
        });
    }
}
```

Concept:

```text
For non-HTTP entry points, create equivalent context and transaction scope.
Do not fake HTTP request context unless framework explicitly supports it.
```

---

# 20. Practical Failure Modes

| Failure | Example | Impact | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SqlSession` singleton | Field in `@ApplicationScoped` repo | Race/session corruption | Use provider per transaction |
| Repository commit | `session.commit()` in repository | Partial transaction | Commit only in transaction interceptor |
| No rollback | Catch exception and ignore | Bad data | Rethrow and rollback |
| External call inside lock | HTTP call after `FOR UPDATE` | Lock held too long | Outbox after commit |
| Query missing tenant | `WHERE id=?` | Cross-tenant leak | Composite key/tenant predicate |
| Blind update | No version/lock | Lost update | Version column or `FOR UPDATE` |
| Retry single query | Retry only failed mapper call | Inconsistent state | Retry whole use-case |
| Retry non-idempotent command | Payment/email duplicate | External double side effect | Idempotency + outbox |
| Long transaction | User interaction/external wait | Deadlock/lock contention | Keep transaction short |
| Deadlock ignored | No SQLSTATE handling | User-facing 500 | Retry and fix lock order |
| Outbox absent | Publish before commit | Ghost event | Insert outbox in transaction |
| Audit outside transaction incorrectly | Audit success but DB rollback | Compliance mismatch | Audit outbox or clear semantics |

---

# 21. Testing Strategy

## 21.1 Unit Test Service Logic

Use fake repository; no CDI, no DB.

```java
@Test
void approveCase_shouldTransitionState() {
    FakeCaseRepository repository = new FakeCaseRepository();
    CaseAccessPolicy policy = new AllowAllPolicy();

    CaseCommandService service = new CaseCommandService(repository, policy);

    ApprovalResult result = service.approveCase("CASE-001", new ApproveCommand("ok"));

    assertEquals("APPROVED", result.status());
}
```

## 21.2 CDI Integration Test

Verify interceptor/proxy/session lifecycle:

```text
- @TransactionalBoundary opens one SqlSession
- success commits once
- exception rollbacks once
- nested service call does not commit twice
- repository never commits
```

## 21.3 PostgreSQL Integration Test

Use real PostgreSQL environment in CI where possible.

Test scenarios:

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| update with correct version | success |
| update with stale version | optimistic lock exception |
| two concurrent approvals | one success, one conflict/retry |
| `FOR UPDATE` lock behavior | second transaction waits/conflicts |
| deadlock simulation | one transaction gets `40P01` |
| serializable conflict | transaction gets `40001` and retry works |
| outbox rollback | no outbox row after rollback |
| outbox commit | outbox row exists after commit |
| tenant boundary | tenant A cannot read tenant B |

---

# 22. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Boundary | Apakah transaction ada di use-case service, bukan repository? |
| Session | Apakah `SqlSession` short-lived dan selalu closed? |
| Commit | Apakah hanya outer transaction boundary yang commit? |
| Rollback | Apakah exception menyebabkan rollback? |
| Nested call | Apakah nested transactional method join existing transaction? |
| Tenant | Apakah semua query business table include `tenant_id`? |
| Optimistic lock | Apakah update command memakai `version` atau lock? |
| Pessimistic lock | Apakah `FOR UPDATE` dipakai hanya saat perlu? |
| Lock duration | Apakah tidak ada external call saat lock masih held? |
| Retry | Apakah retry whole transaction dan hanya untuk safe command? |
| SQLSTATE | Apakah `40001`/`40P01` diklasifikasi benar? |
| Idempotency | Apakah command retryable punya idempotency key? |
| Outbox | Apakah event/audit integration ditulis dalam transaction? |
| Read-only | Apakah read-only hanya hint/guard, bukan security? |
| Non-HTTP | Apakah Kafka/Camunda punya context/unit-of-work sendiri? |

# 24. Roadmap Latihan Praktis Seri 8

Urutan latihan:

```text
1. Buat SqlSessionFactory producer
2. Buat SqlSessionProvider request/transaction-bound
3. Buat @TransactionalBoundary
4. Buat TransactionContext dengan nested transaction depth
5. Buat TransactionInterceptor commit/rollback
6. Refactor repository agar tidak commit/rollback
7. Tambahkan tenant-aware mapper query
8. Tambahkan optimistic locking version column
9. Tambahkan SELECT FOR UPDATE untuk command state transition
10. Tambahkan retry classifier SQLSTATE 40001/40P01
11. Tambahkan idempotency table dengan unique key
12. Tambahkan outbox table dan insert dalam transaksi
13. Buat integration test commit/rollback/conflict/deadlock/outbox
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 8

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. `SqlSessionFactory` adalah shared factory; `SqlSession` adalah unit-of-work object.
2. Repository tidak boleh memiliki transaction ownership.
3. Transaction boundary sebaiknya berada di application service/use-case method.
4. `@TransactionalBoundary` + CDI interceptor bisa dipakai untuk begin/commit/rollback jika stack tidak memakai Spring/JTA.
5. Nested transactional method harus join existing transaction; hanya outer boundary yang commit/rollback.
6. Semua query business multi-tenant harus membawa `tenant_id` atau tenant boundary yang ekuivalen.
7. Lost update dicegah dengan optimistic locking `version` atau pessimistic locking `FOR UPDATE`.
8. PostgreSQL serialization failure `40001` harus di-retry dengan mengulang whole transaction; deadlock `40P01` kadang juga layak di-retry, tetapi root cause lock ordering tetap harus diperbaiki.
9. Jangan melakukan external call ketika row lock masih ditahan.
10. Idempotency key wajib untuk command yang aman di-retry.
11. Outbox pattern mencegah Kafka/event/audit terkirim saat DB transaction rollback.
12. Kafka/Camunda/scheduled job butuh context dan unit-of-work sendiri; jangan bergantung pada HTTP `@RequestScoped`.

**Seri 8/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 9 - Async & Multi-thread Context Propagation: Executor, ThreadLocal, Cleanup**, dengan fokus pada context snapshot, MDC, executor wrapper, request-scope limitation, race condition, cancellation, timeout, backpressure, dan safe shutdown.
