# Seri 9/14 - Async & Multi-thread Context Propagation: Executor, ThreadLocal, Cleanup

Target seri ini: kamu bisa memakai async/multithread di aplikasi **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld** tanpa membuat bug seperti:

- request context hilang
- tenant/user/correlation bocor antar thread
- MDC/log salah tenant
- `@RequestScoped` diakses di worker thread
- executor tidak shutdown
- task menumpuk tanpa backpressure
- timeout/cancellation tidak dihormati
- transaction context dipakai lintas thread

Intinya:

> Async bukan sekadar `executor.submit(...)`.  
> Async berarti membuat boundary runtime baru: context harus di-snapshot, dipropagate secara eksplisit, dibersihkan, diberi timeout, dan tidak boleh membawa object request-scoped/raw transaction ke thread lain.

Java `ExecutorService` menyediakan model eksekusi task asynchronous dan harus di-shutdown; `shutdown()` membiarkan task yang sudah masuk selesai, sedangkan `shutdownNow()` mencoba menghentikan task yang berjalan dan mencegah task pending dimulai. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/util/concurrent/ExecutorService.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Satu HTTP request biasanya berjalan di satu request thread.

```text id="ab8x2t"
HTTP Request Thread
│
├── CDI @RequestScoped active
├── CurrentRequestContext available
├── MDC/log context available
├── Transaction context maybe active
└── Resource/Service method executes
```

Begitu pindah ke worker thread:

```text id="zuktpz"
Worker Thread
│
├── CDI @RequestScoped usually NOT active
├── CurrentRequestContext may fail
├── MDC may be empty or stale
├── Transaction context must NOT be reused
└── Task needs explicit context snapshot
```

Jadi rule utamanya:

```text id="8rjq54"
Jangan membawa CDI request-scoped bean ke thread lain.
Bawa immutable snapshot.
Set MDC di awal task.
Clear MDC di finally.
Buka transaction/unit-of-work baru jika worker butuh DB.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text id="yi5jef"
Async & Multi-thread Context Propagation
│
├── 1. Async Entry Points
│   ├── HTTP request submits background task
│   ├── CompletableFuture
│   ├── ExecutorService
│   ├── scheduled job
│   ├── Kafka consumer
│   ├── Camunda job/delegate
│   └── batch worker
│
├── 2. Context Capture
│   ├── correlation ID
│   ├── trace context
│   ├── tenant ID
│   ├── user ID
│   ├── roles/scopes
│   ├── locale
│   └── causation/business key
│
├── 3. Context Propagation
│   ├── RequestContextSnapshot
│   ├── MessageContextSnapshot
│   ├── WorkflowContextSnapshot
│   ├── MDC setup
│   ├── ThreadLocal setup
│   └── cleanup/finally
│
├── 4. Execution Control
│   ├── bounded executor
│   ├── queue size
│   ├── rejection policy
│   ├── timeout
│   ├── cancellation
│   ├── retry policy
│   └── backpressure
│
├── 5. Data Safety
│   ├── no shared mutable request state
│   ├── no SqlSession reuse
│   ├── no DB transaction reuse
│   ├── idempotency for retry
│   └── outbox for external side effect
│
├── 6. Shutdown
│   ├── stop accepting task
│   ├── drain running task
│   ├── timeout wait
│   ├── interrupt if needed
│   └── resource cleanup
│
└── 7. Failure Modes
    ├── context leak
    ├── lost context
    ├── thread starvation
    ├── unbounded queue
    ├── silent task failure
    ├── zombie task
    ├── duplicate side effect
    └── partial transaction
```

---

# 3. Practical Table

| Area | Praktik | Yang Benar | Yang Salah | Failure Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Context capture | Snapshot | `RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot()` | Inject `CurrentRequestContext` ke worker dan akses nanti | `ContextNotActive`, context salah |
| MDC | Setup/clear | `MDC.put(...)` lalu `MDC.clear()` di `finally` | Set tanpa clear | Log bocor antar request |
| Executor | Bounded | Fixed pool + bounded queue + rejection policy | `newCachedThreadPool` sembarang / unbounded queue | Memory/thread explosion |
| DB work | New unit-of-work | Worker buka transaction sendiri | Reuse `SqlSession` request thread | Session corruption |
| External call | Timeout | Per-call timeout + cancellation | Blocking tanpa timeout | Thread starvation |
| Retry | Idempotent | Retry task yang aman/idempotent | Retry side-effect non-idempotent | Duplicate email/payment |
| Error handling | Observe failure | log/metric/future callback | Fire-and-forget tanpa handler | Silent failure |
| Shutdown | Controlled | `shutdown`, await, then `shutdownNow` | Tidak shutdown executor | Thread/resource leak |
| Backpressure | Reject/degrade | Queue limit, 429/503, outbox | Terima semua task | Latency/memory collapse |
| Workflow | Explicit context | process variables/message headers | HTTP request context | Context mismatch |

---

# 4. Context Snapshot: Jangan Bawa Bean, Bawa Data

Request context CDI:

```java id="l903dd"
@RequestScoped
public class CurrentRequestContext {

    private String correlationId;
    private String traceParent;
    private String tenantId;
    private String userId;
    private Set<String> roles = Set.of();
    private Locale locale = Locale.ENGLISH;

    public RequestContextSnapshot snapshot() {
        return new RequestContextSnapshot(
                correlationId(),
                traceParent,
                tenantId(),
                userId(),
                roles,
                locale
        );
    }

    public String correlationId() {
        return require(correlationId, "correlationId");
    }

    public String tenantId() {
        return require(tenantId, "tenantId");
    }

    public String userId() {
        return require(userId, "userId");
    }

    private static String require(String value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing context: " + name);
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

Snapshot immutable:

```java id="wpjsn5"
public record RequestContextSnapshot(
        String correlationId,
        String traceParent,
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles,
        Locale locale
) {
    public RequestContextSnapshot {
        roles = roles == null ? Set.of() : Set.copyOf(roles);
        locale = locale == null ? Locale.ENGLISH : locale;
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="i49ve1"
Snapshot boleh berisi:
  correlationId, traceParent, tenantId, userId, roles, locale, businessKey

Snapshot tidak boleh berisi:
  raw JWT, password, secret, SqlSession, Connection, EntityManager,
  ContainerRequestContext, HttpServletRequest, CDI request-scoped bean
```

---

# 5. MDC / Logging Context

MDC digunakan untuk menambahkan diagnostic context ke log, misalnya correlation ID per request. Logback mendokumentasikan MDC sebagai teknik untuk “menandai” request agar log bisa dikaitkan dengan request/client tertentu. ([Logback](https://logback.qos.ch/manual/mdc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Helper:

```java id="j22n7g"
public final class MdcContext {

    private MdcContext() {
    }

    public static void put(RequestContextSnapshot snapshot) {
        putIfPresent("correlationId", snapshot.correlationId());
        putIfPresent("traceparent", snapshot.traceParent());
        putIfPresent("tenantId", snapshot.tenantId());
        putIfPresent("userId", snapshot.userId());
    }

    public static void put(MessageContextSnapshot snapshot) {
        putIfPresent("correlationId", snapshot.correlationId());
        putIfPresent("traceparent", snapshot.traceParent());
        putIfPresent("tenantId", snapshot.tenantId());
        putIfPresent("messageId", snapshot.messageId());
        putIfPresent("causationId", snapshot.causationId());
    }

    public static void clear() {
        MDC.clear();
    }

    private static void putIfPresent(String key, String value) {
        if (value != null && !value.isBlank()) {
            MDC.put(key, value);
        }
    }
}
```

Filter request:

```java id="0cn1ec"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION + 100)
public class LoggingContextFilter
        implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public LoggingContextFilter(CurrentRequestContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext request) {
        MdcContext.put(context.snapshot());
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext request,
            ContainerResponseContext response
    ) {
        MdcContext.clear();
    }
}
```

Invariant:

```text id="7xxfqx"
Set MDC di boundary.
Clear MDC di finally/response filter.
Di worker thread, set ulang dari snapshot.
Jangan mengandalkan MDC request thread otomatis ikut ke executor.
```

---

# 6. Context-Aware Runnable / Callable

## Runnable Wrapper

```java id="s7ezvf"
public final class ContextAwareRunnable implements Runnable {

    private final RequestContextSnapshot snapshot;
    private final Runnable delegate;

    public ContextAwareRunnable(
            RequestContextSnapshot snapshot,
            Runnable delegate
    ) {
        this.snapshot = snapshot;
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        try {
            MdcContext.put(snapshot);
            delegate.run();
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

## Callable Wrapper

```java id="0btik0"
public final class ContextAwareCallable<T> implements Callable<T> {

    private final RequestContextSnapshot snapshot;
    private final Callable<T> delegate;

    public ContextAwareCallable(
            RequestContextSnapshot snapshot,
            Callable<T> delegate
    ) {
        this.snapshot = snapshot;
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    @Override
    public T call() throws Exception {
        try {
            MdcContext.put(snapshot);
            return delegate.call();
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan penting:

```text id="9ck4ve"
Wrapper ini hanya mengatur MDC/log context.
Ia tidak otomatis mengaktifkan CDI @RequestScoped.
Kalau worker butuh tenant/user, pass snapshot sebagai parameter eksplisit.
```

---

# 7. Context-Aware Executor

```java id="sg6fhe"
@ApplicationScoped
public class ContextAwareExecutor {

    private final ExecutorService executorService;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public ContextAwareExecutor(
            @BackgroundExecutor ExecutorService executorService,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.executorService = executorService;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public Future<?> submit(Runnable task) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();
        return executorService.submit(new ContextAwareRunnable(snapshot, task));
    }

    public <T> Future<T> submit(Callable<T> task) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();
        return executorService.submit(new ContextAwareCallable<>(snapshot, task));
    }

    public void execute(Runnable task) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();
        executorService.execute(new ContextAwareRunnable(snapshot, task));
    }
}
```

Qualifier:

```java id="5incxi"
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface BackgroundExecutor {
}
```

Producer:

```java id="c7f71i"
@ApplicationScoped
public class ExecutorProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    @BackgroundExecutor
    public ExecutorService backgroundExecutor(AppConfig config) {
        int threads = config.backgroundThreads();
        int queueSize = config.backgroundQueueSize();

        BlockingQueue<Runnable> queue = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(queueSize);

        ThreadFactory threadFactory = new ThreadFactory() {
            private final AtomicInteger counter = new AtomicInteger();

            @Override
            public Thread newThread(Runnable runnable) {
                Thread thread = new Thread(
                        runnable,
                        "case-bg-" + counter.incrementAndGet()
                );
                thread.setDaemon(false);
                return thread;
            }
        };

        return new ThreadPoolExecutor(
                threads,
                threads,
                0L,
                TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                queue,
                threadFactory,
                new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy()
        );
    }

    public void shutdown(
            @Disposes @BackgroundExecutor ExecutorService executorService
    ) {
        ExecutorShutdown.shutdownGracefully(
                executorService,
                Duration.ofSeconds(20)
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 8. Graceful Executor Shutdown

Java docs menekankan executor yang tidak lagi dipakai harus di-shutdown agar resource bisa direklaim; `shutdown()` menolak task baru tetapi membiarkan task yang sudah disubmit selesai, sementara `shutdownNow()` mencoba menghentikan task aktif/pending. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/util/concurrent/ExecutorService.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Helper:

```java id="v5xzk7"
public final class ExecutorShutdown {

    private ExecutorShutdown() {
    }

    public static void shutdownGracefully(
            ExecutorService executor,
            Duration timeout
    ) {
        executor.shutdown();

        try {
            boolean terminated = executor.awaitTermination(
                    timeout.toMillis(),
                    TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS
            );

            if (!terminated) {
                List<Runnable> dropped = executor.shutdownNow();

                // Production: log dropped.size()
                executor.awaitTermination(
                        Math.min(timeout.toMillis(), 5_000),
                        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS
                );
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException ex) {
            executor.shutdownNow();
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}
```

Checklist shutdown:

| Step | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| `shutdown()` | Stop menerima task baru |
| `awaitTermination()` | Beri waktu task selesai |
| `shutdownNow()` | Interrupt task yang terlalu lama |
| preserve interrupt | `Thread.currentThread().interrupt()` |
| log dropped task count | Observability |
| cleanup resource | DB/client/channel/executor |

---

# 9. Worker Pattern: Pass Snapshot Explicitly

Jangan:

```java id="im06qf"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncCaseService {

    private final ContextAwareExecutor executor;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public AsyncCaseService(
            ContextAwareExecutor executor,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.executor = executor;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public void processAsync(String caseId) {
        executor.submit(() -> {
            String tenantId = context.tenantId(); // SALAH
        });
    }
}
```

Lebih benar:

```java id="ewpg7m"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncCaseService {

    private final ContextAwareExecutor executor;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final AsyncCaseWorker worker;

    @Inject
    public AsyncCaseService(
            ContextAwareExecutor executor,
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            AsyncCaseWorker worker
    ) {
        this.executor = executor;
        this.context = context;
        this.worker = worker;
    }

    public Future<?> processAsync(String caseId) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        return executor.submit(() -> {
            worker.process(caseId, snapshot);
        });
    }
}
```

Worker:

```java id="z4rwy4"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncCaseWorker {

    private final TransactionalWorkRunner workRunner;
    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;

    @Inject
    public AsyncCaseWorker(
            TransactionalWorkRunner workRunner,
            CaseRepository caseRepository
    ) {
        this.workRunner = workRunner;
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
    }

    public void process(String caseId, RequestContextSnapshot snapshot) {
        workRunner.run(snapshot, () -> {
            CaseRecord record = caseRepository.findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(
                    snapshot.tenantId(),
                    caseId
            );

            record.markProcessedByAsyncWorker(snapshot.userId());

            caseRepository.update(record);
        });
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Transaction in Worker: New Unit-of-Work

Jangan reuse transaction dari request thread.

```text id="gk7vfh"
Request thread transaction:
  starts and ends with HTTP request use-case

Worker thread transaction:
  must start separately inside worker
```

Work runner:

```java id="2zl3iv"
@ApplicationScoped
public class TransactionalWorkRunner {

    private final TransactionExecutor transactionExecutor;

    @Inject
    public TransactionalWorkRunner(TransactionExecutor transactionExecutor) {
        this.transactionExecutor = transactionExecutor;
    }

    public void run(RequestContextSnapshot snapshot, ThrowingRunnable work) {
        try {
            MdcContext.put(snapshot);

            transactionExecutor.executeInNewTransaction(() -> {
                work.run();
                return null;
            });
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Async transactional work failed", ex);
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="3ut1zt"
@FunctionalInterface
public interface ThrowingRunnable {
    void run() throws Exception;
}
```

Transactional executor conceptual:

```java id="ksc3ui"
@ApplicationScoped
public class TransactionExecutor {

    private final SqlSessionProvider sqlSessionProvider;
    private final TransactionContext transactionContext;

    @Inject
    public TransactionExecutor(
            SqlSessionProvider sqlSessionProvider,
            TransactionContext transactionContext
    ) {
        this.sqlSessionProvider = sqlSessionProvider;
        this.transactionContext = transactionContext;
    }

    public <T> T executeInNewTransaction(ThrowingSupplier<T> supplier) throws Exception {
        if (transactionContext.isActive()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Expected no active transaction");
        }

        transactionContext.begin(false, IsolationLevel.DEFAULT);

        try {
            T result = supplier.get();
            sqlSessionProvider.commit();
            return result;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            sqlSessionProvider.rollback();
            throw ex;
        } finally {
            transactionContext.end();
        }
    }
}
```

Important:

```text id="k8lu04"
Kalau worker thread tidak punya CDI @RequestScoped active,
maka TransactionContext/SqlSessionProvider @RequestScoped juga tidak bisa dipakai langsung.
Untuk worker, gunakan scope khusus, manual unit-of-work object, atau container-supported context propagation.
```

Practical alternative yang lebih eksplisit:

```java id="ix55cu"
@ApplicationScoped
public class ManualMyBatisUnitOfWork {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @Inject
    public ManualMyBatisUnitOfWork(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    public <T> T execute(ThrowingFunction<SqlSession, T> work) throws Exception {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
            try {
                T result = work.apply(session);
                session.commit();
                return result;
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                session.rollback();
                throw ex;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

This is often safer for non-HTTP workers.

---

# 11. Fire-and-Forget is Usually a Trap

Bad:

```java id="ax8u0a"
public void approve(String caseId) {
    executor.submit(() -> notificationClient.send(caseId));
}
```

Problems:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Caller tidak tahu gagal | Silent failure |
| Tidak ada retry policy | Notification hilang |
| Tidak ada idempotency | Duplicate saat retry manual |
| Task hilang saat process crash | Tidak durable |
| Shutdown bisa membatalkan task | Side effect tidak pasti |

Better for durable work:

```text id="cqktma"
Inside DB transaction:
  update business state
  insert outbox event

Outbox publisher:
  asynchronously publish notification/event
  retry safely
```

Use executor for:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| CPU/light background enrichment | Critical financial/payment side effect |
| Local cache warmup | Durable business event |
| Non-critical notification best-effort | Compliance audit |
| Parallel independent reads with timeout | Long-running process orchestration |
| Async polling worker | State-changing command without idempotency |

---

# 12. CompletableFuture

`CompletableFuture` is useful, but dangerous if context and executor are implicit.

Bad:

```java id="xy0h0a"
CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> externalClient.call());
```

Problems:

```text id="qi2x80"
Uses common ForkJoinPool by default.
Context not propagated.
Executor not controlled.
Harder to apply service-level backpressure.
```

Better:

```java id="buxwu7"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncLookupService {

    private final ExecutorService executor;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final ExternalClient client;

    @Inject
    public AsyncLookupService(
            @BackgroundExecutor ExecutorService executor,
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            ExternalClient client
    ) {
        this.executor = executor;
        this.context = context;
        this.client = client;
    }

    public CompletableFuture<LookupResult> lookupAsync(String id) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            try {
                MdcContext.put(snapshot);
                return client.lookup(id, snapshot);
            } finally {
                MdcContext.clear();
            }
        }, executor);
    }
}
```

Timeout:

```java id="vhlb07"
public CompletableFuture<LookupResult> lookupWithTimeout(String id) {
    return lookupAsync(id)
            .orTimeout(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .exceptionally(ex -> {
                throw new CompletionException(
                        new ServiceUnavailableException("Lookup timed out", ex)
                );
            });
}
```

Practical rule:

```text id="m2q7dl"
Always provide executor.
Always capture context before creating async stage.
Always set/clear MDC inside async stage.
Always define timeout/failure path.
```

MicroProfile Context Propagation exists specifically to propagate thread context in a type-safe way and reduce boilerplate; its ManagedExecutor/ThreadContext APIs can propagate context automatically for CompletableFuture stages when available in your runtime. ([MicroProfile](https://microprofile.io/specifications/microprofile-context-propagation/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 13. MicroProfile Context Propagation Option

If your stack allows MicroProfile Context Propagation, it can reduce manual wrapper code.

Conceptual example:

```java id="3r2f6u"
ManagedExecutor executor = ManagedExecutor.builder()
        .propagated(ThreadContext.APPLICATION)
        .cleared(ThreadContext.ALL_REMAINING)
        .maxAsync(10)
        .build();
```

The MicroProfile spec defines `ManagedExecutor` and `ThreadContext` builders; examples include propagating selected context and clearing all remaining contexts. ([Eclipse Downloads](https://download.eclipse.org/microprofile/microprofile-context-propagation-1.2/microprofile-context-propagation-spec-1.2.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

But be careful:

```text id="ct3bz8"
Automatic context propagation is useful,
but you still need to decide:
  - which context types are safe to propagate
  - which must be cleared
  - whether CDI/request/security context should cross async boundary
  - how transaction context is handled
```

Recommended stance:

| Context | Propagate Automatically? |
|---|---:|
| MDC/log context | Usually yes |
| tracing context | Usually yes |
| application context | Maybe |
| CDI request context | Be careful |
| security context | Be careful |
| transaction context | No |
| raw token/secret | No |

---

# 14. ThreadLocal: Use Sparingly

ThreadLocal can be useful for MDC or technical context, but it is dangerous in thread pools.

```java id="iu7rdc"
public final class RequestContextHolder {

    private static final ThreadLocal<RequestContextSnapshot> CURRENT =
            new ThreadLocal<>();

    private RequestContextHolder() {
    }

    public static void set(RequestContextSnapshot snapshot) {
        CURRENT.set(snapshot);
    }

    public static RequestContextSnapshot get() {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = CURRENT.get();

        if (snapshot == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("No request context bound");
        }

        return snapshot;
    }

    public static Optional<RequestContextSnapshot> current() {
        return Optional.ofNullable(CURRENT.get());
    }

    public static void clear() {
        CURRENT.remove();
    }
}
```

Safe wrapper:

```java id="y88m2t"
public final class ThreadLocalContextAwareRunnable implements Runnable {

    private final RequestContextSnapshot snapshot;
    private final Runnable delegate;

    public ThreadLocalContextAwareRunnable(
            RequestContextSnapshot snapshot,
            Runnable delegate
    ) {
        this.snapshot = snapshot;
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        try {
            RequestContextHolder.set(snapshot);
            MdcContext.put(snapshot);
            delegate.run();
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
            RequestContextHolder.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="41xqrw"
Every ThreadLocal.set must have clear/remove in finally.
Never store mutable objects in ThreadLocal if they can be shared.
Never store secrets/raw tokens in ThreadLocal.
```

---

# 15. Backpressure: Jangan Terima Semua Task

Unbounded async queue adalah production incident yang menunggu terjadi.

Bad:

```java id="fxepkd"
Executors.newFixedThreadPool(16); // underlying queue is unbounded
```

Safer:

```java id="bar3re"
new ThreadPoolExecutor(
        16,
        16,
        0L,
        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
        new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(1_000),
        threadFactory,
        new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy()
);
```

Handle rejection:

```java id="eyebai"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncSubmissionService {

    private final ContextAwareExecutor executor;

    @Inject
    public AsyncSubmissionService(ContextAwareExecutor executor) {
        this.executor = executor;
    }

    public void submit(String caseId) {
        try {
            executor.execute(() -> process(caseId));
        } catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) {
            throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Background queue is full");
        }
    }

    private void process(String caseId) {
        // work
    }
}
```

Backpressure options:

| Strategy | Use When |
|---|---|
| Reject with 503 | Request can retry later |
| Persist to outbox/table | Work must be durable |
| Drop low-priority task | Best-effort telemetry/enrichment |
| Rate limit upstream | Prevent overload earlier |
| Caller-runs policy | Small bounded internal systems only; careful |
| Separate pools by workload | Avoid slow task starving fast task |

---

# 16. Timeout and Cancellation

Async task must have a timeout story.

For `Future`:

```java id="xg1nky"
Future<Result> future = executor.submit(task);

try {
    return future.get(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
} catch (TimeoutException ex) {
    future.cancel(true);
    throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Task timed out", ex);
}
```

Task should respect interruption:

```java id="5clfa5"
public void longRunningWork() {
    while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
        doSmallUnitOfWork();
    }

    throw new CancellationException("Task interrupted");
}
```

For blocking external calls:

```text id="n8v2h1"
Set client connect timeout.
Set read/request timeout.
Set overall async timeout.
Do not rely only on Future.cancel(true).
```

Important:

```text id="pqvyyr"
Thread interruption is cooperative.
If task ignores interrupts or blocks on non-interruptible IO,
shutdown/cancel may not stop it quickly.
```

---

# 17. Race Conditions and Shared Mutable State

Bad:

```java id="3d2h5q"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncAccumulator {

    private final List<String> processedIds = new ArrayList<>();

    public void add(String id) {
        processedIds.add(id);
    }
}
```

Problems:

```text id="e77jhu"
ArrayList is not thread-safe.
ApplicationScoped bean is shared.
Concurrent writes can corrupt state.
```

Safer options:

```java id="ff0zfa"
private final Queue<String> processedIds = new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>();
```

or:

```java id="fsz27o"
private final List<String> processedIds =
        Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
```

But better:

```text id="wwizvp"
Avoid shared mutable in service.
Persist state to DB/event/outbox.
Use immutable value objects.
Use local variables inside task.
```

Concurrency checklist:

| Object | Safe? |
|---|---:|
| immutable DTO/record | Yes |
| `ConcurrentHashMap` with clear ownership | Usually |
| `ArrayList` shared in singleton | No |
| mutable field in `@ApplicationScoped` service | Usually no |
| `SqlSession` shared | No |
| configured `ObjectMapper` | Usually yes after configuration |
| Java `HttpClient` | Designed for reuse |
| request DTO reused across threads | No if mutable |

---

# 18. Parallel Calls Pattern

Sometimes you want parallel independent reads.

```java id="v1wd4m"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseDashboardService {

    private final ExecutorService executor;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final CaseClient caseClient;
    private final TaskClient taskClient;

    @Inject
    public CaseDashboardService(
            @BackgroundExecutor ExecutorService executor,
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            CaseClient caseClient,
            TaskClient taskClient
    ) {
        this.executor = executor;
        this.context = context;
        this.caseClient = caseClient;
        this.taskClient = taskClient;
    }

    public DashboardDto loadDashboard(String caseId) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        CompletableFuture<CaseDto> caseFuture =
                CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> callWithContext(
                        snapshot,
                        () -> caseClient.getCase(caseId, snapshot)
                ), executor);

        CompletableFuture<List<TaskDto>> taskFuture =
                CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> callWithContext(
                        snapshot,
                        () -> taskClient.getTasks(caseId, snapshot)
                ), executor);

        return caseFuture
                .thenCombine(taskFuture, DashboardDto::new)
                .orTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
                .join();
    }

    private <T> T callWithContext(
            RequestContextSnapshot snapshot,
            Supplier<T> supplier
    ) {
        try {
            MdcContext.put(snapshot);
            return supplier.get();
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Rules:

```text id="cdw87b"
Parallel reads can improve latency if calls are independent.
Do not parallelize DB writes under same transaction using same SqlSession.
Do not use same mutable request/response object across futures.
```

---

# 19. Virtual Threads: Useful, But Not a Context Strategy

Java 21 finalized virtual threads; Oracle describes them as lightweight threads that can reduce effort for high-throughput concurrent applications. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/core/virtual-threads.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Virtual threads can help with blocking IO scalability:

```java id="6pjw8e"
ExecutorService executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor();
```

But virtual threads do **not** remove context design requirements:

| Misconception | Reality |
|---|---|
| Virtual threads solve context propagation | No, context still must be captured/propagated intentionally |
| Virtual threads make ThreadLocal always safe | No, ThreadLocal usage can still leak or become costly if misused |
| Virtual threads remove timeout need | No |
| Virtual threads remove DB pool limits | No, DB connections are still limited |
| Virtual threads make external calls safe inside transaction | No |

For Java 17 baseline, virtual threads are not available as stable GA. For Java 21+ runtime, they are worth evaluating, but only after context, DB pool, timeout, and backpressure model are clear.

---

# 20. Async + Camunda/Kafka

Camunda jobs and Kafka consumers are already asynchronous entry points. Treat them as separate request models.

## Message Context

```java id="94p85w"
public record MessageContextSnapshot(
        String messageId,
        String causationId,
        String correlationId,
        String traceParent,
        String tenantId,
        String producerService
) {
}
```

Kafka handler:

```java id="58wkjh"
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseEventConsumer {

    private final KafkaMessageContextExtractor contextExtractor;
    private final ManualMyBatisUnitOfWork unitOfWork;
    private final CaseEventHandler handler;

    @Inject
    public CaseEventConsumer(
            KafkaMessageContextExtractor contextExtractor,
            ManualMyBatisUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
            CaseEventHandler handler
    ) {
        this.contextExtractor = contextExtractor;
        this.unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        this.handler = handler;
    }

    public void onMessage(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record) throws Exception {
        MessageContextSnapshot snapshot = contextExtractor.extract(record);

        try {
            MdcContext.put(snapshot);

            unitOfWork.execute(session -> {
                handler.handle(record.value(), snapshot, session);
                return null;
            });
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Camunda delegate pattern:

```java id="yc012w"
@ApplicationScoped
public class NotifyDelegate implements JavaDelegate {

    private final ManualMyBatisUnitOfWork unitOfWork;
    private final NotificationWorker worker;

    @Inject
    public NotifyDelegate(
            ManualMyBatisUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
            NotificationWorker worker
    ) {
        this.unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        this.worker = worker;
    }

    @Override
    public void execute(DelegateExecution execution) throws Exception {
        WorkflowContextSnapshot snapshot =
                WorkflowContextSnapshot.from(execution);

        try {
            MdcContext.put(snapshot.toMessageContext());

            unitOfWork.execute(session -> {
                worker.notify(snapshot, session);
                return null;
            });
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="u77mi6"
Kafka/Camunda/scheduler should not depend on HTTP CurrentRequestContext.
They need their own MessageContextSnapshot / WorkflowContextSnapshot.
```

---

# 21. Error Handling for Async Tasks

Every async task must have an error path.

Bad:

```java id="m129zv"
executor.submit(() -> {
    riskyWork();
});
```

If no one reads the `Future`, failure may be lost.

Better wrapper:

```java id="t85cr8"
public final class SafeRunnable implements Runnable {

    private final Runnable delegate;
    private final Consumer<Throwable> errorHandler;

    public SafeRunnable(
            Runnable delegate,
            Consumer<Throwable> errorHandler
    ) {
        this.delegate = delegate;
        this.errorHandler = errorHandler;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        try {
            delegate.run();
        } catch (Throwable throwable) {
            errorHandler.accept(throwable);
            throw throwable;
        }
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java id="n7wvdd"
executor.submit(new ContextAwareRunnable(
        snapshot,
        new SafeRunnable(
                () -> worker.process(caseId, snapshot),
                ex -> asyncErrorReporter.report("case-async-process", ex, snapshot)
        )
));
```

Async error reporter:

```java id="8a301o"
@ApplicationScoped
public class AsyncErrorReporter {

    public void report(
            String operation,
            Throwable throwable,
            RequestContextSnapshot snapshot
    ) {
        // log with correlation/tenant
        // increment metric
        // optionally insert failed job record
    }
}
```

---

# 22. Safe Shutdown + In-Flight Work

For Kubernetes/on-prem deployment, shutdown must be coordinated.

```text id="2ee11l"
SIGTERM
  ├── mark app not ready
  ├── stop accepting new HTTP traffic
  ├── stop accepting new async tasks
  ├── wait for in-flight tasks
  ├── flush outbox/metrics/logs if applicable
  └── close clients/executors
```

Implementation concepts:

```java id="ywurki"
@ApplicationScoped
public class ShutdownState {

    private final AtomicBoolean shuttingDown = new AtomicBoolean(false);

    public boolean isShuttingDown() {
        return shuttingDown.get();
    }

    public void beginShutdown() {
        shuttingDown.set(true);
    }
}
```

Submission guard:

```java id="bxxhza"
@ApplicationScoped
public class GuardedAsyncSubmitter {

    private final ShutdownState shutdownState;
    private final ContextAwareExecutor executor;

    @Inject
    public GuardedAsyncSubmitter(
            ShutdownState shutdownState,
            ContextAwareExecutor executor
    ) {
        this.shutdownState = shutdownState;
        this.executor = executor;
    }

    public void submit(Runnable task) {
        if (shutdownState.isShuttingDown()) {
            throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Service is shutting down");
        }

        executor.execute(task);
    }
}
```

---

# 23. Anti-pattern

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Access `@RequestScoped` from worker | `context.tenantId()` inside executor task | Context not active |
| Rely on MDC auto-propagation | No wrapper | Missing/stale logs |
| No MDC clear | `MDC.put` only | Cross-request log leak |
| Unbounded queue | default fixed thread pool queue | Memory explosion |
| Fire-and-forget critical task | email/payment/audit in executor | Lost/duplicate side effect |
| Reuse `SqlSession` across threads | pass session to worker | Corruption |
| Retry non-idempotent task | retry payment/email | Duplicate side effect |
| No timeout | blocking external call | Thread starvation |
| Swallow task exception | catch and ignore | Silent data loss |
| One pool for everything | slow task blocks fast task | Starvation |
| Shared mutable singleton | `ArrayList` in `@ApplicationScoped` | Race condition |
| Shutdown absent | no disposer | Thread leak |
| Use common pool blindly | `supplyAsync()` no executor | No isolation/backpressure |

---

# 24. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Context capture | Apakah async boundary membuat immutable snapshot? |
| CDI scope | Apakah worker tidak mengakses `@RequestScoped` bean langsung? |
| MDC | Apakah MDC diset dan di-clear di `finally`? |
| Executor | Apakah executor bounded dan punya nama thread? |
| Queue | Apakah queue size eksplisit? |
| Rejection | Apakah rejection diterjemahkan ke 503/degrade/durable queue? |
| Timeout | Apakah task/external call punya timeout? |
| Cancellation | Apakah interrupt dipertahankan dan dihormati? |
| DB | Apakah worker membuka unit-of-work baru? |
| Transaction | Apakah transaction tidak dipropagate lintas thread? |
| Retry | Apakah retry hanya untuk idempotent/safe work? |
| Errors | Apakah async failure dilog/metric/durable? |
| Shutdown | Apakah executor punya disposer/graceful shutdown? |
| Pools | Apakah workload lambat dan cepat dipisah? |
| Tests | Apakah ada test context leak/lost context/rejection/shutdown? |

---

# 25. Testing Strategy

## Test 1 - Context propagated to worker log

```text id="sp9r7q"
Given request correlationId=corr-001 tenant=tenant-a
When async task runs
Then worker MDC contains corr-001 and tenant-a
And MDC is cleared after task
```

## Test 2 - No request context access in worker

```text id="oujqg6"
Given worker receives RequestContextSnapshot
When HTTP request has ended
Then worker still uses snapshot
And does not call CurrentRequestContext
```

## Test 3 - Queue rejection

```text id="cnckod"
Given executor queue full
When submit async task
Then service returns 503 or persists durable job
And does not block indefinitely
```

## Test 4 - Timeout

```text id="ld9i12"
Given external call hangs
When async flow waits with timeout
Then future is cancelled/failed
And error is reported with correlation ID
```

## Test 5 - Shutdown

```text id="zq93g3"
Given running task
When shutdown begins
Then no new task accepted
And executor waits for existing task
And eventually interrupts if timeout exceeded
```

## Test 6 - Race condition

```text id="ganw78"
Run 1000 concurrent tasks
Assert:
  no shared mutable state corruption
  tenant context not mixed
  all failures visible
```

# 27. Roadmap Latihan Praktis Seri 9

Urutan latihan:

```text id="uzn57k"
1. Buat RequestContextSnapshot immutable
2. Buat MdcContext helper
3. Buat ContextAwareRunnable dan ContextAwareCallable
4. Buat @BackgroundExecutor qualifier
5. Buat bounded ExecutorService producer
6. Tambahkan disposer graceful shutdown
7. Buat ContextAwareExecutor wrapper
8. Refactor async service agar pass snapshot eksplisit
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 9

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. Async/multithread adalah boundary baru; context tidak otomatis ikut.
2. Jangan membawa CDI `@RequestScoped` bean ke worker thread.
3. Bawa immutable `RequestContextSnapshot`, `MessageContextSnapshot`, atau `WorkflowContextSnapshot`.
4. MDC harus diset di awal task dan di-clear di `finally`.
5. Executor harus bounded, punya thread name, queue size eksplisit, rejection policy, dan graceful shutdown.
6. Jangan memakai `CompletableFuture.supplyAsync()` tanpa executor eksplisit.
7. Worker yang butuh DB harus membuka unit-of-work/transaction sendiri.
8. Transaction context, `SqlSession`, `Connection`, dan lock DB tidak boleh dipropagate lintas thread.
9. Fire-and-forget hanya aman untuk non-critical best-effort; untuk side effect penting gunakan outbox/durable job.
10. Timeout, cancellation, rejection, retry, dan error reporting harus didesain dari awal.
11. Virtual threads dapat membantu skala blocking IO di Java 21+, tetapi bukan pengganti context propagation, timeout, DB pool, dan backpressure.
12. Untuk Kafka/Camunda/scheduler, gunakan context model sendiri; jangan bergantung pada HTTP request context.

**Seri 9/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 10 - Service-to-Service Propagation: HTTP, gRPC, Kafka Headers**, dengan fokus pada context contract, safe header forwarding, auth boundary, tracing propagation, retry/idempotency, client wrapper, server interceptor, Kafka producer/consumer headers, dan failure modelling.

## Response:
7/15/2026, 4:16:50 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 10/14**. Fokusnya: service-to-service propagation untuk HTTP, gRPC, dan Kafka—context contract, header/metadata aman, tracing, auth boundary, idempotency, retry, dan failure modelling.

# Seri 10/14 - Service-to-Service Propagation: HTTP, gRPC, Kafka Headers

Target seri ini: kamu bisa mendesain **propagasi context antar service** secara aman untuk aplikasi **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Camunda + HTTP/gRPC/Kafka**.

Fokusnya:

> Saat service A memanggil service B, atau publish event ke Kafka, tidak semua context boleh diteruskan. Yang diteruskan harus **terstandar, tervalidasi, minimal, aman, dan observable**.

W3C Trace Context mendefinisikan header standar seperti `traceparent` dan `tracestate` untuk propagasi distributed tracing antar sistem. gRPC metadata dipakai untuk membawa custom headers/metadata antar client-server RPC. Kafka `ProducerRecord` juga mendukung headers untuk membawa metadata tambahan bersama message. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Service-to-service propagation harus dibagi menjadi tiga lapisan:

```text
Inbound Context
  = context yang diterima dari caller
  = raw headers / metadata / message headers

Trusted Runtime Context
  = context yang sudah divalidasi dan dinormalisasi
  = CurrentRequestContext / MessageContextSnapshot / WorkflowContextSnapshot

Outbound Propagation Context
  = subset aman yang diteruskan ke downstream
  = correlation, trace, tenant, causation, idempotency bila relevan
```

Prinsip utamanya:

```text
Do not forward everything.
Do not trust everything.
Do not lose correlation.
Do not propagate transaction/session.
Do not propagate raw secrets/tokens blindly.
```

---

# 2. Diagram

```text
Service A
│
├── Inbound Boundary
│   ├── HTTP headers
│   ├── gRPC metadata
│   └── Kafka headers
│
├── Context Normalization
│   ├── validate correlation ID
│   ├── validate traceparent
│   ├── authenticate caller
│   ├── validate tenant
│   └── build trusted context
│
├── Service Logic
│   ├── authorization
│   ├── feature flag
│   ├── DB transaction
│   ├── audit
│   └── outbox
│
├── Outbound Propagation
│   ├── HTTP client filter/wrapper
│   ├── gRPC client interceptor
│   └── Kafka producer headers
│
└── Service B / Consumer
    ├── receives propagated context
    ├── validates trust boundary
    ├── creates local trusted context
    └── continues processing
```

---

# 3. Context Contract

Standardisasi key lebih penting daripada sekadar “pakai header”.

## 3.1 Common Context Keys

| Context | HTTP Header | gRPC Metadata | Kafka Header | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Correlation ID | `X-Correlation-Id` | `x-correlation-id` | `x-correlation-id` | Untuk log, audit, debugging |
| Trace Context | `traceparent` | `traceparent` | `traceparent` | Gunakan standar W3C bila tracing aktif |
| Trace State | `tracestate` | `tracestate` | `tracestate` | Optional, preserve sesuai policy |
| Tenant ID | `X-Tenant-Id` | `x-tenant-id` | `x-tenant-id` | Internal/trusted only |
| User ID | `X-User-Id` | `x-user-id` | `x-user-id` | Internal only, jangan untuk public trust |
| Causation ID | `X-Causation-Id` | `x-causation-id` | `x-causation-id` | Untuk event/message chain |
| Message ID | N/A | N/A | `x-message-id` | Required untuk Kafka/event |
| Idempotency Key | `Idempotency-Key` | `idempotency-key` | `x-idempotency-key` | Hanya untuk command semantics |
| Producer Service | N/A | N/A | `x-producer-service` | Debug event source |
| Schema Version | N/A | N/A | `x-schema-version` | Consumer compatibility |

W3C `traceparent`/`tracestate` sebaiknya dibiarkan ditangani oleh tracing library jika perusahaan sudah memakai OpenTelemetry/APM. Jangan membuat trace propagation custom kalau library tracing sudah ada. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/trace-context/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. Apa yang Boleh dan Tidak Boleh Dipropagate

| Data | Propagate? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| `correlationId` | Ya | Observability dan audit chain |
| `traceparent` | Ya | Distributed tracing |
| `tenantId` | Ya, internal/trusted only | Tenant-aware downstream |
| `userId` | Kadang | Perlu trust boundary jelas |
| `roles/scopes` | Kadang | Bisa stale/overexposed; lebih baik downstream authorize ulang |
| raw `Authorization` header | Jangan default | Token forwarding bisa overprivileged |
| raw JWT | Hati-hati | Pakai token exchange/service credential bila perlu |
| password/secret | Tidak | Security leak |
| `SqlSession`/DB connection | Tidak | Local transaction only |
| transaction context | Tidak | Tidak bisa lintas process/service |
| request DTO mentah | Tidak sebagai context | Bisa PII/besar/tidak stabil |
| business key | Ya | Workflow/event correlation |
| idempotency key | Hanya command | Jangan propagate ke unrelated operation |

---

# 5. HTTP Propagation

Jersey mendukung filter/interceptor di server side dan client side; filter cocok untuk request/response metadata, sedangkan interceptor lebih dekat ke entity stream. Untuk outbound HTTP, pattern praktisnya adalah memakai wrapper atau `ClientRequestFilter`. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/filters-and-interceptors.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.1 Propagation Policy

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PropagationPolicy {

    public boolean shouldPropagateUserId(URI target) {
        return isInternalService(target);
    }

    public boolean shouldPropagateTenantId(URI target) {
        return isInternalService(target);
    }

    public boolean shouldForwardAuthorization(URI target) {
        return false; // default deny
    }

    private boolean isInternalService(URI target) {
        String host = target.getHost();
        return host != null && host.endsWith(".svc.cluster.local");
    }
}
```

Default stance:

```text
Forward correlation and trace.
Forward tenant only to trusted internal services.
Do not forward raw Authorization by default.
```

---

## 5.2 Context-Aware HTTP Client Wrapper

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ContextPropagatingHttpClient {

    private final HttpClient httpClient;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final PropagationPolicy propagationPolicy;

    @Inject
    public ContextPropagatingHttpClient(
            HttpClient httpClient,
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            PropagationPolicy propagationPolicy
    ) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.context = context;
        this.propagationPolicy = propagationPolicy;
    }

    public HttpResponse<String> send(HttpRequest.Builder builder, URI target)
            throws IOException, InterruptedException {

        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        builder.header("X-Correlation-Id", snapshot.correlationId());

        if (snapshot.traceParent() != null && !snapshot.traceParent().isBlank()) {
            builder.header("traceparent", snapshot.traceParent());
        }

        if (propagationPolicy.shouldPropagateTenantId(target)) {
            builder.header("X-Tenant-Id", snapshot.tenantId());
        }

        if (propagationPolicy.shouldPropagateUserId(target)) {
            builder.header("X-User-Id", snapshot.userId());
        }

        HttpRequest request = builder.uri(target).build();

        return httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
    }
}
```

---

## 5.3 Jersey ClientRequestFilter Alternative

Kalau menggunakan Jersey Client:

```java
@Provider
public class ContextClientRequestFilter implements ClientRequestFilter {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final PropagationPolicy propagationPolicy;

    @Inject
    public ContextClientRequestFilter(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            PropagationPolicy propagationPolicy
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.propagationPolicy = propagationPolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ClientRequestContext requestContext) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();
        URI target = requestContext.getUri();

        requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                "X-Correlation-Id",
                snapshot.correlationId()
        );

        if (snapshot.traceParent() != null && !snapshot.traceParent().isBlank()) {
            requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                    "traceparent",
                    snapshot.traceParent()
            );
        }

        if (propagationPolicy.shouldPropagateTenantId(target)) {
            requestContext.getHeaders().putSingle(
                    "X-Tenant-Id",
                    snapshot.tenantId()
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Register ke Jersey client:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class JerseyClientProducer {

    private final ContextClientRequestFilter contextFilter;

    @Inject
    public JerseyClientProducer(ContextClientRequestFilter contextFilter) {
        this.contextFilter = contextFilter;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public Client client() {
        return ClientBuilder.newBuilder()
                .register(contextFilter)
                .build();
    }
}
```

---

# 6. HTTP Inbound Validation di Service B

Service B tidak boleh langsung percaya header dari Service A kecuali trust boundary jelas.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class InboundContextValidator {

    public String validateCorrelationId(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        if (value.length() > 128 || !value.matches("[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+")) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Invalid correlation ID");
        }

        return value;
    }

    public String validateTenantId(String tenantId, AuthenticatedPrincipal principal) {
        if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()) {
            return principal.defaultTenantId();
        }

        if (!principal.allowedTenantIds().contains(tenantId)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Tenant not allowed");
        }

        return tenantId;
    }
}
```

Important:

```text
Internal header is not security by itself.
It is only trustworthy if source authentication is trustworthy:
  - mTLS
  - service mesh identity
  - signed internal token
  - trusted gateway
  - private network + strong boundary controls
```

---

# 7. gRPC Propagation

gRPC metadata dapat dipakai untuk membawa custom headers/metadata seperti tracing, auth, atau application-specific data. gRPC menggunakan HTTP/2 headers dan trailers, dan metadata aplikasi terintegrasi dengan mekanisme tersebut. ([gRPC](https://grpc.io/docs/guides/metadata/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 7.1 Metadata Keys

```java
public final class GrpcMetadataKeys {

    private GrpcMetadataKeys() {
    }

    public static final Metadata.Key<String> CORRELATION_ID =
            Metadata.Key.of("x-correlation-id", Metadata.ASCII_STRING_MARSHALLER);

    public static final Metadata.Key<String> TENANT_ID =
            Metadata.Key.of("x-tenant-id", Metadata.ASCII_STRING_MARSHALLER);

    public static final Metadata.Key<String> USER_ID =
            Metadata.Key.of("x-user-id", Metadata.ASCII_STRING_MARSHALLER);

    public static final Metadata.Key<String> TRACEPARENT =
            Metadata.Key.of("traceparent", Metadata.ASCII_STRING_MARSHALLER);
}
```

---

## 7.2 gRPC Client Interceptor

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ContextGrpcClientInterceptor implements ClientInterceptor {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final GrpcPropagationPolicy propagationPolicy;

    @Inject
    public ContextGrpcClientInterceptor(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            GrpcPropagationPolicy propagationPolicy
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.propagationPolicy = propagationPolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public <ReqT, RespT> ClientCall<ReqT, RespT> interceptCall(
            MethodDescriptor<ReqT, RespT> method,
            CallOptions callOptions,
            Channel next
    ) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        return new ForwardingClientCall.SimpleForwardingClientCall<>(
                next.newCall(method, callOptions)
        ) {
            @Override
            public void start(Listener<RespT> responseListener, Metadata headers) {
                headers.put(GrpcMetadataKeys.CORRELATION_ID, snapshot.correlationId());

                if (snapshot.traceParent() != null && !snapshot.traceParent().isBlank()) {
                    headers.put(GrpcMetadataKeys.TRACEPARENT, snapshot.traceParent());
                }

                if (propagationPolicy.shouldPropagateTenant(method)) {
                    headers.put(GrpcMetadataKeys.TENANT_ID, snapshot.tenantId());
                }

                if (propagationPolicy.shouldPropagateUser(method)) {
                    headers.put(GrpcMetadataKeys.USER_ID, snapshot.userId());
                }

                super.start(responseListener, headers);
            }
        };
    }
}
```

---

## 7.3 gRPC Server Interceptor

Server-side interceptor membaca metadata, validasi, lalu membuat context lokal.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ContextGrpcServerInterceptor implements ServerInterceptor {

    private final GrpcInboundContextValidator validator;

    @Inject
    public ContextGrpcServerInterceptor(GrpcInboundContextValidator validator) {
        this.validator = validator;
    }

    @Override
    public <ReqT, RespT> ServerCall.Listener<ReqT> interceptCall(
            ServerCall<ReqT, RespT> call,
            Metadata headers,
            ServerCallHandler<ReqT, RespT> next
    ) {
        String correlationId = validator.correlationId(
                headers.get(GrpcMetadataKeys.CORRELATION_ID)
        );

        String tenantId = validator.tenantId(
                headers.get(GrpcMetadataKeys.TENANT_ID)
        );

        String traceParent = headers.get(GrpcMetadataKeys.TRACEPARENT);

        MessageContextHolder.set(new ServiceCallContextSnapshot(
                correlationId,
                traceParent,
                tenantId
        ));

        try {
            return next.startCall(call, headers);
        } finally {
            // Untuk streaming/async listener, cleanup biasanya perlu wrapper listener,
            // bukan finally sederhana. Jangan asal copy pattern ini untuk streaming RPC.
        }
    }
}
```

Untuk unary sederhana, cleanup bisa dikelola di wrapper listener. Untuk streaming, lifecycle lebih panjang dan perlu desain khusus.

---

# 8. Kafka Propagation

Kafka `ProducerRecord` mendukung constructor dengan `Iterable<Header>` dan record headers. Ini cocok untuk membawa metadata seperti `x-correlation-id`, `x-tenant-id`, `x-message-id`, dan `x-schema-version`. ([Apache Kafka](https://kafka.apache.org/25/javadoc/org/apache/kafka/clients/producer/ProducerRecord.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 8.1 Kafka Header Contract

| Header | Required | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `x-message-id` | Ya | Unique ID per message/event |
| `x-correlation-id` | Ya | Trace chain |
| `x-causation-id` | Recommended | Command/event penyebab |
| `x-tenant-id` | Ya untuk multi-tenant | Consumer isolation |
| `traceparent` | Recommended | Distributed tracing |
| `x-schema-version` | Ya | Consumer compatibility |
| `x-producer-service` | Recommended | Debugging |
| `x-event-type` | Optional jika topic multi-event | Routing |
| `x-idempotency-key` | Conditional | Command/event replay semantics |

---

## 8.2 Producer Helper

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class KafkaPropagationHeaders {

    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public KafkaPropagationHeaders(
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            AppConfig config
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.config = config;
    }

    public Headers fromCurrentContext(
            String eventType,
            String schemaVersion,
            String causationId
    ) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        RecordHeaders headers = new RecordHeaders();

        add(headers, "x-message-id", UUID.randomUUID().toString());
        add(headers, "x-correlation-id", snapshot.correlationId());
        add(headers, "x-tenant-id", snapshot.tenantId());
        add(headers, "x-user-id", snapshot.userId());
        add(headers, "x-producer-service", config.serviceName());
        add(headers, "x-event-type", eventType);
        add(headers, "x-schema-version", schemaVersion);

        if (causationId != null && !causationId.isBlank()) {
            add(headers, "x-causation-id", causationId);
        }

        if (snapshot.traceParent() != null && !snapshot.traceParent().isBlank()) {
            add(headers, "traceparent", snapshot.traceParent());
        }

        return headers;
    }

    private void add(Headers headers, String key, String value) {
        if (value != null && !value.isBlank()) {
            headers.add(key, value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
    }
}
```

Producer usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseEventPublisher {

    private final KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer;
    private final KafkaPropagationHeaders headerFactory;

    @Inject
    public CaseEventPublisher(
            KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer,
            KafkaPropagationHeaders headerFactory
    ) {
        this.producer = producer;
        this.headerFactory = headerFactory;
    }

    public void publishCaseApproved(CaseApprovedEvent event) {
        Headers headers = headerFactory.fromCurrentContext(
                "CASE_APPROVED",
                "1",
                event.commandId()
        );

        ProducerRecord<String, byte[]> record = new ProducerRecord<>(
                "case.events",
                null,
                event.caseId(),
                serialize(event),
                headers
        );

        producer.send(record);
    }

    private byte[] serialize(CaseApprovedEvent event) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Serialize event");
    }
}
```

---

## 8.3 Consumer Extractor

Kafka consumer tidak berada dalam HTTP request context. Buat `MessageContextSnapshot`.

```java
public record MessageContextSnapshot(
        String messageId,
        String correlationId,
        String causationId,
        String traceParent,
        String tenantId,
        String producerService,
        String eventType,
        String schemaVersion
) {
}
```

Extractor:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class KafkaMessageContextExtractor {

    public MessageContextSnapshot extract(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record) {
        return new MessageContextSnapshot(
                required(record, "x-message-id"),
                optional(record, "x-correlation-id")
                        .orElse(UUID.randomUUID().toString()),
                optional(record, "x-causation-id").orElse(null),
                optional(record, "traceparent").orElse(null),
                required(record, "x-tenant-id"),
                optional(record, "x-producer-service").orElse("unknown"),
                optional(record, "x-event-type").orElse("unknown"),
                required(record, "x-schema-version")
        );
    }

    private String required(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record, String key) {
        return optional(record, key)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("Missing Kafka header: " + key));
    }

    private Optional<String> optional(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record, String key) {
        Header header = record.headers().lastHeader(key);

        if (header == null || header.value() == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        return Optional.of(new String(header.value(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }
}
```

Consumer usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseEventConsumer {

    private final KafkaMessageContextExtractor contextExtractor;
    private final ManualMyBatisUnitOfWork unitOfWork;
    private final CaseEventHandler handler;

    @Inject
    public CaseEventConsumer(
            KafkaMessageContextExtractor contextExtractor,
            ManualMyBatisUnitOfWork unitOfWork,
            CaseEventHandler handler
    ) {
        this.contextExtractor = contextExtractor;
        this.unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
        this.handler = handler;
    }

    public void onMessage(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record) throws Exception {
        MessageContextSnapshot snapshot = contextExtractor.extract(record);

        try {
            MdcContext.put(snapshot);

            unitOfWork.execute(session -> {
                handler.handle(record.value(), snapshot, session);
                return null;
            });
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Auth Boundary: Jangan Blind Forward Token

Ini area paling rawan.

## 9.1 Pilihan Auth Propagation

| Model | Cocok Untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Forward user token | User-delegated downstream call | Token overexposure |
| Token exchange | Service B butuh delegated user context | Lebih aman, tapi butuh infra |
| Service-to-service token | Internal service auth | User context harus dibawa terpisah bila perlu |
| mTLS/service mesh identity | Strong internal identity | Perlu platform support |
| Signed internal context token | Propagate tenant/user safely | Perlu key rotation/verifier |
| No auth propagation, only event context | Kafka async event | Consumer authorize by event trust + tenant |

Practical default:

```text
HTTP/gRPC sync:
  use service credential for service identity
  propagate user/tenant as validated context only when needed

Kafka async:
  use message headers + signed/enforced producer trust
  do not put raw Authorization token in message
```

---

# 10. Idempotency Across Services

Idempotency key tidak boleh asal diteruskan ke semua downstream.

## 10.1 Rules

| Scenario | Propagate Idempotency Key? |
|---|---:|
| API Gateway → Payment Service command | Ya |
| Case Service → Payment Service capture command | Ya, operation-scoped |
| Case Service → Notification read preference | Tidak |
| Query/read request | Biasanya tidak |
| Kafka event publication | Gunakan `x-message-id`/event id, bukan request idempotency key mentah |
| Retried HTTP POST to downstream | Ya, jika downstream mendukung |

## 10.2 Derived Idempotency Key

Jangan pakai key yang sama untuk semua downstream operation tanpa scope.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class IdempotencyKeyDeriver {

    public String derive(
            String upstreamKey,
            String downstreamOperation,
            String targetId
    ) {
        String material = upstreamKey + ":" + downstreamOperation + ":" + targetId;

        return sha256(material);
    }

    private String sha256(String value) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Implement SHA-256");
    }
}
```

Example:

```text
Incoming:
  Idempotency-Key: req-abc

Downstream payment:
  Idempotency-Key: sha256(req-abc:PAYMENT_CAPTURE:payment-123)

Downstream document generation:
  Idempotency-Key: sha256(req-abc:DOC_GENERATE:case-456)
```

---

# 11. Retry and Timeout Across Services

Retry harus selaras dengan idempotency.

## 11.1 Retry Matrix

| Operation | Retry? | Syarat |
|---|---:|---|
| HTTP GET | Ya | Timeout pendek, circuit breaker |
| HTTP POST create | Hati-hati | Idempotency key |
| Payment capture | Hati-hati | Provider idempotency |
| Notification send | Hati-hati | Dedup key |
| gRPC unary read | Ya | Deadline |
| gRPC command | Hati-hati | Idempotency/deadline |
| Kafka publish | Ya | Producer config + event id |
| Kafka consume | Ya | Idempotent consumer |

## 11.2 Timeout Budget

```text
Incoming HTTP timeout: 5s
  ├── service local validation: 100ms
  ├── DB query: 500ms
  ├── downstream A: 1s
  ├── downstream B: 1s
  ├── margin/retry: 1s
  └── response serialization: 100ms
```

Jangan membuat downstream timeout lebih panjang dari upstream request timeout.

```java
public record TimeoutBudget(
        Duration total,
        Instant deadline
) {
    public Duration remaining() {
        Duration remaining = Duration.between(Instant.now(), deadline);
        return remaining.isNegative() ? Duration.ZERO : remaining;
    }
}
```

---

# 12. Circuit Breaker / Bulkhead Boundary

Walau detail circuit breaker bisa jadi seri lain, untuk propagation perlu tahu:

```text
Context propagation must still happen when:
  - retry happens
  - fallback happens
  - circuit is open
  - request is rejected by bulkhead
```

Failure response harus tetap membawa correlation ID.

```java
try {
    return downstreamClient.call(request, snapshot);
} catch (BulkheadFullException ex) {
    throw new ServiceUnavailableException(
            "Downstream capacity exhausted. correlationId=" + snapshot.correlationId(),
            ex
    );
}
```

Jangan log raw payload/secret saat fallback.

---

# 13. Service-to-Service Failure Modelling

## 13.1 HTTP/gRPC Failure Categories

| Failure | Meaning | Response |
|---|---|---|
| Timeout | Downstream too slow/unknown outcome | retry if safe, else fail with correlation |
| Connection refused | Downstream unavailable | circuit breaker/retry |
| 401/403 | Auth/service identity issue | do not retry blindly |
| 404 | Resource absent or hidden | depends on domain |
| 409 | State conflict | surface conflict or retry if version conflict safe |
| 429 | Rate limited | backoff/retry-after |
| 5xx | downstream error | retry if safe |
| malformed response | contract violation | fail fast + alert |
| partial success | dangerous | idempotency/reconciliation needed |

## 13.2 Kafka Failure Categories

| Failure | Meaning | Response |
|---|---|---|
| Missing tenant header | Bad/poison message | DLQ/quarantine |
| Unknown schema version | Consumer incompatible | DLQ/park |
| Duplicate message ID | Replay | idempotent ignore |
| Handler exception | Processing failed | retry/backoff |
| Deserialization error | Bad payload | DLQ |
| DB conflict | State race | retry if safe |
| External side effect failed | Do not commit offset unless durable retry exists | outbox/job table |

---

# 14. Idempotent Consumer Pattern

Consumer harus siap menerima duplicate.

```sql
CREATE TABLE processed_message (
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    message_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, message_id)
);
```

Repository:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ProcessedMessageRepository {

    private final SqlSessionProvider sessionProvider;

    @Inject
    public ProcessedMessageRepository(SqlSessionProvider sessionProvider) {
        this.sessionProvider = sessionProvider;
    }

    public boolean tryMarkProcessed(String tenantId, String messageId) {
        int inserted = mapper().insertProcessedMessage(tenantId, messageId);
        return inserted == 1;
    }

    private ProcessedMessageMapper mapper() {
        return sessionProvider.currentSession()
                .getMapper(ProcessedMessageMapper.class);
    }
}
```

SQL:

```sql
INSERT INTO processed_message (tenant_id, message_id)
VALUES (#{tenantId}, #{messageId})
ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, message_id)
DO NOTHING
```

Handler:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public void handle(CaseApprovedEvent event, MessageContextSnapshot context) {
    boolean firstTime = processedMessageRepository.tryMarkProcessed(
            context.tenantId(),
            context.messageId()
    );

    if (!firstTime) {
        return;
    }

    // process event safely
}
```

---

# 15. Outbox + Propagation

Untuk event publication yang reliable:

```text
Business Transaction
  ├── update business row
  ├── insert outbox_event
  │   ├── event_id
  │   ├── tenant_id
  │   ├── correlation_id
  │   ├── causation_id
  │   ├── event_type
  │   ├── schema_version
  │   └── payload
  └── commit

Outbox Publisher
  ├── read NEW outbox rows
  ├── create Kafka headers from outbox metadata
  ├── publish to Kafka
  └── mark PUBLISHED
```

Outbox record:

```java
public record OutboxRecord(
        UUID id,
        String tenantId,
        String correlationId,
        String causationId,
        String eventType,
        String schemaVersion,
        String aggregateId,
        String payloadJson
) {
}
```

Kafka headers from outbox:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class OutboxKafkaHeaderFactory {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public OutboxKafkaHeaderFactory(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    public Headers fromOutbox(OutboxRecord record) {
        RecordHeaders headers = new RecordHeaders();

        add(headers, "x-message-id", record.id().toString());
        add(headers, "x-correlation-id", record.correlationId());
        add(headers, "x-causation-id", record.causationId());
        add(headers, "x-tenant-id", record.tenantId());
        add(headers, "x-event-type", record.eventType());
        add(headers, "x-schema-version", record.schemaVersion());
        add(headers, "x-producer-service", config.serviceName());

        return headers;
    }

    private void add(Headers headers, String key, String value) {
        if (value != null && !value.isBlank()) {
            headers.add(key, value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 16. Observability Contract

Setiap outbound call/event harus punya observability minimal.

## 16.1 HTTP/gRPC Log Fields

```text
correlationId
traceId/spanId if available
tenantId
targetService
targetOperation
method/path or rpc method
status
durationMs
timeoutMs
retryCount
outcome
errorCode
```

## 16.2 Kafka Log Fields

```text
messageId
correlationId
causationId
tenantId
topic
partition
offset
eventType
schemaVersion
producerService
consumerGroup
durationMs
outcome
```

Do not log:

```text
Authorization header
raw JWT
password
secret
full PII payload
large request/response body
```

---

```text
com.company.caseapp
├── propagation
│   ├── PropagationPolicy.java
│   ├── PropagationHeaders.java
│   ├── ContextPropagatingHttpClient.java
│   ├── ContextClientRequestFilter.java
│   ├── GrpcMetadataKeys.java
│   ├── ContextGrpcClientInterceptor.java
│   ├── ContextGrpcServerInterceptor.java
│   ├── KafkaPropagationHeaders.java

│   └── OutboxKafkaHeaderFactory.java
│
├── context
│   ├── CurrentRequestContext.java
│   ├── RequestContextSnapshot.java
│   ├── MessageContextSnapshot.java
│   └── ServiceCallContextSnapshot.java
│
├── idempotency
│   ├── IdempotencyKeyDeriver.java
│   ├── ProcessedMessageRepository.java
│   └── IdempotentConsumerService.java
│
├── outbox
│   ├── OutboxRecord.java
│   ├── OutboxRepository.java
│   └── OutboxPublisher.java
│
└── client
    ├── DecisionEngineClient.java
    ├── PaymentClient.java
    └── DocumentServiceClient.java
```

# 18. Anti-pattern

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Blind copy all headers | `headers.putAll(inboundHeaders)` | Token/PII leak |
| Blind forward Authorization | copy raw bearer token | Overprivileged downstream |
| Trust raw tenant header | `tenant = X-Tenant-Id` | Tenant spoofing |
| Generate new correlation per hop | service B creates new ID | Trace chain putus |
| Use same idempotency key for all operations | one key for payment + document | Key collision/incorrect replay |
| Kafka message without tenant | no `x-tenant-id` | Consumer cannot isolate data |
| Kafka message without message ID | no dedup possible | Duplicate side effects |
| Publish event before DB commit | direct Kafka send inside transaction | Ghost event |
| Consumer not idempotent | process duplicate twice | Duplicate state changes |
| Retry command without idempotency | retry POST/payment | Double side effect |
| Put raw JWT in Kafka header | `authorization` in message | Security incident |
| Store transaction context in headers | transaction ID/session | Broken model |
| Log full outbound payload | debug log body | PII/secret leak |

---

# 19. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Context contract | Apakah header/metadata keys standar dan terdokumentasi? |
| Correlation | Apakah correlation ID dipropagate ke HTTP/gRPC/Kafka? |
| Tracing | Apakah `traceparent` dipreserve bila ada tracing? |
| Tenant | Apakah tenant dipropagate hanya ke trusted boundary? |
| Auth | Apakah raw `Authorization` tidak di-forward sembarangan? |
| User context | Apakah user ID/roles hanya dikirim bila perlu? |
| HTTP client | Apakah ada wrapper/filter outbound? |
| gRPC | Apakah ada client/server interceptor metadata? |
| Kafka producer | Apakah event punya `x-message-id`, tenant, correlation, schema version? |
| Kafka consumer | Apakah header divalidasi dan ada DLQ/quarantine untuk poison message? |
| Idempotency | Apakah command retryable punya idempotency key? |
| Consumer dedup | Apakah consumer idempotent by message ID? |
| Outbox | Apakah event publish terjadi via outbox, bukan sebelum commit? |
| Timeout | Apakah downstream timeout lebih kecil dari upstream budget? |
| Retry | Apakah retry hanya untuk operation safe/idempotent? |
| Logging | Apakah log tidak memuat token/secret/raw PII? |

---

# 20. Testing Strategy

## Test 1 - HTTP propagation

```text
Given request correlationId=corr-001 tenant=tenant-a
When service calls downstream HTTP
Then outbound headers contain:
  X-Correlation-Id=corr-001
  X-Tenant-Id=tenant-a for internal service
And do not contain raw Authorization by default
```

## Test 2 - gRPC propagation

```text
Given CurrentRequestContext
When gRPC client calls method
Then Metadata contains:
  x-correlation-id
  x-tenant-id if allowed
  traceparent if present
```

## Test 3 - Kafka event headers

```text
When CaseApprovedEvent is published
Then record headers contain:
  x-message-id
  x-correlation-id
  x-tenant-id
  x-event-type
  x-schema-version
  x-producer-service
```

## Test 4 - Consumer rejects bad message

```text
Given Kafka record missing x-tenant-id
When consumed
Then message goes to DLQ/quarantine
And business handler is not executed
```

## Test 5 - Idempotent consumer

```text
Given same messageId delivered twice
When consumer processes both
Then business side effect happens once
```

## Test 6 - No ghost event

```text
Given business transaction fails
When outbox insert rolls back
Then Kafka event is not published
```

# 21. Roadmap Latihan Praktis Seri 10

Urutan latihan:

```text
1. Definisikan context header contract
2. Buat PropagationPolicy
3. Buat HTTP client wrapper / ClientRequestFilter
4. Buat inbound validator untuk correlation/tenant
5. Buat GrpcMetadataKeys
6. Buat gRPC client interceptor
7. Buat gRPC server interceptor
8. Buat KafkaPropagationHeaders
9. Buat KafkaMessageContextExtractor
10. Tambahkan x-message-id + x-schema-version di semua event
11. Buat idempotent consumer table
12. Integrasikan outbox → Kafka publisher
13. Tambahkan test HTTP/gRPC/Kafka propagation
14. Tambahkan test token tidak di-forward sembarangan
```
