---

# Ringkasan Seri 10

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. Service-to-service propagation harus berdasarkan **contract**, bukan copy semua header.
2. Pisahkan inbound raw context, trusted runtime context, dan outbound propagation context.
3. `X-Correlation-Id` dan `traceparent` adalah dua hal berbeda; keduanya bisa dipropagate.
4. Tenant ID adalah security boundary; jangan percaya raw tenant header tanpa validasi.
5. Raw `Authorization`/JWT tidak boleh di-forward sembarangan.
6. HTTP propagation bisa dibuat lewat client wrapper atau Jersey `ClientRequestFilter`.
7. gRPC propagation memakai metadata dan client/server interceptor.
8. Kafka propagation memakai headers seperti `x-message-id`, `x-correlation-id`, `x-tenant-id`, `x-schema-version`.
9. Kafka consumer harus idempotent karena duplicate delivery harus diasumsikan mungkin terjadi.
10. Command retry harus punya idempotency strategy.
11. Event publication yang reliable sebaiknya lewat outbox pattern.
12. Timeout, retry, circuit breaker, dan logging harus tetap mempertahankan correlation context.

**Seri 10/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 11 - Camunda Workflow/Process Context Bridging**, dengan fokus pada business key, process variables, tenant/user/correlation propagation, delegate context, async job executor, message correlation, outbox integration, dan failure modelling workflow.

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 11/14**. Fokusnya: bridging context dari HTTP/service layer ke Camunda process/job/message—business key, process variables, tenant/user/correlation, delegate context, outbox, dan failure modelling workflow.

# Seri 11/14 - Camunda Workflow/Process Context Bridging

Target seri ini: kamu bisa menjembatani context dari aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld** ke **Camunda workflow/process** tanpa mencampur lifecycle HTTP request, transaction, async job, dan workflow instance.

Intinya:

> HTTP request context itu pendek.  
> Workflow/process context itu panjang.  
> Job/delegate context itu execution-specific.  
> Jangan menganggap ketiganya sama.

Camunda 7 process variables adalah data runtime proses yang punya nama dan value, berada dalam variable scopes, dan bisa diakses oleh execution/task tertentu sesuai visibility scope. Camunda 8 variables juga disimpan sebagai JSON values dan punya scope/propagation rules; dokumentasinya bahkan menekankan agar tidak menyimpan terlalu banyak data di process context. ([Camunda Documentation](https://docs.camunda.org/manual/7.23/user-guide/process-engine/variables/))

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text
HTTP Request Context
│
├── correlationId
├── tenantId
├── userId
├── roles
├── locale
├── idempotencyKey
└── request lifetime only
        │
        ▼
Workflow Start / Message Correlation
        │
        ▼
Workflow Context
│
├── businessKey / correlationKey
├── caseId / aggregateId
├── tenantId
├── initiatorUserId
├── correlationId
├── commandId / causationId
├── process variables
└── process lifetime
        │
        ▼
Job / Delegate Context
│
├── execution/job metadata
├── process variables snapshot
├── activity/task id
├── retry count
├── local variables
└── job execution lifetime
```

Practical invariant:

```text
Never depend on HTTP @RequestScoped context inside Camunda async job/delegate.
Persist stable workflow context as process variables, business key, message correlation key, or external domain state.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Camunda Workflow/Process Context Bridging
│
├── 1. Entry Context
│   ├── HTTP request
│   ├── Kafka message
│   ├── gRPC call
│   ├── scheduler
│   └── manual/operator action
│
├── 2. Workflow Identity
│   ├── processDefinitionKey / BPMN process id
│   ├── processInstanceId / processInstanceKey
│   ├── businessKey / correlationKey
│   ├── tenantId
│   └── version/deployment binding
│
├── 3. Workflow Context Variables
│   ├── caseId / aggregateId
│   ├── tenantId
│   ├── initiatorUserId
│   ├── correlationId
│   ├── causationId / commandId
│   ├── locale
│   ├── workflowType
│   └── compact process state
│
├── 4. Runtime Execution
│   ├── JavaDelegate / delegateExpression     [Camunda 7]
│   ├── External Task Worker                  [Camunda 7]
│   ├── Zeebe Job Worker                      [Camunda 8]
│   ├── Service Task
│   ├── User Task
│   ├── Message Catch Event
│   └── Timer/Event Subprocess
│
├── 5. Integration Boundary
│   ├── start process
│   ├── correlate message
│   ├── complete task/job
│   ├── publish domain event
│   ├── outbox integration
│   └── audit trail
│
├── 6. Failure Handling
│   ├── BPMN Error
│   ├── technical exception
│   ├── retry
│   ├── incident
│   ├── compensation
│   ├── timeout
│   └── manual repair
│
└── 7. Governance
    ├── variable naming convention
    ├── no secrets in variables
    ├── variable size control
    ├── idempotency
    ├── tenant isolation
    ├── observability
    └── migration C7/C8 awareness
```

---

# 3. Practical Table

| Area | Praktik | Yang Benar | Yang Salah | Failure Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HTTP → Workflow | Snapshot context | Simpan `tenantId`, `initiatorUserId`, `correlationId`, `caseId` | Inject `CurrentRequestContext` ke delegate async | Context not active |
| Business identity | Business key/correlation key | Gunakan stable domain key seperti `caseId` atau `caseId:workflowType` | Pakai random UUID tanpa domain mapping | Sulit correlate/debug |
| Process variables | Compact stable data | ID, status kecil, context metadata | Raw JWT, secret, full request body, file besar | Security/size/serialization incident |
| Tenant | Explicit variable/header | `tenantId` wajib ada dan divalidasi | Ambil tenant dari raw user input di delegate | Cross-tenant leak |
| User | Initiator vs actor | Simpan `initiatorUserId`; task actor disimpan saat action | Anggap initiator selalu executor task | Audit salah |
| Correlation | End-to-end trace | Propagate `correlationId` ke variables/log/outbox | Generate baru tiap delegate | Trace chain putus |
| Message correlation | Unique key | Gunakan correlation key unik untuk reply interaction | Semua instance tunggu key yang sama | Non-deterministic correlation |
| Delegate/job | Own execution context | Extract variables → build `WorkflowContextSnapshot` | Pakai HTTP request context | Context mismatch |
| External call | Durable side effect | Outbox/job table/idempotency | Call external system saat DB/process tx ambiguous | Duplicate/ghost side effect |
| Retry | Designed | Retry technical failure, idempotent worker | Retry non-idempotent payment/email | Duplicate action |
| Error handling | Separate business vs technical | BPMN Error untuk expected business path; exception untuk technical failure | Semua error jadi exception | Incident noise / wrong process path |
| Audit | Workflow-aware | Audit start, task claim/complete, delegate outcome | Audit hanya di HTTP entry | Missing lifecycle evidence |

---

# 4. Context Types: Request vs Workflow vs Job

## 4.1 Request Context

Sumbernya HTTP/JAX-RS:

```java
public record RequestContextSnapshot(
        String correlationId,
        String traceParent,
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles,
        Locale locale,
        Optional<String> idempotencyKey
) {
}
```

Request context berlaku selama request aktif.

## 4.2 Workflow Context

Sumbernya process variables/business key:

```java
public record WorkflowContextSnapshot(
        String processInstanceId,
        String processDefinitionKey,
        String businessKey,
        String workflowType,
        String caseId,
        String tenantId,
        String initiatorUserId,
        String correlationId,
        String causationId,
        String traceParent,
        Locale locale
) {
}
```

Workflow context berlaku selama process instance masih hidup.

## 4.3 Job Context

Sumbernya delegate/job execution:

```java
public record WorkflowJobContext(
        WorkflowContextSnapshot workflow,
        String activityId,
        String activityName,
        String jobId,
        int retryCount
) {
}
```

Job context berlaku hanya saat worker/delegate sedang mengeksekusi satu job/activity.

---

# 5. Apa yang Disimpan di Process Variables?

Camunda 7 variables dapat berupa Java object dan typed values, tetapi value akan dipersist dan diserialisasi; dokumentasi Camunda 7 juga menjelaskan bahwa object serialization bisa bermasalah jika aplikasi pemanggil tidak punya class Java terkait. Camunda 8 variables adalah JSON values dan punya batas payload; docs Camunda 8 menyarankan memberi headroom dan tidak menyimpan terlalu banyak data di process context. ([Camunda Documentation](https://docs.camunda.org/manual/7.23/user-guide/process-engine/variables/))

## Simpan Ini

| Variable | Contoh | Kenapa |
|---|---|---|
| `caseId` | `CASE-001` | Domain correlation |
| `tenantId` | `tenant-a` | Isolation |
| `initiatorUserId` | `user-123` | Audit |
| `correlationId` | `corr-001` | Observability |
| `causationId` | `cmd-001` | Event/command chain |
| `workflowType` | `CASE_APPROVAL` | Debug/routing |
| `locale` | `id-ID` | Human task/notification |
| `approvalLevel` | `2` | Compact process decision |
| `decisionOutcome` | `APPROVED` | Process branching |

## Jangan Simpan Ini

| Jangan | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| raw JWT / Authorization header | Secret/security leak |
| password/API key/secret | Security incident |
| full request body besar | Size/serialization/versioning issue |
| uploaded file/PDF/image | Store externally, keep reference only |
| DB connection / transaction object | Local runtime only |
| `SqlSession` / mapper object | Not serializable and wrong lifecycle |
| mutable Java domain object besar | Serialization compatibility risk |
| PII berlebihan | Compliance/data minimization |
| volatile runtime state | Akan basi saat process long-running |

Practical invariant:

```text
Process variables should be stable, compact, serializable, non-secret, and meaningful without application-local object identity.
```

---

# 6. Variable Naming Convention

Gunakan nama yang konsisten lintas BPMN, Java, audit, dan outbox.

```text
caseId
tenantId
initiatorUserId
correlationId
causationId
commandId
workflowType
approvalDecision
approvalReason
taskAssignee
callbackCorrelationKey
```

Hindari:

```text
case.id
tenant-id
CURRENT_USER
user
data
payload
obj
request
context
```

Camunda 8 docs menyebut variable names case-sensitive, menyarankan `camelCase` atau `snake_case`, dan melarang beberapa karakter/pola seperti whitespace dan operator; jadi untuk Java service, BPMN expression, dan FEEL compatibility, `camelCase` biasanya paling aman. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/variables/))

---

# 7. Start Process dari HTTP Request

## 7.1 Flow

```text
POST /cases/{caseId}/approval/start
│
├── RequestContextBootstrapFilter
│   ├── tenantId
│   ├── userId
│   ├── correlationId
│   └── idempotencyKey
│
├── CaseWorkflowApplicationService
│   ├── validate case exists under tenant
│   ├── check permission
│   ├── create commandId
│   ├── start Camunda process
│   └── persist local mapping if needed
│
└── Response
    ├── workflowInstanceId
    ├── businessKey
    └── correlationId
```

## 7.2 Camunda 7 Style

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseWorkflowStarter {

    private final RuntimeService runtimeService;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;
    private final Clock clock;

    @Inject
    public CaseWorkflowStarter(
            RuntimeService runtimeService,
            CurrentRequestContext context,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.runtimeService = runtimeService;
        this.context = context;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public StartedWorkflow startApproval(String caseId) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        String commandId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        String businessKey = businessKey(caseId, "CASE_APPROVAL");

        Map<String, Object> variables = new HashMap<>();
        variables.put("caseId", caseId);
        variables.put("tenantId", snapshot.tenantId());
        variables.put("initiatorUserId", snapshot.userId());
        variables.put("correlationId", snapshot.correlationId());
        variables.put("causationId", commandId);
        variables.put("commandId", commandId);
        variables.put("workflowType", "CASE_APPROVAL");
        variables.put("startedAt", Instant.now(clock).toString());
        variables.put("locale", snapshot.locale().toLanguageTag());

        ProcessInstance instance = runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                "case_approval",
                businessKey,
                variables
        );

        return new StartedWorkflow(
                instance.getProcessInstanceId(),
                businessKey,
                snapshot.correlationId()
        );
    }

    private String businessKey(String caseId, String workflowType) {
        return caseId + ":" + workflowType;
    }
}
```

## 7.3 Camunda 8 / Zeebe Style

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseZeebeWorkflowStarter {

    private final ZeebeClient zeebeClient;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseZeebeWorkflowStarter(
            ZeebeClient zeebeClient,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.zeebeClient = zeebeClient;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public StartedWorkflow startApproval(String caseId) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        String commandId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        String correlationKey = caseId + ":CASE_APPROVAL";

        Map<String, Object> variables = Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "tenantId", snapshot.tenantId(),
                "initiatorUserId", snapshot.userId(),
                "correlationId", snapshot.correlationId(),
                "causationId", commandId,
                "commandId", commandId,
                "workflowType", "CASE_APPROVAL",
                "callbackCorrelationKey", correlationKey,
                "locale", snapshot.locale().toLanguageTag()
        );

        ProcessInstanceEvent event = zeebeClient
                .newCreateInstanceCommand()
                .bpmnProcessId("case_approval")
                .latestVersion()
                .variables(variables)
                .send()
                .join();

        return new StartedWorkflow(
                String.valueOf(event.getProcessInstanceKey()),
                correlationKey,
                snapshot.correlationId()
        );
    }
}
```

Camunda 8 service tasks create jobs, the process instance waits until a worker completes the job, and the service task’s task definition includes a required `type` used by workers to subscribe. The service task can also define retries, defaulting to three if unspecified. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/service-tasks/))

---

# 8. Business Key / Correlation Key

Gunakan key yang stabil dan domain-meaningful.

| Use Case | Key yang Baik | Key yang Lemah |
|---|---|---|
| Case approval workflow | `caseId:CASE_APPROVAL` | random UUID only |
| Payment collection | `paymentId` atau `orderId:paymentAttempt` | userId |
| External callback reply | generated unique `callbackCorrelationKey` | static customerId |
| Document generation | `caseId:DOC_GEN:documentType` | document title |
| One active workflow per case | `caseId:workflowType` | timestamp |

Camunda 8 message correlation berbasis message name dan correlation key; jika beberapa process instance subscribe pada message name/key yang sama, pemilihan instance bisa non-deterministic, sehingga unique correlation key sangat penting untuk request-reply concurrent workflows. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/))

---

# 9. Message Correlation

## 9.1 Request-Reply Pattern

```text
Process sends outbound request
│
├── generate callbackCorrelationKey
├── store callbackCorrelationKey as process variable
├── send key to external system
│
External system calls back
│
├── callback includes callbackCorrelationKey
├── app validates callback
└── correlate message to waiting process
```

## 9.2 Camunda 7 Correlation Style

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseWorkflowMessageCorrelator {

    private final RuntimeService runtimeService;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseWorkflowMessageCorrelator(
            RuntimeService runtimeService,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.runtimeService = runtimeService;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public void correlateApprovalResult(
            String callbackCorrelationKey,
            ApprovalCallback callback
    ) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        Map<String, Object> variables = Map.of(
                "approvalDecision", callback.decision(),
                "approvalReason", callback.reason(),
                "callbackReceivedBy", snapshot.userId(),
                "callbackCorrelationId", snapshot.correlationId()
        );

        runtimeService.createMessageCorrelation("ApprovalResultReceived")
                .processInstanceBusinessKey(callbackCorrelationKey)
                .setVariables(variables)
                .correlateWithResult();
    }
}
```

## 9.3 Camunda 8 Correlation Style

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseZeebeMessagePublisher {

    private final ZeebeClient zeebeClient;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseZeebeMessagePublisher(
            ZeebeClient zeebeClient,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.zeebeClient = zeebeClient;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public void publishApprovalResult(
            String callbackCorrelationKey,
            ApprovalCallback callback
    ) {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = context.snapshot();

        Map<String, Object> variables = Map.of(
                "approvalDecision", callback.decision(),
                "approvalReason", callback.reason(),
                "callbackCorrelationId", snapshot.correlationId()
        );

        zeebeClient
                .newPublishMessageCommand()
                .messageName("ApprovalResultReceived")
                .correlationKey(callbackCorrelationKey)
                .messageId(callback.messageId())
                .variables(variables)
                .timeToLive(Duration.ofHours(1))
                .send()
                .join();
    }
}
```

Camunda 8 messages can be buffered with TTL and correlated later if a matching subscription appears; if TTL is zero and no subscription is open, the message is discarded. It also supports an optional message ID for uniqueness/idempotency. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/))

---

# 10. JavaDelegate / DelegateExecution Context di Camunda 7

Camunda 7 JavaDelegate menerima `DelegateExecution`. Jangan inject `CurrentRequestContext` dan berharap ada HTTP request.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class NotifyApprovalDelegate implements JavaDelegate {

    private final NotificationService notificationService;
    private final WorkflowContextExtractor contextExtractor;

    @Inject
    public NotifyApprovalDelegate(
            NotificationService notificationService,
            WorkflowContextExtractor contextExtractor
    ) {
        this.notificationService = notificationService;
        this.contextExtractor = contextExtractor;
    }

    @Override
    public void execute(DelegateExecution execution) {
        WorkflowJobContext jobContext = contextExtractor.from(execution);

        try {
            MdcContext.put(jobContext.workflow());

            notificationService.notifyApprover(
                    jobContext.workflow().caseId(),
                    jobContext.workflow()
            );
        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Extractor:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class WorkflowContextExtractor {

    public WorkflowJobContext from(DelegateExecution execution) {
        WorkflowContextSnapshot workflow = new WorkflowContextSnapshot(
                execution.getProcessInstanceId(),
                execution.getProcessDefinitionId(),
                execution.getProcessBusinessKey(),
                requiredString(execution, "workflowType"),
                requiredString(execution, "caseId"),
                requiredString(execution, "tenantId"),
                requiredString(execution, "initiatorUserId"),
                requiredString(execution, "correlationId"),
                optionalString(execution, "causationId"),
                optionalString(execution, "traceParent"),
                Locale.forLanguageTag(optionalString(execution, "locale", "en"))
        );

        return new WorkflowJobContext(
                workflow,
                execution.getCurrentActivityId(),
                execution.getCurrentActivityName(),
                null,
                -1
        );
    }

    private String requiredString(DelegateExecution execution, String name) {
        Object value = execution.getVariable(name);

        if (!(value instanceof String text) || text.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing workflow variable: " + name);
        }

        return text;
    }

    private String optionalString(DelegateExecution execution, String name) {
        Object value = execution.getVariable(name);
        return value instanceof String text ? text : null;
    }

    private String optionalString(
            DelegateExecution execution,
            String name,
            String fallback
    ) {
        String value = optionalString(execution, name);
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? fallback : value;
    }
}
```

Camunda 7 docs describe variable access through Java APIs, including getting/setting variables inside a delegate and variable scope visibility rules. ([Camunda Documentation](https://docs.camunda.org/manual/7.23/user-guide/process-engine/variables/))

---

# 11. Zeebe Job Worker Context di Camunda 8

Camunda 8 service tasks are handled by job workers subscribed to a job type; when the worker completes the job, the process continues. Job completion variables can merge back into the process depending on output mappings. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/service-tasks/))

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ApprovalNotificationWorker {

    private final ZeebeClient zeebeClient;
    private final NotificationService notificationService;

    @Inject
    public ApprovalNotificationWorker(
            ZeebeClient zeebeClient,
            NotificationService notificationService
    ) {
        this.zeebeClient = zeebeClient;
        this.notificationService = notificationService;
    }

    public void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        WorkflowContextSnapshot context = WorkflowContextSnapshot.fromJob(job);

        try {
            MdcContext.put(context);

            notificationService.notifyApprover(context.caseId(), context);

            client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "notificationStatus", "SENT",
                            "notificationCorrelationId", context.correlationId()
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (BusinessWorkflowException ex) {
            client.newThrowErrorCommand(job.getKey())
                    .errorCode(ex.errorCode())
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(Math.max(0, job.getRetries() - 1))
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } finally {
            MdcContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Practical distinction:

```text
Business problem expected by the process → BPMN Error / modeled path.
Technical problem that may recover → fail job / retry / incident.
```

---

# 12. BPMN Error vs Technical Exception

| Failure | Use BPMN Error? | Use Technical Exception/Retry? |
|---|---:|---:|
| Applicant not eligible | Ya | Tidak |
| Approval rejected | Ya, if modeled | Tidak |
| Missing mandatory domain data | Biasanya Ya | Kadang |
| Downstream timeout | Tidak | Ya |
| DB deadlock | Tidak | Ya |
| Invalid BPMN variable due to modeling bug | Tidak | Ya / incident |
| Payment provider declined | Ya, if business-decline path exists | Tidak |
| Payment provider unavailable | Tidak | Ya |
| Unauthorized service credential | Tidak | Ya / incident |
| Tenant mismatch | Usually technical/security incident | Ya / security handling |

Camunda best-practice docs distinguish business errors modeled in BPMN from technical failures that should trigger retry/incident handling. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/development/dealing-with-problems-and-exceptions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 13. Transaction Boundary: App DB vs Camunda Engine

Ini perlu sangat hati-hati.

## 13.1 Common Models

| Model | Deskripsi | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Embedded Camunda 7 engine + same DB tx | App dan engine bisa participate dalam same transaction jika configured | Kompleks, runtime-specific |
| Remote Camunda 7 REST | App DB tx terpisah dari engine call | Butuh idempotency/outbox |
| Camunda 8 Zeebe remote | App DB tx dan workflow command jelas terpisah | Butuh outbox/retry/reconciliation |
| External task/job worker | Worker tx terpisah dari engine lock/complete | Complete job setelah side effect durable |

Camunda 7 transaction handling docs emphasize wait states as transaction boundaries and that technical failures are rolled back and retried by user or job executor depending on execution path. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/8.7/components/best-practices/development/understanding-transaction-handling-c7/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 13.2 Recommended Pattern: Outbox for Workflow Commands

Jika start/correlate workflow harus konsisten dengan update DB lokal:

```text
Business DB transaction
  ├── update case
  ├── insert workflow_command_outbox
  └── commit

Workflow command publisher
  ├── read outbox command
  ├── start process / correlate message
  ├── mark published
  └── retry safely
```

Outbox table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE workflow_command_outbox (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    command_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    business_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    process_definition_key TEXT,
    message_name TEXT,
    correlation_key TEXT,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NEW',
    attempt_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    last_error TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE INDEX idx_workflow_command_outbox_new
ON workflow_command_outbox (status, created_at)
WHERE status = 'NEW';
```

---

# 14. Idempotency for Start Process

Starting workflow is often not naturally idempotent. Guard it.

```sql
CREATE TABLE workflow_instance_mapping (
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    business_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    workflow_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    process_instance_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, business_key, workflow_type)
);
```

Application service:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public StartedWorkflow startCaseApproval(String caseId) {
    String tenantId = context.tenantId();
    String workflowType = "CASE_APPROVAL";
    String businessKey = caseId + ":" + workflowType;

    Optional<WorkflowMapping> existing =
            workflowMappingRepository.find(tenantId, businessKey, workflowType);

    if (existing.isPresent() && existing.get().isActive()) {
        return StartedWorkflow.from(existing.get());
    }

    workflowCommandOutboxRepository.insertStartCommand(
            tenantId,
            businessKey,
            workflowType,
            buildStartPayload(caseId)
    );

    return StartedWorkflow.accepted(businessKey, context.correlationId());
}
```

This prevents double-start on HTTP retry.

---

# 15. Human Task Context

Human task lifecycle has different actors:

```text
initiatorUserId  = user who started workflow
candidateGroup   = group allowed to pick task
assignee         = user currently owning task
completedBy      = user who completed task
```

Jangan pakai `initiatorUserId` sebagai actor untuk semua task actions.

```java
public record TaskActionContext(
        String tenantId,
        String taskId,
        String processInstanceId,
        String caseId,
        String action,
        String actorUserId,
        String correlationId,
        Instant occurredAt
) {
}
```

Audit:

```text
CASE_APPROVAL_STARTED
  actor = initiatorUserId

TASK_CLAIMED
  actor = assignee

TASK_COMPLETED
  actor = completedBy

DELEGATE_EXECUTED
  actor = system/service identity
  initiatorUserId = original requester
```

---

# 16. Tenant Isolation in Workflow

Tenant must be present in:

```text
- process variables
- business key / correlation key design if needed
- workflow mapping table
- task query filters
- delegate DB queries
- audit events
- outbox events
- message correlation validation
```

Example business key options:

```text
caseId:CASE_APPROVAL
tenantId:caseId:CASE_APPROVAL
```

If `caseId` is globally unique, `caseId:workflowType` may be enough. If case IDs are tenant-local, include tenant in uniqueness constraints and/or business key.

Repository query inside delegate:

```java
CaseRecord record = caseRepository.findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(
        workflow.tenantId(),
        workflow.caseId()
);
```

Never:

```java
caseRepository.findById(workflow.caseId());
```

---

# 17. Context to Logs / MDC

Every delegate/job should set MDC from workflow context.

```java
public final class WorkflowMdc {

    private WorkflowMdc() {
    }

    public static void put(WorkflowContextSnapshot context) {
        MDC.put("correlationId", context.correlationId());
        MDC.put("tenantId", context.tenantId());
        MDC.put("caseId", context.caseId());
        MDC.put("businessKey", context.businessKey());
        MDC.put("processInstanceId", context.processInstanceId());
    }

    public static void clear() {
        MDC.clear();
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
try {
    WorkflowMdc.put(workflowContext);
    worker.execute(workflowContext);
} finally {
    WorkflowMdc.clear();
}
```

---

# 18. Context to Outbox/Event

When workflow changes business state, publish events via outbox.

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public void completeApproval(WorkflowContextSnapshot workflow, ApprovalResult result) {
    CaseRecord record = caseRepository.findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(
            workflow.tenantId(),
            workflow.caseId()
    );

    record.applyApproval(result);

    caseRepository.update(record);

    outboxRepository.insert(new OutboxRecord(
            UUID.randomUUID(),
            workflow.tenantId(),
            workflow.correlationId(),
            workflow.causationId(),
            "CASE_APPROVAL_COMPLETED",
            "1",
            workflow.caseId(),
            toJson(result)
    ));
}
```

Outbox headers later:

```text
x-message-id
x-correlation-id
x-causation-id
x-tenant-id
x-event-type
x-schema-version
x-producer-service
```

---

# 19. Variable Scope and Output Mapping

Camunda 8 variable scopes control visibility, and variables from job completion/message correlation can propagate upward depending on mappings. If no output mappings are defined on a job, result variables merge into the process instance; with mappings, behavior becomes more explicit. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/variables/))

Practical rule:

```text
For important process state:
  use explicit output mappings.

For temporary worker-local data:
  keep local or do not complete it back.

For large/technical response:
  store externally and return reference.
```

Example:

```text
Worker result:
  {
    "paymentStatus": "PAID",
    "paymentReference": "PAY-123"
  }

Output mapping:
  paymentStatus      → paymentStatus
  paymentReference   → paymentReference

Do not output:
  rawProviderResponse
  authToken
  debugPayload
```

---

# 20. Process Variable DTO

Make variables explicit, not arbitrary `Map` everywhere.

```java
public record StartCaseApprovalVariables(
        String caseId,
        String tenantId,
        String initiatorUserId,
        String correlationId,
        String causationId,
        String commandId,
        String workflowType,
        String locale
) {
    public Map<String, Object> toMap() {
        return Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "tenantId", tenantId,
                "initiatorUserId", initiatorUserId,
                "correlationId", correlationId,
                "causationId", causationId,
                "commandId", commandId,
                "workflowType", workflowType,
                "locale", locale
        );
    }
}
```

Benefit:

| Tanpa DTO | Dengan DTO |
|---|---|
| Typo variable runtime | Compile-time-ish centralization |
| Map liar tersebar | Convention terjaga |
| Hard to test | Easy test |
| Hidden variable contract | Explicit process contract |

---

# 21. Versioning Variable Contract

Process variable contract bisa berubah.

```text
caseApprovalVariablesVersion = 1
```

Pattern:

```java
variables.put("variablesVersion", 1);
```

When reading:

```java
int version = optionalInt(execution, "variablesVersion", 1);

switch (version) {
    case 1 -> handleV1(execution);
    case 2 -> handleV2(execution);
    default -> throw new IllegalStateException("Unsupported variablesVersion: " + version);
}
```

This matters when process instances live longer than deployment cycles.

---

# 22. Workflow Failure Modelling

## 22.1 Failure Categories

| Failure | Process Meaning | Handling |
|---|---|---|
| Business rejection | Expected domain path | BPMN Error / gateway |
| Validation failed | Expected or technical depending cause | BPMN Error if user-fixable |
| External timeout | Technical | Retry, incident, escalation |
| Downstream unavailable | Technical | Retry/backoff |
| Tenant mismatch | Security incident | Fail hard + alert |
| Missing process variable | Modeling/data contract bug | Incident |
| Duplicate callback | Idempotent ignore or conflict | Message ID/correlation guard |
| Message arrived before subscription | Use TTL/buffering or outbox retry | Avoid lost callback |
| Worker crashed after side effect before complete | Idempotency/outbox/reconciliation | Worker retry safe |

## 22.2 Camunda 8 Message Specifics

If a message is published and no proper subscription is open, behavior depends on TTL: with buffering, it can be correlated later; with TTL zero, it is discarded. Also, a message with same name/correlation key/message ID can be rejected as duplicate while buffered. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/))

Practical implication:

```text
For callbacks that may arrive before process reaches catch event:
  use TTL > 0.

For strict immediate reply:
  use correlate endpoint / synchronous correlation where supported.

For duplicate callback prevention:
  use messageId and your own idempotency table.
```

---

# 23. Camunda 7 vs Camunda 8 Practical Differences

| Area | Camunda 7 | Camunda 8 |
|---|---|---|
| Engine model | BPM engine, often embedded or remote REST | Zeebe-based orchestration cluster |
| Service task implementation | JavaDelegate, delegateExpression, external task | Job worker by task type |
| Process instance identity | `processInstanceId` | `processInstanceKey` |
| Business identity | business key common | correlation key/message/process variables; process instance key is technical |
| Variables | Java object/typed values; serialization concern | JSON variables; size limits and scope rules |
| Message correlation | RuntimeService correlation APIs | message publish/correlation with name + correlation key |
| Retry | Job executor / failed jobs / incidents | job retries / incidents |
| Context strategy | Extract from `DelegateExecution` | Extract from activated job variables/metadata |
| Worker lifecycle | Often inside app JVM for delegate, or external task | External job worker pattern is normal |

Important update: Camunda’s current Camunda 7 docs display a notice that Camunda 7 is reaching end of life, so any new long-term design should be C7/C8-aware rather than assuming C7 patterns remain the future default. ([Camunda Documentation](https://docs.camunda.org/manual/7.23/user-guide/process-engine/variables/))

---

# 24. Anti-pattern

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Delegate injects `CurrentRequestContext` | `context.userId()` in async job | Context not active |
| Store raw JWT in variables | `authorizationToken` variable | Security leak |
| Store huge DTO/file | full PDF/request payload variable | DB/engine payload issue |
| Use same correlation key for all replies | `userId` as correlation key | Wrong instance receives message |
| Generate business key randomly only | UUID with no mapping | Hard to debug/reconcile |
| No idempotency on start workflow | repeated HTTP retry starts duplicate process | Duplicate workflows |
| Delegate calls external API then fails before completion | retry repeats side effect | Duplicate external call |
| Missing tenant in variables | delegate cannot enforce tenant | Cross-tenant risk |
| Query DB by `caseId` only | no tenant predicate | Data leak |
| Treat BPMN Error as technical exception | process incident instead of modeled path | Bad user flow |
| Treat technical timeout as BPMN rejection | process takes wrong business path | Data correctness bug |
| Variable names ad hoc | `data`, `obj`, `context` | Model unreadable |
| No variable contract version | long-running instances break after deploy | Migration incident |

---

# 25. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Start process | Apakah business key/correlation key stable dan domain-meaningful? |
| Context variables | Apakah `tenantId`, `caseId`, `correlationId`, `initiatorUserId` ada? |
| Secret handling | Apakah raw token/secret tidak masuk variables? |
| Variable size | Apakah payload compact dan tidak menyimpan file besar? |
| Tenant isolation | Apakah delegate/job query DB by tenant? |
| User audit | Apakah initiator, assignee, completedBy dibedakan? |
| Delegate context | Apakah delegate extract dari execution/job, bukan HTTP context? |
| Message correlation | Apakah correlation key unik untuk concurrent interaction? |
| Message TTL | Apakah callback early-arrival ditangani? |
| Message ID | Apakah duplicate callback/event bisa dicegah? |
| Idempotency | Apakah start/correlate/complete safe terhadap retry? |
| Error handling | Apakah BPMN Error vs technical failure dibedakan? |
| Outbox | Apakah external side effect durable dan tidak ghost? |
| Variable version | Apakah process variable contract versioned? |
| Observability | Apakah logs/audit/outbox membawa correlation/process info? |

---

# 26. Testing Strategy

## Test 1 - Start process carries context

```text
Given HTTP request tenant=tenant-a user=user-1 correlation=corr-001
When start case approval workflow
Then process variables include:
  caseId
  tenantId=tenant-a
  initiatorUserId=user-1
  correlationId=corr-001
  workflowType=CASE_APPROVAL
```

## Test 2 - Delegate does not require HTTP request context

```text
Given delegate executed by job executor
When CurrentRequestContext is not active
Then delegate still works using process variables
```

## Test 3 - Tenant-safe delegate query

```text
Given workflow variable tenantId=tenant-a caseId=CASE-001
When delegate loads case
Then repository is called with tenantId + caseId
```

## Test 4 - Duplicate start command

```text
Given same tenantId + businessKey + workflowType
When request is retried
Then only one active workflow mapping is created
```

## Test 5 - Message correlation unique key

```text
Given two process instances waiting for callback
When callbackCorrelationKey differs
Then each callback reaches the correct instance
```

## Test 6 - Duplicate callback

```text
Given same messageId delivered twice
When correlate message
Then second is ignored/rejected/idempotently handled
```

## Test 7 - Technical failure retry

```text
Given delegate external timeout
When worker fails
Then technical retry/incident path happens
And business state is not incorrectly marked rejected
```

## Test 8 - BPMN business error

```text
Given approval domain returns NOT_ELIGIBLE
When delegate handles result
Then BPMN Error path is taken
And no technical incident is created
```

# 28. Roadmap Latihan Praktis Seri 11

Urutan latihan:

```text
1. Buat WorkflowContextSnapshot
2. Buat WorkflowVariables DTO untuk start process
3. Buat WorkflowBusinessKeyFactory
4. Implement start process dari HTTP context
5. Simpan workflow mapping table untuk idempotency
6. Buat WorkflowContextExtractor dari DelegateExecution / Zeebe job
7. Refactor delegate agar tidak memakai CurrentRequestContext
8. Tambahkan MDC workflow context
9. Tambahkan tenant-aware repository call di delegate
10. Implement callbackCorrelationKey untuk request-reply
11. Implement message correlation + messageId
12. Tambahkan outbox untuk workflow command/event
13. Pisahkan BPMN Error vs technical exception
14. Tambahkan test duplicate start, duplicate callback, missing variables, tenant mismatch
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 11

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. HTTP request context, workflow context, dan job/delegate context adalah lifecycle yang berbeda.
2. Jangan inject/mengandalkan `@RequestScoped CurrentRequestContext` dalam Camunda async delegate/job worker.
3. Simpan context stabil sebagai business key/correlation key/process variables.
4. Process variables harus compact, stable, serializable/JSON-friendly, non-secret, dan versioned.
5. `tenantId`, `caseId`, `correlationId`, dan `initiatorUserId` adalah minimum context untuk workflow regulatory/case management.
6. Business key/correlation key harus domain-meaningful dan unik sesuai concurrency model.
7. Untuk request-reply, gunakan `callbackCorrelationKey` unik per interaction.
8. Untuk Camunda 8 messages, pahami TTL, message ID, duplicate subscriptions, dan correlation key.
9. Delegate/job worker harus extract context dari execution/job variables, lalu set MDC sendiri.
10. Semua DB access dari workflow harus tenant-aware.
11. Pisahkan business error yang dimodelkan di BPMN dari technical exception yang harus retry/incident.
12. Gunakan outbox/idempotency untuk start process, correlate message, publish event, dan external side effect agar aman terhadap retry.

**Seri 11/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 12 - Config System: Properties, Env Var, System Properties, Classpath Resource, Profile**, dengan fokus pada layered config, precedence, profile, fail-fast validation, CDI producer, Docker/Kubernetes mapping, dan safe config observability.

7/15/2026, 4:20:49 AM

# Seri 12/14 - Config System: Properties, Env Var, System Properties, Classpath Resource, Profile

Target seri ini: kamu bisa mendesain **configuration system** yang praktis untuk aplikasi **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld** yang berjalan di **local, Docker, Kubernetes, on-prem, AWS, Azure**, tanpa config chaos, silent fallback, secret leak, atau environment-specific bug.

Fokusnya:

> Config bukan sekadar `application.properties`.  
> Config adalah **runtime contract** antara source code, deployment pipeline, container runtime, secret/config platform, dan operational behavior.

MicroProfile Config adalah referensi mental model yang bagus karena ia mendefinisikan konfigurasi sebagai gabungan dari banyak `ConfigSource`, dengan default source seperti **system properties**, **environment variables**, dan `META-INF/microprofile-config.properties`; default ordinal-nya umum dipakai sebagai inspirasi precedence: system properties lebih tinggi dari env var, env var lebih tinggi dari classpath properties. ([Eclipse Downloads](https://download.eclipse.org/microprofile/microprofile-config-3.1/microprofile-config-spec-3.1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Config system harus menjawab pertanyaan ini:

```text
Apa value config-nya?
Dari source mana value itu datang?
Source mana yang menang kalau ada duplikasi?
Apakah value wajib?
Apakah value boleh default?
Apakah value secret?
Apakah value valid?
Apakah value immutable atau reloadable?
Apakah aman dicetak di startup log?
```

Layer praktis:

```text
Code default / classpath config
        ↓
application.properties
        ↓
profile-specific config
        ↓
external config file / mounted config
        ↓
environment variables
        ↓
JVM system properties -Dkey=value
        ↓
secret provider / external config provider
        ↓
runtime override / feature flag
```

Tapi precedence harus kamu definisikan eksplisit. Jangan biarkan tiap developer menebak-nebak.

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Config System Map
│
├── 1. Config Sources
│   ├── classpath resource
│   ├── application.properties
│   ├── profile-specific properties
│   ├── system properties
│   ├── environment variables
│   ├── mounted config files
│   ├── Kubernetes ConfigMap
│   ├── Kubernetes Secret
│   ├── Docker env/file
│   ├── AWS/Azure config/secret services
│   └── external config source loader
│
├── 2. Config Resolution
│   ├── key normalization
│   ├── precedence / ordinal
│   ├── fallback
│   ├── required value
│   ├── type conversion
│   ├── validation
│   └── fail-fast startup
│
├── 3. Profile Model
│   ├── local
│   ├── test
│   ├── dev
│   ├── staging
│   ├── prod
│   ├── onprem
│   ├── aws
│   └── azure
│
├── 4. CDI Integration
│   ├── AppConfig interface
│   ├── ConfigSource abstraction
│   ├── ConfigProducer
│   ├── typed config object
│   ├── config validation bean
│   └── injected config consumers
│
├── 5. Runtime Safety
│   ├── sanitized startup summary
│   ├── secret redaction
│   ├── config drift detection
│   ├── immutable vs reloadable config
│   ├── audit of config source
│   └── operational health/readiness
│
└── 6. Failure Modes
    ├── missing required config
    ├── wrong precedence
    ├── silent default in prod
    ├── env var naming mismatch
    ├── secret logged
    ├── config read directly in service
    ├── inconsistent config across instances
    └── reload breaks runtime invariants
```

---

# 3. Practical Table

| Area | Praktik | Yang Benar | Yang Salah | Failure Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Source abstraction | `ConfigSource` | Semua source lewat interface | `System.getenv()` tersebar | Sulit test/audit |
| Precedence | Explicit ordinal | Source menang jelas | Tergantung urutan random | Prod pakai value salah |
| Required config | Fail-fast | Missing DB URL membuat startup gagal | Fallback ke default diam-diam | Incident diam-diam |
| Secret | Separate provider | Secret tidak dicetak/logged | Secret di properties/JAR/log | Security incident |
| Profile | Explicit profile | `APP_PROFILE=prod` tervalidasi | `if env == ...` tersebar | Behavior beda-beda |
| Type conversion | Centralized | Duration/URI/int/boolean divalidasi | Parse manual di service | Runtime NPE/NumberFormat |
| CDI | Typed `AppConfig` | Inject config object | Baca env di business logic | Coupling tinggi |
| K8s | ConfigMap/Secret | Inject via env/file mount | Bake config ke image | Rebuild untuk config |
| Observability | Sanitized summary | Startup log source + redacted value | Print semua config | Secret leak |
| Reload | Explicit policy | Reloadable hanya untuk safe config | Reload semua value | DB/client chaos |
| Test | Override source | Fake config source | Test tergantung env lokal | Flaky test |

---

# 4. Source Types

## 4.1 Classpath Resource

Contoh:

```text
src/main/resources/application.properties
src/main/resources/application-local.properties
src/main/resources/META-INF/microprofile-config.properties
```

Cocok untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| default local value | password |
| non-sensitive default timeout | prod endpoint rahasia |
| feature default off | tenant-specific config |
| schema/template static | cloud credential |

MicroProfile Config default source mencakup property file `META-INF/microprofile-config.properties` di classpath dengan ordinal default lebih rendah dari env var dan system properties. ([GitHub](https://github.com/eclipse/microprofile-config/blob/master/spec/src/main/asciidoc/configsources.asciidoc?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4.2 System Properties

JVM system properties dibaca dengan `System.getProperty(...)`. Java SE 17 menyediakan API `System.getProperty(String key)` dan `System.getProperty(String key, String def)` untuk membaca system property. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/System.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh run:

```bash
java \
  -Dapp.profile=local \
  -Dhttp.port=8080 \
  -Ddb.pool.maxSize=10 \
  -jar case-service.jar
```

Cocok untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| local override | secret sensitif |
| test override | banyak config prod |
| debugging sementara | config kompleks |
| JVM-related toggle | config yang harus dikelola platform |

Catatan: system properties sering muncul di process command line atau diagnostics, jadi jangan jadikan tempat utama untuk secret.

---

## 4.3 Environment Variables

Java memakai `System.getenv(...)` untuk membaca environment variables; Oracle Java tutorial menjelaskan `System.getenv()` mengembalikan map read-only dari environment variables dan `System.getenv(name)` membaca value berdasarkan nama. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/tutorial/essential/environment/env.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```bash
export APP_PROFILE=local
export DB_JDBC_URL=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service
export DB_USERNAME=case_app
export REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=3000
```

Cocok untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| Docker/Kubernetes runtime config | config nested besar |
| endpoint/port/timeout | certificate multi-line kompleks, kecuali hati-hati |
| profile | large JSON config |
| feature default | frequently rotating secret, lebih baik file/secret provider |

Kubernetes Pod bisa mendefinisikan environment variables dengan field `env` atau `envFrom`; keduanya punya efek berbeda. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/define-environment-variable-container/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4.4 Mounted Files

Contoh:

```text
/config/application.properties
/config/db.properties
/secrets/db-password
/secrets/api-key
```

Cocok untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| config besar | value yang harus cepat berubah tanpa reload support |
| secret file mount | logic bisnis |
| certificate/key file | default kecil |
| on-prem deployment | config yang harus di-bundle ke JAR |

---

## 4.5 Kubernetes ConfigMap dan Secret

Kubernetes ConfigMap cocok untuk konfigurasi non-secret, sedangkan Secret adalah object khusus untuk data sensitif; Kubernetes docs menjelaskan Secret dapat diekspos ke container, termasuk sebagai environment variable menggunakan `env[].valueFrom`. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical split:

| Data | Kubernetes Object |
|---|---|
| `APP_PROFILE` | ConfigMap |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` | ConfigMap |
| `PAYMENT_BASE_URL` | ConfigMap |
| `DB_USERNAME` | Secret atau ConfigMap tergantung policy |
| `DB_PASSWORD` | Secret |
| TLS private key | Secret |
| API token | Secret |

Deployment env example:

```yaml
env:
  - name: APP_PROFILE
    valueFrom:
      configMapKeyRef:
        name: case-service-config
        key: APP_PROFILE
  - name: DB_PASSWORD
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: case-service-secret
        key: DB_PASSWORD
```

---

# 5. Config Precedence

Gunakan precedence eksplisit. Contoh yang sehat:

```text
Highest
  500: Runtime override / admin override
  450: External secret/config provider
  400: JVM system properties
  300: Environment variables
  250: Mounted config file
  200: Profile-specific classpath properties
  100: Default classpath application.properties
Lowest
```

MicroProfile Config memakai konsep `ConfigSource` dengan ordinal; default source yang umum adalah system properties ordinal 400, environment variables ordinal 300, dan classpath `META-INF/microprofile-config.properties` ordinal 100. Ini bisa dijadikan baseline desain meskipun kamu tidak memakai MicroProfile Config langsung. ([Eclipse Downloads](https://download.eclipse.org/microprofile/microprofile-config-2.0/microprofile-config-spec-2.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

```text
Semakin dekat ke runtime/deployment, semakin tinggi precedence.
Default classpath tidak boleh mengalahkan env/prod override.
```

---

# 6. Config Key Naming

Pilih satu canonical key format.

Saya rekomendasikan:

```text
app.profile
http.port
db.jdbc.url
db.pool.maxSize
external.payment.baseUrl
external.payment.timeoutMs
feature.caseApproval.enabled
```

Lalu map ke env var:

```text
APP_PROFILE
HTTP_PORT
DB_JDBC_URL
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_BASE_URL
EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_TIMEOUT_MS
FEATURE_CASE_APPROVAL_ENABLED
```

Mapper:

```java
public final class ConfigKeyNormalizer {

    private ConfigKeyNormalizer() {
    }

    public static String toEnvVar(String key) {
        return key
                .replace(".", "_")
                .replace("-", "_")
                .replaceAll("([a-z])([A-Z])", "$1_$2")
                .toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }
}
```

Examples:

| Canonical Key | Env Var |
|---|---|
| `app.profile` | `APP_PROFILE` |
| `db.jdbc.url` | `DB_JDBC_URL` |
| `db.pool.maxSize` | `DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE` |
| `external.payment.baseUrl` | `EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_BASE_URL` |
| `feature.caseApproval.enabled` | `FEATURE_CASE_APPROVAL_ENABLED` |

---

# 7. ConfigSource Abstraction

```java
public interface ConfigSource {

    String name();

    int ordinal();

    Optional<String> get(String key);
}
```

System properties source:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SystemPropertyConfigSource implements ConfigSource {

    @Override
    public String name() {
        return "system-properties";
    }

    @Override
    public int ordinal() {
        return 400;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<String> get(String key) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(System.getProperty(key));
    }
}
```

Environment variable source:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class EnvironmentVariableConfigSource implements ConfigSource {

    @Override
    public String name() {
        return "environment-variables";
    }

    @Override
    public int ordinal() {
        return 300;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<String> get(String key) {
        String envKey = ConfigKeyNormalizer.toEnvVar(key);
        return Optional.ofNullable(System.getenv(envKey));
    }
}
```

Classpath properties source:

```java
public class ClasspathPropertiesConfigSource implements ConfigSource {

    private final String resourceName;
    private final int ordinal;
    private final Properties properties;

    public ClasspathPropertiesConfigSource(
            String resourceName,
            int ordinal
    ) {
        this.resourceName = resourceName;
        this.ordinal = ordinal;
        this.properties = load(resourceName);
    }

    @Override
    public String name() {
        return "classpath:" + resourceName;
    }

    @Override
    public int ordinal() {
        return ordinal;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<String> get(String key) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(properties.getProperty(key));
    }

    private Properties load(String resourceName) {
        try (InputStream input = Thread.currentThread()
                .getContextClassLoader()
                .getResourceAsStream(resourceName)) {

            Properties props = new Properties();

            if (input != null) {
                props.load(input);
            }

            return props;
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    "Failed to load config resource: " + resourceName,
                    ex
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 8. Layered Config Resolver

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class LayeredConfig {

    private final List<ConfigSource> sources;

    @Inject
    public LayeredConfig(Instance<ConfigSource> configSources) {
        this.sources = StreamSupport.stream(configSources.spliterator(), false)
                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(ConfigSource::ordinal).reversed())
                .toList();
    }

    public ConfigValue getValue(String key) {
        for (ConfigSource source : sources) {
            Optional<String> value = source.get(key);

            if (value.isPresent()) {
                return new ConfigValue(
                        key,
                        value.get(),
                        source.name(),
                        source.ordinal()
                );
            }
        }

        return ConfigValue.missing(key);
    }

    public String required(String key) {
        ConfigValue value = getValue(key);

        if (value.missing()) {
            throw new MissingConfigException(key);
        }

        return value.value();
    }

    public String get(String key, String fallback) {
        ConfigValue value = getValue(key);
        return value.missing() ? fallback : value.value();
    }

    public List<ConfigSource> sources() {
        return sources;
    }
}
```

```java
public record ConfigValue(
        String key,
        String value,
        String source,
        int ordinal,
        boolean missing
) {
    public ConfigValue(
            String key,
            String value,
            String source,
            int ordinal
    ) {
        this(key, value, source, ordinal, false);
    }

    public static ConfigValue missing(String key) {
        return new ConfigValue(key, null, null, -1, true);
    }
}
```

```java
public class MissingConfigException extends RuntimeException {

    public MissingConfigException(String key) {
        super("Missing required config: " + key);
    }
}
```

---

# 9. CDI Producer untuk ConfigSource

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ConfigSourceProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public ConfigSource defaultClasspathConfigSource() {
        return new ClasspathPropertiesConfigSource(
                "application.properties",
                100
        );
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public ConfigSource profileClasspathConfigSource(LayeredBootstrapConfig bootstrap) {
        String profile = bootstrap.profile();

        return new ClasspathPropertiesConfigSource(
                "application-" + profile + ".properties",
                200
        );
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public ConfigSource mountedConfigSource() {
        return new FilePropertiesConfigSource(
                Path.of(System.getenv().getOrDefault(
                        "APP_CONFIG_FILE",
                        "/config/application.properties"
                )),
                250
        );
    }
}
```

Bootstrap problem:

```text
Untuk menentukan profile-specific file,
kita perlu profile lebih dulu.

Jadi gunakan bootstrap config minimal:
  1. system property app.profile
  2. env var APP_PROFILE
  3. fallback local/test only
```

Bootstrap config:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class LayeredBootstrapConfig {

    public String profile() {
        String system = System.getProperty("app.profile");

        if (system != null && !system.isBlank()) {
            return validateProfile(system);
        }

        String env = System.getenv("APP_PROFILE");

        if (env != null && !env.isBlank()) {
            return validateProfile(env);
        }

        return "local";
    }

    private String validateProfile(String profile) {
        Set<String> allowed = Set.of(
                "local",
                "test",
                "dev",
                "staging",
                "prod",
                "onprem",
                "aws",
                "azure"
        );

        if (!allowed.contains(profile)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid app.profile: " + profile);
        }

        return profile;
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Typed `AppConfig`

Jangan inject `LayeredConfig` ke semua service. Buat typed config.

```java
public interface AppConfig {

    String profile();

    int httpPort();

    URI databaseJdbcUrl();

    String databaseUsername();

    Duration databaseConnectionTimeout();

    int databasePoolMaxSize();

    URI paymentBaseUrl();

    Duration paymentTimeout();

    boolean caseApprovalEnabled();

    String serviceName();
}
```

Implementation:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class DefaultAppConfig implements AppConfig {

    private final LayeredConfig config;

    @Inject
    public DefaultAppConfig(LayeredConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    public String profile() {
        return requiredEnum(
                "app.profile",
                Set.of("local", "test", "dev", "staging", "prod", "onprem", "aws", "azure")
        );
    }

    @Override
    public int httpPort() {
        return intInRange("http.port", 8080, 1, 65_535);
    }

    @Override
    public URI databaseJdbcUrl() {
        return uriRequired("db.jdbc.url");
    }

    @Override
    public String databaseUsername() {
        return config.required("db.username");
    }

    @Override
    public Duration databaseConnectionTimeout() {
        return durationMillis("db.connectionTimeoutMs", 3_000, 100, 60_000);
    }

    @Override
    public int databasePoolMaxSize() {
        return intInRange("db.pool.maxSize", 10, 1, 100);
    }

    @Override
    public URI paymentBaseUrl() {
        return uriRequired("external.payment.baseUrl");
    }

    @Override
    public Duration paymentTimeout() {
        return durationMillis("external.payment.timeoutMs", 2_000, 100, 30_000);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean caseApprovalEnabled() {
        return bool("feature.caseApproval.enabled", true);
    }

    @Override
    public String serviceName() {
        return config.get("app.serviceName", "case-service");
    }

    private URI uriRequired(String key) {
        try {
            return URI.create(config.required(key));
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            throw new InvalidConfigException(key, "must be a valid URI", ex);
        }
    }

    private int intInRange(
            String key,
            int fallback,
            int min,
            int max
    ) {
        String raw = config.get(key, String.valueOf(fallback));

        try {
            int value = Integer.parseInt(raw);

            if (value < min || value > max) {
                throw new InvalidConfigException(
                        key,
                        "must be between " + min + " and " + max
                );
            }

            return value;
        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            throw new InvalidConfigException(key, "must be integer", ex);
        }
    }

    private Duration durationMillis(
            String key,
            long fallback,
            long min,
            long max
    ) {
        String raw = config.get(key, String.valueOf(fallback));

        try {
            long millis = Long.parseLong(raw);

            if (millis < min || millis > max) {
                throw new InvalidConfigException(
                        key,
                        "must be between " + min + " and " + max + " ms"
                );
            }

            return Duration.ofMillis(millis);
        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            throw new InvalidConfigException(key, "must be milliseconds", ex);
        }
    }

    private boolean bool(String key, boolean fallback) {
        String raw = config.get(key, String.valueOf(fallback));

        if ("true".equalsIgnoreCase(raw)) {
            return true;
        }

        if ("false".equalsIgnoreCase(raw)) {
            return false;
        }

        throw new InvalidConfigException(key, "must be true or false");
    }

    private String requiredEnum(String key, Set<String> allowed) {
        String value = config.required(key);

        if (!allowed.contains(value)) {
            throw new InvalidConfigException(
                    key,
                    "must be one of " + allowed
            );
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

```java
public class InvalidConfigException extends RuntimeException {

    public InvalidConfigException(String key, String message) {
        super("Invalid config " + key + ": " + message);
    }

    public InvalidConfigException(String key, String message, Throwable cause) {
        super("Invalid config " + key + ": " + message, cause);
    }
}
```

---

# 11. Config Usage in Service

Good:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentClient {

    private final AppConfig config;
    private final HttpClient httpClient;

    @Inject
    public PaymentClient(
            AppConfig config,
            HttpClient httpClient
    ) {
        this.config = config;
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
    }

    public PaymentResult capture(PaymentRequest request) {
        URI endpoint = config.paymentBaseUrl()
                .resolve("/payments/capture");

        Duration timeout = config.paymentTimeout();

        // build HTTP request with timeout
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Call payment provider");
    }
}
```

Bad:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentClient {

    public PaymentResult capture(PaymentRequest request) {
        String baseUrl = System.getenv("PAYMENT_BASE_URL");
        int timeout = Integer.parseInt(System.getenv("PAYMENT_TIMEOUT_MS"));
        // scattered config access
        return null;
    }
}
```

Kenapa buruk:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| source tidak jelas | sulit audit |
| parsing tersebar | inconsistent validation |
| test tergantung env | flaky |
| secret/config rawan log | security risk |
| precedence bypass | value tidak konsisten |

---

# 12. Config Validation at Startup

Fail fast.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ConfigStartupValidator {

    private final AppConfig config;
    private final ConfigReporter reporter;

    @Inject
    public ConfigStartupValidator(
            AppConfig config,
            ConfigReporter reporter
    ) {
        this.config = config;
        this.reporter = reporter;
    }

    @PostConstruct
    public void validate() {
        config.profile();
        config.httpPort();
        config.databaseJdbcUrl();
        config.databaseUsername();
        config.databaseConnectionTimeout();
        config.databasePoolMaxSize();
        config.paymentBaseUrl();
        config.paymentTimeout();
        config.caseApprovalEnabled();

        reporter.printSanitizedSummary();
    }
}
```

CDI 4.1 mendefinisikan CDI sebagai model untuk memperoleh object dengan reusability, testability, dan maintainability lebih baik; dalam praktik Jersey/CDI, startup validator sebagai CDI bean membuat validasi config masuk lifecycle aplikasi, bukan tersebar di tiap client/service. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 13. Sanitized Config Reporter

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ConfigReporter {

    private final LayeredConfig config;
    private final List<ConfigDescriptor> descriptors;

    @Inject
    public ConfigReporter(LayeredConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
        this.descriptors = ConfigRegistry.descriptors();
    }

    public void printSanitizedSummary() {
        System.out.println("Config sources by precedence:");

        for (ConfigSource source : config.sources()) {
            System.out.printf(
                    "  ordinal=%d source=%s%n",
                    source.ordinal(),
                    source.name()
            );
        }

        System.out.println("Effective config:");

        for (ConfigDescriptor descriptor : descriptors) {
            ConfigValue value = config.getValue(descriptor.key());

            if (value.missing()) {
                System.out.printf(
                        "  %s=<missing>%n",
                        descriptor.key()
                );
                continue;
            }

            System.out.printf(
                    "  %s=%s source=%s%n",
                    descriptor.key(),
                    descriptor.sensitive() ? "<redacted>" : value.value(),
                    value.source()
            );
        }
    }
}
```

```java
public record ConfigDescriptor(
        String key,
        boolean required,
        boolean sensitive,
        String description
) {
}
```

Registry:

```java
public final class ConfigRegistry {

    private ConfigRegistry() {
    }

    public static List<ConfigDescriptor> descriptors() {
        return List.of(
                new ConfigDescriptor("app.profile", true, false, "Runtime profile"),
                new ConfigDescriptor("http.port", true, false, "HTTP port"),
                new ConfigDescriptor("db.jdbc.url", true, false, "Database JDBC URL"),
                new ConfigDescriptor("db.username", true, false, "Database username"),
                new ConfigDescriptor("db.password", true, true, "Database password"),
                new ConfigDescriptor("external.payment.baseUrl", true, false, "Payment API"),
                new ConfigDescriptor("external.payment.apiKey", true, true, "Payment credential")
        );
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Startup log should show:
  key
  source
  sanitized value

Startup log must not show:
  password
  token
  apiKey
  private key
  secret value
```

---

# 14. Secrets vs Config

Config dan secret beda.

| Type | Example | Handling |
|---|---|---|
| Config | timeout, endpoint, pool size, profile | ConfigMap/env/file |
| Secret | password, API key, private key | Secret provider/secret file |
| Sensitive config | internal endpoint maybe | Redact in logs |
| Dynamic control | kill switch/feature flag | Feature flag/config service |

Interface:

```java
public interface SecretProvider {
    String requiredSecret(String key);
}
```

Env-based local provider:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class EnvironmentSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    @Override
    public String requiredSecret(String key) {
        String envKey = ConfigKeyNormalizer.toEnvVar(key);
        String value = System.getenv(envKey);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new MissingConfigException(key);
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

File-based provider:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FileSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    private final Path basePath;

    public FileSecretProvider() {
        this.basePath = Path.of(
                System.getenv().getOrDefault("SECRET_DIR", "/secrets")
        );
    }

    @Override
    public String requiredSecret(String key) {
        Path path = basePath.resolve(key.replace(".", "_"));

        try {
            String value = Files.readString(path).trim();

            if (value.isBlank()) {
                throw new MissingConfigException(key);
            }

            return value;
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            throw new MissingConfigException(key);
        }
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class DataSourceProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;
    private final SecretProvider secretProvider;

    @Inject
    public DataSourceProducer(
            AppConfig config,
            SecretProvider secretProvider
    ) {
        this.config = config;
        this.secretProvider = secretProvider;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource dataSource() {
        String password = secretProvider.requiredSecret("db.password");

        // build DataSource with password
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Create DataSource");
    }
}
```

---

# 15. Profile Model

Profile bukan environment if-else liar. Profile adalah input untuk memilih config source dan implementation.

Recommended profiles:

| Profile | Use |
|---|---|
| `local` | developer machine |
| `test` | automated test |
| `dev` | shared dev environment |
| `staging` | pre-prod |
| `prod` | production |
| `onprem` | on-prem deployment |
| `aws` | cloud AWS-specific |
| `azure` | cloud Azure-specific |

Anti-pattern:

```java
if (config.profile().equals("prod")) {
    // special behavior deep inside business service
}
```

Better:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretProviderProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public SecretProviderProducer(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public SecretProvider secretProvider() {
        return switch (config.profile()) {
            case "local", "test" -> new EnvironmentSecretProvider();
            case "onprem" -> new FileSecretProvider();
            case "aws" -> new AwsSecretProvider();
            case "azure" -> new AzureSecretProvider();
            case "prod", "staging", "dev" -> new FileSecretProvider();
            default -> throw new IllegalStateException(
                    "Unsupported profile: " + config.profile()
            );
        };
    }
}
```

Caveat:

```text
Kalau implementation punya banyak dependency CDI sendiri,
jangan `new` manual di producer seperti ini.
Gunakan CDI alternatives/qualifier/Instance selection.
```

---

# 16. Application Properties Example

`application.properties`:

```properties
app.profile=local
app.serviceName=case-service

http.port=8080

db.jdbc.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service
db.username=case_app
db.connectionTimeoutMs=3000
db.pool.maxSize=10

external.payment.baseUrl=http://localhost:9001
external.payment.timeoutMs=2000

feature.caseApproval.enabled=true
```

`application-prod.properties`:

```properties
app.profile=prod

http.port=8080

db.connectionTimeoutMs=2000
db.pool.maxSize=30

external.payment.timeoutMs=1500

feature.caseApproval.enabled=true
```

Do not put this in classpath properties:

```properties
db.password=supersecret
external.payment.apiKey=secret-value
```
