# 17. Docker Mapping

Docker run:

```bash
docker run --rm \
  -p 8080:8080 \
  -e APP_PROFILE=local \
  -e DB_JDBC_URL=jdbc:postgresql://host.docker.internal:5432/case_service \
  -e DB_USERNAME=case_app \
  -e DB_PASSWORD=dev-password \
  -e EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_BASE_URL=http://payment-mock:9001 \
  case-service:latest
```

Docker Compose:

```yaml
services:
  case-service:
    image: case-service:latest
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    environment:
      APP_PROFILE: local
      DB_JDBC_URL: jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/case_service
      DB_USERNAME: case_app
      EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_BASE_URL: http://payment-mock:9001
    secrets:
      - db_password

secrets:
  db_password:
    file: ./secrets/db_password
```

---

# 18. Kubernetes Mapping

ConfigMap:

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: case-service-config
data:
  APP_PROFILE: "prod"
  HTTP_PORT: "8080"
  DB_JDBC_URL: "jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/case_service"
  DB_USERNAME: "case_app"
  DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE: "30"
  EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_BASE_URL: "https://payment.internal"
  EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_TIMEOUT_MS: "1500"
```

Secret:

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: case-service-secret
type: Opaque
stringData:
  DB_PASSWORD: "replace-me-via-secret-manager"
  EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_API_KEY: "replace-me"
```

Deployment env:

```yaml
envFrom:
  - configMapRef:
      name: case-service-config
  - secretRef:
      name: case-service-secret
```

Kubernetes supports `env` and `envFrom` in Pod container configuration to define environment variables. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/define-environment-variable-container/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical warning:

```text
envFrom is convenient but can hide which keys are consumed.
For regulated/production services, explicit env entries are often easier to audit.
```

---

# 19. Readiness and Config

Readiness should fail if required config/dependency is invalid.

```java
@Path("/health/ready")
public class ReadinessResource {

    private final ReadinessService readinessService;

    @Inject
    public ReadinessResource(ReadinessService readinessService) {
        this.readinessService = readinessService;
    }

    @GET
    public Response ready() {
        ReadinessStatus status = readinessService.check();

        if (status.ready()) {
            return Response.ok(status).build();
        }

        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
                .entity(status)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Readiness checks:

| Check | Ready? |
|---|---:|
| config validated | required |
| DB pool can connect | usually required |
| external optional service unavailable | depends |
| secret provider unavailable | required if secret needed |
| feature flag provider unavailable | maybe fallback |
| outbox publisher down | app may still be ready but degraded |

---

# 20. Immutable vs Reloadable Config

Not all config should reload.

## Immutable at Startup

| Config | Why |
|---|---|
| `http.port` | server binding already done |
| `db.jdbc.url` | pool/client lifecycle |
| `db.pool.maxSize` | pool needs resize logic |
| `app.profile` | changes behavior globally |
| `secret provider type` | lifecycle/security |

## Potentially Reloadable

| Config | Why |
|---|---|
| feature kill switch | operational control |
| rate limit threshold | runtime tuning |
| external timeout | maybe safe if clients read per request |
| allowlist/routing rule | with validation/versioning |
| logging level | operational |

Reloadable config pattern:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ReloadableConfig {

    private final AtomicReference<RuntimeConfigSnapshot> current =
            new AtomicReference<>();

    @PostConstruct
    void load() {
        current.set(loadSnapshot());
    }

    public RuntimeConfigSnapshot current() {
        return current.get();
    }

    public void reload() {
        RuntimeConfigSnapshot next = loadSnapshot();
        validate(next);
        current.set(next);
    }

    private RuntimeConfigSnapshot loadSnapshot() {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Load runtime config");
    }

    private void validate(RuntimeConfigSnapshot snapshot) {
        // validate before swap
    }
}
```

Important:

```text
Reload must be atomic:
  load → validate → swap

Never partially update config fields.
```

---

# 21. Config for External Clients

Use typed client config.

```java
public record ExternalClientConfig(
        URI baseUrl,
        Duration connectTimeout,
        Duration requestTimeout,
        int maxRetries,
        boolean enabled
) {
}
```

Producer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ExternalClientConfigProducer {

    private final LayeredConfig config;

    @Inject
    public ExternalClientConfigProducer(LayeredConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Produces
    @PaymentClientConfig
    @ApplicationScoped
    public ExternalClientConfig paymentClientConfig() {
        return new ExternalClientConfig(
                URI.create(config.required("external.payment.baseUrl")),
                Duration.ofMillis(Long.parseLong(
                        config.get("external.payment.connectTimeoutMs", "500")
                )),
                Duration.ofMillis(Long.parseLong(
                        config.get("external.payment.timeoutMs", "2000")
                )),
                Integer.parseInt(config.get("external.payment.maxRetries", "1")),
                Boolean.parseBoolean(config.get("external.payment.enabled", "true"))
        );
    }
}
```

Qualifier:

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface PaymentClientConfig {
}
```

Usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentClient {

    private final ExternalClientConfig config;

    @Inject
    public PaymentClient(@PaymentClientConfig ExternalClientConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }
}
```

---

# 22. Config and Feature Flags

Feature flag is not the same as config.

| Config | Feature Flag |
|---|---|
| Controls environment/runtime parameter | Controls behavior rollout |
| Usually service-wide | Often tenant/user/segment-specific |
| Often startup immutable | Often dynamic |
| Owned by platform/app team | Owned by product/ops/team |
| Example: `db.pool.maxSize` | Example: `case.approval.newFlow.enabled` |

Config can supply default flag values:

```properties
feature.caseApproval.enabled=true
feature.caseExport.enabled=false
```

But mature rollout should use flag provider:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureFlagService {

    private final AppConfig config;
    private final Optional<FeatureFlagClient> client;

    @Inject
    public FeatureFlagService(
            AppConfig config,
            Optional<FeatureFlagClient> client
    ) {
        this.config = config;
        this.client = client;
    }

    public boolean caseApprovalEnabled(FeatureContext context) {
        return client
                .map(c -> c.evaluate("case.approval", context))
                .orElse(config.caseApprovalEnabled());
    }
}
```

---

# 23. Config Testing

## Unit Test dengan Fake Config

```java
public class MapConfigSource implements ConfigSource {

    private final String name;
    private final int ordinal;
    private final Map<String, String> values;

    public MapConfigSource(
            String name,
            int ordinal,
            Map<String, String> values
    ) {
        this.name = name;
        this.ordinal = ordinal;
        this.values = Map.copyOf(values);
    }

    @Override
    public String name() {
        return name;
    }

    @Override
    public int ordinal() {
        return ordinal;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<String> get(String key) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(values.get(key));
    }
}
```

Test precedence:

```java
@Test
void systemPropertyShouldWinOverClasspathDefault() {
    LayeredConfig config = new LayeredConfig(List.of(
            new MapConfigSource("classpath", 100, Map.of("http.port", "8080")),
            new MapConfigSource("system", 400, Map.of("http.port", "9090"))
    ));

    assertEquals("9090", config.required("http.port"));
}
```

Test required config:

```java
@Test
void missingRequiredConfigShouldFail() {
    LayeredConfig config = new LayeredConfig(List.of());

    assertThrows(
            MissingConfigException.class,
            () -> config.required("db.jdbc.url")
    );
}
```

Test redaction:

```java
@Test
void reporterShouldRedactSecrets() {
    ConfigDescriptor descriptor =
            new ConfigDescriptor("db.password", true, true, "DB password");

    assertEquals("<redacted>", sanitize(descriptor, "secret"));
}
```

---

# 24. Operational Troubleshooting

When config looks wrong, ask:

```text
1. What key is being read?
2. What env var does it map to?
3. Which ConfigSource returned the value?
4. What ordinal/precedence won?
5. Was profile resolved correctly?
6. Was value parsed/validated?
7. Was a fallback used?
8. Was secret provider involved?
9. Is this instance using same config as others?
10. Was config reloaded or immutable?
```

Startup summary should answer most of this without exposing secrets.

Example sanitized startup output:

```text
Config sources by precedence:
  ordinal=400 source=system-properties
  ordinal=300 source=environment-variables
  ordinal=250 source=file:/config/application.properties
  ordinal=200 source=classpath:application-prod.properties
  ordinal=100 source=classpath:application.properties

Effective config:
  app.profile=prod source=environment-variables
  http.port=8080 source=classpath:application.properties
  db.jdbc.url=jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/case_service source=environment-variables
  db.username=case_app source=environment-variables
  db.password=<redacted> source=environment-variables
  external.payment.baseUrl=https://payment.internal source=environment-variables
  external.payment.apiKey=<redacted> source=environment-variables
```

---

# 25. Anti-pattern

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| `System.getenv()` tersebar | Service baca env langsung | Sulit test/audit |
| Silent fallback in prod | missing DB URL fallback local | Prod incident |
| Secret di JAR | `db.password` di classpath properties | Security leak |
| Print all config | startup log raw values | Secret leak |
| Profile if-else deep in business logic | `if prod` di service | Coupling dan bug |
| No config source info | hanya value, tidak tahu source | Troubleshooting sulit |
| Env var naming inconsistent | `DB_URL` vs `DB_JDBC_URL` | Missing value |
| Config reload semua hal | reload DB URL/pool tanpa lifecycle | Runtime chaos |
| One giant config object mutable | setter everywhere | Drift/race |
| Test uses real env | depends on developer machine | Flaky test |
| Secret as system property | `-Ddb.password=...` | Exposed via process/diagnostics |
| ConfigMap for secret | password in ConfigMap | Security policy violation |
| Default prod value in code | fallback to prod endpoint | Dangerous local/test behavior |

---

# 26. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Source | Apakah config source terpusat dan auditable? |
| Key naming | Apakah canonical key dan env var mapping konsisten? |
| Precedence | Apakah urutan source eksplisit? |
| Required | Apakah required config fail-fast? |
| Default | Apakah default aman untuk local/test saja? |
| Type | Apakah value diparse dan divalidasi terpusat? |
| Secret | Apakah secret dipisah dan redacted? |
| Profile | Apakah profile tervalidasi dari allowlist? |
| CDI | Apakah service inject typed config, bukan baca env langsung? |
| Startup | Apakah ada sanitized config summary? |
| K8s | Apakah ConfigMap/Secret dipisah? |
| Reload | Apakah hanya config yang aman yang reloadable? |
| Test | Apakah ada fake config source dan precedence test? |
| Ops | Apakah source/value effective bisa ditroubleshoot? |

Urutan latihan:

```text
1. Definisikan canonical config keys
2. Buat ConfigSource interface
3. Implement SystemPropertyConfigSource

5. Implement ClasspathPropertiesConfigSource
6. Implement FilePropertiesConfigSource
7. Buat LayeredConfig dengan precedence
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 12

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. Config system adalah runtime contract, bukan sekadar `application.properties`.
2. Source config harus terpusat, punya precedence jelas, dan bisa diaudit.
3. MicroProfile Config memberi mental model bagus: banyak `ConfigSource`, merged view, ordinal/precedence, dan source default seperti system properties, env vars, classpath properties. ([Eclipse Downloads](https://download.eclipse.org/microprofile/microprofile-config-3.1/microprofile-config-spec-3.1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
4. Java system properties dibaca dengan `System.getProperty`, sedangkan env var dibaca dengan `System.getenv`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/System.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
5. Kubernetes runtime umumnya mengisi config lewat `env`, `envFrom`, ConfigMap, dan Secret. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/define-environment-variable-container/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
6. Jangan membaca env/system property langsung di service; inject typed `AppConfig`.
7. Required config harus fail-fast saat startup.
8. Secret harus dipisah dari config biasa dan selalu redacted.
9. Startup log harus menampilkan effective config source tanpa membocorkan secret.
10. Profile harus allowlisted dan tidak boleh menyebarkan `if profile` di business logic.
11. Tidak semua config boleh reload; pisahkan immutable startup config dan runtime reloadable config.
12. Test harus membuktikan precedence, validation, missing config, dan secret redaction.

**Seri 12/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 13 - Secret & External Config Source Loader: Vault/K8s/AWS/Azure Pattern**, dengan fokus pada secret provider abstraction, mounted secret, external secret manager, cache/rotation, fail-fast, redaction, RBAC/IAM, dan production failure modelling.

7/15/2026, 4:22:46 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 13/14**. Fokusnya: secret provider abstraction, external config/secret loader, K8s Secret, Vault/AWS/Azure pattern, cache/rotation, fail-fast, redaction, RBAC/IAM, dan failure modelling production.

# Seri 13/14 - Secret & External Config Source Loader: Vault/K8s/AWS/Azure Pattern

Target seri ini: kamu bisa mendesain **secret management + external config source loader** untuk aplikasi **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld** yang berjalan di **local, Docker, Kubernetes, on-prem, AWS, Azure**, tanpa membocorkan secret ke Git, image, log, process args, audit, Camunda variables, Kafka headers, atau error response.

Intinya:

> Secret bukan config biasa.  
> Secret adalah credential material yang harus punya lifecycle, access control, redaction, rotation, caching, failure policy, dan audit trail.

Kubernetes Secret bisa diekspos ke Pod sebagai environment variable atau volume/file; AWS Secrets Manager Java docs merekomendasikan client-side caching karena mengambil secret dari cache lebih cepat dan mengurangi biaya API call; Azure Key Vault Secrets client library dipakai untuk menyimpan dan mengontrol akses ke tokens, passwords, API keys, dan secrets lain; Vault dynamic secrets memakai lease ID yang bisa direnew/revoke. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/distribute-credentials-secure/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Secret management perlu menjawab:

```text
Secret ini berasal dari mana?
Siapa yang boleh membaca?
Bagaimana app authenticate ke secret store?
Apakah secret static atau dynamic?
Apakah secret di-cache?
Berapa TTL cache?
Bagaimana rotation terjadi?
Apa yang terjadi kalau secret store down?
Apakah app fail-fast atau degraded?
Apakah secret pernah masuk log/error/audit?
Apakah secret pernah masuk JVM args/container image/Git?
```

Layer yang benar:

```text
Application code
  └── SecretProvider interface
        ├── local env/file provider
        ├── Kubernetes mounted secret provider
        ├── Vault provider
        ├── AWS Secrets Manager provider
        ├── Azure Key Vault provider
        └── cached/redacted/validated wrapper
```

Yang salah:

```text
Business service
  ├── System.getenv("DB_PASSWORD")
  ├── print password saat startup
  ├── simpan token di static field tanpa TTL
  ├── forward secret ke downstream header
  └── taruh secret di Camunda variable / Kafka header
```

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Secret & External Config Source Loader
│
├── 1. Secret Categories
│   ├── database password
│   ├── API key
│   ├── OAuth client secret
│   ├── private key / certificate
│   ├── signing key
│   ├── encryption key reference
│   ├── service token
│   └── dynamic database credential
│
├── 2. Secret Sources
│   ├── local env var
│   ├── local secret file
│   ├── Docker secret
│   ├── Kubernetes Secret env
│   ├── Kubernetes Secret volume
│   ├── Vault
│   ├── AWS Secrets Manager
│   ├── Azure Key Vault
│   └── on-prem secret store
│
├── 3. Application Abstraction
│   ├── SecretProvider
│   ├── SecretRef
│   ├── SecretValue
│   ├── CachedSecretProvider
│   ├── RedactingSecretReporter
│   ├── StartupSecretValidator
│   └── SecretRotationHandler
│
├── 4. CDI/Weld Integration
│   ├── producer for SecretProvider
│   ├── producer for DataSource
│   ├── producer for external clients
│   ├── disposer/cleanup
│   └── fail-fast startup validation
│
├── 5. Runtime Policy
│   ├── least privilege
│   ├── fail-fast vs fail-open/degraded
│   ├── cache TTL
│   ├── rotation interval
│   ├── refresh strategy
│   ├── redaction
│   └── audit access
│
└── 6. Failure Modes
    ├── missing secret
    ├── expired secret
    ├── revoked secret
    ├── stale cache
    ├── rotation race
    ├── secret store outage
    ├── permission denied
    ├── secret leaked to log
    ├── secret baked into image
    └── app continues with wrong fallback
```

---

# 3. Practical Table

| Area | Praktik | Yang Benar | Yang Salah | Failure Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Secret access | `SecretProvider` abstraction | Service inject provider/typed client config | `System.getenv()` tersebar | Sulit audit/test |
| Secret value | Short-lived object | Ambil hanya saat perlu, cache sesuai policy | Simpan di public static/global DTO | Leak/stale |
| Logging | Redaction | Print `<redacted>` + source name | Print raw value | Security incident |
| Startup | Fail-fast | Required secret missing → startup gagal | Default password diam-diam | Prod compromise |
| K8s | Secret env/file | Gunakan env/volume sesuai kebutuhan | Hardcode secret di image | Image leak |
| Vault | Lease-aware | Renew/revoke dynamic secret | Treat dynamic secret as static | Expired credential |
| AWS | Cache client-side | Cache TTL/refresh policy | Call secret store every request | Latency/cost/outage coupling |
| Azure | Managed identity/credential chain | Use proper identity + Key Vault client | Put service principal secret in code | Credential leak |
| Rotation | Planned | Reload pool/client or restart safely | Assume secret changes magically | DB auth failures |
| External config | Validate source | Schema/type/required validation | Accept remote config blindly | Bad runtime behavior |
| RBAC/IAM | Least privilege | App can read only needed secrets | Wildcard access to all secrets | Blast radius besar |
| Tests | Fake provider | Unit test pakai fake/in-memory secrets | Test depends real cloud secret | Flaky/unsafe |

---

# 4. Secret vs Config vs Key Material

| Type | Example | Storage | Runtime Handling |
|---|---|---|---|
| Normal config | timeout, endpoint, pool size | ConfigMap, env, file | boleh ditampilkan sanitized |
| Secret | DB password, API key | Secret store, K8s Secret, Vault, AWS/Azure | selalu redacted |
| Key reference | KMS key ID, vault path | config | bukan secret, tapi sensitive-ish |
| Private key | TLS key, signing key | secret store/file mount | strict permission |
| Dynamic secret | Vault DB credential | Vault lease | renew/revoke/rotate |
| Token | OAuth access token | generated runtime | short TTL, jangan persist sembarang |
| Feature flag | rollout control | flag service/config | bukan secret |

Practical rule:

```text
Secret value tidak boleh menjadi:
  - config biasa
  - log field
  - metric tag
  - Kafka header
  - Camunda variable
  - exception message
  - HTTP response
  - GitHub Actions log output
  - Docker image layer
```

---

# 5. Core Abstraction

## 5.1 SecretRef

```java
public record SecretRef(
        String key,
        boolean required,
        SecretKind kind
) {
}
```

```java
public enum SecretKind {
    DATABASE_PASSWORD,
    API_KEY,
    OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET,
    PRIVATE_KEY,
    CERTIFICATE,
    SIGNING_KEY,
    ENCRYPTION_KEY_REFERENCE,
    DYNAMIC_DATABASE_CREDENTIAL
}
```

## 5.2 SecretValue

Jangan override `toString()` dengan raw value.

```java
public final class SecretValue {

    private final String key;
    private final String value;
    private final Instant loadedAt;
    private final Optional<Instant> expiresAt;

    private SecretValue(
            String key,
            String value,
            Instant loadedAt,
            Optional<Instant> expiresAt
    ) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Secret value is empty: " + key);
        }

        this.key = key;
        this.value = value;
        this.loadedAt = loadedAt;
        this.expiresAt = expiresAt;
    }

    public static SecretValue of(String key, String value) {
        return new SecretValue(key, value, Instant.now(), Optional.empty());
    }

    public static SecretValue expiring(String key, String value, Instant expiresAt) {
        return new SecretValue(key, value, Instant.now(), Optional.of(expiresAt));
    }

    public String key() {
        return key;
    }

    public String value() {
        return value;
    }

    public Instant loadedAt() {
        return loadedAt;
    }

    public Optional<Instant> expiresAt() {
        return expiresAt;
    }

    public boolean expiresSoon(Duration threshold) {
        return expiresAt
                .map(expiry -> Instant.now().plus(threshold).isAfter(expiry))
                .orElse(false);
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "SecretValue{key='%s', value='<redacted>', loadedAt=%s, expiresAt=%s}"
                .formatted(key, loadedAt, expiresAt.orElse(null));
    }
}
```

## 5.3 SecretProvider

```java
public interface SecretProvider {

    SecretValue getSecret(SecretRef ref);

    default SecretValue required(String key, SecretKind kind) {
        return getSecret(new SecretRef(key, true, kind));
    }

    default Optional<SecretValue> optional(String key, SecretKind kind) {
        try {
            return Optional.of(getSecret(new SecretRef(key, false, kind)));
        } catch (SecretNotFoundException ex) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
    }
}
```

Exceptions:

```java
public class SecretException extends RuntimeException {
    public SecretException(String message) {
        super(message);
    }

    public SecretException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }
}
```

```java
public class SecretNotFoundException extends SecretException {
    public SecretNotFoundException(String key) {
        super("Secret not found: " + key);
    }
}
```

```java
public class SecretAccessDeniedException extends SecretException {
    public SecretAccessDeniedException(String key, Throwable cause) {
        super("Access denied to secret: " + key, cause);
    }
}
```

```java
public class SecretUnavailableException extends SecretException {
    public SecretUnavailableException(String key, Throwable cause) {
        super("Secret store unavailable for key: " + key, cause);
    }
}
```

---

# 6. Local / Environment Provider

Cocok untuk local dev dan test sederhana. Jangan jadikan production default tanpa policy.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class EnvironmentSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    @Override
    public SecretValue getSecret(SecretRef ref) {
        String envKey = toEnvVar(ref.key());
        String value = System.getenv(envKey);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            if (ref.required()) {
                throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
            }

            throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
        }

        return SecretValue.of(ref.key(), value);
    }

    private String toEnvVar(String key) {
        return key
                .replace(".", "_")
                .replace("-", "_")
                .toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }
}
```

Example:

```bash
export DB_PASSWORD='dev-password'
export EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_API_KEY='dev-api-key'
```

Usage:

```java
SecretValue password = secretProvider.required(
        "db.password",
        SecretKind.DATABASE_PASSWORD
);
```

---

# 7. File / Mounted Secret Provider

Cocok untuk Docker secret, Kubernetes volume mount, on-prem secret file, atau agent-injected secret.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FileSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    private final Path secretDirectory;

    @Inject
    public FileSecretProvider(AppConfig config) {
        this.secretDirectory = config.secretDirectory();
    }

    @Override
    public SecretValue getSecret(SecretRef ref) {
        Path path = secretDirectory.resolve(toFileName(ref.key()));

        if (!Files.exists(path)) {
            throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
        }

        try {
            String value = Files.readString(path, StandardCharsets.UTF_8).trim();

            if (value.isBlank()) {
                throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
            }

            return SecretValue.of(ref.key(), value);
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            throw new SecretUnavailableException(ref.key(), ex);
        }
    }

    private String toFileName(String key) {
        return key.replace(".", "_").replace("-", "_").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }
}
```

Example filesystem:

```text
/secrets
├── DB_PASSWORD
├── EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_API_KEY
└── SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY
```

Practical permission check:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretFilePermissionValidator {

    public void validate(Path secretDirectory) {
        if (!Files.isDirectory(secretDirectory)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Secret directory does not exist: " + secretDirectory);
        }

        if (!Files.isReadable(secretDirectory)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Secret directory is not readable: " + secretDirectory);
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 8. Kubernetes Secret Pattern

Kubernetes Secret bisa dipakai sebagai env var atau file volume. Kubernetes docs menunjukkan Secret dapat dibuat dari key-value dan diekspos melalui `envFrom`, sehingga key Secret menjadi nama environment variable. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/distribute-credentials-secure/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 8.1 K8s Secret as Environment Variable

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: case-service-secret
type: Opaque
stringData:
  DB_PASSWORD: "replace-me"
  EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_API_KEY: "replace-me"
```

Deployment:

```yaml
env:
  - name: DB_PASSWORD
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: case-service-secret
        key: DB_PASSWORD
  - name: EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_API_KEY
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: case-service-secret
        key: EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_API_KEY
```

Pros:

| Pros | Cons |
|---|---|
| Simple | Secret ada di process environment |
| Mudah untuk legacy app | Rotation biasanya butuh restart |
| Cocok untuk sedikit secret | Bisa muncul di diagnostics tertentu |

## 8.2 K8s Secret as Mounted File

```yaml
volumes:
  - name: case-service-secret-volume
    secret:
      secretName: case-service-secret

volumeMounts:
  - name: case-service-secret-volume
    mountPath: /secrets
    readOnly: true
```

Pros:

| Pros | Cons |
|---|---|
| Lebih cocok untuk cert/key/multiline | App perlu file reader |
| Bisa mendukung update volume lebih baik | App harus handle reload |
| Tidak berada di process env | Path/permission perlu dikelola |

Practical recommendation:

```text
Gunakan env var untuk local/simple non-rotating secret.
Gunakan mounted file untuk private key, certificate, multiline secret, dan rotation-aware deployment.
Untuk production regulated system, prefer external secret operator/store + mounted files atau cloud-native identity.
```

---

# 9. Vault Pattern

Vault cocok untuk on-prem/hybrid secret management, dynamic secrets, lease, dan centralized policy. Vault dynamic secrets punya lease; ketika dynamic secret dibaca, Vault mengembalikan `lease_id` yang bisa dipakai untuk renew/revoke. ([HashiCorp Developer](https://developer.hashicorp.com/vault/docs/concepts/lease?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 9.1 Vault Concepts

```text
Vault
├── auth method
│   ├── Kubernetes auth
│   ├── AppRole
│   ├── JWT/OIDC
│   └── cloud IAM auth
│
├── secret engine
│   ├── KV static secret
│   ├── database dynamic credential
│   ├── PKI
│   └── transit
│
├── policy
│   └── path-level read/write capability
│
└── lease
    ├── lease_id
    ├── ttl
    ├── renewable
    ├── renew
    └── revoke
```

## 9.2 Vault Secret Provider Shape

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class VaultSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    private final VaultClient vaultClient;
    private final VaultSecretPathResolver pathResolver;
    private final Clock clock;

    @Inject
    public VaultSecretProvider(
            VaultClient vaultClient,
            VaultSecretPathResolver pathResolver,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.vaultClient = vaultClient;
        this.pathResolver = pathResolver;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Override
    public SecretValue getSecret(SecretRef ref) {
        String path = pathResolver.resolve(ref);

        try {
            VaultSecretResponse response = vaultClient.read(path);

            if (response.value() == null || response.value().isBlank()) {
                throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
            }

            if (response.leaseTtlSeconds() > 0) {
                return SecretValue.expiring(
                        ref.key(),
                        response.value(),
                        clock.instant().plusSeconds(response.leaseTtlSeconds())
                );
            }

            return SecretValue.of(ref.key(), response.value());

        } catch (VaultAccessDeniedException ex) {
            throw new SecretAccessDeniedException(ref.key(), ex);
        } catch (VaultUnavailableException ex) {
            throw new SecretUnavailableException(ref.key(), ex);
        }
    }
}
```

Conceptual DTO:

```java
public record VaultSecretResponse(
        String value,
        String leaseId,
        long leaseTtlSeconds,
        boolean renewable
) {
}
```

## 9.3 Dynamic DB Credential Caveat

Dynamic DB credential berbeda dari static password.

```text
Static DB password:
  app reads password
  pool uses password
  rotation usually restart/recreate pool

Dynamic DB credential:
  app requests credential
  Vault returns username/password + lease
  app must renew/recreate before expiry
  expired lease means credential can stop working
```

Practical rule:

```text
Kalau pakai Vault dynamic DB credential:
  - jangan cache tanpa TTL
  - track lease expiry
  - renew if renewable
  - recreate DataSource/pool safely before expiry
  - revoke on shutdown if appropriate
```

---

# 10. AWS Secrets Manager Pattern

AWS Secrets Manager Java documentation merekomendasikan client-side caching; cache lebih cepat daripada mengambil secret langsung dari Secrets Manager dan mengurangi biaya API call. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/secretsmanager/latest/userguide/retrieving-secrets_cache-java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 10.1 Provider Shape

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AwsSecretsManagerProvider implements SecretProvider {

    private final SecretsManagerClient client;
    private final AwsSecretIdResolver secretIdResolver;

    @Inject
    public AwsSecretsManagerProvider(
            SecretsManagerClient client,
            AwsSecretIdResolver secretIdResolver
    ) {
        this.client = client;
        this.secretIdResolver = secretIdResolver;
    }

    @Override
    public SecretValue getSecret(SecretRef ref) {
        String secretId = secretIdResolver.resolve(ref);

        try {
            GetSecretValueResponse response = client.getSecretValue(
                    GetSecretValueRequest.builder()
                            .secretId(secretId)
                            .build()
            );

            String value = response.secretString();

            if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
                throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
            }

            return SecretValue.of(ref.key(), value);

        } catch (ResourceNotFoundException ex) {
            throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
        } catch (AccessDeniedException ex) {
            throw new SecretAccessDeniedException(ref.key(), ex);
        } catch (SdkException ex) {
            throw new SecretUnavailableException(ref.key(), ex);
        }
    }
}
```

## 10.2 AWS Caching Wrapper

AWS also provides a Java caching client; the official JDBC integration docs note credentials can be cached and that by default cache refreshes every hour and when the secret is rotated. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/secretsmanager/latest/userguide/retrieving-secrets_jdbc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Abstraction tetap milik aplikasi:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CachedSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    private final SecretProvider delegate;
    private final SecretCachePolicy cachePolicy;
    private final ConcurrentMap<String, CachedSecret> cache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @Inject
    public CachedSecretProvider(
            @RawSecretProvider SecretProvider delegate,
            SecretCachePolicy cachePolicy
    ) {
        this.delegate = delegate;
        this.cachePolicy = cachePolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public SecretValue getSecret(SecretRef ref) {
        if (!cachePolicy.cacheable(ref)) {
            return delegate.getSecret(ref);
        }

        CachedSecret cached = cache.get(ref.key());

        if (cached != null && !cached.isExpired()) {
            return cached.value();
        }

        SecretValue fresh = delegate.getSecret(ref);
        cache.put(ref.key(), CachedSecret.of(fresh, cachePolicy.ttl(ref)));

        return fresh;
    }
}
```

```java
public record CachedSecret(
        SecretValue value,
        Instant expiresAt
) {
    public static CachedSecret of(SecretValue value, Duration ttl) {
        return new CachedSecret(value, Instant.now().plus(ttl));
    }

    public boolean isExpired() {
        return Instant.now().isAfter(expiresAt);
    }
}
```

---

# 11. Azure Key Vault Pattern

Azure Key Vault Secrets client library stores and controls access to tokens, passwords, API keys, and other secrets; it supports operations such as create, retrieve, update, delete, purge, backup, restore, and list versions. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/java/api/overview/azure/security-keyvault-secrets-readme?view=azure-java-stable&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Azure Identity’s `DefaultAzureCredential` is designed to simplify authentication across local development and Azure-hosted environments. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/java/api/overview/azure/identity-readme?view=azure-java-stable&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 11.1 Client Producer

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AzureKeyVaultClientProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public AzureKeyVaultClientProducer(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public SecretClient secretClient() {
        return new SecretClientBuilder()
                .vaultUrl(config.azureKeyVaultUrl().toString())
                .credential(new DefaultAzureCredentialBuilder().build())
                .buildClient();
    }
}
```

## 11.2 Provider

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AzureKeyVaultSecretProvider implements SecretProvider {

    private final SecretClient secretClient;
    private final AzureSecretNameResolver nameResolver;

    @Inject
    public AzureKeyVaultSecretProvider(
            SecretClient secretClient,
            AzureSecretNameResolver nameResolver
    ) {
        this.secretClient = secretClient;
        this.nameResolver = nameResolver;
    }

    @Override
    public SecretValue getSecret(SecretRef ref) {
        String name = nameResolver.resolve(ref);

        try {
            KeyVaultSecret secret = secretClient.getSecret(name);

            String value = secret.getValue();

            if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
                throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
            }

            OffsetDateTime expiresOn = secret.getProperties().getExpiresOn();

            if (expiresOn != null) {
                return SecretValue.expiring(
                        ref.key(),
                        value,
                        expiresOn.toInstant()
                );
            }

            return SecretValue.of(ref.key(), value);

        } catch (ResourceNotFoundException ex) {
            throw new SecretNotFoundException(ref.key());
        } catch (HttpResponseException ex) {
            throw new SecretUnavailableException(ref.key(), ex);
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 12. CDI Producer Strategy

## 12.1 Qualifier

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface RawSecretProvider {
}
```

## 12.2 SecretProvider Producer

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretProviderProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;
    private final Instance<EnvironmentSecretProvider> envProvider;
    private final Instance<FileSecretProvider> fileProvider;
    private final Instance<VaultSecretProvider> vaultProvider;
    private final Instance<AwsSecretsManagerProvider> awsProvider;
    private final Instance<AzureKeyVaultSecretProvider> azureProvider;

    @Inject
    public SecretProviderProducer(
            AppConfig config,
            Instance<EnvironmentSecretProvider> envProvider,
            Instance<FileSecretProvider> fileProvider,
            Instance<VaultSecretProvider> vaultProvider,
            Instance<AwsSecretsManagerProvider> awsProvider,
            Instance<AzureKeyVaultSecretProvider> azureProvider
    ) {
        this.config = config;
        this.envProvider = envProvider;
        this.fileProvider = fileProvider;
        this.vaultProvider = vaultProvider;
        this.awsProvider = awsProvider;
        this.azureProvider = azureProvider;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    @RawSecretProvider
    public SecretProvider rawSecretProvider() {
        return switch (config.secretProviderType()) {
            case "env" -> envProvider.get();
            case "file" -> fileProvider.get();
            case "vault" -> vaultProvider.get();
            case "aws-secrets-manager" -> awsProvider.get();
            case "azure-key-vault" -> azureProvider.get();
            default -> throw new IllegalStateException(
                    "Unsupported secret provider type: " + config.secretProviderType()
            );
        };
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public SecretProvider secretProvider(
            @RawSecretProvider SecretProvider rawProvider,
            SecretCachePolicy cachePolicy
    ) {
        return new CachedSecretProvider(rawProvider, cachePolicy);
    }
}
```

Important:

```text
Gunakan Instance<T> agar implementation tetap CDI-managed.
Jangan `new AwsSecretsManagerProvider(...)` manual kalau provider punya dependency CDI sendiri.
```

---

# 13. Secret Cache Policy

Tidak semua secret harus diperlakukan sama.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretCachePolicy {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public SecretCachePolicy(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    public boolean cacheable(SecretRef ref) {
        return switch (ref.kind()) {
            case DATABASE_PASSWORD -> true;
            case API_KEY -> true;
            case OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET -> true;
            case PRIVATE_KEY -> true;
            case CERTIFICATE -> true;
            case SIGNING_KEY -> true;
            case ENCRYPTION_KEY_REFERENCE -> true;
            case DYNAMIC_DATABASE_CREDENTIAL -> false;
        };
    }

    public Duration ttl(SecretRef ref) {
        return switch (ref.kind()) {
            case DATABASE_PASSWORD -> Duration.ofMinutes(10);
            case API_KEY -> Duration.ofMinutes(5);
            case OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET -> Duration.ofMinutes(5);
            case PRIVATE_KEY -> Duration.ofMinutes(30);
            case CERTIFICATE -> Duration.ofMinutes(30);
            case SIGNING_KEY -> Duration.ofMinutes(10);
            case ENCRYPTION_KEY_REFERENCE -> Duration.ofHours(1);
            case DYNAMIC_DATABASE_CREDENTIAL -> Duration.ZERO;
        };
    }
}
```

Policy guidance:

| Secret Type | Cache? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| DB static password | Ya | Rotation perlu pool refresh/restart |
| API key | Ya | TTL pendek-menengah |
| OAuth client secret | Ya | TTL pendek-menengah |
| Private key | Ya | Jangan reload terlalu sering |
| Dynamic DB credential | Hati-hati | Lease-aware, not plain cache |
| Access token | Usually no/simple TTL | Token punya expiry sendiri |
| Signing key set/public JWKS | Ya | Cache with refresh |
| KMS key ID | Ya | Ini reference, bukan raw key |

---

# 14. Startup Secret Validation

Fail-fast untuk secret wajib.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class StartupSecretValidator {

    private final SecretProvider secretProvider;
    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public StartupSecretValidator(
            SecretProvider secretProvider,
            AppConfig config
    ) {
        this.secretProvider = secretProvider;
        this.config = config;
    }

    @PostConstruct
    public void validate() {
        validateRequired("db.password", SecretKind.DATABASE_PASSWORD);

        if (config.paymentIntegrationEnabled()) {
            validateRequired("external.payment.apiKey", SecretKind.API_KEY);
        }

        if (config.jwtSigningEnabled()) {
            validateRequired("jwt.signing.privateKey", SecretKind.PRIVATE_KEY);
        }

        System.out.println("Startup secret validation completed: values redacted");
    }

    private void validateRequired(String key, SecretKind kind) {
        SecretValue value = secretProvider.required(key, kind);

        if (value.value().isBlank()) {
            throw new SecretNotFoundException(key);
        }
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Required secret missing:
  local/test → fail clearly
  prod → fail startup/readiness

Optional integration secret missing:
  disable integration explicitly or fail based on policy
```

---

# 15. DataSource with Secret

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class DataSourceProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;
    private final SecretProvider secretProvider;

    @Inject
    public DataSourceProducer(
            AppConfig config,
            SecretProvider secretProvider
    ) {
        this.config = config;
        this.secretProvider = secretProvider;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource dataSource() {
        SecretValue password = secretProvider.required(
                "db.password",
                SecretKind.DATABASE_PASSWORD
        );

        HikariConfig hikari = new HikariConfig();
        hikari.setJdbcUrl(config.databaseJdbcUrl().toString());
        hikari.setUsername(config.databaseUsername());
        hikari.setPassword(password.value());
        hikari.setMaximumPoolSize(config.databasePoolMaxSize());
        hikari.setConnectionTimeout(config.databaseConnectionTimeout().toMillis());

        return new HikariDataSource(hikari);
    }

    public void close(@Disposes DataSource dataSource) {
        if (dataSource instanceof HikariDataSource hikari) {
            hikari.close();
        }
    }
}
```

Rotation caveat:

```text
Kalau db.password berubah:
  DataSource lama tidak otomatis memakai password baru.
  Biasanya perlu:
    - restart pod
    - recreate pool safely
    - use dynamic credential lifecycle handler
    - or use cloud/vendor JDBC secret integration if approved
```

---

# 16. External Client with API Key

Jangan expose API key ke service method. Bungkus di client.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentClient {

    private final HttpClient httpClient;
    private final AppConfig config;
    private final SecretProvider secretProvider;

    @Inject
    public PaymentClient(
            HttpClient httpClient,
            AppConfig config,
            SecretProvider secretProvider
    ) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.config = config;
        this.secretProvider = secretProvider;
    }

    public PaymentResult capture(PaymentRequest paymentRequest) {
        SecretValue apiKey = secretProvider.required(
                "external.payment.apiKey",
                SecretKind.API_KEY
        );

        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(config.paymentBaseUrl().resolve("/capture"))
                .timeout(config.paymentTimeout())
                .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + apiKey.value())
                .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(serialize(paymentRequest)))
                .build();

        // send request
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Call payment provider");
    }

    private String serialize(PaymentRequest request) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Serialize");
    }
}
```

Logging warning:

```text
Do not log HttpRequest headers if Authorization/API key present.
If you need debug logging, use allowlist logging:
  method, URI host/path, status, duration, correlationId
not headers/body by default.
```

---

# 17. External Config Source Loader

Kadang secret store juga menyimpan config non-secret. Jangan campur tanpa metadata.

## 17.1 ExternalConfigSource

```java
public interface ExternalConfigSource {

    String name();

    Optional<String> get(String key);

    boolean sensitive(String key);
}
```

## 17.2 Secret-backed ConfigSource

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretBackedConfigSource implements ConfigSource {

    private final SecretProvider secretProvider;
    private final SecretConfigKeyRegistry registry;

    @Inject
    public SecretBackedConfigSource(
            SecretProvider secretProvider,
            SecretConfigKeyRegistry registry
    ) {
        this.secretProvider = secretProvider;
        this.registry = registry;
    }

    @Override
    public String name() {
        return "secret-backed-config";
    }

    @Override
    public int ordinal() {
        return 450;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<String> get(String key) {
        if (!registry.isSecretConfigKey(key)) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        SecretKind kind = registry.kindOf(key);

        return Optional.of(
                secretProvider.required(key, kind).value()
        );
    }
}
```

Registry:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretConfigKeyRegistry {

    private final Map<String, SecretKind> secretKeys = Map.of(
            "db.password", SecretKind.DATABASE_PASSWORD,
            "external.payment.apiKey", SecretKind.API_KEY,
            "jwt.signing.privateKey", SecretKind.PRIVATE_KEY
    );

    public boolean isSecretConfigKey(String key) {
        return secretKeys.containsKey(key);
    }

    public SecretKind kindOf(String key) {
        SecretKind kind = secretKeys.get(key);

        if (kind == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Not a registered secret key: " + key);
        }

        return kind;
    }
}
```

Important:

```text
External config source boleh return value ke typed config,
tetapi ConfigReporter harus tahu key itu sensitive dan selalu redact.
```

---

# 18. Redaction Utility

```java
public final class SecretRedactor {

    private static final List<String> SENSITIVE_PATTERNS = List.of(
            "password",
            "passwd",
            "secret",
            "token",
            "apikey",
            "api_key",
            "privatekey",
            "private_key",
            "credential"
    );

    private SecretRedactor() {
    }

    public static String sanitize(String key, String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        if (isSensitiveKey(key)) {
            return "<redacted>";
        }

        return value;
    }

    public static boolean isSensitiveKey(String key) {
        String normalized = key
                .replace(".", "")
                .replace("-", "")
                .replace("_", "")
                .toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);

        return SENSITIVE_PATTERNS.stream()
                .anyMatch(normalized::contains);
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
System.out.printf(
        "config %s=%s%n",
        key,
        SecretRedactor.sanitize(key, value)
);
```

Practical warning:

```text
Redaction by key pattern is a safety net, not the main policy.
Main policy harus pakai ConfigDescriptor.sensitive=true.
```

---

# 19. Rotation Strategy

Rotation bukan hanya “secret value berubah”.

## 19.1 Rotation Impact Matrix

| Secret | Rotation Impact | Strategy |
|---|---|---|
| DB password static | Existing pool may fail on new connections | restart pod/recreate pool/dual credential |
| API key | New outbound calls need new key | cache TTL short + refresh |
| OAuth client secret | Token acquisition uses new secret | refresh credential provider |
| Private signing key | Affects token signing | key versioning + publish public key |
| Verify public key/JWKS | Affects token validation | cache with refresh |
| Vault dynamic DB credential | Expires by lease | renew/recreate before expiry |
| TLS cert | Connection handshake | reload cert or restart |
| Encryption key reference | Depends on KMS | usually reference rotation, not raw key |

## 19.2 Refreshable Secret Provider

```java
public interface RefreshableSecretProvider extends SecretProvider {

    void refresh(String key);

    void refreshAll();
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class RefreshableCachedSecretProvider implements RefreshableSecretProvider {

    private final SecretProvider delegate;
    private final SecretCachePolicy cachePolicy;
    private final ConcurrentMap<String, CachedSecret> cache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @Inject
    public RefreshableCachedSecretProvider(
            @RawSecretProvider SecretProvider delegate,
            SecretCachePolicy cachePolicy
    ) {
        this.delegate = delegate;
        this.cachePolicy = cachePolicy;
    }

    @Override
    public SecretValue getSecret(SecretRef ref) {
        CachedSecret cached = cache.get(ref.key());

        if (cached != null && !cached.isExpired()) {
            return cached.value();
        }

        SecretValue fresh = delegate.getSecret(ref);

        if (cachePolicy.cacheable(ref)) {
            cache.put(ref.key(), CachedSecret.of(fresh, cachePolicy.ttl(ref)));
        }

        return fresh;
    }

    @Override
    public void refresh(String key) {
        cache.remove(key);
    }

    @Override
    public void refreshAll() {
        cache.clear();
    }
}
```

## 19.3 Rotation Failure Modes

| Failure | Example | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Stale cache | old API key used after rotation | TTL/refresh event |
| Pool still uses old DB password | new connections fail | restart/recreate pool |
| Key version mismatch | signer uses new key, verifier lacks public key | overlap/rollout strategy |
| Secret store outage during refresh | app loses ability to call downstream | cache stale-if-safe policy |
| Dynamic secret expired | DB auth fails | lease renewal before expiry |
| Partial rollout | some pods use old/new secret | dual-secret window |

---

# 20. Fail-Fast vs Stale Cache vs Degraded

Define policy per secret.

| Scenario | Policy |
|---|---|
| DB password missing at startup | Fail-fast |
| Payment API key missing and payment mandatory | Fail-fast |
| Optional integration API key missing | Disable integration explicitly |
| Secret store down after startup | Use cached secret until TTL if policy allows |
| Secret expired | Do not use; refresh or fail |
| Dynamic credential near expiry | renew/recreate |
| Signing private key unavailable | Fail signing operation |
| Public verification keys stale | allow short stale window only if policy allows |

Stale cache policy:

```java
public enum SecretFailurePolicy {
    FAIL_CLOSED,
    USE_STALE_IF_NOT_EXPIRED,
    USE_STALE_WITH_MAX_STALENESS,
    DISABLE_FEATURE
}
```

Practical guidance:

```text
Security-sensitive mutation path:
  fail closed.

Read-only/cosmetic optional integration:
  degrade or disable feature.

Payment/financial/regulatory audit:
  fail closed or durable retry, never silent success.
```

---

# 21. IAM/RBAC/Least Privilege

Application identity should only read the secrets it needs.

```text
case-service identity:
  can read:
    /prod/case-service/db-password
    /prod/case-service/payment-api-key

  cannot read:
    /prod/other-service/*
    /prod/admin/*
    /prod/root/*
```

Kubernetes-side:

```text
ServiceAccount
  └── Role/RoleBinding
       └── only required secret access if app calls K8s API directly
```

Cloud-side:

```text
AWS IAM role:
  secretsmanager:GetSecretValue only for specific ARNs

Azure Managed Identity:
  Key Vault Secret Get/List only as needed

Vault policy:
  read on specific paths only
```

Practical rule:

```text
Do not grant wildcard secret read to application runtime identity.
Limit by:
  environment
  service
  secret path/name
  operation
```

---

# 22. Secrets in CI/CD and GitHub Actions

Common leak points:

```text
- echo secret in shell
- Maven test prints env
- Docker build ARG with secret
- secret baked into image layer
- failed test dumps config
- debug logs print headers
- GitHub Actions artifact contains .env
```

Safer pattern:

```text
Build image:
  no production secret

Deploy:
  inject secret at runtime via K8s/cloud/on-prem secret manager

Test:
  use fake/local generated secret

Logs:
  redact
```

Docker warning:

```text
Avoid:
  docker build --build-arg DB_PASSWORD=...

Prefer:
  runtime env/secret mount
```

---

# 23. Secret Observability

Do log:

```text
secret key logical name
provider type
source path/name without value
loaded timestamp
cache hit/miss count
refresh success/failure count
expiry time metadata if not sensitive
```

Do not log:

```text
secret value
raw token
Authorization header
private key material
full secret JSON
provider response body
```

Metric examples:

```text
secret.load.success.count{provider, keyAlias}
secret.load.failure.count{provider, keyAlias, reason}
secret.cache.hit.count{keyAlias}
secret.cache.miss.count{keyAlias}
secret.refresh.success.count{keyAlias}
secret.refresh.failure.count{keyAlias}
secret.expiring_soon.count{keyAlias}
```

Use key alias, not raw secret path if path itself is sensitive.

---

# 24. Health/Readiness for Secret Store

Readiness should reflect required secret status carefully.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class SecretReadinessCheck {

    private final SecretProvider secretProvider;
    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public SecretReadinessCheck(
            SecretProvider secretProvider,
            AppConfig config
    ) {
        this.secretProvider = secretProvider;
        this.config = config;
    }

    public ReadinessComponentStatus check() {
        try {
            secretProvider.required("db.password", SecretKind.DATABASE_PASSWORD);

            if (config.paymentIntegrationEnabled()) {
                secretProvider.required("external.payment.apiKey", SecretKind.API_KEY);
            }

            return ReadinessComponentStatus.up("secrets");
        } catch (SecretException ex) {
            return ReadinessComponentStatus.down("secrets", safeReason(ex));
        }
    }

    private String safeReason(Exception ex) {
        return ex.getClass().getSimpleName();
    }
}
```

Caution:

```text
Health endpoint must not reveal:
  secret names that expose business meaning
  full paths
  raw error responses from provider
  credential values
```

---

# 25. Camunda/Kafka/HTTP Secret Safety

## Camunda

Never:

```text
process variable:
  dbPassword
  accessToken
  apiKey
  privateKey
```

Better:

```text
process variable:
  credentialRef = "payment-provider-primary"
```

Then worker resolves credential at runtime via `SecretProvider`.

## Kafka

Never:

```text
Kafka header:
  Authorization
  apiKey
  secret
```

Better:

```text
Kafka header:
  x-correlation-id
  x-tenant-id
  x-message-id
```

Consumer resolves secret locally if needed.

## HTTP/gRPC

Never blindly forward:

```text
Authorization: Bearer <raw-user-token>
X-Api-Key: <secret>
```

Better:

```text
Use service credential/token exchange/mTLS.
Propagate user/tenant as validated context only where needed.
```

---

# 26. External Config Loader: Validation Model

If external config source provides config, validate schema before use.

```java
public record ExternalRuntimeConfig(
        boolean paymentEnabled,
        Duration paymentTimeout,
        int maxConcurrentRequests,
        Instant loadedAt,
        long version
) {
}
```

Loader:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ExternalRuntimeConfigLoader {

    private final ExternalConfigSource externalConfigSource;
    private final AtomicReference<ExternalRuntimeConfig> current =
            new AtomicReference<>();

    @PostConstruct
    public void loadInitial() {
        current.set(loadAndValidate());
    }

    public ExternalRuntimeConfig current() {
        return current.get();
    }

    public void reload() {
        ExternalRuntimeConfig next = loadAndValidate();
        current.set(next);
    }

    private ExternalRuntimeConfig loadAndValidate() {
        boolean paymentEnabled = bool("payment.enabled", false);
        Duration paymentTimeout = duration("payment.timeoutMs", 2_000, 100, 10_000);
        int maxConcurrentRequests = intRange("limits.maxConcurrentRequests", 100, 1, 10_000);

        return new ExternalRuntimeConfig(
                paymentEnabled,
                paymentTimeout,
                maxConcurrentRequests,
                Instant.now(),
                version()
        );
    }

    private boolean bool(String key, boolean fallback) {
        return externalConfigSource.get(key)
                .map(Boolean::parseBoolean)
                .orElse(fallback);
    }

    private Duration duration(String key, long fallback, long min, long max) {
        long value = externalConfigSource.get(key)
                .map(Long::parseLong)
                .orElse(fallback);

        if (value < min || value > max) {
            throw new InvalidConfigException(key, "out of range");
        }

        return Duration.ofMillis(value);
    }

    private int intRange(String key, int fallback, int min, int max) {
        int value = externalConfigSource.get(key)
                .map(Integer::parseInt)
                .orElse(fallback);

        if (value < min || value > max) {
            throw new InvalidConfigException(key, "out of range");
        }

        return value;
    }

    private long version() {
        return externalConfigSource.get("config.version")
                .map(Long::parseLong)
                .orElse(0L);
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
External config reload:
  load → parse → validate → atomic swap

Never:
  partially update mutable fields one by one.
```

---

# 27. Testing Strategy

## Test 1 - Secret redaction

```text
Given secret key db.password
When config reporter prints startup summary
Then value is <redacted>
And raw value never appears
```

## Test 2 - Required secret fail-fast

```text
Given db.password missing
When app starts
Then StartupSecretValidator fails
And service does not become ready
```

## Test 3 - Provider selection

```text
Given secretProviderType=aws-secrets-manager
When SecretProvider is produced
Then AWS provider is selected via CDI-managed instance
```

## Test 4 - Cache TTL

```text
Given cached secret with TTL 5 minutes
When called twice before TTL
Then delegate provider called once
When TTL expires
Then delegate provider called again
```

## Test 5 - Access denied

```text
Given cloud provider returns access denied
When required secret is loaded
Then SecretAccessDeniedException is thrown
And raw provider response is not logged
```

## Test 6 - Rotation refresh

```text
Given cached API key old value
When refresh("external.payment.apiKey") called
Then next getSecret loads new value
```

## Test 7 - Secret never enters Kafka/Camunda

```text
Scan produced Kafka headers and Camunda variables.
Assert no key matches:
  password, secret, token, apiKey, privateKey
```

---

# 28. Anti-pattern

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Secret in Git | `application-prod.properties` contains password | Permanent leak |
| Secret in Docker image | COPY `.env` into image | Image registry leak |
| Secret in JVM args | `-Ddb.password=...` | Process diagnostics leak |
| Secret in logs | print config map | Security incident |
| Secret in exception | `throw new RuntimeException(secret)` | Client/log leak |
| Secret in Camunda variable | `apiKey` process variable | Long-lived leak |
| Secret in Kafka header | `Authorization` header | Wide broadcast leak |
| Blind cloud IAM wildcard | `secretsmanager:*` on `*` | Huge blast radius |
| No cache | fetch secret every request | latency/cost/outage coupling |
| Infinite cache | never refresh | rotation failure |
| Dynamic secret treated static | no lease renewal | expired credential |
| Silent default password | fallback `"password"` | compromise |
| Manual provider `new` | bypass CDI dependencies | lifecycle/test issues |
| Health endpoint exposes path | provider raw error shown | reconnaissance risk |

---

# 29. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Abstraction | Apakah semua secret lewat `SecretProvider`? |
| Source | Apakah provider dipilih eksplisit dari config/profile? |
| CDI | Apakah provider implementation CDI-managed? |
| Required | Apakah secret wajib divalidasi saat startup? |
| Redaction | Apakah logs/config reporter selalu redact? |
| Caching | Apakah TTL sesuai secret kind? |
| Rotation | Apakah ada refresh/restart/recreate strategy? |
| Dynamic secret | Apakah lease/expiry dipahami? |
| IAM/RBAC | Apakah app hanya boleh baca secret yang dibutuhkan? |
| K8s | Apakah ConfigMap dan Secret dipisah? |
| Cloud | Apakah menggunakan runtime identity, bukan credential hardcoded? |
| DataSource | Apakah pool lifecycle jelas saat secret berubah? |
| HTTP/Kafka/Camunda | Apakah secret tidak dipropagate sebagai context? |
| CI/CD | Apakah secret tidak masuk image/artifact/log? |
| Testing | Apakah ada fake provider, redaction test, missing secret test? |

Urutan latihan:
