# Ringkasan Seri 13

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. Secret bukan config biasa; secret butuh abstraction, redaction, access control, lifecycle, cache, rotation, dan audit.
2. Semua secret harus lewat `SecretProvider`, bukan `System.getenv()` tersebar di service.
3. `SecretValue.toString()` harus selalu redacted.
4. Required secret harus fail-fast saat startup.
5. Kubernetes Secret bisa diekspos sebagai env var atau mounted file; pilih sesuai kebutuhan rotation, multiline secret, dan auditability. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/distribute-credentials-secure/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
6. AWS Secrets Manager Java usage sebaiknya memakai client-side caching untuk speed dan cost; official docs juga menyediakan Java SDK dan caching examples. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/secretsmanager/latest/userguide/retrieving-secrets-java-sdk.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
7. Azure Key Vault Secrets Java client cocok untuk tokens/passwords/API keys/secrets, dan `DefaultAzureCredential` membantu auth lintas local dan Azure-hosted environment. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/java/api/overview/azure/security-keyvault-secrets-readme?view=azure-java-stable&utm_source=chatgpt.com))
8. Vault dynamic secrets perlu lease-aware handling: TTL, renew, revoke, dan expiry. ([HashiCorp Developer](https://developer.hashicorp.com/vault/docs/concepts/lease?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
9. Caching secret harus TTL-aware; infinite cache merusak rotation.
10. Rotation perlu strategi per secret: restart pod, recreate pool/client, refresh cache, renew lease, atau dual-key window.
11. IAM/RBAC harus least privilege, bukan wildcard read semua secret.
12. Secret tidak boleh masuk log, metrics tag, HTTP response, Kafka header, Camunda variable, Docker image layer, Git, atau CI artifact.

**Seri 13/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 14 - Feature Flag + Runtime Control + Production Hardening**, dengan fokus pada feature flag abstraction, kill switch, tenant/user targeting, rollout, config vs flag boundary, operational controls, observability, cleanup lifecycle, dan failure modelling production.

---

**Sources:**

- [Distribute Credentials Securely Using Secrets](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/distribute-credentials-secure/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Lease, Renew, and Revoke | Vault](https://developer.hashicorp.com/vault/docs/concepts/lease?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Get a Secrets Manager secret value using Java with client- ...](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/secretsmanager/latest/userguide/retrieving-secrets_cache-java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Azure Key Vault Secret client library for Java](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/java/api/overview/azure/security-keyvault-secrets-readme?view=azure-java-stable&utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 4:24:56 AM

# Seri 14/14 - Feature Flag + Runtime Control + Production Hardening

Target seri ini: kamu bisa mendesain **feature flag dan runtime control** untuk aplikasi **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + CDI/Weld** yang aman dipakai di production, terutama untuk use case seperti:

- rollout fitur baru per tenant/user/role
- kill switch saat incident
- migration toggle
- canary release
- operational override
- degraded mode
- experimentation terbatas
- emergency disable untuk integrasi eksternal
- hardening agar flag tidak menjadi technical debt

Intinya:

> Feature flag bukan sekadar `if (enabled)`.  
> Feature flag adalah **runtime decision system** yang harus punya owner, default, targeting context, failure policy, observability, audit, expiry, dan cleanup lifecycle.

OpenFeature adalah spesifikasi vendor-agnostic untuk feature flagging. Ia menyediakan API standar dan konsep seperti provider, evaluation context, dan hooks; provider menjadi abstraction antara SDK dan sistem flag management yang sebenarnya, sehingga logic evaluasi bisa diganti tanpa refactor besar. ([OpenFeature](https://openfeature.dev/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Feature flag menjawab:

```text
Untuk request/user/tenant/context ini,
apakah fitur X aktif,
menggunakan variant apa,
dengan alasan evaluasi apa,
dan apa fallback kalau flag provider gagal?
```

Model yang benar:

```text
Request / Job / Message Context
│
├── tenantId
├── userId
├── roles/scopes
├── locale
├── app profile
├── service version
├── region/cloud/onprem
└── correlationId
        │
        ▼
Feature Evaluation Context
        │
        ▼
FeatureFlagService
        │
        ├── local default
        ├── external provider
        ├── cache
        ├── timeout
        ├── fallback policy
        └── audit/metric
        │
        ▼
Decision
├── enabled / disabled
├── variant
├── reason
├── source
└── fallback?
```

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Feature Flag + Runtime Control + Production Hardening
│
├── 1. Flag Types
│   ├── release flag
│   ├── experiment flag
│   ├── ops flag / kill switch
│   ├── permission flag
│   ├── migration flag
│   ├── config-like threshold
│   └── emergency override
│
├── 2. Evaluation Context
│   ├── tenant ID
│   ├── user ID
│   ├── role/scope
│   ├── profile/environment
│   ├── region/cloud/onprem
│   ├── service version
│   ├── request path/use case
│   └── correlation ID
│
├── 3. Feature Flag Abstraction
│   ├── FeatureFlagService
│   ├── FeatureFlagProvider
│   ├── FeatureContext
│   ├── FlagDecision
│   ├── FlagFallbackPolicy
│   ├── FlagRegistry
│   └── FlagAuditService
│
├── 4. Runtime Controls
│   ├── kill switch
│   ├── read-only mode
│   ├── integration disable
│   ├── queue drain mode
│   ├── maintenance mode
│   ├── traffic shaping
│   └── degraded mode
│
├── 5. Integration Points
│   ├── JAX-RS filter
│   ├── CDI interceptor
│   ├── service policy
│   ├── downstream client
│   ├── Kafka consumer/producer
│   ├── Camunda workflow
│   └── config fallback
│
├── 6. Production Hardening
│   ├── fail-open/fail-closed policy
│   ├── timeout/cache
│   ├── observability
│   ├── audit
│   ├── owner/expiry
│   ├── cleanup
│   ├── safe defaults
│   └── testing
│
└── 7. Failure Modes
    ├── provider outage
    ├── stale flag
    ├── wrong targeting
    ├── silent fallback
    ├── flag debt
    ├── inconsistent rollout
    ├── security bypass
    ├── migration split-brain
    └── kill switch too slow
```

---

# 3. Practical Table

| Area | Praktik | Yang Benar | Yang Salah | Failure Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Flag abstraction | `FeatureFlagService` | Service evaluate via abstraction | `if (System.getenv(...))` tersebar | Tidak auditable |
| Context | `FeatureContext` | Tenant/user/env/role jelas | Evaluate tanpa context | Rollout salah sasaran |
| Default | Safe default | Flag missing punya fallback eksplisit | Missing flag dianggap false/true diam-diam | Incident sulit trace |
| Fail policy | Per flag | Security/payment fail-closed | Semua fail-open | Risk exposure |
| Timeout | Strict | Provider timeout cepat + cache | Blocking flag provider | Latency cascade |
| Kill switch | Ops flag | Local/cache/emergency override cepat | Butuh deploy untuk disable | Incident lama |
| Audit | Flag change/evaluation critical | Audit perubahan flag | Tidak tahu siapa mengubah flag | Compliance gap |
| Cleanup | Expiry owner | Flag punya expiry/ticket | Flag hidup selamanya | Code complexity |
| Migration | Phase-based | dual-read/dual-write terkontrol | satu flag besar | Data split-brain |
| Test | Both branches | Test enabled/disabled/fallback | Hanya test happy path | Prod branch rusak |

---

# 4. Flag Types

| Type | Tujuan | Umur Ideal | Contoh |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Release flag | Rollout fitur baru | Pendek | `case.approval.v2.enabled` |
| Experiment flag | A/B atau variant | Pendek-menengah | `case.dashboard.variant` |
| Ops flag / kill switch | Disable fitur saat incident | Panjang tapi sedikit | `payment.capture.enabled` |
| Permission flag | Entitlement/customer-specific | Panjang | `tenant.bulkExport.enabled` |
| Migration flag | Data/model migration | Pendek-terkendali | `case.read.fromNewTable` |
| Config flag | Runtime threshold | Menengah | `risk.score.threshold` |
| Debug/diagnostic flag | Temporary diagnostics | Sangat pendek | `debug.decision.trace.enabled` |

Anti-pattern:

```text
Satu flag dipakai untuk banyak makna:
  case.newFlow.enabled
    ├── enable UI
    ├── enable DB write
    ├── enable Kafka publish
    ├── enable Camunda workflow
    └── enable external payment

Ini terlalu luas dan berbahaya.
```

Lebih baik:

```text
case.approval.v2.api.enabled
case.approval.v2.workflow.enabled
case.approval.v2.dualWrite.enabled
case.approval.v2.readFromNewModel.enabled
case.approval.v2.publishEvent.enabled
```

---

# 5. Config vs Feature Flag vs Secret

| Kategori | Contoh | Dynamic? | Sensitive? | Owner |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| Config | `db.pool.maxSize` | Jarang | Tidak | Platform/app |
| Secret | `db.password` | Rotated | Ya | Security/platform |
| Feature flag | `case.approval.v2.enabled` | Ya | Tidak | Product/engineering/ops |
| Kill switch | `payment.capture.enabled` | Ya | Tidak, tapi critical | Ops/incident commander |
| Runtime threshold | `risk.maxScore` | Bisa | Tidak | Domain/ops |
| Permission/entitlement | `tenant.bulkExport.enabled` | Ya | Tidak | Product/commercial |

Rule:

```text
Jangan memakai feature flag untuk secret.
Jangan memakai secret store untuk rollout logic tanpa metadata.
Jangan memakai config immutable untuk emergency kill switch.
```

---

# 6. Core Model

## 6.1 FeatureKey

```java
public enum FeatureKey {

    CASE_APPROVAL_V2("case.approval.v2.enabled"),
    CASE_BULK_EXPORT("case.bulkExport.enabled"),
    PAYMENT_CAPTURE("payment.capture.enabled"),
    PAYMENT_PROVIDER_V2("payment.provider.v2.enabled"),
    CAMUNDA_NEW_APPROVAL_WORKFLOW("camunda.caseApproval.newWorkflow.enabled"),
    KAFKA_CASE_EVENT_PUBLISH("kafka.caseEvent.publish.enabled");

    private final String key;

    FeatureKey(String key) {
        this.key = key;
    }

    public String key() {
        return key;
    }
}
```

Jangan pakai string literal tersebar:

```java
if (featureFlagService.enabled("new-flow")) { ... } // buruk
```

Gunakan enum/registry:

```java
if (featureFlagService.enabled(FeatureKey.CASE_APPROVAL_V2)) { ... }
```

---

## 6.2 FeatureContext

OpenFeature mendefinisikan **evaluation context** sebagai container untuk contextual data yang bisa dipakai sebagai basis dynamic evaluation; data static bisa global, sedangkan data dynamic seperti user/request bisa diberikan saat evaluasi. ([OpenFeature](https://openfeature.dev/docs/reference/concepts/evaluation-context/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java
public record FeatureContext(
        String tenantId,
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles,
        String profile,
        String region,
        String runtimePlatform,
        String serviceVersion,
        Locale locale,
        String correlationId
) {
    public FeatureContext {
        roles = roles == null ? Set.of() : Set.copyOf(roles);
        locale = locale == null ? Locale.ENGLISH : locale;
    }
}
```

Factory dari request context:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureContextFactory {

    private final CurrentRequestContext requestContext;
    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public FeatureContextFactory(
            CurrentRequestContext requestContext,
            AppConfig config
    ) {
        this.requestContext = requestContext;
        this.config = config;
    }

    public FeatureContext current() {
        RequestContextSnapshot snapshot = requestContext.snapshot();

        return new FeatureContext(
                snapshot.tenantId(),
                snapshot.userId(),
                snapshot.roles(),
                config.profile(),
                config.region(),
                config.runtimePlatform(),
                config.serviceVersion(),
                snapshot.locale(),
                snapshot.correlationId()
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 6.3 FlagDecision

```java
public record FlagDecision<T>(
        FeatureKey key,
        T value,
        String variant,
        String reason,
        String source,
        boolean fallback
) {
}
```

Example:

```text
key=payment.capture.enabled
value=false
variant=off
reason=KILL_SWITCH
source=external-provider
fallback=false
```

---

# 7. FeatureFlagProvider

```java
public interface FeatureFlagProvider {

    FlagDecision<Boolean> booleanFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            boolean defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    );

    FlagDecision<String> stringFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            String defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    );

    FlagDecision<Integer> intFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            int defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    );
}
```

---

# 8. Local / Config-backed Provider

Cocok untuk local, test, bootstrap, dan fallback.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ConfigBackedFeatureFlagProvider implements FeatureFlagProvider {

    private final LayeredConfig config;

    @Inject
    public ConfigBackedFeatureFlagProvider(LayeredConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    public FlagDecision<Boolean> booleanFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            boolean defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    ) {
        String raw = config.get(key.key(), String.valueOf(defaultValue));
        boolean value = Boolean.parseBoolean(raw);

        return new FlagDecision<>(
                key,
                value,
                value ? "on" : "off",
                "CONFIG_BACKED",
                "config",
                false
        );
    }

    @Override
    public FlagDecision<String> stringFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            String defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    ) {
        String value = config.get(key.key(), defaultValue);

        return new FlagDecision<>(
                key,
                value,
                value,
                "CONFIG_BACKED",
                "config",
                false
        );
    }

    @Override
    public FlagDecision<Integer> intFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            int defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    ) {
        String raw = config.get(key.key(), String.valueOf(defaultValue));

        return new FlagDecision<>(
                key,
                Integer.parseInt(raw),
                raw,
                "CONFIG_BACKED",
                "config",
                false
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 9. OpenFeature-backed Provider

OpenFeature Java SDK provides a standard server-side Java SDK, and providers are the abstraction between OpenFeature and the actual flag management system. The OpenFeature Java SDK docs show registering a provider with `OpenFeatureAPI.getInstance().setProvider(...)`. ([OpenFeature](https://openfeature.dev/docs/reference/sdks/server/java?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Conceptual adapter:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class OpenFeatureFlagProvider implements FeatureFlagProvider {

    private final Client client;

    @Inject
    public OpenFeatureFlagProvider(Client client) {
        this.client = client;
    }

    @Override
    public FlagDecision<Boolean> booleanFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            boolean defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    ) {
        EvaluationContext evaluationContext = toEvaluationContext(context);

        Boolean value = client.getBooleanValue(
                key.key(),
                defaultValue,
                evaluationContext
        );

        return new FlagDecision<>(
                key,
                value,
                value ? "on" : "off",
                "PROVIDER_EVALUATION",
                "openfeature",
                false
        );
    }

    @Override
    public FlagDecision<String> stringFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            String defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    ) {
        EvaluationContext evaluationContext = toEvaluationContext(context);

        String value = client.getStringValue(
                key.key(),
                defaultValue,
                evaluationContext
        );

        return new FlagDecision<>(
                key,
                value,
                value,
                "PROVIDER_EVALUATION",
                "openfeature",
                false
        );
    }

    @Override
    public FlagDecision<Integer> intFlag(
            FeatureKey key,
            int defaultValue,
            FeatureContext context
    ) {
        EvaluationContext evaluationContext = toEvaluationContext(context);

        Integer value = client.getIntegerValue(
                key.key(),
                defaultValue,
                evaluationContext
        );

        return new FlagDecision<>(
                key,
                value,
                String.valueOf(value),
                "PROVIDER_EVALUATION",
                "openfeature",
                false
        );
    }

    private EvaluationContext toEvaluationContext(FeatureContext context) {
        MutableContext mutable = new MutableContext(context.userId());

        mutable.add("tenantId", context.tenantId());
        mutable.add("profile", context.profile());
        mutable.add("region", context.region());
        mutable.add("runtimePlatform", context.runtimePlatform());
        mutable.add("serviceVersion", context.serviceVersion());
        mutable.add("locale", context.locale().toLanguageTag());
        mutable.add("correlationId", context.correlationId());

        return mutable;
    }
}
```

Catatan: kode OpenFeature di atas adalah pola integrasi. Sesuaikan import/class detail dengan versi SDK Java yang dipakai di project.

---

# 10. FeatureFlagService

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FeatureFlagService {

    private final FeatureFlagProvider provider;
    private final FeatureContextFactory contextFactory;
    private final FlagRegistry registry;
    private final FlagMetricRecorder metricRecorder;

    @Inject
    public FeatureFlagService(
            FeatureFlagProvider provider,
            FeatureContextFactory contextFactory,
            FlagRegistry registry,
            FlagMetricRecorder metricRecorder
    ) {
        this.provider = provider;
        this.contextFactory = contextFactory;
        this.registry = registry;
        this.metricRecorder = metricRecorder;
    }

    public boolean enabled(FeatureKey key) {
        FlagDefinition definition = registry.definitionOf(key);
        FeatureContext context = contextFactory.current();

        long started = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            FlagDecision<Boolean> decision = provider.booleanFlag(
                    key,
                    definition.defaultBoolean(),
                    context
            );

            metricRecorder.recordEvaluation(
                    key,
                    decision.value(),
                    decision.fallback(),
                    System.nanoTime() - started
            );

            return decision.value();

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            boolean fallback = definition.failurePolicy().fallbackValue();

            metricRecorder.recordFailure(key, ex);

            if (definition.failurePolicy().failClosed()) {
                return false;
            }

            return fallback;
        }
    }

    public String variant(FeatureKey key, String defaultValue) {
        FeatureContext context = contextFactory.current();
        return provider.stringFlag(key, defaultValue, context).value();
    }
}
```

---

# 11. Flag Registry

Flag registry membuat flag tidak liar.

```java
public record FlagDefinition(
        FeatureKey key,
        String description,
        String owner,
        String ticket,
        LocalDate expiresAt,
        FlagType type,
        boolean defaultBoolean,
        FlagFailurePolicy failurePolicy,
        boolean auditEvaluation
) {
}
```

```java
public enum FlagType {
    RELEASE,
    EXPERIMENT,
    OPS_KILL_SWITCH,
    PERMISSION,
    MIGRATION,
    RUNTIME_THRESHOLD,
    DIAGNOSTIC
}
```

```java
public record FlagFailurePolicy(
        boolean failClosed,
        boolean fallbackValue
) {
    public static FlagFailurePolicy failClosed() {
        return new FlagFailurePolicy(true, false);
    }

    public static FlagFailurePolicy failOpen() {
        return new FlagFailurePolicy(false, true);
    }

    public static FlagFailurePolicy fallback(boolean value) {
        return new FlagFailurePolicy(false, value);
    }
}
```

Registry:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FlagRegistry {

    private final Map<FeatureKey, FlagDefinition> definitions = Map.of(
            FeatureKey.CASE_APPROVAL_V2,
            new FlagDefinition(
                    FeatureKey.CASE_APPROVAL_V2,
                    "Enable v2 case approval flow",
                    "case-platform-team",
                    "CASE-1234",
                    LocalDate.of(2026, 9, 30),
                    FlagType.RELEASE,
                    false,
                    FlagFailurePolicy.failClosed(),
                    false
            ),

            FeatureKey.PAYMENT_CAPTURE,
            new FlagDefinition(
                    FeatureKey.PAYMENT_CAPTURE,
                    "Kill switch for payment capture",
                    "payment-team",
                    "PAY-7788",
                    LocalDate.of(2099, 12, 31),
                    FlagType.OPS_KILL_SWITCH,
                    true,
                    FlagFailurePolicy.failClosed(),
                    true
            )
    );

    public FlagDefinition definitionOf(FeatureKey key) {
        FlagDefinition definition = definitions.get(key);

        if (definition == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unregistered feature flag: " + key);
        }

        return definition;
    }

    public Collection<FlagDefinition> all() {
        return definitions.values();
    }
}
```

---

# 12. Feature Flag Evaluation Context untuk Tenant/User

Targeting harus eksplisit.

| Target | Contoh |
|---|---|
| tenant | aktif untuk `tenant-a`, `tenant-b` |
| user | aktif untuk user beta |
| role | hanya `CASE_ADMIN` |
| profile | aktif di `staging`, off di `prod` |
| region | aktif `ap-southeast-1` |
| runtime | `aws`, `azure`, `onprem` |
| service version | aktif mulai `2.4.0` |
| percentage rollout | 5%, 25%, 50%, 100% |
| workflow type | hanya `CASE_APPROVAL` |

Contoh deterministic percentage rollout sederhana:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PercentageRollout {

    public boolean included(String key, String targetId, int percentage) {
        if (percentage <= 0) {
            return false;
        }

        if (percentage >= 100) {
            return true;
        }

        int bucket = Math.floorMod(
                Objects.hash(key, targetId),
                100
        );

        return bucket < percentage;
    }
}
```

Target ID biasanya:

```text
tenantId untuk tenant rollout
userId untuk user rollout
tenantId:userId untuk more granular rollout
```

---

# 13. CDI Interceptor: `@FeatureGate`

Gunakan interceptor untuk use-case boundary.

```java
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD })
public @interface FeatureGate {

    @Nonbinding
    FeatureKey value();

    @Nonbinding
    boolean notFoundWhenDisabled() default true;
}
```

Interceptor:

```java
@FeatureGate(FeatureKey.CASE_APPROVAL_V2)
@Interceptor
@Priority(AppInterceptorPriority.FEATURE_GUARD)
public class FeatureGateInterceptor {

    private final FeatureFlagService featureFlagService;

    @Inject
    public FeatureGateInterceptor(FeatureFlagService featureFlagService) {
        this.featureFlagService = featureFlagService;
    }

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        FeatureGate gate = resolve(ctx);

        if (!featureFlagService.enabled(gate.value())) {
            if (gate.notFoundWhenDisabled()) {
                throw new NotFoundException("Feature is not available");
            }

            throw new ForbiddenException("Feature is disabled");
        }

        return ctx.proceed();
    }

    private FeatureGate resolve(InvocationContext ctx) {
        FeatureGate method = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(FeatureGate.class);

        if (method != null) {
            return method;
        }

        FeatureGate type = ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(FeatureGate.class);

        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }

        throw new IllegalStateException("@FeatureGate not found");
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseApprovalV2Service {

    @FeatureGate(FeatureKey.CASE_APPROVAL_V2)
    public ApprovalResult approve(String caseId, ApproveCommand command) {
        return null;
    }
}
```

---

# 14. JAX-RS Filter untuk Endpoint-level Flag

Untuk endpoint tertentu:

```java
@NameBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.METHOD })
public @interface RequiresFeature {
    FeatureKey value();
}
```

Filter:

```java
@Provider
@RequiresFeature(FeatureKey.CASE_APPROVAL_V2)
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION + 50)
public class FeatureEndpointFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Context
    ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    private final FeatureFlagService featureFlagService;

    @Inject
    public FeatureEndpointFilter(FeatureFlagService featureFlagService) {
        this.featureFlagService = featureFlagService;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        RequiresFeature annotation = resolve();

        if (annotation == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (!featureFlagService.enabled(annotation.value())) {
            throw new NotFoundException("Feature is not available");
        }
    }

    private RequiresFeature resolve() {
        Method method = resourceInfo.getResourceMethod();

        if (method != null && method.isAnnotationPresent(RequiresFeature.class)) {
            return method.getAnnotation(RequiresFeature.class);
        }

        Class<?> resourceClass = resourceInfo.getResourceClass();

        if (resourceClass != null && resourceClass.isAnnotationPresent(RequiresFeature.class)) {
            return resourceClass.getAnnotation(RequiresFeature.class);
        }

        return null;
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/approval-v2")
@RequiresFeature(FeatureKey.CASE_APPROVAL_V2)
public class CaseApprovalV2Resource {
}
```

---

# 15. Kill Switch Pattern

Kill switch harus sangat cepat dan aman.

Contoh payment capture:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentCommandService {

    private final FeatureFlagService featureFlagService;
    private final PaymentClient paymentClient;
    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;

    @Inject
    public PaymentCommandService(
            FeatureFlagService featureFlagService,
            PaymentClient paymentClient,
            OutboxRepository outboxRepository
    ) {
        this.featureFlagService = featureFlagService;
        this.paymentClient = paymentClient;
        this.outboxRepository = outboxRepository;
    }

    @TransactionalBoundary
    public PaymentResult capture(String paymentId, CaptureCommand command) {
        if (!featureFlagService.enabled(FeatureKey.PAYMENT_CAPTURE)) {
            throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Payment capture is temporarily disabled");
        }

        // Prefer outbox/job if external side effect is critical.
        return paymentClient.capture(paymentId, command);
    }
}
```

Kill switch design:

| Requirement | Why |
|---|---|
| Fail closed | Kalau provider gagal, payment disabled |
| Cached local emergency override | Provider outage saat incident tetap bisa disable |
| Metrics | Tahu berapa request diblok |
| Audit | Siapa mengubah kill switch |
| Runbook | Ops tahu flag mana harus diubah |
| Test | Simulasi disabled path |

---

# 16. Runtime Control Types

## 16.1 Read-only Mode

```java
public enum RuntimeMode {
    NORMAL,
    READ_ONLY,
    MAINTENANCE
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class RuntimeControlService {

    private final FeatureFlagService flags;

    @Inject
    public RuntimeControlService(FeatureFlagService flags) {
        this.flags = flags;
    }

    public boolean writeAllowed() {
        return flags.enabled(FeatureKey.PAYMENT_CAPTURE); // example only
    }

    public void assertWritesAllowed() {
        if (!writeAllowed()) {
            throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Service is in read-only mode");
        }
    }
}
```

Use for:

```text
DB migration
incident
downstream outage
manual maintenance
```

## 16.2 Integration Disable

```text
external.payment.enabled=false
external.documentGeneration.enabled=false
external.notification.enabled=false
```

When disabled:

| Integration | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Payment | reject command or queue durable retry |
| Notification | skip best-effort / enqueue later |
| Document generation | return accepted + pending |
| Risk scoring | fallback manual review |
| LLM/OCR | fallback human processing |

---

# 17. Feature Flag + Camunda

Workflow flags perlu hati-hati karena process bisa long-running.

## Start-time decision

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public StartedWorkflow startApproval(String caseId) {
    boolean useV2 = featureFlagService.enabled(
            FeatureKey.CAMUNDA_NEW_APPROVAL_WORKFLOW
    );

    String processKey = useV2
            ? "case_approval_v2"
            : "case_approval_v1";

    workflowStarter.start(processKey, caseId, Map.of(
            "approvalWorkflowVersion", useV2 ? "v2" : "v1"
    ));
}
```

Rule:

```text
Keputusan workflow version sebaiknya diambil saat start,
lalu disimpan sebagai variable.

Jangan membuat running process berubah jalur besar hanya karena flag berubah di tengah jalan,
kecuali memang BPMN dirancang untuk itu.
```

## Delegate-time decision

Boleh untuk behavior kecil:

```java
public void execute(DelegateExecution execution) {
    WorkflowContextSnapshot workflow = extractor.from(execution);

    if (featureFlagService.enabledForWorkflow(
            FeatureKey.KAFKA_CASE_EVENT_PUBLISH,
            workflow
    )) {
        outboxRepository.insert(...);
    }
}
```

But for critical path, prefer explicit process variable:

```text
publishCaseEvent=true
```

---

# 18. Feature Flag + Kafka

Producer:

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
public void approveCase(String caseId) {
    CaseRecord record = repository.findByTenantAndIdForUpdate(context.tenantId(), caseId);
    record.approve();
    repository.update(record);

    if (featureFlagService.enabled(FeatureKey.KAFKA_CASE_EVENT_PUBLISH)) {
        outboxRepository.insert(caseApprovedEvent(record));
    }
}
```

Consumer:

```java
public void onMessage(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record) {
    MessageContextSnapshot snapshot = extractor.extract(record);

    if (!featureFlagService.enabledForMessage(
            FeatureKey.CASE_APPROVAL_V2,
            snapshot
    )) {
        return; // or route to legacy handler
    }

    handlerV2.handle(record, snapshot);
}
```

Important:

```text
Kafka consumers must be idempotent.
Flag change can cause different handler path.
Store enough metadata to avoid reprocessing same message differently without control.
```

---

# 19. Migration Flags

Migration flag biasanya paling berbahaya.

## Safe phased migration

```text
Phase 0: old read, old write
Phase 1: old read, dual write old+new
Phase 2: compare old vs new in background
Phase 3: read new for selected tenants
Phase 4: read new for all, still dual write
Phase 5: write new only
Phase 6: cleanup old path
```

Flags:

```text
case.model.dualWrite.enabled
case.model.shadowCompare.enabled
case.model.readFromNew.enabled
case.model.writeOld.enabled
```

Anti-pattern:

```text
case.newDatabase.enabled
```

Satu flag besar untuk semua phase terlalu berbahaya.

---

# 20. Flag Observability

OpenFeature supports hooks that can add behavior at flag evaluation lifecycle points, such as validation, adding evaluation context, logging, telemetry, and tracking. ([OpenFeature](https://openfeature.dev/docs/reference/intro?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Metrics:

```text
feature.evaluation.count{flag, value, fallback, source}
feature.evaluation.failure.count{flag, reason}
feature.disabled.request.count{flag, endpoint}
feature.kill_switch.blocked.count{flag}
feature.provider.latency.ms{provider}
feature.cache.hit.count{provider}
feature.cache.miss.count{provider}
```

Avoid high-cardinality labels:

| Jangan jadi metric tag | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| userId | high cardinality + privacy |
| caseId | high cardinality |
| raw tenantId untuk ribuan tenant | bisa mahal; boleh bucket/tier |
| correlationId | unique per request |
| raw error message | cardinality explosion |

Logs for critical flag:

```text
correlationId
tenantId or tenantTier
flagKey
decision
variant
reason
fallback
provider
durationMs
```

---

# 21. Flag Audit

Audit perubahan flag, bukan semua evaluasi biasa.

Audit event:

```java
public record FeatureFlagAuditEvent(
        String flagKey,
        String action,
        String actor,
        String oldValue,
        String newValue,
        String reason,
        String ticket,
        Instant occurredAt
) {
}
```

Flag change should record:

| Field | Why |
|---|---|
| actor | siapa mengubah |
| old/new value | apa berubah |
| target rule | tenant/user/percentage |
| reason | kenapa |
| ticket/incident id | traceability |
| time | audit |
| approval if needed | change control |

For regulatory/payment flags:

```text
Payment capture disabled by incident commander
Reason: provider outage
Ticket: INC-2026-0715-001
Effective: all tenants
```

---

# 22. Flag Cleanup / Technical Debt

Feature toggle misuse can cause code complexity, dead code, and system failures; empirical work on feature toggle practices identifies management, initialization, implementation, and clean-up practices as important categories. ([arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1907.06157?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Registry check:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class FlagDebtChecker {

    private final FlagRegistry registry;

    @Inject
    public FlagDebtChecker(FlagRegistry registry) {
        this.registry = registry;
    }

    @PostConstruct
    public void checkExpiredFlags() {
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now();

        for (FlagDefinition flag : registry.all()) {
            if (flag.expiresAt().isBefore(today)) {
                System.err.printf(
                        "Expired feature flag: %s owner=%s ticket=%s expiresAt=%s%n",
                        flag.key().key(),
                        flag.owner(),
                        flag.ticket(),
                        flag.expiresAt()
                );
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Cleanup checklist:

```text
1. Feature fully rolled out?
2. Old branch unused?
3. Metrics show no disabled-path traffic?
4. Product/ops agree?
5. Remove flag from provider.
6. Remove code branch.
7. Remove tests for obsolete branch.
8. Remove docs/runbook entry.
9. Remove config fallback.
```

---

# 23. Failure Modelling

| Failure | Risk | Policy |
|---|---|---|
| Provider down | request latency/fallback | timeout + cache + per-flag failure policy |
| Flag missing | wrong default | registry default + alert |
| Wrong targeting | tenant sees wrong feature | dry-run + staged rollout |
| Stale cache | delayed kill switch | short TTL for kill switch |
| Fail-open security flag | unauthorized access | fail closed |
| Flag deleted while code uses it | fallback path | registry/test checks |
| Variant change during workflow | inconsistent process | persist start-time decision |
| Migration flag flipped incorrectly | data corruption | phase flags + guardrails |
| Evaluation slow | latency cascade | local cache + timeout |
| Flag debt | complexity | expiry and cleanup owner |

---

# 24. Testing Strategy

## Unit tests

```text
Flag enabled:
  service uses new path

Flag disabled:
  service uses old path or rejects

Provider exception:
  per-flag fallback policy works

Missing context:
  evaluation fails clearly or uses safe default

Expired flag:
  startup checker reports
```

## Integration tests

```text
JAX-RS endpoint with @RequiresFeature returns 404/403 when disabled.
CDI @FeatureGate blocks service method.
Payment kill switch blocks capture.
Kafka event publish skipped when disabled.
Camunda workflow starts v1/v2 based on flag and stores workflow version.
```

## Production simulation

```text
Flag provider timeout
Provider returns malformed value
Kill switch flips during load
Cache stale after provider recovery
Tenant-targeted rollout to tenant-a only
Migration dual-write compare mismatch
```

---

# 25. Anti-pattern

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| String flag key tersebar | `"new-flow"` everywhere | typo/silent fallback |
| No owner/expiry | flag permanent | technical debt |
| No safe default | missing flag unknown | incident |
| Fail-open security flag | auth flag provider down → allow | security breach |
| Flag evaluated without tenant | all tenants same | rollout salah |
| Flag controls too many concerns | one giant flag | unsafe migration |
| Provider call every request without timeout | blocking | latency cascade |
| No audit on flag changes | unknown actor | compliance gap |
| Secrets in flag values | API key in flag provider | secret leak |
| Flag used as permission system only | entitlement bypass | security gap |
| Long-running workflow depends on live flag every step | behavior changes mid-process | inconsistent process |
| No test disabled path | old path broken | incident during rollback |
| Cleanup ignored | dead code | complexity/system failure |

---

# 26. Code Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Registry | Apakah flag terdaftar dengan owner, ticket, type, expiry? |
| Context | Apakah evaluation context punya tenant/user/profile/version? |
| Default | Apakah default aman dan eksplisit? |
| Failure policy | Apakah fail-open/fail-closed sesuai risk? |
| Timeout/cache | Apakah provider call bounded? |
| Kill switch | Apakah ops-critical flag bisa bekerja saat provider degraded? |
| Security | Apakah flag bukan pengganti authorization utama? |
| Secret | Apakah flag tidak menyimpan secret/token? |
| Observability | Apakah evaluasi penting punya metric/log? |
| Audit | Apakah perubahan flag critical diaudit? |
| Migration | Apakah migration dipecah per phase? |
| Camunda | Apakah workflow version decision disimpan sebagai variable? |
| Kafka | Apakah flag path consumer idempotent? |
| Tests | Apakah enabled/disabled/fallback/provider-down path dites? |
| Cleanup | Apakah expiry/ticket cleanup jelas? |

---

# 27. Minimal Package Structure

```text
com.company.caseapp
├── feature
│   ├── FeatureKey.java
│   ├── FeatureFlagService.java
│   ├── FeatureFlagProvider.java
│   ├── FeatureContext.java
│   ├── FeatureContextFactory.java
│   ├── FlagDecision.java
│   ├── FlagDefinition.java
│   ├── FlagType.java
│   ├── FlagFailurePolicy.java
│   ├── FlagRegistry.java
│   ├── FlagMetricRecorder.java
│   ├── FlagDebtChecker.java
│   └── PercentageRollout.java
│
├── feature.provider
│   ├── ConfigBackedFeatureFlagProvider.java
│   ├── OpenFeatureFlagProvider.java
│   └── FeatureFlagProviderProducer.java
│
├── feature.interceptor
│   ├── FeatureGate.java
│   └── FeatureGateInterceptor.java
│
├── api.annotation
│   └── RequiresFeature.java
│
├── api.filter
│   └── FeatureEndpointFilter.java
│
└── runtime
    ├── RuntimeControlService.java
    ├── RuntimeMode.java
    └── MaintenanceModeGuard.java
```

---

# 28. Roadmap Latihan Praktis Seri 14

Urutan latihan:

```text
1. Definisikan FeatureKey enum
2. Buat FlagDefinition + FlagRegistry
3. Buat FeatureContext + FeatureContextFactory
4. Implement ConfigBackedFeatureFlagProvider
5. Implement FeatureFlagService dengan failure policy
6. Tambahkan metrics untuk evaluation/failure/fallback
7. Buat @FeatureGate CDI interceptor
8. Buat @RequiresFeature JAX-RS filter
9. Implement payment kill switch
10. Implement tenant-targeted rollout sederhana
11. Integrasikan flag decision ke Camunda start process
12. Integrasikan flag decision ke Kafka publish/consumer path
13. Tambahkan FlagDebtChecker untuk expired flags
14. Tambahkan tests enabled/disabled/provider-failure/fallback/expired flag
15. Dokumentasikan runbook kill switch dan cleanup process
```

---

# 29. Production Runbook Mini

Untuk kill switch:

```text
1. Identify feature flag:
   payment.capture.enabled

2. Confirm blast radius:
   all tenants / selected tenants / region

3. Disable flag:
   value=false

4. Verify:
   - metric feature.kill_switch.blocked.count increases
   - payment capture calls stop
   - API returns expected 503/accepted/degraded response
   - no secret/token in logs

5. Communicate:
   incident channel + ticket

6. Recovery:
   enable for internal tenant
   canary selected tenants
   full enable

7. Post-incident:
   audit flag changes
   review fallback behavior
   improve detection
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 14

Yang harus dikuasai dari seri ini:

1. Feature flag adalah runtime decision system, bukan sekadar `if`.
2. OpenFeature memberi standard API/vendor-agnostic mental model: provider, evaluation context, hooks, dan SDK abstraction. ([OpenFeature](https://openfeature.dev/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
3. Semua flag harus lewat `FeatureFlagService`, bukan string literal/config scattered.
4. Evaluation context minimal harus membawa tenant, user, profile, runtime platform, service version, dan correlation ID.
5. Flag registry harus punya owner, ticket, type, default, failure policy, expiry, dan audit requirement.
6. Kill switch harus fail-closed, cepat, observable, auditable, dan punya runbook.
7. Feature flag bukan pengganti authorization/security policy.
8. Migration flag harus dipecah per phase: dual-write, shadow compare, read new, write new, cleanup.
9. Workflow long-running harus menyimpan decision penting sebagai process variable agar tidak berubah liar di tengah process.
10. Kafka consumer path harus idempotent saat flag mengubah handler/routing.
11. Observability harus mencakup evaluation count, fallback, provider latency, kill-switch blocked count, dan failure count.
12. Setiap flag perlu cleanup lifecycle agar tidak menjadi technical debt.

**Seri 14/14 selesai.**

Seluruh seri **CDI/Weld Practical Implementation untuk Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice** sudah selesai: dari mental model IoC/DI, bootstrapping, bean discovery, scope, request pipeline, interceptor, context propagation, transaction, async, service-to-service, Camunda, config, secret, sampai feature flag dan production hardening.

---

**Sources:**

- [OpenFeature](https://openfeature.dev/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Software Development with Feature Toggles: Practices used by Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/1907.06157?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:29:05 PM

Saya akan buat cheat sheet praktis berbasis CDI/Weld modern, dipisah antara **standard CDI/Jakarta** dan **Weld-specific**, supaya jelas mana yang portable dan mana yang vendor-specific.

# Cheat Sheet CDI/Weld - Annotations, Classes, Methods, Properties

Versi mental model yang dipakai di sini: **Jakarta CDI 4.1+** dan **Weld modern**. Untuk stack Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS modern, pakai package **`jakarta.*`**, bukan `javax.*`. CDI 4.x default `beans.xml` memakai `bean-discovery-mode="annotated"`, artinya hanya class dengan bean-defining annotation yang otomatis dianggap bean kecuali kamu set mode lain. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

---

# 0. Portable vs Weld-specific

| Jenis | Contoh | Aman Dipakai Lintas CDI Implementation? |
|---|---|---:|
| Standard CDI/Jakarta | `@Inject`, `@ApplicationScoped`, `@Produces`, `Instance<T>`, `Event<T>`, `BeanManager` | Ya |
| Jakarta Interceptors | `@InterceptorBinding`, `@AroundInvoke`, `InvocationContext` | Ya |
| Jakarta Decorators | `@Decorator`, `@Delegate` | Ya |
| CDI SE | `SeContainerInitializer`, `SeContainer` | Ya |
| Weld SE | `org.jboss.weld.environment.se.Weld`, `WeldContainer` | Tidak, Weld-specific |
| Weld Bound Context | `BoundRequestContext`, `BoundSessionContext` | Tidak, Weld-specific |
| Weld config properties | `org.jboss.weld.*` | Tidak, Weld-specific |

---

# 1. Core CDI Annotations

## 1.1 Injection & Bean Declaration

| Annotation | Package | Fungsi | Contoh Praktis |
|---|---|---|---|
| `@Inject` | `jakarta.inject` | Constructor/field/method injection | Inject service/repository/config |
| `@Named` | `jakarta.inject` | Memberi bean name untuk EL/named lookup | Jarang perlu di microservice |
| `@Typed` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Membatasi bean types yang diekspos | Hindari ambiguous injection |
| `@Vetoed` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Exclude class/package dari CDI discovery | Exclude generated/helper class |
| `@Alternative` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Implementation pengganti yang hanya aktif jika enabled | Fake provider/test/onprem/cloud impl |
| `@Specializes` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Subclass menggantikan bean superclass | Override default impl |
| `@Priority` | `jakarta.annotation` | Enable alternative/interceptor dan menentukan order | Interceptor order, selected alternative |

Contoh constructor injection yang direkomendasikan:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    private final CaseRepository repository;
    private final CurrentRequestContext context;

    @Inject
    public CaseService(
            CaseRepository repository,
            CurrentRequestContext context
    ) {
        this.repository = repository;
        this.context = context;
    }
}
```

---

## 1.2 Scope Annotations

| Annotation | Package | Lifecycle | Cocok Untuk | Hindari |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `@ApplicationScoped` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Selama aplikasi hidup | Stateless service, repository, config, shared client | Mutable request/user/tenant state |
| `@RequestScoped` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Satu request/context aktif | `CurrentRequestContext`, user/tenant/correlation | Async worker/thread lain |
| `@Dependent` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Mengikuti owner/injection target | Helper kecil, object transient | Resource mahal tanpa cleanup |
| `@SessionScoped` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Session; passivating | Web session state | Jarang cocok untuk stateless microservice |
| `@ConversationScoped` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Long-running conversation | JSF/conversation apps | Jarang cocok untuk REST microservice |
| `@NormalScope` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Meta-annotation custom normal scope | Custom scope extension | Jangan buat custom scope tanpa kebutuhan kuat |
| `@Scope` | `jakarta.inject` | Meta-annotation pseudo/custom scope | Advanced custom scope | Jarang dipakai langsung |

CDI Lite standard scope minimal mencakup `@RequestScoped`, `@ApplicationScoped`, dan `@Dependent`; CDI Full menambahkan built-in scope seperti `@SessionScoped` dan `@ConversationScoped`. Request/application context lifecycle dikelola oleh container dan tidak otomatis propagate ke remote invocation atau async process. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

---

## 1.3 Lifecycle Callback

| Annotation | Package | Fungsi | Contoh |
|---|---|---|---|
| `@PostConstruct` | `jakarta.annotation` | Dipanggil setelah dependency injection selesai | Validate config, initialize cache |
| `@PreDestroy` | `jakarta.annotation` | Dipanggil sebelum bean dihancurkan | Close client, stop executor |
| `@Disposes` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Parameter disposer method untuk cleanup object hasil producer | Close `DataSource`, `KafkaProducer` |

Contoh disposer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class ClientProducer {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public HttpClient httpClient() {
        return HttpClient.newHttpClient();
    }

    public void close(@Disposes SomeExternalClient client) {
        client.close();
    }
}
```

---

# 2. Qualifier & Bean Selection

## 2.1 Standard Qualifiers

| Annotation | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@Default` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Qualifier default jika tidak ada qualifier lain |
| `@Any` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Semua bean punya qualifier ini; useful untuk `Instance<T>` dynamic lookup |
| `@Named` | `jakarta.inject` | Qualifier dengan name/string |

Setiap bean memiliki `@Any`; jika bean tidak mendeklarasikan qualifier selain `@Named` atau `@Any`, bean juga punya `@Default`. CDI memakai type + qualifier untuk typesafe resolution. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

## 2.2 Custom Qualifier

| Annotation | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@Qualifier` | `jakarta.inject` | Membuat qualifier sendiri |
| `@Nonbinding` | `jakarta.enterprise.util` | Member annotation tidak ikut resolution |

Contoh:

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface ReadReplica {
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class DataSourceProducer {

    @Produces
    @ReadReplica
    @ApplicationScoped
    public DataSource readReplicaDataSource() {
        return buildReadReplica();
    }
}
```

Injection:

```java
@Inject
public ReportRepository(@ReadReplica DataSource dataSource) {
    this.dataSource = dataSource;
}
```

---

# 3. Producer, Disposer, Injection Metadata

| Annotation/Class | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@Produces` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Membuat bean dari method/field |
| `@Disposes` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Cleanup object hasil producer |
| `InjectionPoint` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject.spi` | Metadata injection point saat producer dipanggil |

Producer cocok untuk object dari library eksternal yang tidak bisa kamu annotate langsung: `DataSource`, `SqlSessionFactory`, `HttpClient`, `KafkaProducer`, `ObjectMapper`, `Clock`, config object, secret provider.

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class MyBatisProducer {

    private final AppConfig config;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisProducer(AppConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory() {
        return buildSqlSessionFactory(config);
    }
}
```

---

# 4. Events

## 4.1 Event Annotations & Classes

| Item | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `Event<T>` | `jakarta.enterprise.event` | Fire event synchronous/asynchronous |
| `@Observes` | `jakarta.enterprise.event` | Observer method synchronous |
| `@ObservesAsync` | `jakarta.enterprise.event` | Observer method asynchronous |
| `Reception` | `jakarta.enterprise.event` | Observer hanya jika bean existing / always |
| `TransactionPhase` | `jakarta.enterprise.event` | Observer pada fase transaksi tertentu |
| `NotificationOptions` | `jakarta.enterprise.event` | Opsi async event |

`Event<T>` menyediakan `fire(T event)`, `fireAsync(U event)`, dan `fireAsync(U event, NotificationOptions)` untuk event synchronous/asynchronous. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

Contoh:

```java
public record CaseApprovedEvent(
        String tenantId,
        String caseId,
        String correlationId
) {
}
```

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class CaseService {

    @Inject
    Event<CaseApprovedEvent> events;

    public void approve(String caseId) {
        events.fire(new CaseApprovedEvent("tenant-a", caseId, "corr-001"));
    }
}
```

Observer:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class AuditObserver {

    public void onCaseApproved(@Observes CaseApprovedEvent event) {
        // write audit/outbox
    }
}
```

---

# 5. Interceptor / AOP

## 5.1 Core Annotations

| Annotation/Class | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@InterceptorBinding` | `jakarta.interceptor` | Membuat annotation binding untuk interceptor |
| `@Interceptor` | `jakarta.interceptor` | Menandai class sebagai interceptor |
| `@AroundInvoke` | `jakarta.interceptor` | Method wrapper business invocation |
| `@AroundConstruct` | `jakarta.interceptor` | Intercept constructor |
| `InvocationContext` | `jakarta.interceptor` | Metadata invocation, parameters, proceed |
| `@Priority` | `jakarta.annotation` | Enable/order interceptor |
| `@ExcludeClassInterceptors` | `jakarta.interceptor` | Exclude class-level interceptor |
| `@ExcludeDefaultInterceptors` | `jakarta.interceptor` | Exclude default interceptors |

Contoh binding:

```java
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface Audited {

    @Nonbinding
    String action();
}
```

Interceptor:

```java
@Audited(action = "")
@Interceptor
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 500)
public class AuditInterceptor {

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        try {
            Object result = ctx.proceed();
            // audit success
            return result;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            // audit failure
            throw ex;
        }
    }
}
```

CDI 4.1/Jakarta Interceptors 2.2 juga menambahkan cara standar untuk interceptor mengakses binding annotations dari `InvocationContext`, berguna untuk membaca metadata binding secara lebih langsung pada interceptor modern. ([jakartaee.github.io](https://jakartaee.github.io/cdi/2024/02/27/whats-new-in-cdi41.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. Decorator

| Annotation/Class | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@Decorator` | `jakarta.decorator` | Membungkus bean dengan interface tertentu |
| `@Delegate` | `jakarta.decorator` | Injection point delegate yang didecorate |
| `@Decorated` | `jakarta.decorator` | Metadata decorated bean |

Contoh:

```java
@Decorator
public abstract class AuditingPaymentClient implements PaymentClient {

    @Inject
    @Delegate
    PaymentClient delegate;

    @Override
    public PaymentResult capture(PaymentRequest request) {
        // before
        PaymentResult result = delegate.capture(request);
        // after
        return result;
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Interceptor:
  cocok untuk cross-cutting generic: audit, metric, transaction, permission.

Decorator:
  cocok untuk memperkaya behavior interface tertentu:
  PaymentClient, NotificationClient, Repository, ExternalClient.
```

---

# 7. Alternatives, Stereotypes, Specialization

| Annotation | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@Alternative` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Bean pengganti, perlu enabled |
| `@Priority` | `jakarta.annotation` | Enable alternative globally by priority |
| `@Specializes` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Subclass menggantikan bean superclass |
| `@Stereotype` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Gabungan annotation CDI |
| `@Model` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Stereotype untuk model web; jarang dipakai di microservice |

Contoh alternative:

```java
@Alternative
@Priority(1)
@ApplicationScoped
public class FakePaymentClient implements PaymentClient {
}
```

Atau enable via `beans.xml`.

---

# 8. Context Lifecycle Events

| Annotation | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@Initialized` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Event saat context initialized |
| `@BeforeDestroyed` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Event sebelum context destroyed |
| `@Destroyed` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Event setelah context destroyed |

Contoh:

```java
public void onRequestStart(
        @Observes @Initialized(RequestScoped.class) Object event
) {
    // request context initialized
}
```

Praktisnya di Jersey microservice, lebih sering kamu pakai JAX-RS filter untuk request setup daripada observer context lifecycle, tapi observer ini berguna untuk framework integration/debugging.

---

# 9. Programmatic Lookup

## 9.1 `Instance<T>`

`Instance<T>` menyediakan programmatic lookup dengan method seperti `select(...)`, `stream()`, `isUnsatisfied()`, `isAmbiguous()`, `isResolvable()`, `destroy(T)`, `getHandle()`, `handles()`, dan `handlesStream()`. CDI 4.1 juga mendefinisikan `Instance.Handle<T>` dengan `get()`, `getBean()`, `destroy()`, dan `close()`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

| Method | Fungsi Praktis |
|---|---|
| `get()` | Ambil bean, error jika unsatisfied/ambiguous |
| `select(qualifiers...)` | Narrow by qualifier |
| `select(Class<U>, qualifiers...)` | Narrow by subtype |
| `stream()` | Iterate semua matching beans |
| `isUnsatisfied()` | Tidak ada bean |
| `isAmbiguous()` | Lebih dari satu bean |
| `isResolvable()` | Tepat satu bean |
| `destroy(instance)` | Destroy contextual instance |
| `getHandle()` | Lazy contextual handle |
| `handles()` / `handlesStream()` | Iterate handle + metadata |

Contoh plugin-like registry:

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class NotificationHandlerRegistry {

    private final Map<String, NotificationHandler> handlers;

    @Inject
    public NotificationHandlerRegistry(@Any Instance<NotificationHandler> instance) {
        this.handlers = instance.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toUnmodifiableMap(
                        NotificationHandler::channel,
                        Function.identity()
                ));
    }

    public NotificationHandler handlerFor(String channel) {
        NotificationHandler handler = handlers.get(channel);

        if (handler == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported channel: " + channel);
        }

        return handler;
    }
}
```

---

## 9.2 `CDI.current()`

| Class | Package | Important Methods |
|---|---|---|
| `CDI<T>` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject.spi` | `current()`, `select(...)`, `getBeanManager()` |

Contoh:

```java
CaseService service = CDI.current()
        .select(CaseService.class)
        .get();
```

Gunakan hemat. Untuk app code biasa, constructor injection tetap lebih baik.

---

## 9.3 `BeanManager`

`BeanManager` adalah SPI penting untuk memperoleh contextual reference, resolve bean, events, extension integration, dan validasi injection point. CDI menyediakan built-in bean `BeanManager` dengan scope `@Dependent` dan qualifier `@Default`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

| Method | Fungsi Praktis |
|---|---|
| `getBeans(Type, Annotation...)` | Cari candidate beans |
| `resolve(Set<Bean<?>>)` | Resolve ambiguity |
| `getReference(...)` | Dapatkan contextual reference |
| `createCreationalContext(...)` | Buat creational context |
| `fireEvent(...)` | Fire CDI event via SPI |
| `getEvent()` | Dapatkan event builder |
| `validate(InjectionPoint)` | Validasi injection point |
| `resolveDecorators(...)` | Resolve decorators |
| `resolveInterceptors(...)` | Resolve interceptors |
| `getPassivationCapableBean(id)` | Lookup passivation capable bean |

Practical warning:

```text
BeanManager adalah advanced SPI.
Untuk business code, hindari kecuali membuat framework/integration/extension.
```

---

# 10. CDI SE Bootstrap

| Class | Package | Important Methods |
|---|---|---|
| `SeContainerInitializer` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject.se` | `newInstance()`, `initialize()`, `disableDiscovery()`, `addBeanClasses(...)`, `addPackages(...)`, `addExtensions(...)`, `setProperties(...)` |
| `SeContainer` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject.se` | `select(...)`, `getBeanManager()`, `isRunning()`, `close()` |

`SeContainer` extends `Instance<Object>` dan `AutoCloseable`, sehingga bisa dipakai untuk lookup bean dan ditutup via try-with-resources. CDI spec menyebut `SeContainer` menyediakan `close()`, `isRunning()`, dan `getBeanManager()`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

Contoh portable CDI SE:

```java
try (SeContainer container = SeContainerInitializer.newInstance()
        .initialize()) {

    CaseService service = container.select(CaseService.class).get();
    service.run();
}
```

---

# 11. `beans.xml` Cheat Sheet

## 11.1 Minimal Modern `beans.xml`

Path:

```text
src/main/resources/META-INF/beans.xml
```

Content:

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<beans xmlns="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee"
       xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
       xsi:schemaLocation="
         https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee
         https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee/beans_4_1.xsd"
       bean-discovery-mode="annotated"
       version="4.1">
</beans>
```

CDI 4.1 schema memakai namespace `https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee`, dan default `bean-discovery-mode` adalah `annotated`; CDI Full mendukung `all`, `annotated`, dan `none`, sedangkan CDI Lite lebih terbatas. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

## 11.2 `bean-discovery-mode`

| Mode | Arti | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|---|
| `annotated` | Hanya class dengan bean-defining annotation | Default recommended |
| `all` | Semua class dalam archive discan sebagai candidate | Hati-hati, CDI Full only |
| `none` | Archive bukan bean archive | Untuk disable discovery |

## 11.3 Enable Interceptor

```xml
<beans ... bean-discovery-mode="annotated" version="4.1">
    <interceptors>
        <class>com.company.audit.AuditInterceptor</class>
        <class>com.company.transaction.TransactionInterceptor</class>
    </interceptors>
</beans>
```

Interceptor juga bisa enabled dengan `@Priority`; interceptor enabled via `@Priority` dipanggil sebelum interceptor yang enabled via `beans.xml`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/cdi/4.1/jakarta-cdi-spec-4.1))

## 11.4 Enable Decorator

```xml
<decorators>
    <class>com.company.payment.AuditingPaymentClient</class>
</decorators>
```

## 11.5 Enable Alternative

```xml
<alternatives>
    <class>com.company.payment.FakePaymentClient</class>
</alternatives>
```

## 11.6 Scan Exclude

```xml
<scan>
    <exclude name="com.company.generated.*"/>
</scan>
```

---

# 12. Weld-specific Classes

## 12.1 Weld SE Bootstrap

| Class | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `Weld` | `org.jboss.weld.environment.se` | Builder/bootstrap Weld SE |
| `WeldContainer` | `org.jboss.weld.environment.se` | Running Weld SE container |
| `WeldInstance<T>` | `org.jboss.weld.inject` | Weld extension dari `Instance<T>` |
| `WeldManager` | `org.jboss.weld.manager.api` | Weld-specific manager/SPI |

Weld SE menyediakan bootstrap API dengan `Weld` dan `WeldContainer`; `WeldContainer` implements `AutoCloseable` sehingga aman dipakai dengan try-with-resources. Weld builder bisa `disableDiscovery()`, `packages(...)`, `beanClasses(...)`, `interceptors(...)`, `property(...)`, `containerId(...)`, lalu `initialize()`. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest/en-US/html_single/))

Contoh:

```java
import org.jboss.weld.environment.se.Weld;
import org.jboss.weld.environment.se.WeldContainer;

public class Main {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        try (WeldContainer container = new Weld()
                .disableDiscovery()
                .packages(Main.class)
                .interceptors(AuditInterceptor.class)
                .property("org.jboss.weld.proxy.dump", "/tmp/weld-proxies")
                .initialize()) {

            container.select(CaseApplication.class)
                    .get()
                    .start();
        }
    }
}
```

Important methods:

| Class | Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `Weld` | `initialize()` | Start container |
| `Weld` | `shutdown()` | Shutdown containers dari builder |
| `Weld` | `disableDiscovery()` | Disable automatic scanning |
| `Weld` | `beanClasses(...)` | Register classes explicitly |
| `Weld` | `packages(...)` | Register package(s) |
| `Weld` | `interceptors(...)` | Register interceptor classes |
| `Weld` | `decorators(...)` | Register decorator classes |
| `Weld` | `alternatives(...)` | Register alternatives |
| `Weld` | `property(key, value)` | Set Weld-specific property |
| `Weld` | `containerId(id)` | Named independent container |
| `WeldContainer` | `select(...)` | Lookup bean |
| `WeldContainer` | `event()` | Fire events |
| `WeldContainer` | `getBeanManager()` | Access BeanManager |
| `WeldContainer` | `shutdown()` / `close()` | Shutdown |

---

## 12.2 Weld Bound Contexts

| Class | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `BoundRequestContext` | `org.jboss.weld.context.bound` | Request context outside servlet request |
| `BoundSessionContext` | `org.jboss.weld.context.bound` | Session context bound to custom storage |
| `BoundConversationContext` | `org.jboss.weld.context.bound` | Conversation context bound to custom storage |

Weld docs menyebut `BoundRequestContext` bisa dipakai untuk menyediakan request scope di luar Servlet request atau EJB invocation. Ini berguna untuk worker/thread/test, tapi harus sangat disiplin activate/deactivate/cleanup. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest/en-US/html_single/))

Typical shape:

```java
@Inject
BoundRequestContext requestContext;

public void runWithRequestContext(Runnable work) {
    Map<String, Object> storage = new HashMap<>();

    requestContext.associate(storage);
    requestContext.activate();

    try {
        work.run();
    } finally {
        requestContext.invalidate();
        requestContext.deactivate();
        requestContext.dissociate(storage);
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

```text
BoundRequestContext adalah Weld-specific.
Untuk async worker production, sering lebih aman memakai explicit snapshot
daripada mengaktifkan CDI request scope manual.
```

---

## 12.3 Weld Servlet Integration Classes

| Class | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `ConversationFilter` | `org.jboss.weld.servlet` | CDI Conversation Filter |
| `WeldInitialListener` | `org.jboss.weld.servlet` | Bootstrap/listener servlet integration |
| `WeldTerminalListener` | `org.jboss.weld.servlet` | Cleanup/listener integration |

Untuk Jersey-on-Servlet/Tomcat/Jetty integration, biasanya framework/dependency integration yang mendaftarkan listener/filter ini, bukan kamu manual kecuali deployment custom.

---

# 13. Weld Configuration Properties

Weld punya banyak property vendor-specific. Pakai hanya saat perlu, jangan jadikan default tanpa alasan. Weld docs mencantumkan property seperti executor thread pool, non-portable mode, proxy/final-method handling, resolution cache, proxy dump, conversation timeout, dan optimasi lain. ([JBoss Docs](https://docs.jboss.org/weld/reference/latest/en-US/html_single/))

## 13.1 Bootstrap / Executor

| Property | Default / Value | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `org.jboss.weld.executor.threadPoolType` | `FIXED`, `FIXED_TIMEOUT`, `NONE`, `SINGLE_THREAD`, `COMMON` | Thread pool bootstrap Weld |
| `org.jboss.weld.executor.threadPoolSize` | available processors | Jumlah thread deployment/loading |
| `org.jboss.weld.executor.threadPoolKeepAliveTime` | 60s | Keep alive untuk `FIXED_TIMEOUT` |
| `org.jboss.weld.executor.threadPoolDebug` | `false` | Print debug timing |

## 13.2 Compatibility / Debug

| Property | Default | Fungsi | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `org.jboss.weld.nonPortableMode` | `false` | Non-portable behavior for legacy extensions | Hindari di production |
| `org.jboss.weld.proxy.ignoreFinalMethods` | unset | Regex class yang final methods-nya diabaikan proxy | Darurat/legacy only |
| `org.jboss.weld.proxy.dump` | unset | Dump generated proxy bytecode ke filesystem | Debug only |
| `org.jboss.weld.resolution.cacheSize` | `65536` | Bound cache resolver injection point | Tuning advanced |
| `org.jboss.weld.injection.injectableReferenceOptimization` | `false` | Optimasi lookup injectable reference | Bisa memengaruhi destroy context |
| `org.jboss.weld.serialization.beanIdentifierIndexOptimization` | `true` / false in weld-servlet | Optimasi session replication | Advanced |

## 13.3 Conversation

| Property | Default | Fungsi |
|---|---:|---|
| `org.jboss.weld.conversation.timeout` | `600000` ms | Timeout conversation |
| `org.jboss.weld.conversation.concurrentAccessTimeout` | `1000` ms | Timeout lock concurrent conversation |

## 13.4 Discovery / Unused Beans / Legacy

| Property | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `org.jboss.weld.bootstrap.vetoTypesWithoutBeanDefiningAnnotation` | Regex untuk veto annotated type tanpa bean-defining annotation |
| `org.jboss.weld.bootstrap.unusedBeans.excludeType` | Konfigurasi eliminasi unused beans |
| `org.jboss.weld.environment.servlet.emptyBeansXmlModeAll` | Servlet legacy empty `beans.xml` treated as `all` |
| `Weld.LEGACY_EMPTY_BEANS_XML_TREATMENT` | Weld SE equivalent legacy behavior |

Contoh Weld SE property:

```java
try (WeldContainer container = new Weld()
        .property("org.jboss.weld.nonPortableMode", false)
        .property("org.jboss.weld.resolution.cacheSize", 32768)
        .initialize()) {
    // app
}
```

---

# 14. Important SPI / Extension Classes

## 14.1 Portable Extension

| Class/Event | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `Extension` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject.spi` | Marker portable extension |
| `BeforeBeanDiscovery` | SPI | Sebelum bean discovery |
| `AfterTypeDiscovery` | SPI | Setelah type discovery |
| `ProcessAnnotatedType<T>` | SPI | Modifikasi/veto annotated type |
| `ProcessBean<T>` | SPI | Observe discovered bean |
| `ProcessInjectionPoint<T, X>` | SPI | Inspect/modify injection point |
| `ProcessProducer<T, X>` | SPI | Inspect/modify producer |
| `ProcessObserverMethod<T, X>` | SPI | Inspect observer method |
| `AfterBeanDiscovery` | SPI | Register beans/contexts/observers |
| `AfterDeploymentValidation` | SPI | Setelah deployment validation |
| `BeforeShutdown` | SPI | Sebelum container shutdown |

Contoh portable extension skeleton:

```java
public class MyExtension implements Extension {

    void observe(@Observes ProcessAnnotatedType<?> event) {
        // inspect/veto types
    }

    void afterBeanDiscovery(@Observes AfterBeanDiscovery event) {
        // register custom bean/context/observer
    }
}
```

## 14.2 Build Compatible Extensions

CDI 4 memperkenalkan build-compatible extension model untuk CDI Lite/Full, dengan fase seperti discovery/enhancement/registration/synthesis/validation. Gunakan ini hanya jika kamu benar-benar membuat framework/library CDI modern, bukan application code biasa.

---

# 15. Exception Cheat Sheet

| Exception | Package | Biasanya Terjadi Karena |
|---|---|---|
| `UnsatisfiedResolutionException` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Tidak ada bean dengan type/qualifier yang diminta |
| `AmbiguousResolutionException` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Lebih dari satu bean cocok |
| `ContextNotActiveException` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Akses `@RequestScoped`/scope lain saat context tidak aktif |
| `CreationException` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Error saat create bean |
| `InjectionException` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Error injection |
| `DeploymentException` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject.spi` | Deployment problem CDI |
| `DefinitionException` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject.spi` | Definisi bean/producer/interceptor salah |
| `UnproxyableResolutionException` | `jakarta.enterprise.inject` | Bean normal-scoped tidak bisa diproxy |
| `BusyConversationException` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Concurrent conversation access |
| `NonexistentConversationException` | `jakarta.enterprise.context` | Conversation ID tidak ada |

---

# 16. Practical Patterns

## 16.1 Qualifier untuk Multiple Implementation

```java
@Qualifier
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ FIELD, PARAMETER, METHOD, TYPE })
public @interface PrimaryDb {
}
```

```java
@Produces
@PrimaryDb
@ApplicationScoped
public DataSource primaryDataSource() {
    return buildPrimary();
}
```

```java
@Inject
public CaseRepository(@PrimaryDb DataSource dataSource) {
    this.dataSource = dataSource;
}
```

---

## 16.2 Producer + Disposer

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class KafkaProducerFactory {

    @Produces
    @ApplicationScoped
    public KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> kafkaProducer(AppConfig config) {
        return new KafkaProducer<>(props(config));
    }

    public void close(@Disposes KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer) {
        producer.close();
    }
}
```

---

## 16.3 `Instance<T>` untuk Optional/Dynamic Bean

```java
@ApplicationScoped
public class OptionalFeatureClient {

    private final Instance<FeatureFlagClient> clients;

    @Inject
    public OptionalFeatureClient(Instance<FeatureFlagClient> clients) {
        this.clients = clients;
    }

    public boolean enabled(String key) {
        if (!clients.isResolvable()) {
            return false;
        }

        return clients.get().enabled(key);
    }
}
```

---

## 16.4 CDI Event

```java
@Inject
Event<AuditEvent> auditEvents;

public void approve(String caseId) {
    auditEvents.fire(new AuditEvent("CASE_APPROVE", caseId));
}
```

```java
public void onAudit(@Observes AuditEvent event) {
    auditRepository.insert(event);
}
```

---

## 16.5 Interceptor Binding

```java
@InterceptorBinding
@Retention(RUNTIME)
@Target({ TYPE, METHOD })
public @interface TransactionalBoundary {
}
```

```java
@TransactionalBoundary
@Interceptor
@Priority(Interceptor.Priority.APPLICATION + 400)
public class TransactionInterceptor {

    @AroundInvoke
    public Object around(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
        // begin
        try {
            Object result = ctx.proceed();
            // commit
            return result;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            // rollback
            throw ex;
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 16.6 Manual Request Context Control

Portable option:

```java
@Inject
RequestContextController requestContextController;

public void run() {
    requestContextController.activate();
    try {
        // work requiring request context
    } finally {
        requestContextController.deactivate();
    }
}
```

Practical warning: untuk async/multithread production, lebih aman memakai **snapshot context** daripada mengaktifkan request scope manual, kecuali kamu benar-benar memahami lifecycle dan cleanup.

---

# 17. Recommended Defaults untuk Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

| Area | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Bean discovery | `bean-discovery-mode="annotated"` |
| Service | `@ApplicationScoped`, stateless |
| Repository | `@ApplicationScoped`, no mutable request state |
| Request context | `@RequestScoped CurrentRequestContext` |
| Config | `@ApplicationScoped AppConfig` via producer |
| Secret | `@ApplicationScoped SecretProvider` |
| MyBatis `SqlSessionFactory` | `@ApplicationScoped` |
| MyBatis `SqlSession` | request/transaction-bound, never singleton |
| External clients | `@ApplicationScoped`, close with disposer if needed |
| Interceptor | `@Priority`, explicit ordering |
| Qualifier | Semantic role: `@PrimaryDb`, `@ReadReplica`, `@PaymentGateway` |
| Alternatives | Use for test/profile replacement |
| Events | Use for in-process decoupling, not durable integration |
| Kafka/outbox | Use DB outbox for durable event integration |
| Async | Use context snapshot, not `@RequestScoped` bean directly |

---

# 18. Anti-pattern Checklist

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya |
|---|---|
| Field injection everywhere | Sulit test, dependency tersembunyi |
| Semua class `@ApplicationScoped` | Request state leak/race |
| Mutable field di `@ApplicationScoped` service | Cross-request tenant/user leak |
| Manual `new` untuk CDI bean | Injection/interceptor/lifecycle tidak jalan |
| `@RequestScoped` di async worker | Context not active |
| No qualifier untuk multiple implementation | Ambiguous dependency |
| Producer tanpa disposer untuk resource | Resource leak |
| Secret/config dibaca langsung via `System.getenv()` di service | Tidak auditable/testable |
| Interceptor method self-invocation | Interceptor tidak terpanggil |
| Final class/method untuk intercepted normal-scoped bean | Proxy/interceptor bermasalah |
| Events dipakai untuk durable messaging | CDI event tidak menggantikan Kafka/outbox |
| Weld-specific API tersebar di business code | Vendor lock-in tinggi |
| `bean-discovery-mode="all"` tanpa alasan | Startup lambat/bean tak sengaja |

---

# 20. Ringkasan Praktis

Untuk aplikasi Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, 80% kebutuhan CDI/Weld biasanya cukup dengan:

```text
@Inject
@ApplicationScoped
@RequestScoped
@Dependent
@Qualifier
@Produces
@Disposes
@Any
@Default
Instance<T>
Event<T>
@Observes
@InterceptorBinding
@Interceptor
@AroundInvoke
@Priority
@PostConstruct
@PreDestroy
beans.xml bean-discovery-mode="annotated"
```

Weld-specific yang paling sering berguna:

```text
Weld
WeldContainer
.disableDiscovery()
.beanClasses(...)
.packages(...)
.interceptors(...)
.property(...)
.initialize()
.shutdown()

BoundRequestContext
.associate(...)
.activate()
.invalidate()
.deactivate()
.dissociate(...)
```

Untuk production-grade microservice, prinsip paling penting:

```text
CDI untuk composition/lifecycle.
JAX-RS filter untuk HTTP request pipeline.
Interceptor untuk business method cross-cutting.
Producer untuk external resources.
Qualifier untuk multiple implementation.
RequestScoped untuk request metadata.
ApplicationScoped harus stateless/thread-safe.
Weld-specific API jangan bocor ke business logic kecuali memang intentional.
```
